8\. MeshLoaderSystem â†’ PhysicsSystemï¼ˆCCT ãŒ velocity Ã— dt ã§ç§»å‹•ï¼‰

\## 5. èª°ãŒä½•ã‚’æ›¸ãã‹ï¼ˆPlayer ç§»å‹•ã®ä½œã‚Šç›´ã—ã§æ±ºå®šä¸­ï¼‰

===== main.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\main.cpp 
#include <time.h>

// ‹N“®‚·‚éApp
// RUN_GAME_APP ‚Í GameApp ‚ÌƒvƒƒWƒFƒNƒgİ’è(ƒvƒŠƒvƒƒZƒbƒT‚Ì’è‹`)‚Å’è‹`‚³‚ê‚é
// ‚±‚Ìƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚Ì’†‚Å‚Í’è‹`‚µ‚È‚¢‚±‚Æ

#ifdef RUN_GAME_APP
#include "App/GameApp.h"
using AppType = GameApp;
#else
#include "App/EditorApp.h"
using AppType = EditorApp;
#endif


#define APPLICATION_NAME L"X3DGP"

// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEƒTƒCƒY
#define SCREEN_WIDTH 1280
#define SCREEN_HEIGHT 720

LRESULT CALLBACK window_procedure(HWND hwnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wparam, LPARAM lparam)
{
	Application* p{ reinterpret_cast<Application*>(GetWindowLongPtr(hwnd, GWLP_USERDATA)) };
	return p ? p->HandleMessage(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam) : DefWindowProc(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam);
}

int WINAPI WinMain(_In_ HINSTANCE instance, _In_opt_  HINSTANCE prev_instance, _In_ LPSTR cmd_line, _In_ int cmd_show)
{
	srand(static_cast<unsigned int>(time(nullptr)));

#if defined(DEBUG) | defined(_DEBUG)
	_CrtSetDbgFlag(_CRTDBG_ALLOC_MEM_DF | _CRTDBG_LEAK_CHECK_DF);
	//_CrtSetBreakAlloc(####);
#endif

	WNDCLASSEXW wcex{};
	wcex.cbSize = sizeof(WNDCLASSEX);
	wcex.style = CS_HREDRAW | CS_VREDRAW;
	wcex.lpfnWndProc = window_procedure;
	wcex.cbClsExtra = 0;
	wcex.cbWndExtra = 0;
	wcex.hInstance = instance;
	wcex.hIcon = 0;
	wcex.hCursor = LoadCursor(NULL, IDC_ARROW);
	wcex.hbrBackground = (HBRUSH)(COLOR_WINDOW + 1);
	wcex.lpszMenuName = NULL;
	wcex.lpszClassName = APPLICATION_NAME;
	wcex.hIconSm = 0;
	RegisterClassExW(&wcex);

	RECT rc{ 0, 0, SCREEN_WIDTH, SCREEN_HEIGHT };
	AdjustWindowRect(&rc, WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW, FALSE);

	//HWND hwnd = CreateWindowExW(0, APPLICATION_NAME, L"",
	//	WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW ^ WS_MAXIMIZEBOX ^ WS_THICKFRAME | WS_VISIBLE,
	//	CW_USEDEFAULT, CW_USEDEFAULT,
	//	rc.right - rc.left, rc.bottom - rc.top, NULL, NULL, instance, NULL);

	HWND hwnd = CreateWindowExW(0, APPLICATION_NAME, L"",
		WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW | WS_VISIBLE,
		CW_USEDEFAULT, CW_USEDEFAULT,
		rc.right - rc.left, rc.bottom - rc.top, NULL, NULL, instance, NULL);

	ShowWindow(hwnd, cmd_show);

	AppType app(hwnd);
	app.Initialize();
	SetWindowLongPtrW(hwnd, GWLP_USERDATA, reinterpret_cast<LONG_PTR>(&app));
	return app.Run();
}

// ƒGƒfƒBƒ^‚ÌƒAƒvƒŠƒP[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“
class EditorApp : public Application
{

	using Application::Application;

	bool IsEditorActive() const;

	// ƒfƒoƒbƒOƒJƒƒ‰‚ÌXV‚ÆADebug/Game‚ÌØ‚è‘Ö‚¦
	void UpdateDebugCamera(float elapsedTime);

	// Studio‚ğŠJ‚­
	void OpenStudioWindow(const std::string& assetPath = "", const std::string& graphPath = "");

	// ƒƒjƒ…[ƒo[‚Ì’†g
	void DrawMainMenu();

	// ƒƒjƒ…[‚ª—§‚Ä‚½AƒV[ƒ“‚Ì•Û‘¶E“Ç‚İ‚İƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg‚ğÀs‚·‚é
	void ProcessSceneRequest();

	ImGuiManager imguiManager;
	EditorContext editorContext;
	ViewportWindow viewportWindow;
	DebugCameraSystem debugCameraSystem;

	ProjectWindow   projectWindow;
	HierarchyWindow hierarchyWindow;

	PrefabWindow    prefabWindow;
	LogWindow       logWindow;

	MovementMonitorWindow movementMonitor;

	StudioSharedContext studioShared;
	std::vector<std::unique_ptr<StudioWindow>> studioWindows;
	int nextStudioWindowId = 0;

	// Open‚ÌŠm”Fƒ|ƒbƒvƒAƒbƒv‚ÅŠJ‚­ƒpƒX
	std::string pendingOpenPath;

	// F1‚ÅƒQ[ƒ€’P‘Ì‚ÌŒ©‚½–Ú‚ğƒvƒŒƒrƒ…[
	bool previewRelease = false;
};

===== EditorApp.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\App\EditorApp.cpp 
#include "EditorApp.h"
#include "tracy/Tracy.hpp"
#include <filesystem>
#include <algorithm>

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/ControllerComponent.h"
#include "Engine/Resource/SceneSerializer.h"
#include "Game/Scene/SceneGame.h"
#include "Game/ECS/GameComponents.h"
#include "Tool/Editor/ComponentRegistryInitializer.h"
#include "Tool/Editor/FileDialog.h"
#include "GameTool/GameInspectors.h"

// ‰Šú‰»
void EditorApp::OnInitialize()
{
	// ImGuiManager‰Šú‰»
	imguiManager.Initialize(hwnd, graphics.GetDevice(), graphics.GetDeviceContext());

	// Viewport‚ÆƒfƒoƒbƒOƒJƒƒ‰
	viewportWindow.Initialize();

	// Project‚ÌƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^(Engine‚Ì‚à‚Ì‚ÍŠî”Õ‚ª“o˜^Ï‚İ)
	GameComponents::Register();

	// ƒCƒ“ƒXƒyƒNƒ^[‚Ì“Æ©•`‰æ‚Ì“o˜^
	ComponentRegistryInitializer::Initialize();
	GameInspectors::Register();

	sceneManager.ChangeScene(std::make_unique<SceneGame>());

	// ProjectWindow‰Šú‰»
	projectWindow.Initialize("Data");
	projectWindow.LoadIcons(graphics.GetDevice());
	// PrefabWindow‰Šú‰»
	prefabWindow.Initialize("Data/Prefab");

	// Studio‹¤—LƒŠƒ\[ƒX‰Šú‰»
	studioShared.Initialize(graphics.GetDevice());
}

// ƒV[ƒ“XV‚Ì‘O
void EditorApp::OnPreUpdate(float elapsedTime)
{
	// F1‚ÅƒQ[ƒ€’P‘Ì‚ÌŒ©‚½–Ú(ImGui–³‚µ)‚ğƒvƒŒƒrƒ…[Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦
	static bool prevF1 = false;
	bool currF1 = (GetAsyncKeyState(VK_F1) & 0x8000) != 0;
	if (currF1 && !prevF1)
	{
		previewRelease = !previewRelease;
	}
	prevF1 = currF1;

	// ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ğ‰Ÿ‚µ‚½uŠÔAGame‚Ö‚Ì“ü—Í‚ª—LŒø‚Å‚ ‚ê‚ÎDebugƒ‚[ƒh‚ÉØ‚è‘Ö‚¦
	static bool prevRButton = false;
	bool currRButton = (GetAsyncKeyState(VK_RBUTTON) & 0x8000) != 0;
	if (currRButton && !prevRButton && IsGameInputActive())
	{
		editorContext.cameraMode = CameraMode::Debug;
	}
	prevRButton = currRButton;
}

// ƒV[ƒ“XV‚ÌŒã
void EditorApp::OnPostUpdate(float elapsedTime)
{
	// ƒfƒoƒbƒOƒJƒƒ‰XV(ƒV[ƒ“‚ÌŒã‚És‚¤)
	UpdateDebugCamera(elapsedTime);

	// ˆÚ“®‚Ì‹L˜^
	if (auto* scene = sceneManager.GetCurrentScene())
	{
		movementMonitor.Record(scene->GetRegistry(), elapsedTime);
	}

	// StudioXV
	for (auto& win : studioWindows)
	{
		win->Update(elapsedTime);
	}
}

// –ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€1‰ñ(ImGui)
void EditorApp::OnFrame()
{
	ZoneScoped;

	// ƒvƒŒƒrƒ…[’†‚ÍImGui©‘Ì‚ğˆêØg‚í‚È‚¢
	if (!IsEditorActive()) return;

	// ImGuiƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ŠJn
	imguiManager.BeginFrame();

	// GameƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE(Viewport‚ÆƒhƒbƒLƒ“ƒOƒŒƒCƒAƒEƒg)
	viewportWindow.Draw(gameView, [this]() { DrawMainMenu(); });

	if (auto* scene = sceneManager.GetCurrentScene())
	{
		projectWindow.Draw(scene->GetRegistry(), editorContext);
		hierarchyWindow.Draw(scene->GetRegistry(), editorContext);
		inspectorWindow.Draw(scene->GetRegistry(), editorContext);
		prefabWindow.Draw(scene->GetRegistry(), editorContext);
	}
	// ƒV[ƒ“‚ª–³‚­‚Ä‚àAƒƒO‚ÍŒ©‚ç‚ê‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
	logWindow.Draw();
	// Às’†‚ÉŠm”F‚·‚éƒc[ƒ‹
	movementMonitor.Draw();

	// StudioOpenRequest‚ğˆ—
	if (editorContext.studioRequest.pending)
	{
		OpenStudioWindow(editorContext.studioRequest.assetPath, editorContext.studioRequest.graphPath);
		editorContext.studioRequest.pending = false;
		editorContext.studioRequest.assetPath = "";
		editorContext.studioRequest.graphPath = "";
	}
	for (auto& win : studioWindows)
	{
		std::string title = "Studio##" + std::to_string(win->GetWindowId());
		win->DrawWindow(title.c_str());
	}
	// •Â‚¶‚éƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg‚ªo‚½ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚ğíœ
	studioWindows.erase(
		std::remove_if(studioWindows.begin(), studioWindows.end(),
			[](const std::unique_ptr<StudioWindow>& w)
			{
				return w->IsCloseRequested();
			}),
		studioWindows.end()
	);

	// ‘S‚Ä‚ÌƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚Ì•`‰æŒã‚ÉƒV[ƒ“•Û‘¶E“Ç‚İ‚İ‚ğÀs‚·‚é
	ProcessSceneRequest();

	// Open‚ÌŠm”Fƒ|ƒbƒvƒAƒbƒv
	if (ImGui::BeginPopupModal("Open Scene##Confirm", nullptr, ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize))
	{
		ImGui::Text("Current scene will be discarded.\nAre you sure you want to continue?");
		ImGui::Spacing();

		if (ImGui::Button("Open", ImVec2(120, 0)))
		{
			Scene* scene = sceneManager.GetCurrentScene();
			if (scene)
			{
				// ”jŠü‚·‚éŒÃ‚¢Entity‚Ö‚ÌQÆ‚ªc‚ç‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤A“Ç‚İ‚İ‚Ì‘O‚ÉA‘I‘ğE–¼‘Oƒ}ƒbƒv‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg‚·‚é
				editorContext.Deselect();
				editorContext.entityNames.clear();

				auto setName = [this](entt::entity e, const std::string& name) { editorContext.SetName(e, name); };

				if (SceneSerializer::LoadFromFile(scene->GetRegistry(), pendingOpenPath, setName))
				{
					editorContext.currentScenePath = pendingOpenPath;
				}
			}
			ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
		}
		ImGui::SameLine();
		if (ImGui::Button("Cancel", ImVec2(120, 0)))
		{
			ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}
}

// GameView‚É•`‚¢‚½ƒQ[ƒ€‚ğA‰æ–Ê‚Öo‚·
void EditorApp::OnPresent()
{
	if (IsEditorActive())
	{
		// Studio•`‰æ
		{
			ZoneScopedN("Studio::Render");
			for (auto& win : studioWindows)
			{
				win->Render();
			}
		}

		// Studio‚ª•`‰ææ‚ğ•Ï‚¦‚é‚Ì‚ÅAƒƒCƒ“RTV‚ğÄƒZƒbƒg
		graphics.SetRenderTargets();

		// ImGui•`‰æ(GameView‚ÌƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ‚àA‚±‚±‚ÅViewportŒo—R‚Å•\¦‚³‚ê‚é)
		{
			ZoneScopedN("ImGui::EndFrame");
			imguiManager.EndFrame();
		}
	}
	else
	{
		// ƒvƒŒƒrƒ…[’†FƒQ[ƒ€‚ÌƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ‚ğ‰æ–Ê‘S‘Ì‚É“\‚é
		ZoneScopedN("PresentTexture");
		graphics.PresentTexture(gameView.GetSRV());
	}
}

// ƒƒbƒZ[ƒW
bool EditorApp::OnMessage(HWND hwnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wparam, LPARAM lparam)
{
	// ImGuiƒƒbƒZ[ƒWˆ—
	return imguiManager.HandleMessage(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam);
}

// •`‰æ‚Ég‚¤ƒJƒƒ‰(Debug‚È‚çƒfƒoƒbƒOƒJƒƒ‰AGame‚È‚çƒQ[ƒ€ƒJƒƒ‰)
Camera& EditorApp::GetRenderCamera()
{
	return (editorContext.cameraMode == CameraMode::Debug)
		? viewportWindow.GetDebugCamera()
		: gameView.GetCamera();
}

// Game‚Ö‚Ì“ü—Í‚ğó‚¯•t‚¯‚Ä‚æ‚¢‚©(ƒGƒfƒBƒ^•\¦’†‚ÍViewport‚Éƒ}ƒEƒX‚ª‚ ‚é‚Æ‚«‚¾‚¯)
bool EditorApp::IsGameInputActive() const
{
	return IsEditorActive() ? viewportWindow.IsViewportHovered() : true;
}

// ƒGƒfƒBƒ^‚ÌUI‚ğ•\¦‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é‚©
bool EditorApp::IsEditorActive() const
{
	return !previewRelease;
}

// ƒfƒoƒbƒOƒJƒƒ‰‚ÌXV‚ÆADebug/Game‚ÌØ‚è‘Ö‚¦
void EditorApp::UpdateDebugCamera(float elapsedTime)
{
	if (editorContext.cameraMode != CameraMode::Debug) return;

	Scene* scene = sceneManager.GetCurrentScene();
	if (!scene) return;
	entt::registry& registry = scene->GetRegistry();

	debugCameraSystem.Update(registry, elapsedTime, IsGameInputActive());
	debugCameraSystem.ApplyToCamera(registry, viewportWindow.GetDebugCamera());

	// Game‚Ö‚Ì“ü—Í‚ª—LŒø‚Å‚È‚¯‚ê‚ÎAØ‚è‘Ö‚¦‚Ís‚í‚È‚¢
	if (!IsGameInputActive()) return;

	// ƒXƒeƒBƒbƒN“ü—Í‚ª‚ ‚ê‚ÎGameƒ‚[ƒh‚É–ß‚·
	// (Project‚Ì“ü—Í‚Å‚Í‚È‚­AEngine‚ÌƒRƒ“ƒgƒ[ƒ‰[‚Ì¶‚Ì’l‚ğŒ©‚é)
	const float threshold = 0.2f;
	auto view = registry.view<ControllerComponent>();
	for (auto entity : view)
	{
		const auto& ctrl = view.get<ControllerComponent>(entity);
		if (fabsf(ctrl.leftStickX) > threshold ||
			fabsf(ctrl.leftStickY) > threshold ||
			fabsf(ctrl.rightStickX) > threshold ||
			fabsf(ctrl.rightStickY) > threshold)
		{
			editorContext.cameraMode = CameraMode::Game;
			break;
		}
	}
}

// Studio‚ğŠJ‚­
void EditorApp::OpenStudioWindow(const std::string& assetPath, const std::string& graphPath)
{
	auto win = std::make_unique<StudioWindow>();
	win->Initialize(graphics.GetDevice(), &studioShared);
	win->GetContext().deviceContext = graphics.GetDeviceContext();
	win->SetWindowId(nextStudioWindowId++);

	// ƒpƒX‚ªw’è‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚½‚çƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚ğƒ[ƒh
	if (!assetPath.empty())
	{
		win->LoadAsset(assetPath);
	}

	// ƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ÌƒpƒX‚ªw’è‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚½‚ç•R•t‚¯ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
	if (!graphPath.empty())
	{
		win->GetContext().attachedGraphPath = graphPath;
	}

	studioWindows.push_back(std::move(win));
}

// ƒƒjƒ…[ƒo[‚Ì’†g
void EditorApp::DrawMainMenu()
{
	if (ImGui::BeginMenu("File"))
	{
		if (ImGui::MenuItem("Save"))
		{
			editorContext.sceneRequest = SceneAction::Save;
		}
		if (ImGui::MenuItem("Save As..."))
		{
			editorContext.sceneRequest = SceneAction::SaveAs;
		}
		if (ImGui::MenuItem("Open Scene..."))
		{
			editorContext.sceneRequest = SceneAction::Open;
		}

		ImGui::Separator();

		if (ImGui::BeginMenu("System"))
		{
			ImGui::MenuItem("VSync", nullptr, &vsync);
			ImGui::EndMenu();
		}
		if (ImGui::BeginMenu("Tool"))
		{
			ImGui::MenuItem("Movement Monitor", nullptr, &movementMonitor.isOpen);
			ImGui::EndMenu();
		}

		ImGui::EndMenu();
	}

	// ¡ŠJ‚¢‚Ä‚¢‚éƒV[ƒ“iSave‚Ìã‘‚«æj
	std::string sceneName = editorContext.currentScenePath.empty()
		? "(unsaved)"
		: std::filesystem::path(editorContext.currentScenePath).filename().string();
	ImGui::TextDisabled("Scene: %s", sceneName.c_str());
}

// ƒV[ƒ“‚Ì•Û‘¶E“Ç‚İ‚İƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg‚ğÀs‚·‚é
void EditorApp::ProcessSceneRequest()
{
	SceneAction action = editorContext.sceneRequest;
	if (action == SceneAction::None) return;
	editorContext.sceneRequest = SceneAction::None;

	Scene* scene = sceneManager.GetCurrentScene();
	if (!scene) return;

	if (action == SceneAction::Open)
	{
		timer.stop();
		std::string path;
		bool selected = FileDialog::OpenScene(hwnd, path);
		timer.start();

		if (selected)
		{
			// Šù‘¶‚ÌEntity‚ğ”jŠü‚·‚é‚½‚ßAŠm”Fƒ|ƒbƒvƒAƒbƒv‚ğŸ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ÅŠJ‚­
			pendingOpenPath = path;
			ImGui::OpenPopup("Open Scene##Confirm");
		}
		return;
	}

	std::string path = editorContext.currentScenePath;

	// Save AsA‚Ü‚½‚Í‚Ü‚¾•Û‘¶æ‚ª–³‚¢Save‚ÍAƒ_ƒCƒAƒƒO‚Å•Û‘¶æ‚ğ‘I‚Ô
	if (action == SceneAction::SaveAs || path.empty())
	{
		timer.stop();
		bool selected = FileDialog::SaveScene(hwnd, path);
		timer.start();

		if (!selected) return; // ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹
	}

	auto getName = [this](entt::entity e) { return editorContext.GetName(e); };
	if (SceneSerializer::SaveToFile(scene->GetRegistry(), path, getName))
	{
		editorContext.currentScenePath = path;
	}
}

#include "GameApp.h"
#include "tracy/Tracy.hpp"
#include <fstream>
#include <json.hpp>

#include "Engine/Core/Log.h"
#include "Engine/Resource/SceneSerializer.h"
#include "Game/Scene/SceneGame.h"
#include "Game/ECS/GameComponents.h"

namespace
{
	// ƒvƒƒWƒFƒNƒgİ’èƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹
	const char* kProjectSettingsPath = "Data/ProjectSettings.json";
}

// ‰Šú‰»
void GameApp::OnInitialize()
{
	// Project‚ÌƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^(Engine‚Ì‚à‚Ì‚ÍŠî”Õ‚ª“o˜^Ï‚İ)
	GameComponents::Register();

	sceneManager.ChangeScene(std::make_unique<SceneGame>());

	// Å‰‚É“Ç‚İ‚ŞƒV[ƒ“
	startScenePath = LoadStartScenePath();

#ifndef _DEBUG
	// ƒŠƒŠ[ƒX‚Íƒ{[ƒ_[ƒŒƒXƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“‚Å‹N“®
	graphics.ToggleFullscreen();
#endif
}

// ƒV[ƒ“XV‚Ì‘O
void GameApp::OnPreUpdate(float elapsedTime)
{
	// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚Ìc‰¡”ä‚É‡‚í‚¹‚ÄAƒQ[ƒ€ƒJƒƒ‰‚ÌƒvƒƒWƒFƒNƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ğXV‚·‚é
	RECT rc{};
	GetClientRect(hwnd, &rc);
	float width = static_cast<float>(rc.right - rc.left);
	float height = static_cast<float>(rc.bottom - rc.top);
	if (width > 0.0f && height > 0.0f)
	{
		gameView.SetAspect(width / height);
	}
}

// ƒV[ƒ“XV‚ÌŒã
void GameApp::OnPostUpdate(float elapsedTime)
{
	// SceneGame‚ÍÅ‰‚ÌƒV[ƒ“XV‚Å‰Šú‰»‚³‚ê‚é‚Ì‚ÅA‚»‚ÌŒã‚É1‰ñ‚¾‚¯“Ç‚İ‚Ş
	if (startSceneLoaded) return;

	Scene* scene = sceneManager.GetCurrentScene();
	if (!scene) return;

	startSceneLoaded = true;

	if (startScenePath.empty()) return;

	// ƒGƒ“ƒeƒBƒeƒB‚Ì–¼‘O‚ÍƒGƒfƒBƒ^ê—p‚È‚Ì‚ÅAó‚¯æ‚ç‚È‚¢
	if (!SceneSerializer::LoadFromFile(scene->GetRegistry(), startScenePath))
	{
		Log(u8"Å‰‚ÌƒV[ƒ“‚Ì“Ç‚İ‚İ‚É¸”s‚µ‚Ü‚µ‚½: %s", startScenePath.c_str());
	}
}

// GameView‚É•`‚¢‚½ƒQ[ƒ€‚ğA‰æ–Ê‘S‘Ì‚É“\‚é
void GameApp::OnPresent()
{
	ZoneScopedN("PresentTexture");
	graphics.PresentTexture(gameView.GetSRV());
}

// ƒvƒƒWƒFƒNƒgİ’è‚©‚çAÅ‰‚É“Ç‚İ‚ŞƒV[ƒ“‚ÌƒpƒX‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
std::string GameApp::LoadStartScenePath()
{
	std::ifstream file(kProjectSettingsPath);
	if (!file)
	{
		Log(u8"ƒvƒƒWƒFƒNƒgİ’è‚ªŒ©‚Â‚©‚è‚Ü‚¹‚ñ: %s", kProjectSettingsPath);
		return "";
	}

	nlohmann::json json = nlohmann::json::parse(file, nullptr, false);
	if (json.is_discarded() || !json.is_object())
	{
		Log(u8"ƒvƒƒWƒFƒNƒgİ’è‚ÌŒ`®‚ª³‚µ‚­‚ ‚è‚Ü‚¹‚ñ: %s", kProjectSettingsPath);
		return "";
	}

	std::string path = json.value("startScene", "");
	if (path.empty())
	{
		Log(u8"ƒvƒƒWƒFƒNƒgİ’è‚É startScene ‚ª‚ ‚è‚Ü‚¹‚ñ");
	}
	return path;
}

        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& eye,

    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& GetFront()         const { return front; }

===== JsonFileCache.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Core\JsonFileCache.h 
#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <functional>
#include <fstream>
#include <filesystem>
#include <json.hpp>

template<typename T>
class JsonFileCache
{
public:
	T& GetOrLoad(const std::string& path, const std::function<void(T&, const nlohmann::json&)>& loadFunc)
	{
		std::error_code ec;
		auto writeTime = std::filesystem::last_write_time(path, ec);

		auto it = cache.find(path);
		if (it != cache.end())
		{
			// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹©‘Ì‚ªÁ‚¦‚Ä‚¢‚éê‡‚ÍA¡‚ÌƒLƒƒƒbƒVƒ…‚ğ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü•Ô‚·
			if (!ec && writeTime == it->second.lastWriteTime)
			{
				return it->second.data;
			}
			// XV‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚½ê‡‚ÍAŠù‘¶‚ÌƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ‚ğˆê’UÁ‚µ‚ÄA‰º‚ÌV‹K“Ç‚İ‚İ‚Öi‚Ş
			cache.erase(it);
		}

		// ƒ}ƒbƒv‚Ì’†‚É’¼Ú\’z‚µ‚Ä‚©‚ç“Ç‚İ‚Ş
		auto result = cache.try_emplace(path);
		Entry& entry = result.first->second;
		entry.lastWriteTime = writeTime;

		std::ifstream ifs(path);
		if (ifs.is_open())
		{
			try
			{
				nlohmann::json j;
				ifs >> j;
				loadFunc(entry.data, j);
			}
			catch (const std::exception&) {}
		}

		return entry.data;
	}

private:
	struct Entry
	{
		T data;
		std::filesystem::file_time_type lastWriteTime;
	};

	std::unordered_map<std::string, Entry> cache;
}; 
 
===== Log.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Core\Log.h 
#pragma once
#include <Windows.h>
#include <cstdarg>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <deque>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

// ƒƒO‚Ì•Û‘¶æBƒƒbƒZ[ƒW‚ÍUTF-8‚Å‚Âi“ú–{Œê‚Í u8"..." ‚ğ•t‚¯‚Ä‘‚­j
namespace LogBuffer
{
	struct Entry
	{
		std::string time;    // "HH:MM:SS"
		std::string message; // UTF-8
	};

	namespace detail
	{
		struct Storage
		{
			std::mutex        mutex;
			std::deque<Entry> entries;
			uint64_t          version = 0; // ‘‚«‚İ‚Ì‚½‚Ñ‚É‘‚¦‚éi•\¦‘¤‚ªXV‚ğŒŸ’m‚·‚éj
		};

		// ŠÖ”“àstatic‚É‚µ‚ÄA‹N“®‚Ì‰Šú‰»‡‚Ì–â‘è‚ğ”ğ‚¯‚é
		inline Storage& GetStorage()
		{
			static Storage storage;
			return storage;
		}

		inline std::wstring Utf8ToWide(const std::string& text)
		{
			int length = MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, text.c_str(), -1, nullptr, 0);
			if (length <= 0) return std::wstring();

			std::wstring result(length, L'\0');
			MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, text.c_str(), -1, result.data(), length);
			return result;
		}
	}

	constexpr size_t MaxEntries = 2000; // ‚±‚ê‚ğ’´‚¦‚½‚çAŒÃ‚¢‚à‚Ì‚©‚çÌ‚Ä‚é

	inline void Add(const char* message)
	{
		SYSTEMTIME now;
		GetLocalTime(&now);
		char timestamp[16];
		snprintf(timestamp, sizeof(timestamp), "%02d:%02d:%02d", now.wHour, now.wMinute, now.wSecond);

		// Visual Studio‚Ìuo—ÍvƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE
		OutputDebugStringW(detail::Utf8ToWide(std::string(message) + "\n").c_str());

		// ƒƒ‚ƒŠã‚Ìƒoƒbƒtƒ@i«—ˆA•ÊƒXƒŒƒbƒh‚©‚ç‘‚©‚ê‚Ä‚àˆÀ‘S‚È‚æ‚¤‚ÉƒƒbƒN‚·‚éj
		auto& storage = detail::GetStorage();
		std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(storage.mutex);
		storage.entries.push_back({ timestamp, message });
		if (storage.entries.size() > MaxEntries)
		{
			storage.entries.pop_front();
		}
		++storage.version;
	}

	inline uint64_t Version()
	{
		auto& storage = detail::GetStorage();
		std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(storage.mutex);
		return storage.version;
	}

	inline std::vector<Entry> Snapshot()
	{
		auto& storage = detail::GetStorage();
		std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(storage.mutex);
		return std::vector<Entry>(storage.entries.begin(), storage.entries.end());
	}
}

// ƒƒO‚ğo‚·Bprintf‚Æ“¯‚¶‘®‚ªg‚¦‚é
// —áFLog(u8"ƒvƒŒƒnƒu‚ğ“Ç‚İ‚ß‚Ü‚¹‚ñ: %s", path.c_str());
inline void Log(const char* format, ...)
{
	char message[1024];
	va_list args;
	va_start(args, format);
	vsnprintf(message, sizeof(message), format, args);
	va_end(args);

	LogBuffer::Add(message);
}

            GetQueue().push_back(actor);

    {

#pragma once
#include <PxPhysicsAPI.h>


// ƒJƒeƒSƒ‰ƒCƒY
namespace PhysicsLayer
{
    constexpr physx::PxU32 Static    = 1 << 0; // Ã“I„‘Ì
    constexpr physx::PxU32 Dynamic   = 1 << 1; // “®“I„‘Ì
    constexpr physx::PxU32 Character = 1 << 2; // ƒLƒƒƒ‰ƒNƒ^[ƒRƒ“ƒgƒ[ƒ‰[

    // PxFilterDataì¬
    inline physx::PxFilterData MakeFilterData(physx::PxU32 layer)
    {
        return physx::PxFilterData(layer, 0, 0, 0);
    }
}

// ƒtƒBƒ‹ƒ^ƒŠƒ“ƒO
// ƒtƒBƒ‹ƒ^ƒŠƒ“ƒO
class PhysicsQueryFilter : public physx::PxQueryFilterCallback
{
public:
    explicit PhysicsQueryFilter(physx::PxU32 wantedLayers) : wantedLayers(wantedLayers) 
    {
    }

    physx::PxQueryHitType::Enum preFilter(
        const physx::PxFilterData& filterData,
        const physx::PxShape* shape,
        const physx::PxRigidActor* actor,
        physx::PxHitFlags& queryFlags) override
    {
        if (shape->getQueryFilterData().word0 & wantedLayers)
        {
            return physx::PxQueryHitType::eBLOCK;
        }
        return physx::PxQueryHitType::eNONE;
    }

    physx::PxQueryHitType::Enum postFilter(
        const physx::PxFilterData&, const physx::PxQueryHit&,
        const physx::PxShape*, const physx::PxRigidActor*) override
    {
        return physx::PxQueryHitType::eBLOCK;
    }

private:
    physx::PxU32 wantedLayers;
};

===== Application.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Core\Application.cpp 
#include "Application.h"
#include "tracy/Tracy.hpp"
#include "Engine/ECS/EngineComponents.h"
#include <sstream>

Application::Application(HWND hwnd) : hwnd(hwnd)
{
}

Application::~Application()
{
	// PhysXI—¹ˆ—
	physX.Cleanup();

	// GraphicsI—¹ˆ—
	graphics.Cleanup();
}

// ‰Šú‰»
void Application::Initialize()
{
	// Graphics‰Šú‰»
	graphics.Initialize(hwnd);

	// PhysX‰Šú‰»
	physX.Initialize();

	// ƒQ[ƒ€‚Ì•`‰ææ‚ÆƒQ[ƒ€ƒJƒƒ‰
	gameView.Initialize(graphics.GetDevice());

	// Engine‚ÌƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	EngineComponents::Register();

	// ”h¶ƒNƒ‰ƒX‚Ì‰Šú‰»
	OnInitialize();

	UpdateSceneContext();
}

// ƒAƒvƒŠƒP[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ƒ‹[ƒv
int Application::Run()
{
	MSG msg{};

	// ŒÅ’èƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒXƒeƒbƒvİ’è(60fps)
	const double fixedTimestep = 1.0 / 60.0;
	double accumulator = 0.0;

	while (WM_QUIT != msg.message)
	{
		if (PeekMessage(&msg, NULL, 0, 0, PM_REMOVE))
		{
			TranslateMessage(&msg);
			DispatchMessage(&msg);
		}
		else
		{
			timer.tick();
			CalculateFrameStats();

			// Œo‰ßŠÔ‚ğ’~Ï
			double frameTime = (double)timer.time_interval();
			if (frameTime > 0.25) frameTime = 0.25;
			accumulator += frameTime;

			// ŒÅ’èƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒXƒeƒbƒv‚ÅUpdate‚ğÀs
			while (accumulator >= fixedTimestep)
			{
				Update((float)fixedTimestep);
				accumulator -= fixedTimestep;
				updateCountBuf++;
			}

			// –ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€1‰ñ
			OnFrame();

			// •`‰æ‚Í–ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€
			Render();
		}
	}

	// I—¹ˆ—(”h¶ƒNƒ‰ƒX ¨ ƒV[ƒ“‚Ì‡)
	OnFinalize();
	sceneManager.ClearScene();

	return static_cast<int>(msg.wParam);
}

// XV
void Application::Update(float elapsedTime)
{
	ZoneScoped;

	OnPreUpdate(elapsedTime);

	// SceneContextXV
	UpdateSceneContext();

	// ƒV[ƒ“XV
	sceneManager.Update(elapsedTime, sceneContext);

	OnPostUpdate(elapsedTime);

	// •`‰æ‚Ég‚¤ƒJƒƒ‰‚ÌŒ‹‰Ê‚ğcontext‚É”½‰f
	Camera& renderCamera = GetRenderCamera();
	sceneContext.renderCamera = &renderCamera;
	sceneContext.view = renderCamera.GetView();
	sceneContext.projection = renderCamera.GetProjection();
}

// •`‰æ
void Application::Render()
{
	ZoneScoped;

	// ‰æ–ÊƒNƒŠƒA
	graphics.Clear(0.2f, 0.2f, 0.5f, 1.0f);
	graphics.SetRenderTargets();

	// ƒQ[ƒ€‚ğGameView‚ÌƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ‚É•`‚­
	gameView.Begin(graphics.GetDeviceContext());
	sceneManager.Render(sceneContext);
	{
		ZoneScopedN("GameView::End");
		gameView.End(graphics.GetDeviceContext());
	}

	// ƒƒCƒ“RTV‚ğÄƒZƒbƒg‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çA‰æ–Ê‚Öo‚·
	graphics.SetRenderTargets();
	OnPresent();

	// ‰æ–Ê”½‰f
	{
		ZoneScopedN("Present");
		graphics.Present(vsync);
	}

	FrameMark;
}

// SceneContext‚ğÅVó‘Ô‚ÉXV‚·‚é
void Application::UpdateSceneContext()
{
	sceneContext.device = graphics.GetDevice();
	sceneContext.deviceContext = graphics.GetDeviceContext();
	sceneContext.resourceManager = &resourceManager;
	sceneContext.physX = &physX;

	// GameView‚Ìê—pƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ‚ğ•`‰ææ‚Æ‚µ‚Ä“n‚·
	sceneContext.renderTargetView = gameView.GetRTV();
	sceneContext.depthStencilView = gameView.GetDSV();
	sceneContext.camera = &gameView.GetCamera();

	Camera& renderCamera = GetRenderCamera();
	sceneContext.renderCamera = &renderCamera;
	sceneContext.view = renderCamera.GetView();
	sceneContext.projection = renderCamera.GetProjection();

	sceneContext.screenWidth = gameView.GetRenderWidth();
	sceneContext.screenHeight = gameView.GetRenderHeight();
	sceneContext.gameInputActive = IsGameInputActive();
}

// ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ŒvZ
void Application::CalculateFrameStats()
{
	static int frames = 0;
	static float timeElapsed = 0.0f;

	frames++;

	if ((timer.time_stamp() - timeElapsed) >= 1.0f)
	{
		float fps = static_cast<float>(frames);
		float mspf = 1000.0f / fps;

		updateCount = updateCountBuf;
		updateCountBuf = 0;

		std::ostringstream outs;
		outs.precision(6);
		outs << "FPS : " << fps
			<< " / Frame Time : " << mspf << " ms"
			<< " / Update : " << updateCount << " fps";
		SetWindowTextA(hwnd, outs.str().c_str());

		frames = 0;
		timeElapsed += 1.0f;
	}
}

// ƒƒbƒZ[ƒWƒnƒ“ƒhƒ‰
LRESULT Application::HandleMessage(HWND hwnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wparam, LPARAM lparam)
{
	// ”h¶ƒNƒ‰ƒX‚ªˆ—‚µ‚½‚à‚Ì‚ÍA‚±‚±‚ÅI‚í‚è
	if (OnMessage(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam)) { return true; }

	switch (msg)
	{
	case WM_PAINT:
	{
		PAINTSTRUCT ps{};
		BeginPaint(hwnd, &ps);
		EndPaint(hwnd, &ps);
	}
	break;

	case WM_DESTROY:
		PostQuitMessage(0);
		break;
	case WM_CREATE:
		break;
	case WM_KEYDOWN:
		if (wparam == VK_ESCAPE)
		{
			PostMessage(hwnd, WM_CLOSE, 0, 0);
		}
		break;
	case WM_ENTERSIZEMOVE:
		timer.stop();
		break;
	case WM_SIZE:
		if (wparam == SIZE_MINIMIZED)
		{
			// Å¬‰»‚Íƒ^ƒCƒ}[’â~‚Ì‚İ(ƒŠƒTƒCƒY•s—v)
			timer.stop();
		}
		else
		{
			// Å‘å‰»/•œŒ³/’ÊíƒŠƒTƒCƒY
			timer.start();
			graphics.ResizeBuffers();
			UpdateSceneContext();
		}
		break;
	case WM_EXITSIZEMOVE:
		timer.start();
		// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEƒTƒCƒY•ÏX‚Éƒoƒbƒtƒ@Ä¶¬
		graphics.ResizeBuffers();
		UpdateSceneContext();
		break;
	default:
		return DefWindowProc(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam);
	}
	return 0;
} 
 
===== Camera.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Core\Camera.cpp 
#include "Camera.h"

// ‰Šú‰»
void Camera::Initialize(float screenWidth, float screenHeight)
{
    SetLookAt(
        DirectX::XMFLOAT3(0, 5, 10),
        DirectX::XMFLOAT3(0, 0, 0),
        DirectX::XMFLOAT3(0, 1, 0)
    );
    SetPerspectiveFov(
        DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(viewingAngle),
        screenWidth / screenHeight,
        0.1f,
        1000.0f
    );
}

// ’‹“_İ’è
void Camera::SetLookAt(
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& eye,
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& target,
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& up)
{
    DirectX::XMVECTOR Eye = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&eye);
    DirectX::XMVECTOR Target = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&target);
    DirectX::XMVECTOR Up = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&up);
    DirectX::XMMATRIX View = DirectX::XMMatrixLookAtLH(Eye, Target, Up);
    DirectX::XMStoreFloat4x4(&this->view, View);

    // •ûŒüƒxƒNƒgƒ‹æ“¾
    this->right.x = view._11;
    this->right.y = view._21;
    this->right.z = view._31;

    this->up.x = view._12;
    this->up.y = view._22;
    this->up.z = view._32;

    this->front.x = view._13;
    this->front.y = view._23;
    this->front.z = view._33;

    this->eye = eye;
    this->target = target;
}

// ƒp[ƒXƒyƒNƒeƒBƒuİ’è
void Camera::SetPerspectiveFov(
    float fovY, float aspect,
    float nearZ, float farZ)
{
    DirectX::XMMATRIX Projection =
        DirectX::XMMatrixPerspectiveFovLH(fovY, aspect, nearZ, farZ);
    DirectX::XMStoreFloat4x4(&projection, Projection);
} 
 
===== GameView.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Core\GameView.cpp 
#include "GameView.h"
#include <cassert>

// ‰Šú‰»
void GameView::Initialize(ID3D11Device* dev, UINT width, UINT height)
{
    device = dev;

    // ƒJƒƒ‰‰Šú‰»
    camera.Initialize(static_cast<float>(width), static_cast<float>(height));

    ResizeRenderTarget(width, height);
}

// iBeginEEndEResizeRenderTarget ‚Í GameWindow.cpp ‚Ì’†g‚ğ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Üj

// •`‰ææ‚ÌØ‚è‘Ö‚¦
void GameView::Begin(ID3D11DeviceContext* dc)
{
    if (!dc) return;

    // Œ»İ‚Ìƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒg‚ğ•Û‘¶
    UINT numVP = 1;
    dc->RSGetViewports(&numVP, &savedViewport);

    // ©‘O‚Ìƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒg‚ğƒZƒbƒg
    D3D11_VIEWPORT vp{};
    vp.Width = static_cast<float>(renderWidth);
    vp.Height = static_cast<float>(renderHeight);
    vp.MinDepth = 0.0f;
    vp.MaxDepth = 1.0f;
    dc->RSSetViewports(1, &vp);

    // •`‰ææ‚ğ©‘ORTV‚ÉØ‚è‘Ö‚¦
    dc->OMSetRenderTargets(1, rtv.GetAddressOf(), dsv.Get());

    // ƒNƒŠƒA
    float clear[4] = { 0.2f, 0.2f, 0.5f, 1.0f };
    dc->ClearRenderTargetView(rtv.Get(), clear);
    dc->ClearDepthStencilView(dsv.Get(), D3D11_CLEAR_DEPTH | D3D11_CLEAR_STENCIL, 1.0f, 0);
}
void GameView::End(ID3D11DeviceContext* dc)
{
    if (!dc) return;

    // ƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒg‚ğŒ³‚É–ß‚·
    dc->RSSetViewports(1, &savedViewport);

    // •`‰ææ‚ğƒƒCƒ“‚É–ß‚·
    ID3D11RenderTargetView* nullRTV = nullptr;
    dc->OMSetRenderTargets(1, &nullRTV, nullptr);
}


// ƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ‚ÌÄ¶¬
void GameView::ResizeRenderTarget(UINT width, UINT height)
{
    if (!device) return;
    if (width == 0 || height == 0) return;

    renderWidth = width;
    renderHeight = height;

    // ŒÃ‚¢ƒŠƒ\[ƒX‚ğ‰ğ•ú
    srv.Reset();
    rtv.Reset();
    renderTexture.Reset();
    dsv.Reset();
    depthTexture.Reset();

    HRESULT hr;

    // ƒJƒ‰[ƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒÄ¶¬
    {
        D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC desc{};
        desc.Width = renderWidth;
        desc.Height = renderHeight;
        desc.MipLevels = 1;
        desc.ArraySize = 1;
        desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT;
        desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
        desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
        desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_RENDER_TARGET | D3D11_BIND_SHADER_RESOURCE;

        hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&desc, nullptr, renderTexture.GetAddressOf());
        assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
        hr = device->CreateRenderTargetView(renderTexture.Get(), nullptr, rtv.GetAddressOf());
        assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
        hr = device->CreateShaderResourceView(renderTexture.Get(), nullptr, srv.GetAddressOf());
        assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
    }

    // [“xƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒÄ¶¬
    {
        D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC desc{};
        desc.Width = renderWidth;
        desc.Height = renderHeight;
        desc.MipLevels = 1;
        desc.ArraySize = 1;
        desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_D24_UNORM_S8_UINT;
        desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
        desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
        desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_DEPTH_STENCIL;

        hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&desc, nullptr, depthTexture.GetAddressOf());
        assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
        hr = device->CreateDepthStencilView(depthTexture.Get(), nullptr, dsv.GetAddressOf());
        assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
    }
}

// •\¦æ‚Ìc‰¡”ä‚É‡‚í‚¹‚ÄAƒQ[ƒ€ƒJƒƒ‰‚ÌƒvƒƒWƒFƒNƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ğXV‚·‚é
void GameView::SetAspect(float aspect)
{
    camera.SetPerspectiveFov(
        DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(camera.GetViewingAngle()),
        aspect,
        0.1f,
        1000.0f
    );
}

#include "Graphics.h"
#include "Engine/Utility/misc.h"
#include "Engine/Resource/GpuResourceUtils.h"

// ‰Šú‰»
void Graphics::Initialize(HWND hWnd)
{
	// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEƒnƒ“ƒhƒ‹•Û‘¶
	this->hWnd = hWnd;

	// ƒNƒ‰ƒCƒAƒ“ƒg—ÌˆæƒTƒCƒYæ“¾
	RECT rc;
	// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE“à•”ƒTƒCƒYæ“¾
	GetClientRect(hWnd, &rc);
	UINT w = rc.right - rc.left; // •
	UINT h = rc.bottom - rc.top; // ‚‚³
	// •Û‘¶
	screenWidth = static_cast<float>(w);
	screenHeight = static_cast<float>(h);

	// HRESULT•Û‘¶
	HRESULT hr{ S_OK };

	// ƒfƒoƒCƒX¶¬ƒtƒ‰ƒO
	UINT createDeviceFlags = 0;


#ifdef _DEBUG 
	// ƒfƒoƒbƒOƒŒƒCƒ„[—LŒø‰»
	createDeviceFlags |= D3D11_CREATE_DEVICE_DEBUG;
#endif

	// DirectX11.0‹@”\ƒŒƒxƒ‹‚Å“®ì
	D3D_FEATURE_LEVEL featureLevels[] =
	{
		D3D_FEATURE_LEVEL_11_0,
	};

	// ƒXƒƒbƒvƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“İ’è
	DXGI_SWAP_CHAIN_DESC scDesc{};
	scDesc.BufferCount = 2;                                // ƒoƒbƒtƒ@”2
	scDesc.BufferDesc.Width = w;                           // ƒoƒbƒtƒ@•
	scDesc.BufferDesc.Height = h;                          // ƒoƒbƒtƒ@‚‚³
	scDesc.BufferDesc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM; // RGBA8bitƒtƒH[ƒ}ƒbƒg
	scDesc.BufferDesc.RefreshRate.Numerator = 60;          // ƒŠƒtƒŒƒbƒVƒ…ƒŒ[ƒg60Hz
	scDesc.BufferDesc.RefreshRate.Denominator = 1;         // •ª•êi60/1=60Hzj
	scDesc.BufferUsage = DXGI_USAGE_RENDER_TARGET_OUTPUT;  // ƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒg—p“r
	scDesc.OutputWindow = hWnd;                            // •`‰æ‘ÎÛƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE
	scDesc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;                           // MSAAƒTƒ“ƒvƒ‹”
	scDesc.SampleDesc.Quality = 0;                         // •i¿ƒŒƒxƒ‹	
	scDesc.Windowed = TRUE;                                // ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEƒ‚[ƒh
	scDesc.SwapEffect = DXGI_SWAP_EFFECT_FLIP_DISCARD;     // FlipDiscard•û®
	scDesc.Flags = DXGI_SWAP_CHAIN_FLAG_ALLOW_TEARING;     // ƒeƒAƒŠƒ“ƒO‹–‰Â


#if 0
	// ƒfƒoƒCƒX/ƒXƒƒbƒvƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“¶¬
	D3D_FEATURE_LEVEL featureLevel;
	hr = D3D11CreateDeviceAndSwapChain(
		nullptr,
		D3D_DRIVER_TYPE_HARDWARE,
		nullptr,
		createDeviceFlags,
		featureLevels,
		ARRAYSIZE(featureLevels),
		D3D11_SDK_VERSION,
		&scDesc,
		swapChain.GetAddressOf(),
		device.GetAddressOf(),
		&featureLevel,
		immediateContext.GetAddressOf()
	);
	_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), hr_trace(hr));

#else
	// ƒAƒ_ƒvƒ^[‘I‘ğ
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<IDXGIFactory1> factory;
	CreateDXGIFactory1(__uuidof(IDXGIFactory1), (void**)factory.GetAddressOf());

	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<IDXGIAdapter1> selectedAdapter;
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<IDXGIAdapter1> adapter;

	for (UINT i = 0; factory->EnumAdapters1(i, adapter.GetAddressOf()) != DXGI_ERROR_NOT_FOUND; i++)
	{
		DXGI_ADAPTER_DESC1 desc;
		adapter->GetDesc1(&desc);

		// ƒ\ƒtƒgƒEƒFƒAƒAƒ_ƒvƒ^[‚ÍœŠO
		if (desc.Flags & DXGI_ADAPTER_FLAG_SOFTWARE)
		{
			adapter.Reset();
			continue;
		}

		// NVIDIA or AMD (‘¦Œˆ’è)
		if (desc.VendorId == 0x10DE || desc.VendorId == 0x1002)
		{
			selectedAdapter = adapter;
			break;
		}
		adapter.Reset();
	}

	// ƒfƒoƒCƒX/ƒXƒƒbƒvƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“¶¬
	D3D_FEATURE_LEVEL featureLevel;
	hr = D3D11CreateDeviceAndSwapChain(
		selectedAdapter.Get(),
		selectedAdapter ? D3D_DRIVER_TYPE_UNKNOWN    // NVIDIA/AMDŒ©‚Â‚©‚Á‚½
		: D3D_DRIVER_TYPE_HARDWARE,                  // Œ©‚Â‚©‚ç‚È‚©‚Á‚½‚çWindows‚É”C‚¹‚é
		nullptr,
		createDeviceFlags,
		featureLevels,
		ARRAYSIZE(featureLevels),
		D3D11_SDK_VERSION,
		&scDesc,
		swapChain.GetAddressOf(),
		device.GetAddressOf(),
		&featureLevel,
		immediateContext.GetAddressOf()
	);
	_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), hr_trace(hr));

#endif


	// ƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒgƒrƒ…[¶¬
	// ƒXƒƒbƒvƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“‚ÌƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@æ“¾
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Texture2D> backBuffer;
	hr = swapChain->GetBuffer(
		0,
		__uuidof(ID3D11Texture2D),
		reinterpret_cast<void**>(backBuffer.GetAddressOf())
	);
	_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), hr_trace(hr));

	// ƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@‚©‚çƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒgƒrƒ…[¶¬
	hr = device->CreateRenderTargetView(
		backBuffer.Get(),
		nullptr,
		renderTargetView.GetAddressOf()
	);
	_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), hr_trace(hr));


	// [“xƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹¶¬
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Texture2D> depthStencilBuffer; // [“xƒoƒbƒtƒ@ƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ
	D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC dsDesc{};
	dsDesc.Width = w;                   // ƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒƒTƒCƒY(•)
	dsDesc.Height = h;                  // ƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒƒTƒCƒY(‚‚³)
	dsDesc.MipLevels = 1;               // ƒ~ƒbƒvƒ}ƒbƒv”
	dsDesc.ArraySize = 1;               // ”z—ñƒTƒCƒY
	dsDesc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_D24_UNORM_S8_UINT; // Depth24 + Stencil8
	dsDesc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;        // MSAAİ’è
	dsDesc.SampleDesc.Quality = 0;      // MSAA‚ğg‚í‚È‚¢ê‡‚Í0ŒÅ’è
	dsDesc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT; // GPU“Ç‚İ‘‚«—p
	dsDesc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_DEPTH_STENCIL;

	// [“xƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹ƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ¶¬
	hr = device->CreateTexture2D(
		&dsDesc,
		nullptr,
		depthStencilBuffer.GetAddressOf()
	);
	_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), hr_trace(hr));

	// ƒfƒvƒXƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹ƒrƒ…[¶¬
	hr = device->CreateDepthStencilView(
		depthStencilBuffer.Get(),
		nullptr,
		depthStencilView.GetAddressOf()
	);
	_ASSERT_EXPR(SUCCEEDED(hr), hr_trace(hr));

	// ƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒgİ’è
	viewport.TopLeftX = 0.0f;       // ¶ãÀ•W
	viewport.TopLeftY = 0.0f;
	viewport.Width = screenWidth;   // •`‰æ—ÌˆæƒTƒCƒY
	viewport.Height = screenHeight;
	viewport.MinDepth = 0.0f;       // [“x”ÍˆÍ
	viewport.MaxDepth = 1.0f;


	// PresentTexture—pƒŠƒ\[ƒX‰Šú‰»
	InitializePresentResources();
}

// ‰æ–ÊƒNƒŠƒA
void Graphics::Clear(float r, float g, float b, float a)
{
	// ƒNƒŠƒAƒJƒ‰[ì¬
	float color[4]{ r, g, b, a };

	// ƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@‚ğw’èF‚ÅƒNƒŠƒA
	immediateContext->ClearRenderTargetView(renderTargetView.Get(), color);
	// [“xƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹’l‰Šú‰»
	immediateContext->ClearDepthStencilView(
		depthStencilView.Get(),
		D3D11_CLEAR_DEPTH |
		D3D11_CLEAR_STENCIL,
		1.0f, // [“x‰Šú’l(1.0 = Å‰“)
		0     // ƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹‰Šú’l
	);
}

// ƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒgİ’è
void Graphics::SetRenderTargets()
{
	// ƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒgİ’è
	immediateContext->RSSetViewports(1, &viewport);
	// ƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒg‚Æ[“xƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹‚ğOMƒXƒe[ƒW‚Éİ’è
	immediateContext->OMSetRenderTargets(1, renderTargetView.GetAddressOf(), depthStencilView.Get());
}

// w’èƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ‚ğƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“‚ÅƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@‚Ö•`‰æ
void Graphics::PresentTexture(ID3D11ShaderResourceView* srv)
{
	if (!srv) return;

	// ƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@‚Ö•`‰æ
	immediateContext->RSSetViewports(1, &viewport);
	immediateContext->OMSetRenderTargets(1, renderTargetView.GetAddressOf(), nullptr);

	// ƒuƒŒƒ“ƒhE[“xƒXƒe[ƒg‚ğŠù’è‚É–ß‚·
	immediateContext->OMSetBlendState(nullptr, nullptr, 0xffffffff);
	immediateContext->OMSetDepthStencilState(nullptr, 1);

	// ƒVƒF[ƒ_[İ’è
	immediateContext->VSSetShader(presentVS.Get(), nullptr, 0);
	immediateContext->PSSetShader(presentPS.Get(), nullptr, 0);

	// “ü—ÍƒŒƒCƒAƒEƒg‚È‚µEƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“OŠpŒ`ƒXƒgƒŠƒbƒv
	immediateContext->IASetInputLayout(nullptr);
	immediateContext->IASetPrimitiveTopology(D3D11_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_TRIANGLESTRIP);
	immediateContext->RSSetState(presentRasterizer.Get());

	// ƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒEƒTƒ“ƒvƒ‰[İ’è
	immediateContext->PSSetShaderResources(0, 1, &srv);
	immediateContext->PSSetSamplers(0, 1, presentSampler.GetAddressOf());

	// ƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“•`‰æ
	immediateContext->Draw(4, 0);

	// SRV‚Ì‘©”›‰ğœ
	ID3D11ShaderResourceView* nullSrv = nullptr;
	immediateContext->PSSetShaderResources(0, 1, &nullSrv);
}

// ‰æ–ÊƒvƒŒƒ[ƒ“ƒg
void Graphics::Present(bool vsync)
{
	//swapChain->Present(0, DXGI_PRESENT_ALLOW_TEARING); // í‚ÉƒeƒBƒAƒŠƒ“ƒO‹–‰Â

	if (vsync)
	{
		// VSync ONFƒ‚ƒjƒ^[‚ÌƒŠƒtƒŒƒbƒVƒ…ƒŒ[ƒg‚É“¯Šú
		swapChain->Present(1, 0);
	}
	else
	{
		// VSync OFFF‰Â”\‚ÈŒÀ‚è‘¬‚­•`‰æ
		swapChain->Present(0, DXGI_PRESENT_ALLOW_TEARING);
	}
}

// ƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦
void Graphics::ToggleFullscreen()
{
	// ƒŠƒTƒCƒY’†
	isResizing = true;

	// ƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦
	if (!isFullscreen)
	{
		// Œ»İƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEˆÊ’u•Û‘¶
		GetWindowRect(hWnd, &windowedRect);
		// Œ»İƒ‚ƒjƒ^[æ“¾
		HMONITOR monitor = MonitorFromWindow(hWnd, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST);
		// ƒ‚ƒjƒ^[î•ñæ“¾
		MONITORINFO monitorInfo{ sizeof(MONITORINFO) };
		GetMonitorInfo(monitor, &monitorInfo);
		// ƒ‚ƒjƒ^[‹éŒ`
		RECT& mr = monitorInfo.rcMonitor;

		// Š®‘Sƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“—p
		SetWindowLong(hWnd, GWL_STYLE, WS_POPUP | WS_VISIBLE); // ƒ^ƒCƒgƒ‹ƒo[‚â˜g‚È‚µ‚ÌŠ®‘Sƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“—pƒXƒ^ƒCƒ‹

		// ƒ‚ƒjƒ^[‘S‘ÌƒTƒCƒY‚Ö•ÏX
		SetWindowPos(
			hWnd,
			HWND_TOP,
			mr.left, mr.top,
			mr.right - mr.left, mr.bottom - mr.top,
			SWP_FRAMECHANGED
		);

		// ƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“’†
		isFullscreen = true;
	}
	// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEƒ‚[ƒh‚Ö–ß‚·
	else
	{
		// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEƒXƒ^ƒCƒ‹‚É–ß‚·
		SetWindowLong(hWnd, GWL_STYLE, WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW | WS_VISIBLE);
		// •Û‘¶‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚½ˆÊ’u‚Ö–ß‚·
		SetWindowPos(hWnd, HWND_TOP,
			windowedRect.left, windowedRect.top,
			windowedRect.right - windowedRect.left,
			windowedRect.bottom - windowedRect.top,
			SWP_FRAMECHANGED
		);

		// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE’†
		isFullscreen = false;
	}

	// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEXV
	UpdateWindow(hWnd);

	// ƒoƒbƒtƒ@Ä¶¬
	ResizeBuffers();

	// ƒŠƒTƒCƒYI—¹
	isResizing = false;
}

// ƒoƒbƒtƒ@Ä¶¬
void Graphics::ResizeBuffers()
{
	// Œ»İƒNƒ‰ƒCƒAƒ“ƒgƒTƒCƒYæ“¾
	RECT rc;
	GetClientRect(hWnd, &rc);
	UINT w = rc.right - rc.left;
	UINT h = rc.bottom - rc.top;
	// Å¬‰»ƒXƒLƒbƒv
	if (w == 0 || h == 0) return;

	// ƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒg‰ğœ
	immediateContext->OMSetRenderTargets(0, nullptr, nullptr);

	// ƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@ƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒgƒrƒ…[‰ğ•ú
	renderTargetView.Reset();
	// [“xƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹‰ğ•ú
	depthStencilView.Reset();

	// ƒXƒƒbƒvƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“Ä¶¬
	swapChain->ResizeBuffers(
		0,
		w, h,
		DXGI_FORMAT_UNKNOWN,
		DXGI_SWAP_CHAIN_FLAG_ALLOW_TEARING // í‚ÉƒeƒBƒAƒŠƒ“ƒO‹–‰Â
	);

	// ƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒgƒrƒ…[Ä¶¬
	// VƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@æ“¾
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Texture2D> backBuffer;
	swapChain->GetBuffer(
		0,
		__uuidof(ID3D11Texture2D),
		reinterpret_cast<void**>(backBuffer.GetAddressOf())
	);
	// ƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@—pƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒgƒrƒ…[¶¬
	device->CreateRenderTargetView(
		backBuffer.Get(),
		nullptr,
		renderTargetView.GetAddressOf()
	);

	// [“xƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹ƒrƒ…[Ä¶¬
	// V[“xƒoƒbƒtƒ@¶¬
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Texture2D> dsBuffer;
	D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC dsDesc{};
	dsDesc.Width = w;                   // ƒoƒbƒtƒ@ƒTƒCƒY
	dsDesc.Height = h;                  // ƒoƒbƒtƒ@ƒTƒCƒY
	dsDesc.MipLevels = 1;               // ƒ~ƒbƒv–³‚µ
	dsDesc.ArraySize = 1;               // ”z—ñƒTƒCƒY
	dsDesc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_D24_UNORM_S8_UINT; // Depth24 + Stencil8
	dsDesc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;        // MSAAİ’è
	dsDesc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT; // GPUg—p
	dsDesc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_DEPTH_STENCIL; // [“xƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹—p“r

	// [“xƒoƒbƒtƒ@¶¬
	device->CreateTexture2D(
		&dsDesc,
		nullptr,
		dsBuffer.GetAddressOf()
	);

	// ƒfƒvƒXƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹ƒrƒ…[¶¬
	device->CreateDepthStencilView(
		dsBuffer.Get(),
		nullptr,
		depthStencilView.GetAddressOf()
	);

	// ‰æ–ÊƒTƒCƒYXV
	screenWidth = static_cast<float>(w);
	screenHeight = static_cast<float>(h);
	// ƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒgƒTƒCƒYXV
	viewport.Width = screenWidth;
	viewport.Height = screenHeight;
}

// I—¹ˆ—
void Graphics::Cleanup()
{
	if (immediateContext)
	{
		immediateContext->OMSetRenderTargets(0, nullptr, nullptr);
		immediateContext->ClearState();
	}

	renderTargetView.Reset();
	depthStencilView.Reset();
}

// PresentTexture—pƒŠƒ\[ƒX‚Ì‰Šú‰»
void Graphics::InitializePresentResources()
{
	// ’¸“_ƒVƒF[ƒ_[
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11InputLayout> dummyLayout;
	GpuResourceUtils::LoadVertexShader(
		device.Get(), "Data/Shader/Lighting_VS.cso",
		nullptr, 0, dummyLayout, presentVS
	);

	// ƒsƒNƒZƒ‹ƒVƒF[ƒ_[
	GpuResourceUtils::LoadPixelShader(
		device.Get(), "Data/Shader/Present_PS.cso", presentPS
	);

	// ƒTƒ“ƒvƒ‰[ƒXƒe[ƒg
	D3D11_SAMPLER_DESC samplerDesc{};
	samplerDesc.Filter = D3D11_FILTER_MIN_MAG_MIP_LINEAR;
	samplerDesc.AddressU = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
	samplerDesc.AddressV = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
	samplerDesc.AddressW = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
	samplerDesc.ComparisonFunc = D3D11_COMPARISON_NEVER;
	samplerDesc.MinLOD = 0;
	samplerDesc.MaxLOD = D3D11_FLOAT32_MAX;
	device->CreateSamplerState(&samplerDesc, presentSampler.GetAddressOf());

	// ƒ‰ƒXƒ^ƒ‰ƒCƒU[ƒXƒe[ƒg
	D3D11_RASTERIZER_DESC rasterizerDesc{};
	rasterizerDesc.FillMode = D3D11_FILL_SOLID;
	rasterizerDesc.CullMode = D3D11_CULL_NONE;
	rasterizerDesc.FrontCounterClockwise = FALSE;
	device->CreateRasterizerState(&rasterizerDesc, presentRasterizer.GetAddressOf());
} 
 
===== PhysX.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Core\PhysX.cpp 
#include "PhysX.h"

using namespace physx;

// ‰Šú‰»
void PhysX::Initialize()
{
	// Foundatioì¬
	foundation = PxCreateFoundation(PX_PHYSICS_VERSION, allocator, errorCallback);

#ifdef _DEBUG
	// PhysXVisualDebugger‚ÉÚ‘±
	pvd = PxCreatePvd(*foundation);
	pvdTransport = PxDefaultPvdSocketTransportCreate("127.0.0.1", 5425, 10);
	pvd->connect(*pvdTransport, physx::PxPvdInstrumentationFlag::eALL);
#endif

	// PxPhysics–{‘Ìì¬
	physics = PxCreatePhysics(PX_PHYSICS_VERSION, *foundation, PxTolerancesScale(), true, pvd);

	// ƒVƒ~ƒ…ƒŒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ğCPUƒXƒŒƒbƒh‚ÉŠ„‚èU‚éƒfƒBƒXƒpƒbƒ`ƒƒ‚ğì¬
	dispatcher = PxDefaultCpuDispatcherCreate(1); // ƒXƒŒƒbƒh”‚P(‰ÄŠúuÀ‚¾‚Æ2ƒXƒŒƒbƒh‚¾‚Á‚½)

	// ƒfƒtƒHƒ‹ƒgƒ}ƒeƒŠƒAƒ‹(Ã–€C, “®–€C, ”½”­ŒW”)ì¬
	defaultMaterial = physics->createMaterial(0.5f, 0.5f, 0.6f);
}
// I—¹ˆ—
void PhysX::Cleanup()
{
	// ì¬‚µ‚½‡‚Æ‹t‡‚É‰ğ•ú‚·‚é
	if (defaultMaterial) { defaultMaterial->release(); defaultMaterial = nullptr; }
	if (dispatcher)      { dispatcher->release();      dispatcher      = nullptr; }
	if (physics)         { physics->release();         physics         = nullptr; }
	if (pvd)             { pvd->disconnect();
				           pvd->release();             pvd             = nullptr; }
	if (pvdTransport)    { pvdTransport->release();    pvdTransport    = nullptr; }
	if (foundation)      { foundation->release();      foundation      = nullptr; }
}

// ƒƒbƒVƒ…ƒf[ƒ^‚©‚çPxTriangleMesh‚ğ¶¬
physx::PxTriangleMesh* PhysX::CreateTriangleMesh(
	const void* vertexData, 
	uint32_t vertexCount,
	uint32_t vertexStride,
	const uint32_t* indices,
	uint32_t triangleCount)
{
	// ƒƒbƒVƒ…î•ñ‘g‚İ—§‚Ä
	PxTriangleMeshDesc meshDesc;
	meshDesc.points.count = vertexCount;
	meshDesc.points.stride = vertexStride;
	meshDesc.points.data = vertexData;

	meshDesc.triangles.count = triangleCount;
	meshDesc.triangles.stride = 3 * sizeof(uint32_t);
	meshDesc.triangles.data = indices;

	// Cookingƒpƒ‰ƒ[ƒ^
	PxCookingParams cookingParams(physics->getTolerancesScale());

	// ƒƒbƒVƒ…¶¬
	return PxCreateTriangleMesh(
		cookingParams,
		meshDesc,
		physics->getPhysicsInsertionCallback()
	);
}
 
 
===== Scene.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Core\Scene.cpp 
#include "Scene.h"

// •¨—ƒV[ƒ“‚Ìì¬
void Scene::InitializePhysicsScene(PhysX& physX)
{
	// ƒV[ƒ“‘g‚İ—§‚Ä
	physx::PxSceneDesc sceneDesc(physX.GetPhysics()->getTolerancesScale());
	sceneDesc.gravity = physx::PxVec3(0.0f, -9.81f, 0.0f);
	sceneDesc.cpuDispatcher = physX.GetDispatcher();
	sceneDesc.filterShader = physx::PxDefaultSimulationFilterShader;

	pxScene = physX.GetPhysics()->createScene(sceneDesc);

	// PhysXVisualDebugger‚É‘—‚éî•ñ‚ğİ’è
	if (physx::PxPvdSceneClient* pvdClient = pxScene->getScenePvdClient())
	{
		pvdClient->setScenePvdFlag(physx::PxPvdSceneFlag::eTRANSMIT_CONSTRAINTS,  true);
		pvdClient->setScenePvdFlag(physx::PxPvdSceneFlag::eTRANSMIT_CONTACTS,     true);
		pvdClient->setScenePvdFlag(physx::PxPvdSceneFlag::eTRANSMIT_SCENEQUERIES, true);
	}

	// ƒLƒƒƒ‰ƒNƒ^[ƒRƒ“ƒgƒ[ƒ‰[ƒ}ƒl[ƒWƒƒ[ì¬
	controllerManager = PxCreateControllerManager(*pxScene);
}

// •¨—ƒV[ƒ“‚Ì”jŠü
void Scene::FinalizePhysicsScene()
{
	if (controllerManager)
	{
		controllerManager->release();
		controllerManager = nullptr;
	}

	if (pxScene)
	{
		pxScene->release();
		pxScene = nullptr;
	}
}

// •¨—ƒV[ƒ“‚ÌXV
void Scene::UpdatePhysicsScene(float elapsedTime)
{
	pxScene->simulate(elapsedTime);
	pxScene->fetchResults(true);
} 
 
===== SceneManager.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Core\SceneManager.cpp 
#include "SceneManager.h"

// XV
void SceneManager::Update(float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	// ƒV[ƒ“Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦ˆ—
	if (nextScene)
	{
		// Œ»İƒV[ƒ“I—¹‰»
		if (currentScene)
		{
			currentScene->Finalize();
		}

		// ƒV[ƒ“Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦
		currentScene = std::move(nextScene);
		nextScene = nullptr;

		// ƒRƒ“ƒeƒLƒXƒg‚ğ“n‚µ‚Ä‰Šú‰»
		currentScene->Initialize(context);
	}

	if (currentScene)
	{
		currentScene->Update(elapsedTime, context);
	}
}

// •`‰æ
void SceneManager::Render(const SceneContext& context)
{
	if (currentScene)
	{
		currentScene->Render(context);
	}
}

// ƒV[ƒ“Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦—\–ñ
void SceneManager::ChangeScene(std::unique_ptr<Scene> scene)
{
	// ŸƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ÅØ‚è‘Ö‚¦
	nextScene = std::move(scene);
}

// ƒV[ƒ“ƒNƒŠƒA
void SceneManager::ClearScene()
{
	if (currentScene)
	{
		currentScene->Finalize();
		currentScene.reset();
	}
	nextScene.reset();
}

===== ConditionGraphData.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Data\ConditionGraphData.h 
#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <json.hpp>
#include "Engine/ECS/Reflection.h"

// \‘¢‘Ì‚Ì‚Ç‚Ì¬•ª‚ğæ‚èo‚·‚©
enum class FloatComponent
{
	None,
	X, Y, Z, W,
};

// ”äŠr‰‰Zq‚Ìí—Ş
enum class CompareOp
{
	Greater,      // >
	Less,         // <
	Equal,        // ==
	NotEqual,     // !=
	GreaterEqual, // >=
	LessEqual,    // <=
};

// ˜_—‰‰Zq‚Ìí—Ş
enum class LogicOp
{
	And, // ‚©‚Â
	Or,  // ‚Ü‚½‚Í
};

// ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì’l‚ğæ“¾‚·‚éƒm[ƒh
struct ConditionGetterNode
{
	int id = -1;
	float posX = 0.0f;
	float posY = 0.0f;
	std::string componentName; // Œ^–¼
	std::string fieldName;     // ƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ÌƒR[ƒhã‚Ì–¼‘O
	FloatComponent selectedComponent = FloatComponent::None;
};

// •Ï”ƒm[ƒh
struct ConditionVariableNode
{
	int id = -1;
	float posX = 0.0f;
	float posY = 0.0f;
	std::string name; // ‹ó‚ÌŠÔ‚Í"Variable"‚Æ•\¦
	ValueType type = ValueType::Float;
	int intValue = 0;
	float floatValue = 0.0f;
	bool boolValue = false;
};

// ”äŠrƒm[ƒh
struct ConditionOperatorNode
{
	int id = -1;
	float posX = 0.0f;
	float posY = 0.0f;
	CompareOp op = CompareOp::Greater;
};

// ˜_—‰‰Zƒm[ƒh
struct ConditionLogicNode
{
	int id = -1;
	float posX = 0.0f;
	float posY = 0.0f;
	LogicOp op = LogicOp::And;
};

// ”Û’èƒm[ƒh
struct ConditionNotNode
{
	int id = -1;
	float posX = 0.0f;
	float posY = 0.0f;
};

// ğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ÌI’…“_
struct ConditionReturnNode
{
	int id = -1;
	float posX = 500.0f;
	float posY = 200.0f;
};

// ğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚Ìí—Ş
enum class ConditionNodeKind
{
	None,     // Œ©‚Â‚©‚ç‚È‚¢
	Return,
	Variable,
	Getter,
	Operator,
	Logic,
	Not,
};

// ğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt“à‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN
struct ConditionLink
{
	int id = -1;
	int fromId = -1;
	int toId = -1;
	bool toPinB = false;
};


// ‘JˆÚ1–{•ª‚ÌğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt
struct ConditionGraph
{
	ConditionReturnNode returnNode;
	std::vector<ConditionVariableNode> variableNodes;
	std::vector<ConditionGetterNode>   getterNodes;
	std::vector<ConditionOperatorNode> operatorNodes;
	std::vector<ConditionLogicNode>    logicNodes;
	std::vector<ConditionNotNode>      notNodes;
	std::vector<ConditionLink>         links;

	// Ÿ‚ÉŠ„‚è“–‚Ä‚éID(‚±‚ÌğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt‚Ì’†‚Å‚ÌÌ”Ô)
	int nextId = 2;

	ConditionGraph() { returnNode.id = 1; }

	int GenerateId() { return nextId++; }

	// g—p’†‚ÌID‚ÌÅ‘å’l+1‚ÉAÌ”Ô‚ğ‚»‚ë‚¦‚é(“Ç‚İ‚İŒã‚È‚Ç‚ÉŒÄ‚Ô)
	void RecalculateNextId();

	// Return‚É‰½‚à‚Â‚È‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢‚©(ğŒ‚ª‹ó‚©)
	bool IsEmpty() const;

	// ƒm[ƒh‚Ìí—Ş
	ConditionNodeKind GetNodeKind(int nodeId) const;
	// o—Íƒsƒ“‚ªo‚·’l‚ÌŒ^
	bool GetOutputType(int nodeId, ValueType& outType) const;

	// •Ï”ƒm[ƒh‚Ì’Ç‰Á
	int AddVariableNode(ValueType type, float x, float y);
	// ƒQƒbƒ^[ƒm[ƒh‚Ì’Ç‰Á
	int AddGetterNode(float x, float y);
	// ”äŠrƒm[ƒh’Ç‰Á
	int AddOperatorNode(CompareOp op, float x, float y);
	// ˜_—‰‰Zƒm[ƒh’Ç‰Á
	int AddLogicNode(LogicOp op, float x, float y);
	// ”Û’èƒm[ƒh’Ç‰Á
	int AddNotNode(float x, float y);

	// ƒm[ƒh‚Ìíœ
	void RemoveNode(int nodeId);

	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğì‚ê‚é‚©
	bool CanLink(int fromNodeId, int toNodeId, bool toPinB, std::string* reason = nullptr) const;
	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğì‚é
	int AddLink(int fromNodeId, int toNodeId, bool toPinB);
	
	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìíœ
	void RemoveLink(int linkId);

	// ¡‚Ìİ’è‚ÅAƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ªŒˆ‚Ü‚è‚É‡‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚©
	bool IsLinkValid(const ConditionLink& link, std::string* reason = nullptr) const;

private:
	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÌŒˆ‚Ü‚è‚ÌŠm”F
	bool CheckLink(int fromNodeId, int toNodeId, bool toPinB, int ignoreLinkId, std::string* reason) const;
	// nodeId‚Ì“ü—Í‚ğŒã‚ë‚Ö‚½‚Ç‚Á‚ÄAtargetId‚É‚½‚Ç‚è’…‚­‚©
	bool DependsOn(int nodeId, int targetId, int ignoreLinkId) const;
};

// JSON‚Æ‚Ì•ÏŠ·
nlohmann::ordered_json ConditionGraphToJson(const ConditionGraph& graph);
void ConditionGraphFromJson(ConditionGraph& graph, const nlohmann::json& j);

// ƒQƒbƒ^[‚ª“Ç‚ŞƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ÌŒ^
ValueType GetGetterFieldType(const ConditionGetterNode& node);
// ƒQƒbƒ^[‚Å‘I‚ñ‚Å‚¢‚é¬•ª‚ªA‚»‚ÌŒ^‚Åg‚¦‚é‚©
bool IsGetterAxisValid(const ConditionGetterNode& node);

===== StateMachineData.h =====

#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <json.hpp>
#include "Engine/Data/ConditionGraphData.h"

struct EditorPosition
{
	float x = 0.0f;
	float y = 0.0f;
};

// ƒuƒŒƒ“ƒh‚Ìİ’è
struct BlendSetting
{
	bool enabled = true;   // ƒuƒŒƒ“ƒh‚·‚é‚©
	float duration = 0.2f; // ƒuƒŒƒ“ƒh‚Ì•b”(enabled‚ªfalse‚È‚çg‚í‚È‚¢)
};

// eŠK‘w : ‘JˆÚ(State ¨ State)
struct StateTransition
{
	int id = -1;
	int toStateId = -1;
	bool always = false; // true‚È‚çğŒ‚ÉŠÖŒW‚È‚­¬—§‚·‚é
	BlendSetting blend;
	ConditionGraph condition;
};

// qŠK‘wFÄ¶‚·‚éƒNƒŠƒbƒv
struct ClipNode
{
	int id = -1;
	std::string name;
	std::string clipName;
	bool loop = true;
	bool rootMotion = false;
	EditorPosition editorPos;
};

// qŠK‘wF‘JˆÚ(ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[ ¨ ƒNƒŠƒbƒv)
struct EntryLink
{
	int id = -1;
	int entryTransitionId = -1; // ‚Ç‚Ì‘JˆÚ‚©‚ç—ˆ‚½ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚©
	int toClipId = -1;
	bool always = false;
	ConditionGraph condition;
};

// qŠK‘wF‘JˆÚ(ƒNƒŠƒbƒv ¨ ƒNƒŠƒbƒv)
struct ClipLink
{
	int id = -1;
	int fromClipId = -1;
	int toClipId = -1;
	bool always = false;
	BlendSetting blend;
	ConditionGraph condition;
};

// qŠK‘w(‚»‚ÌState‚Å‰½‚ğÄ¶‚·‚é‚©)
struct ChildGraph
{
	std::vector<ClipNode>  clips;
	std::vector<EntryLink> entryLinks;
	std::vector<ClipLink>  clipLinks;

	// ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚ÌƒGƒfƒBƒ^ã‚ÌˆÊ’u
	std::unordered_map<int, EditorPosition> entryEditorPos;

	const ClipNode* FindClip(int clipId) const;
};

// State(e)
struct StateNode
{
	int id = -1;
	std::string name;
	std::vector<StateTransition> transitions; // •À‚Ñ‡‚ª‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü—Dæ“x
	ChildGraph child;

	EditorPosition editorPos;
	float editorColor[3] = { 0.27f, 0.35f, 0.47f };
};

// e‚ÌEntry(Å‰‚ÌState‚Ö‚Ì‘JˆÚ‚ğ‚Â)
struct StateMachineEntry
{
	std::vector<StateTransition> transitions;
	EditorPosition editorPos;
};

// Stateƒ}ƒVƒ“‚ÌƒOƒ‰ƒt‘S‘Ì
class StateMachineData
{
public:
	static constexpr int kVersion = 2;

	StateMachineEntry entry;
	std::vector<StateNode> states;

	// IDÌ”Ô
	int GenerateId() { return nextId++; }

	// ŒŸõ
	const StateNode* FindState(int stateId) const;
	const StateNode* FindStateByName(const std::string& name) const;
	const StateTransition* FindTransition(int transitionId) const; // Entry‚Ì‘JˆÚ‚àŠÜ‚Ş

	// ‘JˆÚŒ³‚ÉEntry‚ğw’è‚·‚é‚Æ‚«‚ÌID
	static constexpr int kEntryId = 0;

	
	
	// ‹ó‚ÌƒOƒ‰ƒt‚É‚·‚é
	void Clear();

	// State
	int  AddState(const std::string& baseName, const EditorPosition& pos); // ì¬
	bool RenameState(int stateId, const std::string& newName);             // –¼‘O•ÏX
	void RemoveState(int stateId);                                         // íœ

	// ‘JˆÚ
	int  AddTransition(int fromStateId, int toStateId); // ì¬
	void RemoveTransition(int transitionId);            // íœ
	bool MoveTransition(int transitionId, int offset);  // —Dæ“x‚Ì“ü‚ê‘Ö‚¦

	// qŠK‘wFƒNƒŠƒbƒv
	int  AddClip(int stateId, const std::string& name, const EditorPosition& pos); // ì¬
	void RemoveClip(int stateId, int clipId);                                      // íœ

	// qŠK‘wF(ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[ ¨ ƒNƒŠƒbƒv)
	int  AddEntryLink(int stateId, int transitionId, int toClipId); // ì¬
	void RemoveEntryLink(int stateId, int linkId);                  // íœ
	bool MoveEntryLink(int stateId, int linkId, int offset);        // —Dæ“x‚Ì“ü‚ê‘Ö‚¦

	// qŠK‘wF(ƒNƒŠƒbƒv ¨ ƒNƒŠƒbƒv)
	int  AddClipLink(int stateId, int fromClipId, int toClipId); // ì¬
	void RemoveClipLink(int stateId, int linkId);                // íœ
	bool MoveClipLink(int stateId, int linkId, int offset);      // —Dæ“x‚Ì“ü‚ê‘Ö‚¦

	// •ÒW—p‚ÌŒŸõ
	StateNode* FindState(int stateId);
	StateTransition* FindTransition(int transitionId);

	// ‘¼‚ÌState‚Æd•¡‚µ‚È‚¢–¼‘O‚ğì‚é
	std::string MakeUniqueStateName(const std::string& baseName) const;



	// ®‡«ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
	bool Validate(const std::string& sourceName) const;

	// JSON‚Æ‚Ì•ÏŠ·
	nlohmann::ordered_json ToJson() const;
	bool FromJson(const nlohmann::json& j, const std::string& sourceName);

private:
	int nextId = 1;

	// ‘JˆÚ‚ğ‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚éƒŠƒXƒg(EntryA‚Ü‚½‚Í‚Ç‚ê‚©‚ÌState)‚ğ’T‚·
	std::vector<StateTransition>* FindTransitionOwner(int transitionId);
};

#include "AnimationSpeedData.h"
#include <algorithm>

// w’è‚µ‚½ƒNƒŠƒbƒv–¼‚Ìƒf[ƒ^‚ğ’T‚·
AnimationSpeedClipData* AnimationSpeedData::FindOrCreateClip(const std::string& clipName)
{
	for (auto& clip : clips)
	{
		if (clip.clipName == clipName) return &clip;
	}

	AnimationSpeedClipData newClip;
	newClip.clipName = clipName;

	// n’[/I’[‚ÌŒÅ’èƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ğ1.0”{‚Å”z’u‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­
	AnimationSpeedPoint startPoint;
	startPoint.id = newClip.GenerateId();
	startPoint.time = 0.0f;
	startPoint.speed = 1.0f;
	startPoint.isEndpoint = true;
	newClip.points.push_back(startPoint);

	AnimationSpeedPoint endPoint;
	endPoint.id = newClip.GenerateId();
	endPoint.time = 1.0f;
	endPoint.speed = 1.0f;
	endPoint.isEndpoint = true;
	newClip.points.push_back(endPoint);

	clips.push_back(newClip);
	return &clips.back();
}

// ³‹K‰»ŠÔ‚É‚¨‚¯‚é‘¬“x‚ğüŒ`•âŠ®‚Å‹‚ß‚é
float AnimationSpeedClipData::EvaluateSpeed(float normalizedTime) const
{
	if (points.empty()) return 1.0f;

	// ƒ\[ƒg—pˆê”z—ñ
	std::vector<const AnimationSpeedPoint*> sorted;
	sorted.reserve(points.size());
	for (const auto& p : points) sorted.push_back(&p);
	std::sort(sorted.begin(), sorted.end(),
		[](const AnimationSpeedPoint* a, const AnimationSpeedPoint* b) { return a->time < b->time; });

	// —¼’[
	if (normalizedTime <= sorted.front()->time) return sorted.front()->speed;
	if (normalizedTime >= sorted.back()->time)  return sorted.back()->speed;

	// ‹²‚ñ‚Å‚¢‚é2“_‚ğ’T‚µ‚ÄüŒ`•âŠÔ
	for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < sorted.size(); ++i)
	{
		const AnimationSpeedPoint* a = sorted[i];
		const AnimationSpeedPoint* b = sorted[i + 1];
		if (normalizedTime >= a->time && normalizedTime <= b->time)
		{
			float range = b->time - a->time;
			float t = (range > 0.0f) ? (normalizedTime - a->time) / range : 0.0f;
			return a->speed + (b->speed - a->speed) * t;
		}
	}

	return 1.0f;
}

// •Û‘¶
nlohmann::ordered_json AnimationSpeedData::ToJson() const
{
	nlohmann::ordered_json j;
	j["clips"] = nlohmann::json::array();

	for (const auto& clip : clips)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jc;
		jc["clipName"] = clip.clipName;
		jc["nextId"] = clip.nextId;

		jc["points"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& p : clip.points)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jp;
			jp["id"] = p.id;
			jp["time"] = p.time;
			jp["speed"] = p.speed;
			jp["isEndpoint"] = p.isEndpoint;
			jc["points"].push_back(jp);
		}

		j["clips"].push_back(jc);
	}

	return j;
}

// “Ç‚İ‚İ
void AnimationSpeedData::FromJson(const nlohmann::json& j)
{
	clips.clear();

	if (!j.contains("clips")) return;

	for (const auto& jc : j["clips"])
	{
		AnimationSpeedClipData clip;
		clip.clipName = jc.value("clipName", "");
		clip.nextId = jc.value("nextId", 1);

		if (jc.contains("points"))
		{
			for (const auto& jp : jc["points"])
			{
				AnimationSpeedPoint p;
				p.id = jp.value("id", -1);
				p.time = jp.value("time", 0.0f);
				p.speed = jp.value("speed", 1.0f);
				p.isEndpoint = jp.value("isEndpoint", false);
				clip.points.push_back(p);
			}
		}

		clips.push_back(clip);
	}
} 
 
===== ConditionGraphData.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Data\ConditionGraphData.cpp 
#include "ConditionGraphData.h"
#include <algorithm>

namespace
{
	// ValueType
	std::string ToString(ValueType type)
	{
		switch (type)
		{
		case ValueType::Int:         return "Int";
		case ValueType::Float:       return "Float";
		case ValueType::Bool:        return "Bool";
		case ValueType::Float2:      return "Float2";
		case ValueType::Float3:      return "Float3";
		case ValueType::Float4:      return "Float4";
		case ValueType::Unsupported: return "Unsupported";
		default:                     return "Float";
		}
	}
	ValueType ToValueType(const std::string& s)
	{
		if (s == "Int")         return ValueType::Int;
		if (s == "Bool")        return ValueType::Bool;
		if (s == "Float2")      return ValueType::Float2;
		if (s == "Float3")      return ValueType::Float3;
		if (s == "Float4")      return ValueType::Float4;
		if (s == "Unsupported") return ValueType::Unsupported;
		return ValueType::Float;
	}

	// FloatComponent
	std::string ToString(FloatComponent c)
	{
		switch (c)
		{
		case FloatComponent::X: return "X";
		case FloatComponent::Y: return "Y";
		case FloatComponent::Z: return "Z";
		case FloatComponent::W: return "W";
		default:                return "None";
		}
	}
	FloatComponent ToFloatComponent(const std::string& s)
	{
		if (s == "X") return FloatComponent::X;
		if (s == "Y") return FloatComponent::Y;
		if (s == "Z") return FloatComponent::Z;
		if (s == "W") return FloatComponent::W;
		return FloatComponent::None;
	}

	// CompareOp
	std::string ToString(CompareOp op)
	{
		switch (op)
		{
		case CompareOp::Greater:      return "Greater";
		case CompareOp::Less:         return "Less";
		case CompareOp::Equal:        return "Equal";
		case CompareOp::NotEqual:     return "NotEqual";
		case CompareOp::GreaterEqual: return "GreaterEqual";
		case CompareOp::LessEqual:    return "LessEqual";
		default:                      return "Greater";
		}
	}
	CompareOp ToCompareOp(const std::string& s)
	{
		if (s == "Less")         return CompareOp::Less;
		if (s == "Equal")        return CompareOp::Equal;
		if (s == "NotEqual")     return CompareOp::NotEqual;
		if (s == "GreaterEqual") return CompareOp::GreaterEqual;
		if (s == "LessEqual")    return CompareOp::LessEqual;
		return CompareOp::Greater;
	}

	// LogicOp
	std::string ToString(LogicOp op)
	{
		switch (op)
		{
		case LogicOp::And: return "And";
		case LogicOp::Or:  return "Or";
		default:           return "And";
		}
	}
	LogicOp ToLogicOp(const std::string& s)
	{
		if (s == "Or") return LogicOp::Or;
		return LogicOp::And;
	}

	// ID‚Å—v‘f‚ğ’T‚·
	template<typename T>
	const T* FindById(const std::vector<T>& list, int id)
	{
		for (const auto& e : list)
		{
			if (e.id == id) return &e;
		}
		return nullptr;
	}

	// ID‚Å—v‘f‚ğÁ‚·
	template<typename T>
	bool EraseById(std::vector<T>& list, int id)
	{
		auto it = std::remove_if(list.begin(), list.end(), [id](const T& e) { return e.id == id; });
		if (it == list.end()) return false;
		list.erase(it, list.end());
		return true;
	}

	// ‘å¬‚Ì”äŠr‚©
	bool IsOrderCompareOp(CompareOp op)
	{
		return op == CompareOp::Greater || op == CompareOp::Less
			|| op == CompareOp::GreaterEqual || op == CompareOp::LessEqual;
	}
}

// g—p’†‚ÌID‚ÌÅ‘å’l+1‚ÉAÌ”Ô‚ğ‚»‚ë‚¦‚é
void ConditionGraph::RecalculateNextId()
{
	int maxId = returnNode.id;
	for (const auto& n : variableNodes) maxId = (std::max)(maxId, n.id);
	for (const auto& n : getterNodes)   maxId = (std::max)(maxId, n.id);
	for (const auto& n : operatorNodes) maxId = (std::max)(maxId, n.id);
	for (const auto& n : logicNodes)    maxId = (std::max)(maxId, n.id);
	for (const auto& n : notNodes)      maxId = (std::max)(maxId, n.id);
	for (const auto& l : links)         maxId = (std::max)(maxId, l.id);
	nextId = maxId + 1;
}

// Return‚É‰½‚à‚Â‚È‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢‚©
bool ConditionGraph::IsEmpty() const
{
	for (const auto& l : links)
	{
		if (l.toId == returnNode.id) return false;
	}
	return true;
}

// ƒm[ƒh‚Ìí—Ş
ConditionNodeKind ConditionGraph::GetNodeKind(int nodeId) const
{
	if (nodeId == returnNode.id)            return ConditionNodeKind::Return;
	if (FindById(variableNodes, nodeId))    return ConditionNodeKind::Variable;
	if (FindById(getterNodes, nodeId))      return ConditionNodeKind::Getter;
	if (FindById(operatorNodes, nodeId))    return ConditionNodeKind::Operator;
	if (FindById(logicNodes, nodeId))       return ConditionNodeKind::Logic;
	if (FindById(notNodes, nodeId))         return ConditionNodeKind::Not;
	return ConditionNodeKind::None;
}

// o—Íƒsƒ“‚ªo‚·’l‚ÌŒ^
bool ConditionGraph::GetOutputType(int nodeId, ValueType& outType) const
{
	switch (GetNodeKind(nodeId))
	{
	case ConditionNodeKind::Variable:
		outType = FindById(variableNodes, nodeId)->type;
		return true;

	case ConditionNodeKind::Getter:
	{
		const ConditionGetterNode* g = FindById(getterNodes, nodeId);
		// Float2`4‚ÍAg‚¦‚é¬•ª(.xyz)‚ğ‘I‚ñ‚Å‚¢‚ê‚ÎFloat‚Æ‚µ‚Äˆµ‚¤
		outType = IsGetterAxisValid(*g) ? ValueType::Float : GetGetterFieldType(*g);
		return true;
	}
	// ”äŠrE˜_—E”Û’è‚Ìo—Í‚Íí‚ÉBool
	case ConditionNodeKind::Operator:
	case ConditionNodeKind::Logic:
	case ConditionNodeKind::Not:
		outType = ValueType::Bool;
		return true;

		// Return‚Ío—Íƒsƒ“‚ğ‚½‚È‚¢
	default:
		return false;
	}
}

// •Ï”ƒm[ƒh‚Ì’Ç‰Á
int ConditionGraph::AddVariableNode(ValueType type, float x, float y)
{
	ConditionVariableNode n;
	n.id = GenerateId();
	n.posX = x;
	n.posY = y;
	n.type = type;
	variableNodes.push_back(n);
	return n.id;
}
// ƒQƒbƒ^[ƒm[ƒh‚Ì’Ç‰Á
int ConditionGraph::AddGetterNode(float x, float y)
{
	ConditionGetterNode n;
	n.id = GenerateId();
	n.posX = x;
	n.posY = y;
	getterNodes.push_back(n);
	return n.id;
}
// ”äŠrƒm[ƒh’Ç‰Á
int ConditionGraph::AddOperatorNode(CompareOp op, float x, float y)
{
	ConditionOperatorNode n;
	n.id = GenerateId();
	n.posX = x;
	n.posY = y;
	n.op = op;
	operatorNodes.push_back(n);
	return n.id;
}
// ˜_—‰‰Zƒm[ƒh’Ç‰Á
int ConditionGraph::AddLogicNode(LogicOp op, float x, float y)
{
	ConditionLogicNode n;
	n.id = GenerateId();
	n.posX = x;
	n.posY = y;
	n.op = op;
	logicNodes.push_back(n);
	return n.id;
}
// ”Û’èƒm[ƒh’Ç‰Á
int ConditionGraph::AddNotNode(float x, float y)
{
	ConditionNotNode n;
	n.id = GenerateId();
	n.posX = x;
	n.posY = y;
	notNodes.push_back(n);
	return n.id;
}

// ƒm[ƒh‚Ìíœ
void ConditionGraph::RemoveNode(int nodeId)
{
	// Return‚ÍğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt‚É•K‚¸1‚Â‚ ‚é‚Ì‚ÅÁ‚³‚È‚¢
	if (nodeId == returnNode.id) return;

	bool removed = EraseById(variableNodes, nodeId)
		|| EraseById(getterNodes, nodeId)
		|| EraseById(operatorNodes, nodeId)
		|| EraseById(logicNodes, nodeId)
		|| EraseById(notNodes, nodeId);
	if (!removed) return;

	// ‚Â‚È‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚½ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚àÁ‚·
	links.erase(
		std::remove_if(links.begin(), links.end(),
			[nodeId](const ConditionLink& l) { return l.fromId == nodeId || l.toId == nodeId; }),
		links.end());
}

// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğì‚ê‚é‚©
bool ConditionGraph::CanLink(int fromNodeId, int toNodeId, bool toPinB, std::string* reason) const
{
	return CheckLink(fromNodeId, toNodeId, toPinB, -1, reason);
}
// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğì‚é
int ConditionGraph::AddLink(int fromNodeId, int toNodeId, bool toPinB)
{
	if (!CanLink(fromNodeId, toNodeId, toPinB)) return -1;

	// “ü—Íƒsƒ“‚É‚Í1–{‚¾‚¯
	links.erase(
		std::remove_if(links.begin(), links.end(),
			[=](const ConditionLink& l) { return l.toId == toNodeId && l.toPinB == toPinB; }),
		links.end());

	ConditionLink l;
	l.id = GenerateId();
	l.fromId = fromNodeId;
	l.toId = toNodeId;
	l.toPinB = toPinB;
	links.push_back(l);
	return l.id;
}

// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìíœ
void ConditionGraph::RemoveLink(int linkId)
{
	EraseById(links, linkId);
}

// ¡‚Ìİ’è‚ÅAƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ªŒˆ‚Ü‚è‚É‡‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚©
bool ConditionGraph::IsLinkValid(const ConditionLink& link, std::string* reason) const
{
	return CheckLink(link.fromId, link.toId, link.toPinB, link.id, reason);
}


// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÌŒˆ‚Ü‚è‚ÌŠm”F
bool ConditionGraph::CheckLink(int fromNodeId, int toNodeId, bool toPinB, int ignoreLinkId, std::string* reason) const
{
	auto fail = [reason](const char* message)
		{
			if (reason) *reason = message;
			return false;
		};

	if (fromNodeId == toNodeId) return fail("Cannot link a node to itself.");

	// o‚·‘¤‚ÌŒ^
	ValueType fromType;
	if (!GetOutputType(fromNodeId, fromType)) return fail("Source has no output.");

	// ƒQƒbƒ^[‚ÍA“Ç‚ß‚éƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ğ‘I‚ñ‚Å‚¢‚È‚¢‚Æ‚Â‚È‚°‚È‚¢
	if (GetNodeKind(fromNodeId) == ConditionNodeKind::Getter)
	{
		const ConditionGetterNode* g = FindById(getterNodes, fromNodeId);
		if (g->componentName.empty() || g->fieldName.empty()) return fail("Select a field.");
		if (!Reflection::FindField(g->componentName, g->fieldName)) return fail("Field not found.");
	}
	if (fromType == ValueType::Unsupported) return fail("This field cannot be read in conditions.");

	// Float2`4‚ÍA¬•ª(X/Y/Z/W)‚ğ‘I‚Î‚È‚¢‚Æ”ä‚×‚ç‚ê‚È‚¢
	if (Reflection::GetComponentCount(fromType) > 0) return fail("Select an axis (X/Y/Z/W).");

	// ó‚¯‚é‘¤‚ÉA‚»‚Ìƒsƒ“‚ª‚ ‚é‚©
	ConditionNodeKind toKind = GetNodeKind(toNodeId);
	bool hasPinA = (toKind == ConditionNodeKind::Operator || toKind == ConditionNodeKind::Logic
		|| toKind == ConditionNodeKind::Not || toKind == ConditionNodeKind::Return);
	bool hasPinB = (toKind == ConditionNodeKind::Operator || toKind == ConditionNodeKind::Logic);
	if (toPinB ? !hasPinB : !hasPinA) return fail("Target has no such input.");

	// Œ^‚ÌŒˆ‚Ü‚è
	if (toKind == ConditionNodeKind::Operator)
	{
		// Bool‚Í == ‚Æ != ‚¾‚¯
		const ConditionOperatorNode* o = FindById(operatorNodes, toNodeId);
		if (fromType == ValueType::Bool && IsOrderCompareOp(o->op))
		{
			return fail("Bool can only be compared with == or !=.");
		}

		// A‚ÆB‚Í“¯‚¶Œ^
		for (const auto& l : links)
		{
			if (l.id == ignoreLinkId) continue;
			if (l.toId != toNodeId || l.toPinB == toPinB) continue;

			ValueType otherType;
			if (GetOutputType(l.fromId, otherType) && otherType != fromType)
			{
				return fail("A and B must be the same type.");
			}
		}
	}
	else
	{
		// ˜_—E”Û’èEReturn‚ÍABool‚¾‚¯
		if (fromType != ValueType::Bool) return fail("Input must be Bool.");
	}

	// —Ö‚ğì‚ç‚È‚¢
	if (DependsOn(fromNodeId, toNodeId, ignoreLinkId)) return fail("Cannot create a loop.");

	return true;
}

// nodeId‚Ì“ü—Í‚ğŒã‚ë‚Ö‚½‚Ç‚Á‚ÄAtargetId‚É‚½‚Ç‚è’…‚­‚©
bool ConditionGraph::DependsOn(int nodeId, int targetId, int ignoreLinkId) const
{
	// ‚½‚Ç‚é“r’†‚Å“¯‚¶ƒm[ƒh‚ğ‰½“x‚àŒ©‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚ÉAŒ©‚½‚à‚Ì‚ğŠo‚¦‚Ä‚¨‚­
	std::vector<int> stack{ nodeId };
	std::vector<int> visited;

	while (!stack.empty())
	{
		int current = stack.back();
		stack.pop_back();
		if (current == targetId) return true;
		if (std::find(visited.begin(), visited.end(), current) != visited.end()) continue;
		visited.push_back(current);

		// current‚É“ü‚Á‚Ä‚­‚éƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÌŒ³‚ğ‚½‚Ç‚é
		for (const auto& l : links)
		{
			if (l.id == ignoreLinkId) continue;
			if (l.toId == current) stack.push_back(l.fromId);
		}
	}
	return false;
}

// JSON‚Ö•ÏŠ·
nlohmann::ordered_json ConditionGraphToJson(const ConditionGraph& graph)
{
	nlohmann::ordered_json jc;
	jc["returnNode"]["id"] = graph.returnNode.id;
	jc["returnNode"]["posX"] = graph.returnNode.posX;
	jc["returnNode"]["posY"] = graph.returnNode.posY;

	jc["variableNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& v : graph.variableNodes)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jv;
		jv["id"] = v.id;
		jv["posX"] = v.posX;
		jv["posY"] = v.posY;
		jv["name"] = v.name;
		jv["type"] = ToString(v.type);
		jv["intValue"] = v.intValue;
		jv["floatValue"] = v.floatValue;
		jv["boolValue"] = v.boolValue;
		jc["variableNodes"].push_back(jv);
	}

	jc["operatorNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& o : graph.operatorNodes)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jo;
		jo["id"] = o.id;
		jo["posX"] = o.posX;
		jo["posY"] = o.posY;
		jo["op"] = ToString(o.op);
		jc["operatorNodes"].push_back(jo);
	}

	jc["logicNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& l : graph.logicNodes)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jl;
		jl["id"] = l.id;
		jl["posX"] = l.posX;
		jl["posY"] = l.posY;
		jl["op"] = ToString(l.op);
		jc["logicNodes"].push_back(jl);
	}

	jc["notNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& n : graph.notNodes)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jn;
		jn["id"] = n.id;
		jn["posX"] = n.posX;
		jn["posY"] = n.posY;
		jc["notNodes"].push_back(jn);
	}

	jc["getterNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& g : graph.getterNodes)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jg;
		jg["id"] = g.id;
		jg["posX"] = g.posX;
		jg["posY"] = g.posY;
		jg["componentName"] = g.componentName;
		jg["fieldName"] = g.fieldName;
		jg["selectedComponent"] = ToString(g.selectedComponent);
		jc["getterNodes"].push_back(jg);
	}

	jc["links"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& cl : graph.links)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jcl;
		jcl["id"] = cl.id;
		jcl["fromId"] = cl.fromId;
		jcl["toId"] = cl.toId;
		jcl["toPinB"] = cl.toPinB;
		jc["links"].push_back(jcl);
	}

	return jc;
}

// JSON‚©‚ç•œŒ³
void ConditionGraphFromJson(ConditionGraph& graph, const nlohmann::json& jc)
{
	graph = ConditionGraph{};

	if (jc.contains("returnNode"))
	{
		graph.returnNode.id = jc["returnNode"].value("id", 1);
		graph.returnNode.posX = jc["returnNode"].value("posX", 500.0f);
		graph.returnNode.posY = jc["returnNode"].value("posY", 200.0f);
	}

	if (jc.contains("variableNodes"))
	{
		for (const auto& jv : jc["variableNodes"])
		{
			ConditionVariableNode v;
			v.id = jv.value("id", -1);
			v.posX = jv.value("posX", 0.0f);
			v.posY = jv.value("posY", 0.0f);
			v.name = jv.value("name", "");
			v.type = ToValueType(jv.value("type", "Float"));
			v.intValue = jv.value("intValue", 0);
			v.floatValue = jv.value("floatValue", 0.0f);
			v.boolValue = jv.value("boolValue", false);
			graph.variableNodes.push_back(v);
		}
	}

	if (jc.contains("operatorNodes"))
	{
		for (const auto& jo : jc["operatorNodes"])
		{
			ConditionOperatorNode o;
			o.id = jo.value("id", -1);
			o.posX = jo.value("posX", 0.0f);
			o.posY = jo.value("posY", 0.0f);
			o.op = ToCompareOp(jo.value("op", "Greater"));
			graph.operatorNodes.push_back(o);
		}
	}

	if (jc.contains("logicNodes"))
	{
		for (const auto& jl : jc["logicNodes"])
		{
			ConditionLogicNode l;
			l.id = jl.value("id", -1);
			l.posX = jl.value("posX", 0.0f);
			l.posY = jl.value("posY", 0.0f);
			l.op = ToLogicOp(jl.value("op", "And"));
			graph.logicNodes.push_back(l);
		}
	}

	if (jc.contains("notNodes"))
	{
		for (const auto& jn : jc["notNodes"])
		{
			ConditionNotNode n;
			n.id = jn.value("id", -1);
			n.posX = jn.value("posX", 0.0f);
			n.posY = jn.value("posY", 0.0f);
			graph.notNodes.push_back(n);
		}
	}

	if (jc.contains("getterNodes"))
	{
		for (const auto& jg : jc["getterNodes"])
		{
			ConditionGetterNode g;
			g.id = jg.value("id", -1);
			g.posX = jg.value("posX", 0.0f);
			g.posY = jg.value("posY", 0.0f);
			g.componentName = jg.value("componentName", "");
			g.fieldName = jg.value("fieldName", "");

			// ŒÃ‚¢•Û‘¶ƒf[ƒ^‚Í•\¦–¼‚Å•Û‘¶‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚ÅAƒR[ƒhã‚Ì–¼‘O‚É’u‚«Š·‚¦‚é
			if (!g.componentName.empty() && !g.fieldName.empty() &&
				!Reflection::FindField(g.componentName, g.fieldName))
			{
				if (const FieldEntry* f = Reflection::FindFieldByLabel(g.componentName, g.fieldName))
				{
					g.fieldName = f->fieldName;
				}
			}
			g.selectedComponent = ToFloatComponent(jg.value("selectedComponent", "None"));
			graph.getterNodes.push_back(g);
		}
	}

	if (jc.contains("links"))
	{
		for (const auto& jcl : jc["links"])
		{
			ConditionLink cl;
			cl.id = jcl.value("id", -1);
			cl.fromId = jcl.value("fromId", -1);
			cl.toId = jcl.value("toId", -1);
			cl.toPinB = jcl.value("toPinB", false);
			graph.links.push_back(cl);
		}
	}

	// “Ç‚İ‚ñ‚¾ID‚Ì‘±‚«‚©‚çÌ”Ô‚·‚é
	graph.RecalculateNextId();
}

// ƒQƒbƒ^[‚ª“Ç‚ŞƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ÌŒ^
ValueType GetGetterFieldType(const ConditionGetterNode& node)
{
	if (node.componentName.empty() || node.fieldName.empty()) return ValueType::Unsupported;

	const FieldEntry* field = Reflection::FindField(node.componentName, node.fieldName);
	return field ? field->type : ValueType::Unsupported;
}
// ƒQƒbƒ^[‚Å‘I‚ñ‚Å‚¢‚é¬•ª‚ªA‚»‚ÌŒ^‚Åg‚¦‚é‚©
bool IsGetterAxisValid(const ConditionGetterNode& node)
{
	int count = Reflection::GetComponentCount(GetGetterFieldType(node));
	int index = static_cast<int>(node.selectedComponent); // None=0, X=1, Y=2, Z=3, W=4
	return count > 0 && index >= 1 && index <= count;
}
 
 
===== SequencerData.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\Data\SequencerData.cpp 
#include "SequencerData.h"

namespace
{
	std::string ToString(SequencerFlag flag)
	{
		switch (flag)
		{
		case SequencerFlag::AnimExit:   return "AnimExit";
		case SequencerFlag::SuperArmor: return "SuperArmor";
		case SequencerFlag::Attack:     return "Attack";
		case SequencerFlag::Trimming:   return "Trimming";
		}
		return "AnimExit";
	}
	SequencerFlag ToSequencerFlag(const std::string& s)
	{
		if (s == "SuperArmor") return SequencerFlag::SuperArmor;
		if (s == "Attack")     return SequencerFlag::Attack;
		if (s == "Trimming")   return SequencerFlag::Trimming;
		return SequencerFlag::AnimExit;
	}
}


// w’è‚µ‚½ƒNƒŠƒbƒv–¼‚Ìƒf[ƒ^‚ğ’T‚·
SequencerClipData* SequencerData::FindOrCreateClip(const std::string& clipName)
{
	for (auto& clip : clips)
	{
		if (clip.clipName == clipName) return &clip;
	}

	SequencerClipData newClip;
	newClip.clipName = clipName;
	clips.push_back(newClip);
	return &clips.back();
}


// •Û‘¶
nlohmann::ordered_json SequencerData::ToJson() const
{
	nlohmann::ordered_json j;
	j["clips"] = nlohmann::json::array();

	for (const auto& clip : clips)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jc;
		jc["clipName"] = clip.clipName;
		jc["nextId"] = clip.nextId;

		jc["ranges"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& r : clip.ranges)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jr;
			jr["id"] = r.id;
			jr["start"] = r.start;
			jr["end"] = r.end;
			jr["flag"] = ToString(r.flag);
			jc["ranges"].push_back(jr);
		}

		jc["points"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& p : clip.points)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jp;
			jp["id"] = p.id;
			jp["time"] = p.time;
			jp["flag"] = ToString(p.flag);
			jc["points"].push_back(jp);
		}

		j["clips"].push_back(jc);
	}

	return j;
}

// “Ç‚İ‚İ
void SequencerData::FromJson(const nlohmann::json& j)
{
	clips.clear();

	if (!j.contains("clips")) return;

	for (const auto& jc : j["clips"])
	{
		SequencerClipData clip;
		clip.clipName = jc.value("clipName", "");
		clip.nextId = jc.value("nextId", 1);

		if (jc.contains("ranges"))
		{
			for (const auto& jr : jc["ranges"])
			{
				SequencerRange r;
				r.id = jr.value("id", -1);
				r.start = jr.value("start", 0.0f);
				r.end = jr.value("end", 1.0f);
				r.flag = ToSequencerFlag(jr.value("flag", "AnimExit"));
				clip.ranges.push_back(r);
			}
		}

		if (jc.contains("points"))
		{
			for (const auto& jp : jc["points"])
			{
				SequencerPoint p;
				p.id = jp.value("id", -1);
				p.time = jp.value("time", 0.0f);
				p.flag = ToSequencerFlag(jp.value("flag", "AnimExit"));
				clip.points.push_back(p);
			}
		}

		clips.push_back(clip);
	}
}
