#include "StateMachineData.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <utility>

#include "Engine/Core/Log.h"

namespace
{
	// ƒGƒfƒBƒ^ã‚ÌˆÊ’u
	nlohmann::ordered_json ToJson(const EditorPosition& p)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json j;
		j["x"] = p.x;
		j["y"] = p.y;
		return j;
	}
	EditorPosition PositionFromJson(const nlohmann::json& j)
	{
		EditorPosition p;
		p.x = j.value("x", 0.0f);
		p.y = j.value("y", 0.0f);
		return p;
	}

	// ‘JˆÚE‚Â‚È‚ª‚è‚Ì‹¤’Ê•”•ª(always / condition)
	void WriteCommon(nlohmann::ordered_json& j, bool always, const ConditionGraph& condition)
	{
		j["always"] = always;
		j["condition"] = ConditionGraphToJson(condition);
	}
	void ReadCommon(const nlohmann::json& j, bool& always, ConditionGraph& condition)
	{
		always = j.value("always", false);
		if (j.contains("condition")) ConditionGraphFromJson(condition, j["condition"]);
	}

	// ƒuƒŒƒ“ƒh‚Ìİ’è
	void WriteBlend(nlohmann::ordered_json& j, const BlendSetting& blend)
	{
		j["blend"] = blend.enabled;
		j["blendDuration"] = blend.duration;
	}
	void ReadBlend(const nlohmann::json& j, BlendSetting& blend)
	{
		blend.enabled = j.value("blend", true);
		blend.duration = j.value("blendDuration", 0.2f);
	}

	// ‘JˆÚ
	nlohmann::ordered_json ToJson(const StateTransition& t)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json j;
		j["id"] = t.id;
		j["to"] = t.toStateId;
		WriteCommon(j, t.always, t.condition);
		WriteBlend(j, t.blend);
		return j;
	}
	StateTransition TransitionFromJson(const nlohmann::json& j)
	{
		StateTransition t;
		t.id = j.value("id", -1);
		t.toStateId = j.value("to", -1);
		ReadCommon(j, t.always, t.condition);
		ReadBlend(j, t.blend);
		return t;
	}

	// “¯‚¶ƒOƒ‹[ƒv‚Ì’†‚Å—×‚Ì‚à‚Ì‚Æ“ü‚ê‘Ö‚¦‚é
	template<typename T, typename GroupFn>
	bool MoveInGroup(std::vector<T>& list, int linkId, int offset, GroupFn groupOf)
	{
		int index = -1;
		for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(list.size()); ++i)
		{
			if (list[i].id == linkId) index = i;
		}
		if (index < 0 || offset == 0) return false;

		// offset‚ÌŒü‚«‚ÉA“¯‚¶ƒOƒ‹[ƒv‚Ì—v‘f‚ğ’T‚µ‚Ä“ü‚ê‘Ö‚¦‚é
		int group = groupOf(list[index]);
		int step = (offset < 0) ? -1 : 1;
		for (int i = index + step; i >= 0 && i < static_cast<int>(list.size()); i += step)
		{
			if (groupOf(list[i]) == group)
			{
				std::swap(list[index], list[i]);
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false; // ’[‚È‚Ì‚Å“®‚©‚¹‚È‚¢
	}
}

// ŒŸõ 
const ClipNode* ChildGraph::FindClip(int clipId) const
{
	for (const auto& c : clips)
	{
		if (c.id == clipId) return &c;
	}
	return nullptr;
}

const StateNode* StateMachineData::FindState(int stateId) const
{
	for (const auto& s : states)
	{
		if (s.id == stateId) return &s;
	}
	return nullptr;
}

const StateNode* StateMachineData::FindStateByName(const std::string& name) const
{
	for (const auto& s : states)
	{
		if (s.name == name) return &s;
	}
	return nullptr;
}

const StateTransition* StateMachineData::FindTransition(int transitionId) const
{
	for (const auto& t : entry.transitions)
	{
		if (t.id == transitionId) return &t;
	}
	for (const auto& s : states)
	{
		for (const auto& t : s.transitions)
		{
			if (t.id == transitionId) return &t;
		}
	}
	return nullptr;
}



// ‹ó‚ÌƒOƒ‰ƒt‚É‚·‚é
void StateMachineData::Clear()
{
	entry = StateMachineEntry{};
	entry.editorPos = { 50.0f, 200.0f };
	states.clear();
	nextId = 1;
}


// State
int StateMachineData::AddState(const std::string& baseName, const EditorPosition& pos)
{
	StateNode s;
	s.id = GenerateId();
	s.name = MakeUniqueStateName(baseName);
	s.editorPos = pos;
	states.push_back(s);
	return s.id;
}
bool StateMachineData::RenameState(int stateId, const std::string& newName)
{
	StateNode* s = FindState(stateId);
	if (!s || newName.empty()) return false;
	if (s->name == newName) return true;

	// ‘¼‚ÌState‚Æd•¡‚·‚é–¼‘O‚Í•t‚¯‚ç‚ê‚È‚¢
	if (FindStateByName(newName)) return false;

	s->name = newName;
	return true;
}
void StateMachineData::RemoveState(int stateId)
{
	// Á‚¦‚é‘JˆÚF‚±‚ÌState‚©‚ço‚é‘JˆÚ‚ÆA‚±‚ÌState‚É“ü‚Á‚Ä‚­‚é‘JˆÚ
	std::vector<int> removedTransitions;
	for (const auto& t : entry.transitions)
	{
		if (t.toStateId == stateId) removedTransitions.push_back(t.id);
	}
	for (const auto& s : states)
	{
		for (const auto& t : s.transitions)
		{
			if (s.id == stateId || t.toStateId == stateId) removedTransitions.push_back(t.id);
		}
	}

	// ‘JˆÚ‚Ìíœ(s‚«æ‚ÌƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚àˆê‚ÉÁ‚¦‚é)
	for (int transitionId : removedTransitions)
	{
		RemoveTransition(transitionId);
	}

	// State–{‘Ì‚Ìíœ
	states.erase(
		std::remove_if(states.begin(), states.end(),
			[stateId](const StateNode& s) { return s.id == stateId; }),
		states.end());
}

// ‘JˆÚ
int StateMachineData::AddTransition(int fromStateId, int toStateId)
{
	if (!FindState(toStateId)) return -1;

	std::vector<StateTransition>* owner = nullptr;
	if (fromStateId == kEntryId)
	{
		owner = &entry.transitions;
	}
	else if (StateNode* from = FindState(fromStateId))
	{
		owner = &from->transitions;
	}
	if (!owner) return -1;

	StateTransition t;
	t.id = GenerateId();
	t.toStateId = toStateId;
	owner->push_back(t); // ÅŒã‚É’Ç‰Á(—Dæ“x‚Íˆê”Ô‰º)

	// s‚«æ‚ÌState‚É‚ÍA‚±‚Ì‘JˆÚ‚ÌƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚ª©“®‚Å‘¶İ‚·‚é
	return t.id;
}
void StateMachineData::RemoveTransition(int transitionId)
{
	std::vector<StateTransition>* owner = FindTransitionOwner(transitionId);
	if (!owner) return;

	// s‚«æ‚ÌState‚©‚çA‚±‚Ì‘JˆÚ‚ÌƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚ÉŠÖ‚·‚é‚à‚Ì‚ğÁ‚·
	int toStateId = -1;
	for (const auto& t : *owner)
	{
		if (t.id == transitionId) toStateId = t.toStateId;
	}
	if (StateNode* to = FindState(toStateId))
	{
		auto& links = to->child.entryLinks;
		links.erase(
			std::remove_if(links.begin(), links.end(),
				[transitionId](const EntryLink& l) { return l.entryTransitionId == transitionId; }),
			links.end());
		to->child.entryEditorPos.erase(transitionId);
	}

	// ‘JˆÚ–{‘Ì‚Ìíœ
	owner->erase(
		std::remove_if(owner->begin(), owner->end(),
			[transitionId](const StateTransition& t) { return t.id == transitionId; }),
		owner->end());
}
bool StateMachineData::MoveTransition(int transitionId, int offset)
{
	std::vector<StateTransition>* owner = FindTransitionOwner(transitionId);
	if (!owner) return false;

	int index = -1;
	for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(owner->size()); ++i)
	{
		if ((*owner)[i].id == transitionId) index = i;
	}

	int newIndex = index + offset;
	if (index < 0 || newIndex < 0 || newIndex >= static_cast<int>(owner->size())) return false;

	std::swap((*owner)[index], (*owner)[newIndex]);
	return true;
}

// qŠK‘wFƒNƒŠƒbƒv
int StateMachineData::AddClip(int stateId, const std::string& name, const EditorPosition& pos)
{
	StateNode* s = FindState(stateId);
	if (!s) return -1;

	ClipNode c;
	c.id = GenerateId();
	c.name = name;
	c.editorPos = pos;
	s->child.clips.push_back(c);
	return c.id;
}
void StateMachineData::RemoveClip(int stateId, int clipId)
{
	StateNode* s = FindState(stateId);
	if (!s) return;

	// ‚±‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚É‚Â‚È‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚½‚à‚Ì‚ğÁ‚·
	auto& entryLinks = s->child.entryLinks;
	entryLinks.erase(
		std::remove_if(entryLinks.begin(), entryLinks.end(),
			[clipId](const EntryLink& l) { return l.toClipId == clipId; }),
		entryLinks.end());

	auto& clipLinks = s->child.clipLinks;
	clipLinks.erase(
		std::remove_if(clipLinks.begin(), clipLinks.end(),
			[clipId](const ClipLink& l) { return l.fromClipId == clipId || l.toClipId == clipId; }),
		clipLinks.end());

	// ƒNƒŠƒbƒv–{‘Ì‚Ìíœ
	auto& clips = s->child.clips;
	clips.erase(
		std::remove_if(clips.begin(), clips.end(),
			[clipId](const ClipNode& c) { return c.id == clipId; }),
		clips.end());
}

// qŠK‘wF(ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[ ¨ ƒNƒŠƒbƒv)
int StateMachineData::AddEntryLink(int stateId, int transitionId, int toClipId)
{
	StateNode* s = FindState(stateId);
	if (!s || !s->child.FindClip(toClipId)) return -1;

	// ‚±‚ÌState‚É“ü‚Á‚Ä‚­‚é‘JˆÚ‚Å‚È‚¯‚ê‚ÎAƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚Í‘¶İ‚µ‚È‚¢
	const StateTransition* t = FindTransition(transitionId);
	if (!t || t->toStateId != stateId) return -1;

	EntryLink l;
	l.id = GenerateId();
	l.entryTransitionId = transitionId;
	l.toClipId = toClipId;
	s->child.entryLinks.push_back(l);
	return l.id;
}
void StateMachineData::RemoveEntryLink(int stateId, int linkId)
{
	StateNode* s = FindState(stateId);
	if (!s) return;

	auto& links = s->child.entryLinks;
	links.erase(
		std::remove_if(links.begin(), links.end(),
			[linkId](const EntryLink& l) { return l.id == linkId; }),
		links.end());
}

// —Dæ“x‚Ì“ü‚ê‘Ö‚¦
bool StateMachineData::MoveEntryLink(int stateId, int linkId, int offset)
{
	StateNode* s = FindState(stateId);
	if (!s) return false;

	return MoveInGroup(s->child.entryLinks, linkId, offset,
		[](const EntryLink& l) { return l.entryTransitionId; });
}

// qŠK‘wF(ƒNƒŠƒbƒv ¨ ƒNƒŠƒbƒv)
int StateMachineData::AddClipLink(int stateId, int fromClipId, int toClipId)
{
	StateNode* s = FindState(stateId);
	if (!s) return -1;
	if (fromClipId == toClipId) return -1; // ©•ª©g‚Ö‚Ì‚Â‚È‚ª‚è‚Íì‚ç‚È‚¢
	if (!s->child.FindClip(fromClipId) || !s->child.FindClip(toClipId)) return -1;

	ClipLink l;
	l.id = GenerateId();
	l.fromClipId = fromClipId;
	l.toClipId = toClipId;
	s->child.clipLinks.push_back(l);
	return l.id;
}
void StateMachineData::RemoveClipLink(int stateId, int linkId)
{
	StateNode* s = FindState(stateId);
	if (!s) return;

	auto& links = s->child.clipLinks;
	links.erase(
		std::remove_if(links.begin(), links.end(),
			[linkId](const ClipLink& l) { return l.id == linkId; }),
		links.end());
}

// —Dæ“x‚Ì“ü‚ê‘Ö‚¦
bool StateMachineData::MoveClipLink(int stateId, int linkId, int offset)
{
	StateNode* s = FindState(stateId);
	if (!s) return false;

	return MoveInGroup(s->child.clipLinks, linkId, offset,
		[](const ClipLink& l) { return l.fromClipId; });
}



// ®‡«ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
bool StateMachineData::Validate(const std::string& sourceName) const
{
	int errorCount = 0;

	auto error = [&](const std::string& message)
		{
			Log(u8"[StateMachine] ƒGƒ‰[(%s): %s", sourceName.c_str(), message.c_str());
			++errorCount;
		};
	auto warning = [&](const std::string& message)
		{
			Log(u8"[StateMachine] Œx(%s): %s", sourceName.c_str(), message.c_str());
		};

	// ID‚Ìd•¡
	std::unordered_set<int> usedIds;
	auto checkId = [&](int id, const std::string& what)
		{
			if (id < 0)
			{
				error(what + u8" ‚ÌID‚ªİ’è‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚Ü‚¹‚ñ");
			}
			else if (!usedIds.insert(id).second)
			{
				error(what + u8" ‚ÌID " + std::to_string(id) + u8" ‚ªd•¡‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚Ü‚·");
			}
		};

	for (const auto& t : entry.transitions) checkId(t.id, u8"Entry‚Ì‘JˆÚ");
	for (const auto& s : states)
	{
		checkId(s.id, u8"Stateu" + s.name + u8"v");
		for (const auto& t : s.transitions)        checkId(t.id, u8"u" + s.name + u8"v‚Ì‘JˆÚ");
		for (const auto& c : s.child.clips)        checkId(c.id, u8"u" + s.name + u8"v‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv");
		for (const auto& l : s.child.entryLinks)   checkId(l.id, u8"u" + s.name + u8"v‚ÌƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚Ì‚Â‚È‚ª‚è");
		for (const auto& l : s.child.clipLinks)    checkId(l.id, u8"u" + s.name + u8"v‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚Ì‚Â‚È‚ª‚è");
	}

	// State‚Ì–¼‘O(Às‘¤‚Í–¼‘O‚ÅQÆ‚·‚é‚Ì‚ÅA‹ó‚Æd•¡‚ğ‹Ö~)
	std::unordered_set<std::string> usedNames;
	for (const auto& s : states)
	{
		if (s.name.empty())
		{
			error(u8"–¼‘O‚Ì–³‚¢State‚ª‚ ‚è‚Ü‚·(ID " + std::to_string(s.id) + u8")");
		}
		else if (!usedNames.insert(s.name).second)
		{
			error(u8"State–¼u" + s.name + u8"v‚ªd•¡‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚Ü‚·");
		}
	}

	// Entry
	if (entry.transitions.empty())
	{
		error(u8"Entry‚©‚ço‚é‘JˆÚ‚ª‚ ‚è‚Ü‚¹‚ñ");
	}

	// ‘JˆÚE‚Â‚È‚ª‚è‚Ì‹¤’Êƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
	auto checkCommon = [&](bool always, const ConditionGraph& condition, const std::string& what)
		{
			if (!always && condition.IsEmpty())
			{
				error(what + u8" ‚ÌğŒ‚ª‹ó‚Å‚·(í‚É¬—§‚³‚¹‚éê‡‚Íalways‚ğw’è)");
			}

			// Getter‚ª“Ç‚ŞƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ªA¡‚ÌƒR[ƒh‚É‚ ‚é‚©(–¼‘O‚ğ•Ï‚¦‚½EÁ‚µ‚½‚Æ‚«‚É‹C‚Ã‚¯‚é‚æ‚¤‚É)
			for (const auto& g : condition.getterNodes)
			{
				if (g.componentName.empty() || g.fieldName.empty()) continue; // –¢İ’è‚ÍƒGƒfƒBƒ^‚Å•\¦‚·‚é
				if (!Reflection::FindField(g.componentName, g.fieldName))
				{
					error(what + u8" ‚ÌğŒ‚ªA‘¶İ‚µ‚È‚¢ƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒhu" + g.componentName + "." + g.fieldName + u8"v‚ğ“Ç‚ñ‚Å‚¢‚Ü‚·");
				}
			}

			// Œˆ‚Ü‚è‚É‡‚í‚È‚¢ƒŠƒ“ƒN(ƒGƒfƒBƒ^‚ÅÔ‚­•\¦‚³‚ê‚é‚à‚Ì)
			for (const auto& l : condition.links)
			{
				std::string reason;
				if (!condition.IsLinkValid(l, &reason))
				{
					error(what + u8" ‚ÌğŒ‚ÉAŒˆ‚Ü‚è‚É‡‚í‚È‚¢ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ª‚ ‚è‚Ü‚·(ƒŠƒ“ƒNID " + std::to_string(l.id) + u8"): " + reason);
				}
			}
		};

	auto checkBlend = [&](const BlendSetting& blend, const std::string& what)
		{
			if (blend.enabled && blend.duration <= 0.0f)
			{
				warning(what + u8" ‚ÌƒuƒŒƒ“ƒhŠÔ‚ª0ˆÈ‰º‚Å‚·(ƒuƒŒƒ“ƒh‚µ‚È‚¢ˆµ‚¢‚É‚È‚è‚Ü‚·)");
			}
		};

	auto checkTransition = [&](const StateTransition& t, const std::string& fromName)
		{
			std::string what = fromName + u8" ‚Ì‘JˆÚ(ID " + std::to_string(t.id) + u8")";
			if (!FindState(t.toStateId))
			{
				error(what + u8" ‚Ìs‚«æ‚ÌState‚ª‚ ‚è‚Ü‚¹‚ñ");
			}
			checkCommon(t.always, t.condition, what);
			checkBlend(t.blend, what);
		};

	for (const auto& t : entry.transitions) checkTransition(t, u8"Entry");
	for (const auto& s : states)
	{
		for (const auto& t : s.transitions) checkTransition(t, u8"u" + s.name + u8"v");
	}

	// qƒOƒ‰ƒt
	for (const auto& s : states)
	{
		// ‚±‚ÌState‚É“ü‚Á‚Ä‚­‚é‘JˆÚ‚ÌID
		std::vector<int> incoming;
		for (const auto& t : entry.transitions)
		{
			if (t.toStateId == s.id) incoming.push_back(t.id);
		}
		for (const auto& from : states)
		{
			for (const auto& t : from.transitions)
			{
				if (t.toStateId == s.id) incoming.push_back(t.id);
			}
		}

		// ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[ ¨ ƒNƒŠƒbƒv
		for (const auto& l : s.child.entryLinks)
		{
			std::string what = u8"u" + s.name + u8"v‚ÌƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚Ì‚Â‚È‚ª‚è(ID " + std::to_string(l.id) + u8")";
			if (std::find(incoming.begin(), incoming.end(), l.entryTransitionId) == incoming.end())
			{
				error(what + u8" ‚É‘Î‰‚·‚éA“ü‚Á‚Ä‚­‚é‘JˆÚ‚ª‚ ‚è‚Ü‚¹‚ñ");
			}
			if (!s.child.FindClip(l.toClipId))
			{
				error(what + u8" ‚Ìs‚«æ‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ª‚ ‚è‚Ü‚¹‚ñ");
			}
			checkCommon(l.always, l.condition, what);
		}

		// ‚Â‚È‚ª‚è‚Ì–³‚¢ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[
		for (int transitionId : incoming)
		{
			bool connected = std::any_of(s.child.entryLinks.begin(), s.child.entryLinks.end(),
				[transitionId](const EntryLink& l) { return l.entryTransitionId == transitionId; });
			if (!connected)
			{
				error(u8"u" + s.name + u8"v‚ÉA‚Ç‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚É‚à‚Â‚È‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚ª‚ ‚è‚Ü‚·(‘JˆÚID " + std::to_string(transitionId) + u8")");
			}
		}

		// ƒNƒŠƒbƒv ¨ ƒNƒŠƒbƒv
		for (const auto& l : s.child.clipLinks)
		{
			std::string what = u8"u" + s.name + u8"v‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚Ì‚Â‚È‚ª‚è(ID " + std::to_string(l.id) + u8")";
			if (!s.child.FindClip(l.fromClipId))
			{
				error(what + u8" ‚ÌŒ³‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ª‚ ‚è‚Ü‚¹‚ñ");
			}
			if (!s.child.FindClip(l.toClipId))
			{
				error(what + u8" ‚Ìs‚«æ‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ª‚ ‚è‚Ü‚¹‚ñ");
			}
			checkCommon(l.always, l.condition, what);
			checkBlend(l.blend, what);
		}
	}

	return errorCount == 0;
}

// JSON
nlohmann::ordered_json StateMachineData::ToJson() const
{
	nlohmann::ordered_json j;
	j["version"] = kVersion;
	j["nextId"] = nextId;

	// Entry
	nlohmann::ordered_json je;
	je["transitions"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& t : entry.transitions) je["transitions"].push_back(::ToJson(t));
	je["editor"] = ::ToJson(entry.editorPos);
	j["entry"] = je;

	// States
	j["states"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& s : states)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json js;
		js["id"] = s.id;
		js["name"] = s.name;

		js["transitions"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& t : s.transitions) js["transitions"].push_back(::ToJson(t));

		// qƒOƒ‰ƒt
		nlohmann::ordered_json jc;
		jc["clips"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& c : s.child.clips)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jclip;
			jclip["id"] = c.id;
			jclip["name"] = c.name;
			jclip["clip"] = c.clipName;
			jclip["loop"] = c.loop;
			jclip["rootMotion"] = c.rootMotion;
			jclip["editor"] = ::ToJson(c.editorPos);
			jc["clips"].push_back(jclip);
		}

		jc["entryLinks"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& l : s.child.entryLinks)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jl;
			jl["id"] = l.id;
			jl["entry"] = l.entryTransitionId;
			jl["to"] = l.toClipId;
			WriteCommon(jl, l.always, l.condition);
			jc["entryLinks"].push_back(jl);
		}

		jc["clipLinks"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& l : s.child.clipLinks)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jl;
			jl["id"] = l.id;
			jl["from"] = l.fromClipId;
			jl["to"] = l.toClipId;
			WriteCommon(jl, l.always, l.condition);
			WriteBlend(jl, l.blend);
			jc["clipLinks"].push_back(jl);
		}

		// ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚ÌƒGƒfƒBƒ^ã‚ÌˆÊ’u (ƒL[‚ÍA“ü‚Á‚Ä‚­‚é‘JˆÚ‚ÌID)
		nlohmann::ordered_json jentries = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& [transitionId, pos] : s.child.entryEditorPos)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jp = ::ToJson(pos);
			jp["entry"] = transitionId;
			jentries.push_back(jp);
		}
		jc["editor"]["entries"] = jentries;

		js["child"] = jc;

		js["editor"] = ::ToJson(s.editorPos);
		js["editor"]["color"] = { s.editorColor[0], s.editorColor[1], s.editorColor[2] };

		j["states"].push_back(js);
	}

	return j;
}

bool StateMachineData::FromJson(const nlohmann::json& j, const std::string& sourceName)
{
	entry = StateMachineEntry{};
	states.clear();
	nextId = 1;

	int version = j.value("version", 0);
	if (version != kVersion)
	{
		Log(u8"[StateMachine] ƒGƒ‰[(%s): ‘Î‰‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢Œ`®‚Å‚·(version %d)", sourceName.c_str(), version);
		return false;
	}

	// Entry
	if (j.contains("entry"))
	{
		const auto& je = j["entry"];
		if (je.contains("transitions"))
		{
			for (const auto& jt : je["transitions"]) entry.transitions.push_back(TransitionFromJson(jt));
		}
		if (je.contains("editor")) entry.editorPos = PositionFromJson(je["editor"]);
	}

	// States
	if (j.contains("states"))
	{
		for (const auto& js : j["states"])
		{
			StateNode s;
			s.id = js.value("id", -1);
			s.name = js.value("name", "");

			if (js.contains("transitions"))
			{
				for (const auto& jt : js["transitions"]) s.transitions.push_back(TransitionFromJson(jt));
			}

			if (js.contains("child"))
			{
				const auto& jc = js["child"];

				if (jc.contains("clips"))
				{
					for (const auto& jclip : jc["clips"])
					{
						ClipNode c;
						c.id = jclip.value("id", -1);
						c.name = jclip.value("name", "");
						c.clipName = jclip.value("clip", "");
						c.loop = jclip.value("loop", true);
						c.rootMotion = jclip.value("rootMotion", false);
						if (jclip.contains("editor")) c.editorPos = PositionFromJson(jclip["editor"]);
						s.child.clips.push_back(c);
					}
				}

				if (jc.contains("entryLinks"))
				{
					for (const auto& jl : jc["entryLinks"])
					{
						EntryLink l;
						l.id = jl.value("id", -1);
						l.entryTransitionId = jl.value("entry", -1);
						l.toClipId = jl.value("to", -1);
						ReadCommon(jl, l.always, l.condition);
						s.child.entryLinks.push_back(l);
					}
				}

				if (jc.contains("clipLinks"))
				{
					for (const auto& jl : jc["clipLinks"])
					{
						ClipLink l;
						l.id = jl.value("id", -1);
						l.fromClipId = jl.value("from", -1);
						l.toClipId = jl.value("to", -1);
						ReadCommon(jl, l.always, l.condition);
						ReadBlend(jl, l.blend);
						s.child.clipLinks.push_back(l);
					}
				}

				if (jc.contains("editor") && jc["editor"].contains("entries"))
				{
					for (const auto& jp : jc["editor"]["entries"])
					{
						s.child.entryEditorPos[jp.value("entry", -1)] = PositionFromJson(jp);
					}
				}
			}

			if (js.contains("editor"))
			{
				const auto& jed = js["editor"];
				s.editorPos = PositionFromJson(jed);
				if (jed.contains("color") && jed["color"].is_array() && jed["color"].size() == 3)
				{
					s.editorColor[0] = jed["color"][0].get<float>();
					s.editorColor[1] = jed["color"][1].get<float>();
					s.editorColor[2] = jed["color"][2].get<float>();
				}
			}

			states.push_back(s);
		}
	}

	// Ì”Ô‚ÍAƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚Ì’l‚ÆAÀÛ‚Ég‚í‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚éÅ‘åID‚Ì‘å‚«‚¢‚Ù‚¤‚É‡‚í‚¹‚é
	int maxId = 0;
	for (const auto& t : entry.transitions) maxId = (std::max)(maxId, t.id);
	for (const auto& s : states)
	{
		maxId = (std::max)(maxId, s.id);
		for (const auto& t : s.transitions)      maxId = (std::max)(maxId, t.id);
		for (const auto& c : s.child.clips)      maxId = (std::max)(maxId, c.id);
		for (const auto& l : s.child.entryLinks) maxId = (std::max)(maxId, l.id);
		for (const auto& l : s.child.clipLinks)  maxId = (std::max)(maxId, l.id);
	}
	nextId = (std::max)(j.value("nextId", 1), maxId + 1);

	return Validate(sourceName);
}

// •ÒW—p‚ÌŒŸõ
StateNode* StateMachineData::FindState(int stateId)
{
	return const_cast<StateNode*>(std::as_const(*this).FindState(stateId));
}
StateTransition* StateMachineData::FindTransition(int transitionId)
{
	return const_cast<StateTransition*>(std::as_const(*this).FindTransition(transitionId));
}

// ‘¼‚ÌState‚Æd•¡‚µ‚È‚¢–¼‘O‚ğì‚é
std::string StateMachineData::MakeUniqueStateName(const std::string& baseName) const
{
	std::string base = baseName.empty() ? "State" : baseName;
	if (!FindStateByName(base)) return base;

	// “¯‚¶–¼‘O‚ª‚ ‚ê‚ÎA––”ö‚É”Ô†‚ğ•t‚¯‚é("New State 1"A"New State 2"c)
	for (int i = 1; ; ++i)
	{
		std::string name = base + " " + std::to_string(i);
		if (!FindStateByName(name)) return name;
	}
}

// ‘JˆÚ‚ğ‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚éƒŠƒXƒg(EntryA‚Ü‚½‚Í‚Ç‚ê‚©‚ÌState)‚ğ’T‚·
std::vector<StateTransition>* StateMachineData::FindTransitionOwner(int transitionId)
{
	for (const auto& t : entry.transitions)
	{
		if (t.id == transitionId) return &entry.transitions;
	}
	for (auto& s : states)
	{
		for (const auto& t : s.transitions)
		{
			if (t.id == transitionId) return &s.transitions;
		}
	}
	return nullptr;
}

===== EntityFinder.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\ECS\EntityFinder.h 
#pragma once
#include "entt.hpp"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/CategoryComponent.h"

#include <vector>

// w’è‚µ‚½‘®«‚ğ‚ÂÅ‰‚ÌEntity‚ğ—ñ‹“
inline entt::entity FindFirstByCategory(entt::registry& registry, EntityCategory category)
{
	auto view = registry.view<ComponentCategory>();
	for (auto entity : view)
	{
		if (view.get<ComponentCategory>(entity).category == category)
		{
			return entity;
		}
	}
	return entt::null;
}

// w’è‚µ‚½‘®«‚ğ‚Â‘S•”‚ÌEntity‚ğ—ñ‹“
inline std::vector<entt::entity> FindAllByCategory(entt::registry& registry, EntityCategory category)
{
	std::vector<entt::entity> result;

	auto view = registry.view<ComponentCategory>();
	for (auto entity : view)
	{
		if (view.get<ComponentCategory>(entity).category == category)
		{
			result.push_back(entity);
		}
	}
	return result;
}

#pragma once
#include "entt.hpp"
#include "Engine/ECS/Reflection.h"

using namespace entt::literals;

// Œ^‚»‚Ì‚à‚Ì‚Ì“o˜^‚ğŠJn‚·‚é
#define REFLECT_BEGIN(Type, DisplayName)                                            \
private:                                                                            \
    using ReflectSelf = Type;                                                       \
    static inline bool _reflect_begin = []                                          \
    {                                                                               \
        entt::meta_factory<Type>{}                                                  \
            .type(#Type)                                                            \
            .custom<TypeInfo>(TypeInfo{ DisplayName });                             \
        return true;                                                                \
    }();                                                                            \
public:

// FieldInfo‚ğAˆÊ’uˆø”{ƒfƒtƒHƒ‹ƒgˆø”‚Å‘g‚İ—§‚Ä‚éƒwƒ‹ƒp[
inline FieldInfo MakeFieldInfo(
    std::string label,
    float minValue = 0.0f,
    float maxValue = 0.0f,
    float dragSpeed = 0.1f,
    std::string dragDropPayload = "",
    FieldFlags flags = FieldFlags::None)
{
    return FieldInfo{ label, minValue, maxValue, dragSpeed, dragDropPayload, flags };
}

// 1ƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh•ª‚ÌéŒ¾{“o˜^
#define REFLECT_FIELD(FieldType, FieldName, DefaultArgs, FieldInfoArgs) \
    FieldType FieldName = FieldType DefaultArgs;                        \
    static inline bool _reflect_##FieldName##_registered = []           \
    {                                                                   \
        entt::meta_factory<ReflectSelf>{}                               \
            .data<&ReflectSelf::FieldName>(#FieldName)                  \
            .custom<FieldInfo>(MakeFieldInfo FieldInfoArgs);            \
        return true;                                                    \
    }();

// enum‚Ì“o˜^
#define REFLECT_ENUM(EnumType)                          \
namespace ReflectEnumDetail_##EnumType                  \
{                                                       \
    using ReflectEnumSelf = EnumType;                   \
    inline bool _reflect_enum_type_registered = []      \
    {                                                   \
        entt::meta_factory<EnumType>{}.type(#EnumType); \
        return true;                                    \
    }();

#define REFLECT_ENUM_VALUE(ValueName, Value, Label)         \
    inline bool _reflect_##ValueName##_registered = []      \
    {                                                       \
        entt::meta_factory<ReflectEnumSelf>{}               \
            .data<Value>(#ValueName)                        \
            .custom<EnumValueInfo>(EnumValueInfo{ Label }); \
        return true;                                        \
    }();

#define REFLECT_ENUM_END() }

#define REFLECT_END() 
 
===== Reflection.h =====

#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <cstdint>
#include "entt.hpp"

// ’l‚ÌŒ^
enum class ValueType
{
	Int,
	Float,
	Bool,
	Float2,
	Float3,
	Float4,

	Unsupported, // ğŒ‚Å“Ç‚ß‚È‚¢Œ^(strong,enum‚È‚Ç)
};

// ƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚Ìˆµ‚¢‚ğ•\‚·ƒtƒ‰ƒO
enum class FieldFlags : uint32_t
{
	None = 0,
	VisibleOnly = 1 << 0, // ƒGƒfƒBƒ^‚Å•\¦‚Ì‚İ
	Transient = 1 << 1,   // •Û‘¶‚µ‚È‚¢
};
inline FieldFlags operator|(FieldFlags a, FieldFlags b)
{
	return static_cast<FieldFlags>(static_cast<uint32_t>(a) | static_cast<uint32_t>(b));
}
inline bool HasFlag(FieldFlags value, FieldFlags flag)
{
	return (static_cast<uint32_t>(value) & static_cast<uint32_t>(flag)) != 0;
}

// ƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh1‚Â‚É“Y•t‚·‚é•t‰Áî•ñ
struct FieldInfo
{
	std::string label;      // ƒGƒfƒBƒ^ã‚Ì•\¦–¼
	float minValue = 0.0f;  // ‰ºŒÀ’l
	float maxValue = 0.0f;  // ãŒÀ’l
	float dragSpeed = 0.1f; // ƒhƒ‰ƒbƒO‚Ì•Ï‰»—Ê
	std::string dragDropPayload;
	FieldFlags flags = FieldFlags::None;
};

// ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg1‚Â‚É“Y•t‚·‚é•t‰Áî•ñ
struct TypeInfo
{
	std::string category;
};

// enum‚ÌA1‚Â‚Ì’l‚É•R‚Ã‚­•\¦–¼
struct EnumValueInfo
{
	std::string label;
};


// ƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh1Œ•ª‚Ìî•ñ(ğŒ‚Ì•]‰¿‚âGetter‘I‘ğUI‚Åg‚¤)
struct FieldEntry
{
	entt::meta_type componentType; // ‘®‚·‚éƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ÌŒ^
	entt::id_type fieldId = 0;     // entt::metaã‚ÌƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒhID
	entt::meta_type nestedType;    // ƒlƒXƒg‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚éê‡‚ÌŒ^
	entt::id_type subFieldId = 0;  // ƒlƒXƒg‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚éê‡‚ÌƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒhID
	std::string componentName;     // ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ÌŒ^–¼
	std::string category;          // ƒJƒeƒSƒŠ
	std::string fieldLabel;        // ƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚Ì•\¦–¼
	std::string fieldName;         // ƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ÌƒR[ƒhã‚Ì–¼‘O

	ValueType type = ValueType::Float;
};


class Reflection
{
public:
	// “o˜^Ï‚İ‚ÌƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒhˆê——‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
	static const std::vector<FieldEntry>& GetAllFields();

	// ƒR[ƒhã‚Ì–¼‘O‚ÅƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ğ’T‚·
	static const FieldEntry* FindField(const std::string& componentName, const std::string& fieldName);
	// •\¦–¼‚ÅƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ğ’T‚·
	static const FieldEntry* FindFieldByLabel(const std::string& componentName, const std::string& label);

	// Float2/3/4Œ^‚ÌA¬•ª”‚ğ•Ô‚·(‚»‚êˆÈŠO‚Í0)
	static int GetComponentCount(ValueType type);
};

#include "EngineComponents.h"
#include "Engine/Resource/ComponentRegistry.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/CategoryComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MaterialComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/ControllerComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/AnimatorComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/FreeCameraComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/PhysicsComponents.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/IKComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/SequencerComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/AnimationSpeedComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StateMachineComponent.h"

void EngineComponents::Register()
{
	REGISTER_COMPONENT_TYPE(ComponentCategory, "Category");
	REGISTER_COMPONENT_TYPE(TransformComponent, "Transform");
	REGISTER_COMPONENT_TYPE(MeshComponent, "Mesh");
	REGISTER_COMPONENT_TYPE(MaterialComponent, "Material");
	REGISTER_COMPONENT_TYPE(AnimatorComponent, "Animator");
	REGISTER_COMPONENT_TYPE(FreeCameraComponent, "FreeCamera");
	REGISTER_COMPONENT_TYPE(ControllerComponent, "Controller");
	REGISTER_COMPONENT_TYPE(IKComponent, "IK");
	REGISTER_COMPONENT_TYPE(SequencerComponent, "Sequencer");
	REGISTER_COMPONENT_TYPE(AnimationSpeedComponent, "Animation Speed");
	REGISTER_COMPONENT_TYPE(StateMachineComponent, "State Machine");

	// •¨—Fí•Ê(PhysicsBodyType)‚ğ‘ã•\‚Æ‚µ‚Ä“o˜^‚µA
	// ŠO‚·‚Æ‚«‚Íí•Ê‚²‚Æ‚É•t—^‚³‚ê‚½ŒÂ•Ê‚ÌƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚à‚Ü‚Æ‚ß‚ÄŠO‚·
	ComponentRegistry::Register({
		"PhysicsBodyTypeComponent",
		"Physics",
		// Add: PhysicsBodyTypeComponent‚Ì‚İ•t—^(í•Ê–¢‘I‘ğó‘Ô)
		[](entt::registry& reg, entt::entity e)
		{
			if (!reg.any_of<PhysicsBodyTypeComponent>(e))
				reg.emplace<PhysicsBodyTypeComponent>(e);
		},
		// Remove: ‹¤’ÊƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Æ•R‚Ã‚¢‚½ŒÂ•Ê‚ÌƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ğ—¼•ûíœ
		[](entt::registry& reg, entt::entity e)
		{
			// À‘Ì(PxActor/PxController)‚Ì”jŠü—\–ñ‚ÍAon_destroy‚Ì’Ê’m‚Å©“®“I‚És‚í‚ê‚é
			if (reg.any_of<StaticBodyComponent>(e))
				reg.remove<StaticBodyComponent>(e);

			if (reg.any_of<CharacterControllerComponent>(e))
				reg.remove<CharacterControllerComponent>(e);

			if (reg.any_of<MovementComponent>(e))
				reg.remove<MovementComponent>(e);

			reg.remove<PhysicsBodyTypeComponent>(e);
		},
		// Has
		[](entt::registry& reg, entt::entity e) { return reg.any_of<PhysicsBodyTypeComponent>(e); },
		// GetMeta
		[](entt::registry& r, entt::entity e) -> entt::meta_any
		{
			auto* c = r.try_get<PhysicsBodyTypeComponent>(e);
			if (!c) return entt::meta_any{};
			return entt::forward_as_meta(*c);
		}
		});
} 
 
===== Reflection.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\ECS\Reflection.cpp 
#include "Reflection.h"
#include <DirectXMath.h>

// ’l‚ÌŒ^‚ğ”»•Ê‚·‚éƒwƒ‹ƒp[
namespace
{
	ValueType ResolveValueType(entt::meta_type type)
	{
		if (type == entt::resolve<bool>())              return ValueType::Bool;
		if (type == entt::resolve<int>())               return ValueType::Int;
		if (type == entt::resolve<float>())             return ValueType::Float;
		if (type == entt::resolve<DirectX::XMFLOAT2>()) return ValueType::Float2;
		if (type == entt::resolve<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>()) return ValueType::Float3;
		if (type == entt::resolve<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>()) return ValueType::Float4;
		
		// ‚»‚êˆÈŠO(string,enum‚È‚Ç)‚ÍAğŒ‚Å‚Í“Ç‚ß‚È‚¢
		return ValueType::Unsupported;
	}

	// leafŒ^‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
	bool IsLeafType(entt::meta_type type)
	{
		return type == entt::resolve<bool>()
			|| type == entt::resolve<int>()
			|| type == entt::resolve<float>()
			|| type == entt::resolve<std::string>()
			|| type == entt::resolve<DirectX::XMFLOAT2>()
			|| type == entt::resolve<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>()
			|| type == entt::resolve<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>();
	}
}

// “o˜^Ï‚İ‚ÌƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒhˆê——‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
const std::vector<FieldEntry>& Reflection::GetAllFields()
{
	static std::vector<FieldEntry> fieldEntries;

	if (fieldEntries.empty())
	{
		for (auto&& [id, metaType] : entt::resolve())
		{
			TypeInfo* typeInfo = metaType.custom();

			if (!typeInfo) continue;

			std::string category = typeInfo->category;
			std::string componentName = std::string(metaType.name());

			if (componentName.empty()) continue;

			for (auto&& [fieldId, metaData] : metaType.data())
			{
				FieldInfo* fieldInfo = metaData.custom();
				std::string label = fieldInfo ? fieldInfo->label : std::string(metaData.name());

				entt::meta_type fieldType = metaData.type();

				if (!IsLeafType(fieldType) && fieldType && !fieldType.is_enum() && fieldType.data().begin() != fieldType.data().end())
				{
					// ƒlƒXƒg‚µ‚½ƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒhF“à‘¤‚ÌŒ^‚ª‚ÂƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ğA1’iŠK‚¾‚¯Œ@‚è‰º‚°‚Ä’Ç‰Á‚·‚é
					for (auto&& [subFieldId, subMetaData] : fieldType.data())
					{
						FieldInfo* subFieldInfo = subMetaData.custom();
						std::string subLabel = subFieldInfo ? subFieldInfo->label : std::string(subMetaData.name());

						ValueType valueType = ResolveValueType(subMetaData.type());
						std::string combinedLabel = label + "." + subLabel;
						// ƒR[ƒhã‚Ì–¼‘O‚àA•\¦–¼‚Æ“¯‚¶‚­ue.qv‚Å‚Â‚È‚®
						std::string combinedName = std::string(metaData.name()) + "." + std::string(subMetaData.name());

						fieldEntries.push_back(FieldEntry{
							metaType, fieldId, fieldType, subFieldId, componentName, category, combinedLabel, combinedName, valueType
							});
					}
					continue;
				}

				ValueType valueType = ResolveValueType(fieldType);

				fieldEntries.push_back(FieldEntry{
					metaType, fieldId, entt::meta_type{}, 0, componentName, category, label, std::string(metaData.name()), valueType
					});
			}
		}
	}

	return fieldEntries;
}

// ƒR[ƒhã‚Ì–¼‘O‚ÅƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ğ’T‚·
const FieldEntry* Reflection::FindField(const std::string& componentName, const std::string& fieldName)
{
	for (const auto& f : GetAllFields())
	{
		if (f.componentName == componentName && f.fieldName == fieldName) return &f;
	}
	return nullptr;
}
// •\¦–¼‚ÅƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ğ’T‚·
const FieldEntry* Reflection::FindFieldByLabel(const std::string& componentName, const std::string& label)
{
	for (const auto& f : GetAllFields())
	{
		if (f.componentName == componentName && f.fieldLabel == label) return &f;
	}
	return nullptr;
}

// Float2/3/4Œ^‚ÌA¬•ª”‚ğ•Ô‚·(‚»‚êˆÈŠO‚Í0)
int Reflection::GetComponentCount(ValueType type)
{
	switch (type)
	{
	case ValueType::Float2: return 2;
	case ValueType::Float3: return 3;
	case ValueType::Float4: return 4;
	default: return 0;
	}
} 
 
===== AdaptiveTrigger.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\ECS\Components\AdaptiveTrigger.h 
#pragma once
#include <vector>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

namespace AdaptiveTrigger
{
    struct CurvePoint
    {
        // ‰Ÿ‚µ‚İ—Ê
        float position = 0.0f; // (0,0`1.0)
        // ’ïR‚Ì‹­‚³
        float value = 0.0f;    // (0.0`1.0)
    };

    struct TriggerEffect
    {
        // o—ÍƒŠƒXƒg
        std::vector<CurvePoint> forcePoints;
    };

    // Œ»İ’n(‰Ÿ‚µ‚İ—Ê)‚©‚ç‘OŒã‚É‘¶İ‚·‚éƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ğüŒ`•âŠ®‚µ‚ÄŒ»İ‚Ì’ïR’l‚ğ‹‚ß‚é
    inline float SampleCurve(const std::vector<CurvePoint>& points, float position)
    {
        // –³’ïR
        if (points.empty())
        {
            return 0.0f;
        }
        // “_‚ª1‚Â‚¾‚¯A‚Ü‚½‚Í position ‚ªÅ‰‚Ì“_‚æ‚èè‘O‚Ì‚ÍÅ‰‚Ì“_‚Ì’l‚ğ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Üg‚¤
        if (points.size() == 1 || position <= points.front().position)
        {
            return points.front().value;
        }
        // position ‚ªÅŒã‚Ì“_‚æ‚èŒã‚ë‚È‚çÅŒã‚Ì“_‚Ì’l‚ğ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Üg‚¤
        if (position >= points.back().position)
        {
            return points.back().value;
        }

        // ŠY“–‚·‚é2“_‚ğ’T‚µ‚Ä‚»‚ÌŠÔ‚ğüŒ`•âŠÔ‚·‚é
        for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < points.size(); ++i)
        {
            const CurvePoint& a = points[i];
            const CurvePoint& b = points[i + 1];
            if (position >= a.position && position <= b.position)
            {
                float t = (position - a.position) / (b.position - a.position);
                return a.value + (b.value - a.value) * t;
            }
        }
        return points.back().value;
    }
}

namespace AdaptiveTrigger
{
    struct Zone
    {
        // ‚±‚Ìƒ][ƒ“‚É‘®‚·‚éƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ÌƒŠƒXƒg
        std::vector<CurvePoint> points;
    };

    // ƒŒƒ“ƒW“à‚Ìƒ][ƒ“‚ğŒŸo‚·‚é
    inline std::vector<Zone> DetectZones(const std::vector<CurvePoint>& points)
    {
        // ŒŸo‚µ‚½ƒ][ƒ“‚ÌƒŠƒXƒg
        std::vector<Zone> zones;
		// Œ»İ‚Ìƒ][ƒ“
        Zone current;

        // ƒŒƒ“ƒW“à‚Ìƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ğæ“ª‚©‚ç‡‚É‘–¸‚·‚é
        for (const auto& p : points)
        {
			// ’ïR’l‚ª0‚æ‚è‘å‚«‚¯‚ê‚ÎŒ»İ‚Ìƒ][ƒ“‚É’Ç‰Á‚·‚é
            if (p.value > 0.0f)
            {
                current.points.push_back(p);
            }
            else
            {
				// ’ïR’l‚ª0‚È‚ç’ïR‚ª‚È‚¢êŠ‚Ö—ˆ‚½
                if (!current.points.empty())
                {
                    // ˆê‹æØ‚è‚Æ‚·‚é
                    zones.push_back(current);
					// Œ»İ‚Ìƒ][ƒ“‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg
                    current.points.clear();
                }
            }
        }

		// ‰Ÿ‚µ‚İØ‚Á‚½‚É’ïR’l0ˆÈã‚ª‚ ‚ê‚Î‚»‚Ì’n“_‚ğÅŒã‚Ìƒ][ƒ“‚Æ‚·‚é
        if (!current.points.empty())
        {
            zones.push_back(current);
        }

        return zones;
    }
}

namespace AdaptiveTrigger
{
    // “¯‚¶’l‚Æ‚İ‚È‚·Œë·‚Ì”ÍˆÍ
    constexpr float ValueEpsilon = 0.05f;

    // ƒ][ƒ“‚Ì”»’èŒ‹‰Ê
    enum class ResistanceType
    {
        Constant, // ˆê’è’ïR
        Changing, // •Ï‰»’ïR
        Complex,  // •¡G‚ÈƒJ[ƒu
    };

    // ƒ][ƒ“‚Ìí—Ş‚ğ”»’è‚·‚é
    inline ResistanceType ClassifyZone(const Zone& zone)
    {
		// ƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ª‚R“_ˆÈã‚È‚ç•¡G‚ÈƒJ[ƒu‚Æ‚İ‚È‚·
        if (zone.points.size() >= 3)
        {
            return ResistanceType::Complex;
        }

		// 2“_ˆÈ‰º‚È‚çAÅ‰‚ÆÅŒã‚Ì’l‚Ì·‚ğŒ©‚Äˆê’è’ïR‚©”»’f‚·‚é
        float diff = std::abs(zone.points.front().value - zone.points.back().value);
        if (diff < ValueEpsilon)
        {
            return ResistanceType::Constant;
        }
        return ResistanceType::Changing;
    }
}

namespace AdaptiveTrigger
{
    inline float ComputeForce(const TriggerEffect& effect, float currentPosition)
    {
        // Œ»İ’n‚Ì’ïR’l‚ğæ“¾
        float force = SampleCurve(effect.forcePoints, currentPosition);
        // ”ÍˆÍŠO•ÛŒ¯
        return std::clamp(force, 0.0f, 1.0f); 
    }
} 
 
===== AnimationSpeedComponent.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\ECS\Components\AnimationSpeedComponent.h 
#pragma once
#include <string>
#include "Engine/ECS/Reflect.h"

struct AnimationSpeedComponent
{
	REFLECT_BEGIN(AnimationSpeedComponent, "Animation Speed")

struct TriggerHysteresisState
{
    bool wasHeld = false;
};

// ã‚³ãƒ³ãƒˆãƒ­ãƒ¼ãƒ©ãƒ¼ã®ç”Ÿã®å…¥åŠ›å€¤ã‚’ä¿æŒã™ã‚‹ã‚³ãƒ³ãƒãƒ¼ãƒãƒ³ãƒˆ
struct ControllerComponent
{
    // æ¥ç¶šæƒ…å ±
    ControllerType type = ControllerType::None;
    bool           connected = false;

    // ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯
    float leftStickX = 0.0f;
    float leftStickY = 0.0f;
    float rightStickX = 0.0f;
    float rightStickY = 0.0f;

    // ãƒˆãƒªã‚¬ãƒ¼
    float leftTrigger = 0.0f;
    float rightTrigger = 0.0f;

    // ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³
    uint32_t buttons = 0;

    // ã‚¢ãƒ€ãƒ—ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒ–ãƒˆãƒªã‚¬ãƒ¼å‡ºåŠ›
    AdaptiveTrigger::TriggerEffect leftTriggerEffect = {};
    AdaptiveTrigger::TriggerEffect rightTriggerEffect = {};

	// ãƒˆãƒªã‚¬ãƒ¼ã®ãƒ’ã‚¹ãƒ†ãƒªã‚·ã‚¹çŠ¶æ…‹
    TriggerHysteresisState leftTriggerHysteresis;
    TriggerHysteresisState rightTriggerHysteresis;

    // ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³åˆ¤å®šãƒ˜ãƒ«ãƒ‘ãƒ¼
    bool IsPressed(uint32_t mask) const { return (buttons & mask) != 0; }
};

namespace ControllerButtons
{
    constexpr uint32_t Cross = 1 << 0;     // PS5:Ã—  Xbox:A
    constexpr uint32_t Circle = 1 << 1;    // PS5:â—‹  Xbox:B
    constexpr uint32_t Square = 1 << 2;    // PS5:â–¡  Xbox:X
    constexpr uint32_t Triangle = 1 << 3;  // PS5:â–³  Xbox:Y
    constexpr uint32_t L1 = 1 << 4;
    constexpr uint32_t R1 = 1 << 5;
    constexpr uint32_t L2 = 1 << 6;  // ãƒ‡ã‚¸ã‚¿ãƒ«åˆ¤å®šç”¨
    constexpr uint32_t R2 = 1 << 7;  // ãƒ‡ã‚¸ã‚¿ãƒ«åˆ¤å®šç”¨
    constexpr uint32_t L3 = 1 << 8;  // ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯æŠ¼ã—è¾¼ã¿
    constexpr uint32_t R3 = 1 << 9;  // ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯æŠ¼ã—è¾¼ã¿
    constexpr uint32_t Options = 1 << 14; // PS5:Options  Xbox:Menu
    constexpr uint32_t Share = 1 << 15;   // PS5:Create   Xbox:View

===== MaterialComponent.h =====

#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include "Engine/ECS/Reflect.h"

// g—p‚·‚éGBufferƒVƒF[ƒ_[‚Ìí—Ş
enum class ShadingModel : uint8_t
{
    Default = 0,
    Basic,      
};

REFLECT_ENUM(ShadingModel)

    REFLECT_ENUM_VALUE(Default, ShadingModel::Default, "Default (PBR)")
    REFLECT_ENUM_VALUE(Basic, ShadingModel::Basic, "Basic")

REFLECT_ENUM_END()

struct MaterialComponent
{
    REFLECT_BEGIN(MaterialComponent, "Material")
    
        REFLECT_FIELD(ShadingModel, shadingModel, (ShadingModel::Default), ("Shader"))
    
    REFLECT_END()

    // ƒVƒF[ƒ_[–¼‚É•ÏŠ·‚·‚éƒwƒ‹ƒp[
    const char* GetShaderName() const
    {
        switch (shadingModel)
        {
        case ShadingModel::Basic:   return "gbuffer_basic";
        default:                    return "gbuffer_default";
        }
    }
};

===== TransformComponent.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\ECS\Components\TransformComponent.h 
#pragma once
#include <DirectXMath.h>
#include "Engine/ECS/Reflect.h"

struct TransformComponent
{
	REFLECT_BEGIN(TransformComponent, "Transform")

		REFLECT_FIELD(DirectX::XMFLOAT3, position, (0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f), ("Position", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f))  // ˆÊ’u
		REFLECT_FIELD(DirectX::XMFLOAT3, rotation, (0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f), ("Rotation", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.01f)) // ‰ñ“]
		REFLECT_FIELD(DirectX::XMFLOAT3, scale,    (1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f), ("Scale",    0.0f, 0.0f, 0.01f)) // ƒXƒP[ƒ‹
		
	REFLECT_END()

		// ƒ[ƒ‹ƒhs—ñæ“¾
		DirectX::XMMATRIX GetWorldMatrix() const
	{
		DirectX::XMMATRIX S = DirectX::XMMatrixScaling(scale.x, scale.y, scale.z);
		DirectX::XMMATRIX R = DirectX::XMMatrixRotationRollPitchYaw(rotation.x, rotation.y, rotation.z);
		DirectX::XMMATRIX T = DirectX::XMMatrixTranslation(position.x, position.y, position.z);
		return S * R * T;
	}
};

===== RenderSystem.h =====

#pragma once
#include <d3d11.h>
#include <wrl.h>

#include "entt.hpp"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/SkinnedMeshComponent.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/Tags.h"

#include "Engine/Resource/Model.h"
#include "Engine/Rendering/GBuffer.h"

// ’è”ƒoƒbƒtƒ@\‘¢‘Ì
struct CbScene
{
	DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 view;             // ƒrƒ…[s—ñ
	DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 projection;       // ƒvƒƒWƒFƒNƒVƒ‡ƒ“s—ñ
	DirectX::XMFLOAT4   cameraPosition;   // ƒJƒƒ‰ˆÊ’uiw–¢g—pj
};

struct CbObject
{
	DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 world;       // ƒ[ƒ‹ƒhs—ñ
	float               alphaCutoff; // Mask‘fŞ‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚µ‚«‚¢’l
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3   padding;     // 16ƒoƒCƒgƒAƒ‰ƒCƒƒ“ƒg—p
};

#define MAX_BONES 256

struct CbSkinning
{
	DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 boneTransforms[MAX_BONES];
};


class RenderSystem
{
public:
	// ‰Šú‰»
	void Initialize(ID3D11Device* device);

	// •`‰æ
	void Render(
		entt::registry& registry,
		ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
		ID3D11RenderTargetView* rtv,
		ID3D11DepthStencilView* dsv,
		ID3D11Device* device,
		UINT width,
		UINT height
	);

	// ƒtƒ‰ƒXƒ^ƒ€‚ğScene‘¤‚©‚çƒZƒbƒg
	void SetFrustum(
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& view,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& projection)
	{
		cachedFrustum = CalcFrustum(view, projection);
	}

	// IBLƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒƒZƒbƒg
	void SetIBLTextures(
		ID3D11ShaderResourceView* diffuseIem,
		ID3D11ShaderResourceView* specularPmrem,
		ID3D11ShaderResourceView* lutGgx)
	{
		iblDiffuseIem = diffuseIem;
		iblSpecularPmrem = specularPmrem;
		iblLutGgx = lutGgx;
	}

	// ’è”ƒoƒbƒtƒ@ƒQƒbƒ^[
	ID3D11Buffer* GetCbScene() { return cbScene.Get(); }
	ID3D11Buffer** GetCbSceneAddress() { return cbScene.GetAddressOf(); }

private:

	// ƒtƒ‰ƒXƒ^ƒ€•½–Ê
	struct FrustumPlane
	{
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 normal;
		float distance;
	};

	// ƒtƒ‰ƒXƒ^ƒ€i6•½–Êj
	struct Frustum
	{
		FrustumPlane planes[6]; // Left, Right, Top, Bottom, Near, Far
	};

	// ƒtƒ‰ƒXƒ^ƒ€ŒvZiViewProjections—ñ‚©‚çj
	Frustum CalcFrustum(
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& view,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& projection
	);

	// ƒoƒEƒ“ƒfƒBƒ“ƒOƒ{ƒbƒNƒX‚ªƒtƒ‰ƒXƒ^ƒ€“à‚É‚ ‚é‚©”»’è
	bool IsInFrustum(
		const Frustum& frustum,
		const Model::BoundingBox& bbox,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& world
	);


	// GeometryƒpƒX
	void GeometryPass(
		entt::registry& registry,
		ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
		ID3D11DepthStencilView* dsv,
		const Frustum& frustum
	);

	// LightingƒpƒX
	void LightingPass(
		ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
		ID3D11RenderTargetView* rtv,
		ID3D11DepthStencilView* dsv
	);

	// State‚Ì‘JˆÚ‚ğŒˆ‚ß‚é (ƒQ[ƒ€ƒvƒŒƒC‚ÌŠeSystem‚æ‚è‘O‚ÉŒÄ‚Ô)

	// Ä¶‚·‚éƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ğŒˆ‚ß‚é (ƒQ[ƒ€ƒvƒŒƒC‚ÌŠeSystem‚æ‚è‘O‚ÉŒÄ‚Ô)

	template<typename UserComponent>

			[](const entt::registry& registry, entt::entity entity) { return registry.all_of<UserComponent>(entity); });

	using UsesStateFunc = bool(*)(const entt::registry& registry, entt::entity entity);

	void CheckRequiredStates(const StateMachineData& data, const std::string& path, const entt::registry& registry, entt::entity entity);


	// ƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é (“Ç‚İ‚ß‚È‚¢EŒŸØƒGƒ‰[‚Ìê‡‚Ínullptr)
	const StateMachineData* Load(const std::string& path);

	// ğŒ‚ª¬—§‚µ‚½Å‰‚Ì‘JˆÚ‚ğ•Ô‚·(—Dæ“x‡)

===== TwoBoneIKSolver.h =====

#pragma once
#include <DirectXMath.h>

// 2BoneIKƒ\ƒ‹ƒo[
// root-mid-end ‚Ì3ŠÖß‚ğAtarget(‘«ñ‚ğ’u‚«‚½‚¢ˆÊ’u)‚É“Í‚©‚¹‚é
namespace TwoBoneIKSolver
{
    struct Result
    {
        DirectX::XMFLOAT3 midPosition;      // ‰ğ‚¢‚½Œã‚Ìmid(•G)‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒhˆÊ’u
        DirectX::XMFLOAT4 rootRotation;     // ‰ğ‚¢‚½Œã‚Ìroot(˜)‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒh‰ñ“]
        DirectX::XMFLOAT4 midRotation;      // ‰ğ‚¢‚½Œã‚Ìmid(•G)‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒh‰ñ“]
        DirectX::XMFLOAT3 endPosition;      // ÀÛ‚É’u‚©‚ê‚½end(‘«ñ)‚ÌˆÊ’u(ƒNƒ‰ƒ“ƒvŒã‚Ìtarget)
    };

    // root, mid, end : Œ»İ(IK“K—p‘O)‚Ì3ŠÖß‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒhˆÊ’u
    // rootRotation, midRotation : Œ»İ‚Ìroot, mid‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒh‰ñ“](ƒNƒH[ƒ^ƒjƒIƒ“)
    // target : end(‘«ñ)‚ğ“Í‚©‚¹‚½‚¢ƒ[ƒ‹ƒhÀ•W
    // poleVector : mid(•G)‚ğŒü‚¯‚½‚¢•ûŒü‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒhÀ•W(ˆÊ’u‚Å‚Í‚È‚­•ûŒü‚ğ¦‚·1“_)
    Result Solve(
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& root,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& mid,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& end,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT4& rootRotation,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT4& midRotation,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& target,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& poleVector);

    // fromƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ğtoƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ÉŒü‚¯‚ÄAÅ’Z‰ñ“]‚ÌƒNƒH[ƒ^ƒjƒIƒ“‚ğ‹‚ß‚é
    DirectX::XMVECTOR RotationFromToRotation(DirectX::FXMVECTOR from, DirectX::FXMVECTOR to);
} 
 
===== AnimationSpeedSystem.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\ECS\Systems\AnimationSpeedSystem.cpp 
#include "AnimationSpeedSystem.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/SkinnedMeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/AnimatorComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/AnimationSpeedComponent.h"


void AnimationSpeedSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry)
{
	auto view = registry.view<SkinnedMeshComponent, AnimatorComponent, AnimationSpeedComponent>();

	for (auto entity : view)
	{
		auto& skinned = view.get<SkinnedMeshComponent>(entity);
		auto& animator = view.get<AnimatorComponent>(entity);
		auto& animSpeed = view.get<AnimationSpeedComponent>(entity);

		// ƒpƒX‚ª–¢İ’è‚È‚çA‚±‚ÌƒGƒ“ƒeƒBƒeƒB‚Í‘¬“xƒJ[ƒu‚ğg‚í‚È‚¢
		if (animSpeed.animationSpeedPath.empty())
		{
			animator.speedCurveMultiplier = 1.0f;
			continue;
		}

		if (!skinned.model)
		{
			continue;
		}

		AnimationSpeedData& data = dataCache.GetOrLoad(animSpeed.animationSpeedPath,
			[](AnimationSpeedData& data, const nlohmann::json& j) { data.FromJson(j); });

		const AnimationSpeedClipData* clip = nullptr;
		for (const auto& c : data.clips)
		{
			if (c.clipName == animator.currentAnimation) { clip = &c; break; }
		}

		// ƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚Ì’·‚³‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
		float clipLength = 0.0f;
		for (const auto& anim : skinned.model->GetAnimations())
		{
			if (anim.name == animator.currentAnimation)
			{
				clipLength = anim.secondsLength;
				break;
			}
		}

		if (clipLength <= 0.0001f)
		{
			animator.speedCurveMultiplier = 1.0f;
			continue;
		}

		float normalizedTime = animator.animationTime / clipLength;

		animator.speedCurveMultiplier = clip ? clip->EvaluateSpeed(normalizedTime) : 1.0f;
	}
} 
 
===== AnimationSystem.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\ECS\Systems\AnimationSystem.cpp 
#include "AnimationSystem.h"
#include "tracy/Tracy.hpp"
#include "Engine/Resource/SkeletonUtils.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"

// XV
void AnimationSystem::Update(
	entt::registry& registry,
	float elapsedTime)
{
	ZoneScoped;

	auto view = registry.view<AnimatorComponent, SkinnedMeshComponent>();
	view.each(
		[&](auto entity,
			AnimatorComponent& animator,
			SkinnedMeshComponent& skinned)
		{
			ZoneScopedN("AnimationSystem::Entity");

			// ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚ª–³Œø‚È‚ç‰½‚à‚µ‚È‚¢
			if (!skinned.model) return;
			if (!animator.isPlaying) return;

			// ‘JˆÚ‚µ‚½’¼Œã‚È‚çA‘O‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ÌÅIƒ|[ƒY‚ğu¬‚º‚éŒ³v‚Æ‚µ‚Ä•Û‘¶‚·‚é
			if (animator.blendSnapshotPending)
			{
				animator.blendSnapshotPending = false;

				if (skinned.localPositions.empty())
				{
					// ‚Ü‚¾ˆê“x‚àƒ|[ƒY‚ğì‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅA¬‚º‚éŒ³‚ª–³‚¢
					animator.isBlending = false;
				}
				else
				{
					animator.blendFromPositions = skinned.localPositions;
					animator.blendFromRotations = skinned.localRotations;
					animator.blendFromScales = skinned.localScales;
				}
			}

			const auto& animations = skinned.model->GetAnimations();
			if (animations.empty()) return;

			// Ä¶‚·‚éƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ğŒˆ‚ß‚é
			int animIndex = FindAnimationIndex(animations, animator.currentAnimation);
			if (animIndex < 0)
			{
				animIndex = 0;
				animator.currentAnimation = animations[0].name;
			}
			const auto& animation = animations[animIndex];

			// ŠÔ‚ği‚ß‚é
			const ClipTimeStep step = AdvanceClipTime(animator, animation, elapsedTime);

			// ƒ‹[ƒgƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ÌˆÚ“®—Ê
			const int rootMotionNodeIndex = animator.rootMotionNodeName.empty()
				? -1
				: FindNodeIndex(*skinned.model, animator.rootMotionNodeName);

			animator.rootMotionDelta = (rootMotionNodeIndex >= 0)
				? CalcRootMotionDelta(*skinned.model, animation, rootMotionNodeIndex,
					step.oldTime, step.newTime, step.rangeStart, step.rangeEnd)
				: DirectX::XMFLOAT3{ 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };

			// ƒ|[ƒY‚ğì‚é
			CalcBoneTransforms(
				*skinned.model, animation, animator.animationTime,
				skinned.localPositions, skinned.localRotations, skinned.localScales,
				rootMotionNodeIndex);

			// ƒ‹[ƒgƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“—R—ˆ‚Ì‘¬“x
			auto* transform = registry.try_get<TransformComponent>(entity);
			auto* movement = registry.try_get<MovementComponent>(entity);

			RootMotionVelocity velocity;
			if (animator.useRootMotion && rootMotionNodeIndex >= 0 && elapsedTime > 0.0f && transform && movement)
			{
				velocity.value = ToWorldVelocity(animator.rootMotionDelta, *transform, elapsedTime, animator.useRootMotionY);
				velocity.valid = true;
				velocity.writeY = animator.useRootMotionY;
			}

			// ƒNƒƒXƒtƒF[ƒh
			if (animator.isBlending)
			{
				UpdateCrossfade(animator, skinned, elapsedTime);
			}

			// ‘¬“x‚ğ‘‚­
			if (velocity.valid && movement)
			{
				movement->velocity.x = velocity.value.x;
				movement->velocity.z = velocity.value.z;
				if (velocity.writeY) movement->velocity.y = velocity.value.y;
			}

			// ƒXƒLƒjƒ“ƒO
			SkeletonUtils::CalcGlobalTransforms(
				skinned.model->GetNodes(), skinned.localPositions, skinned.localRotations, skinned.localScales,
				skinned.nodeGlobalTransforms);

			SkeletonUtils::CalcSkinningMatrices(
				*skinned.model, skinned.nodeGlobalTransforms, skinned.boneTransforms);
		});
}

// ŠÔ‚ği‚ß‚¸‚ÉAw’è‚µ‚½‚Ìƒ|[ƒY‚¾‚¯ŒvZ‚·‚é
void AnimationSystem::EvaluatePose(entt::registry& registry)
{
	ZoneScoped;

	auto view = registry.view<AnimatorComponent, SkinnedMeshComponent>();
	view.each(
		[&](auto entity, AnimatorComponent& animator, SkinnedMeshComponent& skinned)
		{
			if (!skinned.model) return;

			const auto& animations = skinned.model->GetAnimations();
			if (animations.empty()) return;

			int animIndex = FindAnimationIndex(animations, animator.currentAnimation);
			if (animIndex < 0) return;

			const auto& animation = animations[animIndex];

			CalcBoneTransforms(
				*skinned.model, animation, animator.animationTime,
				skinned.localPositions, skinned.localRotations, skinned.localScales);

			SkeletonUtils::CalcGlobalTransforms(
				skinned.model->GetNodes(), skinned.localPositions, skinned.localRotations, skinned.localScales,
				skinned.nodeGlobalTransforms);

			SkeletonUtils::CalcSkinningMatrices(
				*skinned.model, skinned.nodeGlobalTransforms, skinned.boneTransforms);
		});
}

// –¼‘O‚©‚çƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚Ì”Ô†‚ğ’T‚·
int AnimationSystem::FindAnimationIndex(const std::vector<Model::Animation>& animations, const std::string& name)
{
	for (int i = 0; i < (int)animations.size(); i++)
	{
		if (animations[i].name == name) return i;
	}
	return -1;
}

// ƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ÌŠÔ‚ğ1ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€•ªi‚ß‚é
AnimationSystem::ClipTimeStep AnimationSystem::AdvanceClipTime(AnimatorComponent& animator, const Model::Animation& animation, float elapsedTime)
{
	// ƒgƒŠƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ğ•b”‚É•ÏŠ·
	if (!animator.trimResolved)
	{
		if (animator.trimEnd >= 0.0f)
		{
			animator.trimStart *= animation.secondsLength;
			animator.trimEnd *= animation.secondsLength;
			animator.animationTime = animator.trimStart;
		}
		animator.trimResolved = true;
	}

	ClipTimeStep step;
	step.rangeEnd = (animator.trimEnd >= 0.0f) ? animator.trimEnd : animation.secondsLength;
	step.rangeStart = (animator.trimEnd >= 0.0f) ? animator.trimStart : 0.0f;
	step.oldTime = animator.animationTime;

	animator.animationTime += elapsedTime * animator.playSpeed * animator.speedCurveMultiplier;

	if (animator.loop)
	{
		// ƒ‹[ƒvFI‚í‚è‚ğ‰z‚¦‚½‚çA‰z‚¦‚½•ª‚¾‚¯“ª‚©‚ç
		if (animator.animationTime > step.rangeEnd)
		{
			animator.animationTime = step.rangeStart + (animator.animationTime - step.rangeEnd);
			animator.isEnd = false;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		// ƒ‹[ƒv‚µ‚È‚¢FI‚í‚è‚Å~‚ß‚é
		if (animator.animationTime >= step.rangeEnd)
		{
			animator.animationTime = step.rangeEnd;
			animator.isPlaying = false;
			animator.isEnd = true;
		}
	}

	step.newTime = animator.animationTime;
	return step;
}

// ƒ‹[ƒgƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚Ìƒ[ƒJƒ‹‚ÌˆÚ“®—Ê‚ğAƒ[ƒ‹ƒh‚Ì‘¬“x‚É•ÏŠ·‚·‚é
DirectX::XMFLOAT3 AnimationSystem::ToWorldVelocity(const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& localDelta, const TransformComponent& transform, float elapsedTime, bool useY)
{
	DirectX::XMMATRIX rotationMatrix = DirectX::XMMatrixRotationRollPitchYaw(
		transform.rotation.x, transform.rotation.y, transform.rotation.z);

	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 worldDelta;
	DirectX::XMStoreFloat3(&worldDelta,
		DirectX::XMVector3TransformNormal(DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&localDelta), rotationMatrix));

	return {
		worldDelta.x / elapsedTime,
		useY ? worldDelta.y / elapsedTime : 0.0f,
		worldDelta.z / elapsedTime };
}

// ƒNƒƒXƒtƒF[ƒhF•Û‘¶‚µ‚½ƒ|[ƒY‚©‚çA¡‚Ìƒ|[ƒY‚Ö¬‚º‚é
void AnimationSystem::UpdateCrossfade(AnimatorComponent& animator, SkinnedMeshComponent& skinned, float elapsedTime)
{
	// i‚İ‹ï‡‚ği‚ß‚é
	animator.blendRate += (animator.blendTime > 0.0f) ? (elapsedTime / animator.blendTime) : 1.0f;

	// i‚İ‹ï‡‚Íˆê’è‚Ì‘¬‚³‚Åi‚ßA¬‚º‚éŠ„‡‚É‚¾‚¯ŠÉ‹}‚ğ•t‚¯‚é
	const float linear = std::clamp(animator.blendRate, 0.0f, 1.0f);
	const float rate = linear * linear * (3.0f - 2.0f * linear);
	
	// ¬‚º‚éŒ³ ¨ ¡‚Ìƒ|[ƒY
	BlendPoses(
		animator.blendFromPositions, animator.blendFromRotations, animator.blendFromScales,
		skinned.localPositions, skinned.localRotations, skinned.localScales,
		rate,
		skinned.localPositions, skinned.localRotations, skinned.localScales);

	if (animator.blendRate >= 1.0f)
	{
		animator.isBlending = false;
	}
}

// ƒL[ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€•âŠÔiˆÊ’uj
DirectX::XMFLOAT3 AnimationSystem::InterpolatePosition(
	const std::vector<Model::VectorKeyframe>& keyframes,
	float time)
{
	if (keyframes.size() == 1)
	{
		return keyframes[0].value;
	}

	// ‘OŒã‚ÌƒL[ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ğ’T‚·
	for (int i = 0; i < (int)keyframes.size() - 1; i++)
	{
		if (time <= keyframes[i + 1].seconds)
		{
			float t = (time - keyframes[i].seconds) / (keyframes[i + 1].seconds - keyframes[i].seconds);

			DirectX::XMVECTOR a = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(
				&keyframes[i].value);
			DirectX::XMVECTOR b = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(
				&keyframes[i + 1].value);

			DirectX::XMFLOAT3 result;
			DirectX::XMStoreFloat3(
				&result,
				DirectX::XMVectorLerp(a, b, t));
			return result;
		}
	}
	// ––”ö‚ğ•Ô‚·
	return keyframes.back().value;
}

// ƒL[ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€•âŠÔi‰ñ“]j
DirectX::XMFLOAT4 AnimationSystem::InterpolateRotation(
	const std::vector<Model::QuaternionKeyframe>& keyframes,
	float time)
{
	if (keyframes.size() == 1)
	{
		return keyframes[0].value;
	}

	for (int i = 0; i < (int)keyframes.size() - 1; i++)
	{
		if (time <= keyframes[i + 1].seconds)
		{
			float t = (time - keyframes[i].seconds) / (keyframes[i + 1].seconds - keyframes[i].seconds);

			DirectX::XMVECTOR a = DirectX::XMLoadFloat4(
				&keyframes[i].value);
			DirectX::XMVECTOR b = DirectX::XMLoadFloat4(
				&keyframes[i + 1].value);

			DirectX::XMFLOAT4 result;
			DirectX::XMStoreFloat4(
				&result,
				DirectX::XMQuaternionSlerp(a, b, t));
			return result;
		}
	}
	return keyframes.back().value;
}

// ƒL[ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€•âŠÔiƒXƒP[ƒ‹j
DirectX::XMFLOAT3 AnimationSystem::InterpolateScale(
	const std::vector<Model::VectorKeyframe>& keyframes,
	float time)
{
	if (keyframes.size() == 1)
	{
		return keyframes[0].value;
	}

	for (int i = 0; i < (int)keyframes.size() - 1; i++)
	{
		if (time <= keyframes[i + 1].seconds)
		{
			float t = (time - keyframes[i].seconds) / (keyframes[i + 1].seconds - keyframes[i].seconds);

			DirectX::XMVECTOR a = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(
				&keyframes[i].value);
			DirectX::XMVECTOR b = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(
				&keyframes[i + 1].value);

			DirectX::XMFLOAT3 result;
			DirectX::XMStoreFloat3(
				&result,
				DirectX::XMVectorLerp(a, b, t));
			return result;
		}
	}
	return keyframes.back().value;
}

// ƒm[ƒh‚ÌƒOƒ[ƒoƒ‹s—ñ‚ğÄ‹AŒvZ‚µ‚Äƒ{[ƒ“s—ñ‚É‘‚«‚Ş
void AnimationSystem::CalcBoneTransforms(
	Model& model,
	const Model::Animation& animation,
	float time,
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& outPositions,
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>& outRotations,
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& outScales,
	int rootMotionNodeIndex) 
{
	auto& nodes = model.GetNodes();

	outPositions.resize(nodes.size());
	outRotations.resize(nodes.size());
	outScales.resize(nodes.size());

	for (int i = 0; i < (int)nodes.size(); i++)
	{
		outPositions[i] = nodes[i].position;
		outRotations[i] = nodes[i].rotation;
		outScales[i] = nodes[i].scale;

		// ƒL[ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ª‚ ‚ê‚Î•âŠÔ‚µ‚Äã‘‚«
		if (i < (int)animation.nodeAnims.size())
		{
			const auto& nodeAnim = animation.nodeAnims[i];

			if (!nodeAnim.positionKeyframes.empty())
			{
				outPositions[i] = InterpolatePosition(nodeAnim.positionKeyframes, time);
			}
			if (!nodeAnim.rotationKeyframes.empty())
			{
				outRotations[i] = InterpolateRotation(nodeAnim.rotationKeyframes, time);
			}
			if (!nodeAnim.scaleKeyframes.empty())
			{
				outScales[i] = InterpolateScale(nodeAnim.scaleKeyframes, time);
			}
		}

		if (i == rootMotionNodeIndex)
		{
			outPositions[i] = CalcNodeLocalPosition(animation, i, nodes[i], 0.0f);
		}
	}
}

// 2‚Â‚Ìƒ|[ƒY‚ğrate‚Å•âŠÔ‚·‚é
void AnimationSystem::BlendPoses(
	const std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& posA,
	const std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>& rotA,
	const std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& scaleA,
	const std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& posB,
	const std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>& rotB,
	const std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& scaleB,
	float rate,
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& outPositions,
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>& outRotations,
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& outScales)
{
	size_t count = posA.size();

	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3> tempPos(count);
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT4> tempRot(count);
	std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3> tempScale(count);

	for (size_t i = 0; i < count; i++)
	{
		DirectX::XMStoreFloat3(&tempPos[i],
			DirectX::XMVectorLerp(DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&posA[i]), DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&posB[i]), rate));

		DirectX::XMStoreFloat4(&tempRot[i],
			DirectX::XMQuaternionSlerp(DirectX::XMLoadFloat4(&rotA[i]), DirectX::XMLoadFloat4(&rotB[i]), rate));

		DirectX::XMStoreFloat3(&tempScale[i],
			DirectX::XMVectorLerp(DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&scaleA[i]), DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&scaleB[i]), rate));
	}

	outPositions = std::move(tempPos);
	outRotations = std::move(tempRot);
	outScales = std::move(tempScale);
}

// –¼‘O‚©‚çƒ‹[ƒgƒm[ƒhƒCƒ“ƒfƒbƒNƒX‚ğ’T‚·
int AnimationSystem::FindNodeIndex(const Model& model, const std::string& name)
{
	const auto& nodes = model.GetNodes();
	for (int i = 0; i < (int)nodes.size(); i++)
	{
		if (nodes[i].name == name) return i;
	}
	return -1;
}

// w’è‚Ìƒ[ƒJƒ‹ˆÊ’u‚ğ‹‚ß‚é
DirectX::XMFLOAT3 AnimationSystem::CalcNodeLocalPosition(
	const Model::Animation& animation,
	int nodeIndex,
	const Model::Node& node,
	float time)
{
	// ƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ª–³‚¯‚ê‚ÎƒoƒCƒ“ƒhƒ|[ƒY‚ÌˆÊ’u‚ğ•Ô‚·
	if (nodeIndex >= (int)animation.nodeAnims.size())
	{
		return node.position;
	}

	const auto& nodeAnim = animation.nodeAnims[nodeIndex];
	if (nodeAnim.positionKeyframes.empty())
	{
		return node.position;
	}

	return InterpolatePosition(nodeAnim.positionKeyframes, time);
}

// ƒ‹[ƒgƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ÌˆÚ“®—Ê‚ğŒvZ‚·‚é
DirectX::XMFLOAT3 AnimationSystem::CalcRootMotionDelta(
	const Model& model,
	const Model::Animation& animation,
	int rootMotionNodeIndex,
	float oldTime,
	float newTime,
	float rangeStart,
	float rangeEnd) 
{
	const auto& node = model.GetNodes()[rootMotionNodeIndex];

	// ƒ|[ƒYæ“¾—p‚Ìƒ‰ƒ€ƒ_
	auto getPos = [&](float t)
		{
			return CalcNodeLocalPosition(animation, rootMotionNodeIndex, node, t);
		};

	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 delta{ 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
	float actualRangeEnd = (rangeEnd >= 0.0f) ? rangeEnd : animation.secondsLength;

	if (oldTime > newTime)
	{
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 beginPos = getPos(rangeStart);
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 oldPos = getPos(oldTime);
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 endPos = getPos(actualRangeEnd);
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 newPos = getPos(newTime);

		delta.x = (endPos.x - oldPos.x) + (newPos.x - beginPos.x);
		delta.y = (endPos.y - oldPos.y) + (newPos.y - beginPos.y);
		delta.z = (endPos.z - oldPos.z) + (newPos.z - beginPos.z);
	}
	else
	{
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 oldPos = getPos(oldTime);
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 newPos = getPos(newTime);

		delta.x = newPos.x - oldPos.x;
		delta.y = newPos.y - oldPos.y;
		delta.z = newPos.z - oldPos.z;
	}

	return delta;
}
 
 
===== ConditionGraphEvaluator.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\ECS\Systems\ConditionGraphEvaluator.cpp 
#include "ConditionGraphEvaluator.h"
#include "Engine/Resource/ComponentRegistry.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Reflection.h"
#include <DirectXMath.h>
#include <cmath>

// ‘JˆÚğŒ‚ª¬—§‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚Îtrue‚ğ•Ô‚·
bool ConditionGraphEvaluator::Evaluate(const ConditionGraph& graph, entt::registry& registry, entt::entity entity)
{
	int fromId = -1;
	for (const auto& link : graph.links)
	{
		if (link.toId == graph.returnNode.id)
		{
			fromId = link.fromId;
			break;
		}
	}
	if (fromId == -1) return false; // Return‚É‰½‚à‚Â‚È‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢‚È‚ç•s¬—§

	// Œq‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚ªOperator‚È‚çA‚»‚Ì”äŠrŒ‹‰Ê‚ğ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü•Ô‚·
	for (const auto& o : graph.operatorNodes)
		if (o.id == fromId) return EvaluateOperator(graph, o, registry, entity);
	// Œq‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚ªLogic(AND/OR)‚È‚çA‚»‚ÌŒ‹‰Ê‚ğ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü•Ô‚·
	for (const auto& l : graph.logicNodes)
		if (l.id == fromId) return EvaluateLogic(graph, l, registry, entity);
	// Œq‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚ªGetter(BoolŒ^)‚È‚çA0.5‚æ‚èã‚Åtrue‚Æ‚İ‚È‚·
	for (const auto& g : graph.getterNodes)
		if (g.id == fromId) return GetGetterValue(g, registry, entity) > 0.5f;
	// Œq‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚ªVariable(BoolŒ^)‚È‚çA0.5‚æ‚èã‚Åtrue‚Æ‚İ‚È‚·
	for (const auto& v : graph.variableNodes)
		if (v.id == fromId) return GetVariableValue(v) > 0.5f;
	// Œq‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚ªNot‚È‚çA‚»‚ÌŒ‹‰Ê‚ğ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü•Ô‚·
	for (const auto& n : graph.notNodes)
		if (n.id == fromId) return EvaluateNot(graph, n, registry, entity);

	return false; // ‚Ç‚ê‚É‚àŠY“–‚µ‚È‚¢ê‡
}

// ƒQƒbƒ^[ƒm[ƒh‚Åæ“¾‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é’l‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
float ConditionGraphEvaluator::GetGetterValue(const ConditionGetterNode& node, entt::registry& registry, entt::entity entity)
{
	// ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg–¼‚ÆƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ÌƒR[ƒhã‚Ì–¼‘O‚©‚çAFieldEntry‚ğ’T‚·
	const FieldEntry* found = Reflection::FindField(node.componentName, node.fieldName);
	if (!found) return 0.0f;

	// ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ÌÀ‘Ì‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
	entt::meta_any instance;

	// ComponentRegistry‚É“o˜^‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚ÎA‚»‚¿‚ç‚©‚çæ“¾‚·‚é
	if (const ComponentInfo* info = ComponentRegistry::Get(node.componentName)) 
	{
		instance = info->getMeta(registry, entity);
	}
	else
	{
		// “o˜^‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢(‘¼‚ÌComponent‚Æ‚Ü‚Æ‚ß‚Ä•t—^‚³‚ê‚é“™)ê‡‚ÍA
		// Œ^î•ñ‚©‚çEnTT‚ÌƒXƒgƒŒ[ƒW‚ğ’¼Úˆø‚¢‚Äæ“¾‚·‚é
		if (auto* storage = registry.storage(found->componentType.info().hash()))
		{
			if (storage->contains(entity))
			{
				instance = found->componentType.from_void(storage->value(entity));
			}
		}
	}

	if (!instance) return 0.0f;

	// Œ^î•ñ‚©‚çƒtƒB[ƒh‚Ì’l‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
	entt::meta_data data = found->componentType.data(found->fieldId);
	entt::meta_any value = data.get(instance);

	// ƒlƒXƒg‚µ‚½ƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚Ìê‡A‚à‚¤1’iŠK‚¾‚¯Œ@‚è‰º‚°‚é
	if (found->subFieldId != 0 && found->nestedType)
	{
		entt::meta_data subData = found->nestedType.data(found->subFieldId);
		value = subData.get(value);
	}

	// ŠeŒ^‚ğfloat‚É•ÏŠ·‚·‚é
	if (float* f = value.try_cast<float>()) return *f;
	if (int* i = value.try_cast<int>())     return static_cast<float>(*i);
	if (bool* b = value.try_cast<bool>())   return *b ? 1.0f : 0.0f;

	if (DirectX::XMFLOAT2* v = value.try_cast<DirectX::XMFLOAT2>())
	{
		switch (node.selectedComponent)
		{
		case FloatComponent::X: return v->x;
		case FloatComponent::Y: return v->y;
		default: return 0.0f;
		}
	}
	if (DirectX::XMFLOAT3* v = value.try_cast<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>())
	{
		switch (node.selectedComponent)
		{
		case FloatComponent::X: return v->x;
		case FloatComponent::Y: return v->y;
		case FloatComponent::Z: return v->z;
		default: return 0.0f;
		}
	}
	if (DirectX::XMFLOAT4* v = value.try_cast<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>())
	{
		switch (node.selectedComponent)
		{
		case FloatComponent::X: return v->x;
		case FloatComponent::Y: return v->y;
		case FloatComponent::Z: return v->z;
		case FloatComponent::W: return v->w;
		default: return 0.0f;
		}
	}

	return 0.0f;
}
// •Ï”ƒm[ƒh‚Å•Û‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é’l‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
float ConditionGraphEvaluator::GetVariableValue(const ConditionVariableNode& node)
{
	switch (node.type)
	{
	case ValueType::Int:   return static_cast<float>(node.intValue);
	case ValueType::Float: return node.floatValue;
	case ValueType::Bool:  return node.boolValue ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
	default:               return 0.0f;
	}
}

// ”äŠrƒm[ƒh‚ğ‰‰Z‚µŒ‹‰Ê‚ğ•Ô‚·
bool ConditionGraphEvaluator::EvaluateOperator(const ConditionGraph& graph, const ConditionOperatorNode& op, entt::registry& registry, entt::entity entity)
{
	// A/B‚»‚ê‚¼‚ê‚ÉŒq‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚éƒm[ƒh‚ÌID‚ğ’T‚·
	int fromIdA = -1;
	int fromIdB = -1;
	for (const auto& link : graph.links)
	{
		if (link.toId != op.id) continue;
		if (link.toPinB) fromIdB = link.fromId;
		else             fromIdA = link.fromId;
	}

	if (fromIdA == -1 || fromIdB == -1) return false; // •Ğ•û‚Å‚à–¢Ú‘±‚È‚çA•s¬—§ˆµ‚¢

	float a = ResolveInputValue(graph, fromIdA, registry, entity);
	float b = ResolveInputValue(graph, fromIdB, registry, entity);

	const float epsilon = 0.0001f; // •‚“®¬”“_‚ÌŒë·‚ğ‹zû‚·‚é‚½‚ß‚Ì‹–—e”ÍˆÍ

	switch (op.op)
	{
	case CompareOp::Greater:      return a > b;
	case CompareOp::Less:         return a < b;
	case CompareOp::Equal:        return std::abs(a - b) < epsilon;
	case CompareOp::NotEqual:     return std::abs(a - b) >= epsilon;
	case CompareOp::GreaterEqual: return a >= b;
	case CompareOp::LessEqual:    return a <= b;
	}
	return false;
}

bool ConditionGraphEvaluator::EvaluateLogic(const ConditionGraph& graph, const ConditionLogicNode& logic, entt::registry& registry, entt::entity entity)
{
	// A/B‚»‚ê‚¼‚ê‚ÉŒq‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚éƒm[ƒhID‚ğ’T‚·
	int fromIdA = -1;
	int fromIdB = -1;
	for (const auto& link : graph.links)
	{
		if (link.toId != logic.id) continue;
		if (link.toPinB) fromIdB = link.fromId;
		else             fromIdA = link.fromId;
	}

	if (fromIdA == -1 || fromIdB == -1) return false; // •Ğ•û‚Å‚à–¢Ú‘±‚È‚çA•s¬—§

	bool a = ResolveInputValue(graph, fromIdA, registry, entity) > 0.5f;
	bool b = ResolveInputValue(graph, fromIdB, registry, entity) > 0.5f;

	switch (logic.op)
	{
	case LogicOp::And: return a && b;
	case LogicOp::Or:  return a || b;
	}
	return false;
}

// ”Û’èƒm[ƒh‚ğ‰‰Z‚µŒ‹‰Ê‚ğ•Ô‚·
bool ConditionGraphEvaluator::EvaluateNot(const ConditionGraph& graph, const ConditionNotNode& notNode, entt::registry& registry, entt::entity entity)
{
	// “ü—Íƒsƒ“(1–{‚Ì‚İ)‚ÉŒq‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚éƒm[ƒhID‚ğ’T‚·
	int fromId = -1;
	for (const auto& link : graph.links)
	{
		if (link.toId == notNode.id)
		{
			fromId = link.fromId;
			break;
		}
	}

	if (fromId == -1) return false; // –¢Ú‘±‚È‚ç•s¬—§ˆµ‚¢

	bool input = ResolveInputValue(graph, fromId, registry, entity) > 0.5f;
	return !input;
}

// ƒm[ƒhID‚©‚ç“ü—Í’l‚ğfloat‚Æ‚µ‚Ä‰ğŒˆ‚·‚é
float ConditionGraphEvaluator::ResolveInputValue(const ConditionGraph& graph, int nodeId, entt::registry& registry, entt::entity entity)
{
	for (const auto& g : graph.getterNodes)
		if (g.id == nodeId) return GetGetterValue(g, registry, entity);

	for (const auto& v : graph.variableNodes)
		if (v.id == nodeId) return GetVariableValue(v);

	for (const auto& o : graph.operatorNodes)
		if (o.id == nodeId) return EvaluateOperator(graph, o, registry, entity) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;

	for (const auto& l : graph.logicNodes)
		if (l.id == nodeId) return EvaluateLogic(graph, l, registry, entity) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;

	for (const auto& n : graph.notNodes)
		if (n.id == nodeId) return EvaluateNot(graph, n, registry, entity) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;


	return 0.0f; // ‚Ç‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚É‚àŠY“–‚µ‚È‚¢ê‡
}
 
 
===== ControllerSystem.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Engine\ECS\Systems\ControllerSystem.cpp 
ï»¿#include "ControllerSystem.h"

// ã‚³ãƒ³ã‚¹ãƒˆãƒ©ã‚¯ã‚¿
ControllerSystem::ControllerSystem()
{
    // å°‚ç”¨ã‚¹ãƒ¬ãƒƒãƒ‰ã‚’èµ·å‹•ã™ã‚‹
    ds5ThreadRunning = true;
    ds5Thread = std::thread(&ControllerSystem::DS5ThreadFunc, this);
}

// ãƒ‡ã‚¹ãƒˆãƒ©ã‚¯ã‚¿
ControllerSystem::~ControllerSystem()
{
    // å°‚ç”¨ã‚¹ãƒ¬ãƒƒãƒ‰ã«åœæ­¢ã‚’ä¼ãˆã¦çµ‚ã‚ã‚‹ã¾ã§å¾…ã¤
    ds5ThreadRunning = false;

    if (ds5Thread.joinable())
    {
        ds5Thread.join();
    }

    if (ds5Connected)
    {
        DS5W::freeDeviceContext(&ds5Context);
        ds5Connected = false;
    }
}

// æ›´æ–°
void ControllerSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime)
{

    (void)elapsedTime;

    // Xbox æ¥ç¶šç¢ºèª
    {
        ZoneScopedN("ControllerSystem::XInputGetState");
        xboxConnected = XInputGetState(0, &xboxState) == ERROR_SUCCESS;
    }

    // PS5 å´ã¯å°‚ç”¨ã‚¹ãƒ¬ãƒƒãƒ‰ãŒèª­ã¿å–ã‚Šæ¸ˆã¿ã®æœ€æ–°çŠ¶æ…‹ã‚’ã€ãƒ­ãƒƒã‚¯ã—ã¦å–ã£ã¦ãã‚‹ã ã‘
    bool ds5ConnectedNow = false;
    DS5W::DS5InputState ds5Snapshot = {};
    {
        ZoneScopedN("ControllerSystem::DS5_CopyFromShared");
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(ds5Mutex);
        ds5ConnectedNow = ds5ConnectedShared;
        ds5Snapshot = ds5InputStateShared;
    }

    // ControllerComponent ã‚’æŒã¤ Entity ã‚’æ›´æ–°
    auto view = registry.view<ControllerComponent>();
    view.each([&](ControllerComponent& ctrl)
        {
            // ãƒªã‚»ãƒƒãƒˆ
            ctrl.leftStickX = 0.0f;
            ctrl.leftStickY = 0.0f;
            ctrl.rightStickX = 0.0f;
            ctrl.rightStickY = 0.0f;
            ctrl.leftTrigger = 0.0f;
            ctrl.rightTrigger = 0.0f;
            ctrl.buttons = 0;
            ctrl.connected = false;
            ctrl.type = ControllerType::None;

            // PS5 å„ªå…ˆã€ãªã‘ã‚Œã° Xbox
            if (ds5ConnectedNow)
            {
                ctrl.type = ControllerType::PS5;
                ctrl.connected = true;
                UpdatePS5Input(ds5Snapshot, ctrl);

                // æ¬¡ã«å°‚ç”¨ã‚¹ãƒ¬ãƒƒãƒ‰ãŒé€ä¿¡ã™ã‚‹ãƒˆãƒªã‚¬ãƒ¼åŠ¹æœã‚’ãƒªã‚¯ã‚¨ã‚¹ãƒˆã—ã¦ãŠã
                std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(ds5Mutex);
                ds5RequestedLeftEffect = ctrl.leftTriggerEffect;
                ds5RequestedRightEffect = ctrl.rightTriggerEffect;
            }
            else if (xboxConnected)
            {
                ctrl.type = ControllerType::Xbox;
                ctrl.connected = true;
                UpdateXboxInput(ctrl);
            }
        });
}

// Xbox å…¥åŠ›
void ControllerSystem::UpdateXboxInput(ControllerComponent& ctrl)
{
    const auto& gp = xboxState.Gamepad;

    // ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯ï¼ˆ-32768ï½32767 â†’ -1.0ï½1.0ï¼‰
    ctrl.leftStickX = gp.sThumbLX / 32767.0f;
    ctrl.leftStickY = gp.sThumbLY / 32767.0f;
    ctrl.rightStickX = gp.sThumbRX / 32767.0f;
    ctrl.rightStickY = gp.sThumbRY / 32767.0f;

    // ãƒˆãƒªã‚¬ãƒ¼ï¼ˆ0ï½255 â†’ 0.0ï½1.0ï¼‰
    ctrl.leftTrigger = gp.bLeftTrigger / 255.0f;
    ctrl.rightTrigger = gp.bRightTrigger / 255.0f;

    // ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³
    auto btn = gp.wButtons;
    if (btn & XINPUT_GAMEPAD_A)              ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::Cross;
    if (btn & XINPUT_GAMEPAD_B)              ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::Circle;
    if (btn & XINPUT_GAMEPAD_X)              ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::Square;
    if (btn & XINPUT_GAMEPAD_Y)              ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::Triangle;
    if (btn & XINPUT_GAMEPAD_LEFT_SHOULDER)  ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::L1;
    if (btn & XINPUT_GAMEPAD_RIGHT_SHOULDER) ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::R1;
    if (btn & XINPUT_GAMEPAD_LEFT_THUMB)     ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::L3;
    if (btn & XINPUT_GAMEPAD_RIGHT_THUMB)    ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::R3;
    if (btn & XINPUT_GAMEPAD_START)          ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::Options;
    if (btn & XINPUT_GAMEPAD_BACK)           ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::Share;

    // L2/R2 ãƒ‡ã‚¸ã‚¿ãƒ«åˆ¤å®šï¼ˆãƒ’ã‚¹ãƒ†ãƒªã‚·ã‚¹è¾¼ã¿ï¼‰
    if (UpdateTriggerDigital(ctrl.leftTrigger, settings.leftTriggerPressThreshold, settings.leftTriggerReleaseThreshold, ctrl.leftTriggerHysteresis))
        ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::L2;
    if (UpdateTriggerDigital(ctrl.rightTrigger, settings.rightTriggerPressThreshold, settings.rightTriggerReleaseThreshold, ctrl.rightTriggerHysteresis))
        ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::R2;
}

// PS5 å…¥åŠ›ã‚’ControllerComponentã«æ›¸ã(å…±æœ‰ãƒãƒƒãƒ•ã‚¡ã‹ã‚‰æ›¸ã)
void ControllerSystem::UpdatePS5Input(const DS5W::DS5InputState& snapshot, ControllerComponent& ctrl)
{
    // ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯
    ctrl.leftStickX = snapshot.leftStick.x / 127.0f;
    ctrl.leftStickY = snapshot.leftStick.y / 127.0f;
    ctrl.rightStickX = snapshot.rightStick.x / 127.0f;
    ctrl.rightStickY = snapshot.rightStick.y / 127.0f;

    // ãƒˆãƒªã‚¬ãƒ¼
    ctrl.leftTrigger = snapshot.leftTrigger / 255.0f;
    ctrl.rightTrigger = snapshot.rightTrigger / 255.0f;

    // ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³
    auto btn = snapshot.buttonsAndDpad;
    if (btn & DS5W_ISTATE_BTX_CROSS)    ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::Cross;
    if (btn & DS5W_ISTATE_BTX_CIRCLE)   ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::Circle;
    if (btn & DS5W_ISTATE_BTX_SQUARE)   ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::Square;
    if (btn & DS5W_ISTATE_BTX_TRIANGLE) ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::Triangle;

    // ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³
    auto btnA = snapshot.buttonsA;
    if (btnA & DS5W_ISTATE_BTN_A_LEFT_BUMPER)  ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::L1;
    if (btnA & DS5W_ISTATE_BTN_A_RIGHT_BUMPER) ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::R1;
    if (btnA & DS5W_ISTATE_BTN_A_LEFT_STICK)   ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::L3;
    if (btnA & DS5W_ISTATE_BTN_A_RIGHT_STICK)  ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::R3;
    if (btnA & DS5W_ISTATE_BTN_A_SELECT)       ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::Share;
    if (btnA & DS5W_ISTATE_BTN_A_MENU)         ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::Options;

    // L2/R2 ãƒ‡ã‚¸ã‚¿ãƒ«åˆ¤å®šï¼ˆãƒ’ã‚¹ãƒ†ãƒªã‚·ã‚¹è¾¼ã¿ï¼‰
    if (UpdateTriggerDigital(ctrl.leftTrigger, settings.leftTriggerPressThreshold, settings.leftTriggerReleaseThreshold, ctrl.leftTriggerHysteresis))
        ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::L2;
    if (UpdateTriggerDigital(ctrl.rightTrigger, settings.rightTriggerPressThreshold, settings.rightTriggerReleaseThreshold, ctrl.rightTriggerHysteresis))
        ctrl.buttons |= ControllerButtons::R2;
}

// ãƒˆãƒªã‚¬ãƒ¼ã®ãƒ’ã‚¹ãƒ†ãƒªã‚·ã‚¹åˆ¤å®š
bool ControllerSystem::UpdateTriggerDigital(float triggerValue, float pressThreshold, float releaseThreshold, TriggerHysteresisState& state)
{
    bool nowHeld = state.wasHeld
        ? (triggerValue > releaseThreshold)
        : (triggerValue >= pressThreshold);

    state.wasHeld = nowHeld;

    return nowHeld;
}
