===== GameCameraSystem.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\GameCameraSystem.cpp 
ï»¿#include "GameCameraSystem.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/EntityFinder.h"

#include <algorithm>

void GameCameraSystem::Initialize(ID3D11Device* device)
{
	shapeRenderer = ShapeRenderer(device);
}

void GameCameraSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime)
{
	auto view = registry.view<GameCameraComponent, TransformComponent>();
	view.each([&](auto entity, GameCameraComponent& cam, TransformComponent& transform)
		{
			// è¿½å¾“å¯¾è±¡ãŒæœªè¨­å®šãªã‚‰ Playerå±æ€§ ã‚’æŒã¤Entityã‚’è¦‹ã¤ã‘ã‚‹ã¾ã§æ¢ã™
			if (cam.followTarget == entt::null)
			{
				cam.followTarget = FindFirstByCategory(registry, EntityCategory::Player);
			}

			// ãã‚Œã§ã‚‚è¦‹ã¤ã‹ã‚‰ãªã‘ã‚Œã°ã€ä½•ã‚‚è¡Œã‚ãªã„
			if (cam.followTarget == entt::null) return;
			if (!registry.valid(cam.followTarget)) return;

			// è¿½å¾“å¯¾è±¡ã®TransformComponentã‚’å–å¾—ã€ãªã‘ã‚Œã°ä½•ã‚‚è¡Œã‚ãªã„
			auto* followTransform = registry.try_get<TransformComponent>(cam.followTarget);
			if (!followTransform) return;

			// åˆå›ã ã‘ currentEye / currentTarget ã‚’å³åº§ã«è¨­å®š
			if (cam.base.currentEye.x == 0.0f && 
				cam.base.currentEye.y == 0.0f &&
				cam.base.currentEye.z == 0.0f)
			{
				cam.base.currentTarget = followTransform->position;
				cam.base.currentEye =
				{
					followTransform->position.x,
					followTransform->position.y + cam.followCameraHeight,
					followTransform->position.z - cam.followCameraDistance
				};
			}

			// InputComponent ã‹ã‚‰ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©æ“ä½œå…¥åŠ›ã‚’å–å¾—
			auto* input = registry.try_get<GameInputComponent>(cam.followTarget);
			if (input)
			{
				if (cam.mode == Mode::Follow)
				{
					// Follow ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©
					const float rotSpeed = 2.0f;
					cam.yaw += input->cameraStick.x * rotSpeed * elapsedTime;   // ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯å·¦å³ã§ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©å›è»¢
					cam.pitch += input->cameraStick.y * rotSpeed * elapsedTime; // ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯ä¸Šä¸‹ã§ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©ä¸Šä¸‹è§’åº¦
					cam.pitch = std::clamp(cam.pitch, -0.8f, 1.0f); // ä¸Šä¸‹è§’åº¦ã®åˆ¶é™
				}
				else // LockOn ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©
				{
					// ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯å…¥åŠ›æœ‰
					if (input->cameraStick.hasInput)
					{
						// ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯å·¦å³ã§ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©å›è»¢
						cam.lockOnOrbitAngle -= input->cameraStick.x * cam.lockOnOrbitRotateSpeed * elapsedTime;
						cam.lockOnOrbitPitch += input->cameraStick.y * cam.lockOnOrbitPitchRotateSpeed * elapsedTime;
					}
					else // ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯å…¥åŠ›ç„¡
					{
						// æˆ»ã‚‹å¼·ã•ã®å€ç‡ã€Enemyã«è¿‘ã„ã»ã©å¼±ãã™ã‚‹
						float returnScale = 1.0f;
						if (registry.valid(cam.lookAtTarget))
						{
							DirectX::XMFLOAT3 enemyPos = registry.get<TransformComponent>(cam.lookAtTarget).position;
							float dx = followTransform->position.x - enemyPos.x;
							float dz = followTransform->position.z - enemyPos.z;
							float distToEnemy = sqrtf(dx * dx + dz * dz);
							returnScale = std::clamp(distToEnemy / cam.lockOnFarCenterMaxDistance, 0.0f, 1.0f);
						}

						// lerpã§ãµã‚ã£ã¨æˆ»ã™
						float returnT = std::clamp(cam.lockOnOrbitReturnSpeed * returnScale * elapsedTime, 0.0f, 1.0f);
						cam.lockOnOrbitAngle -= cam.lockOnOrbitAngle * returnT;
						cam.lockOnOrbitPitch -= cam.lockOnOrbitPitch * returnT;
					}

					// -180åº¦ï½180åº¦ã®ç¯„å›²ã«æŠ‘ãˆã¦æœ€çŸ­è·é›¢ã§æˆ»ã™ã‚ˆã†ã«ã™ã‚‹
					cam.lockOnOrbitAngle = fmodf(cam.lockOnOrbitAngle + DirectX::XM_PI, DirectX::XM_2PI);
					if (cam.lockOnOrbitAngle < 0.0f) cam.lockOnOrbitAngle += DirectX::XM_2PI;
					cam.lockOnOrbitAngle -= DirectX::XM_PI;

					cam.lockOnOrbitPitch = std::clamp(cam.lockOnOrbitPitch, -0.8f, 0.8f); // ä¸Šä¸‹è§’åº¦ã®åˆ¶é™
				
					// ãã®å­—ãŒæ›²ãŒã‚‹å‘ãã®åˆ¤å®šï¼ˆãƒ’ã‚¹ãƒ†ãƒªã‚·ã‚¹ï¼‰
					// é–¾å€¤ã‚’è¶…ãˆã¦å€’ã—ãŸæ™‚ã ã‘åˆ‡ã‚Šæ›¿ã‚ã‚Šã€ãã‚Œä»¥å¤–ã¯ä»Šã®å‘ãã‚’ç¶­æŒã™ã‚‹
					if (cam.lockOnOrbitAngle > cam.lockOnSideSwitchThreshold)
					{
						cam.lockOnSide = 1.0f;
					}
					else if (cam.lockOnOrbitAngle < -cam.lockOnSideSwitchThreshold)
					{
						cam.lockOnSide = -1.0f;
					}

					// lockOnSideã¸ã€lerpã§ãªã‚ã‚‰ã‹ã«è¿½å¾“ã•ã›ã‚‹
					float sideT = std::clamp(cam.lockOnSideSmoothSpeed * elapsedTime, 0.0f, 1.0f);
					cam.lockOnSideSmooth += (cam.lockOnSide - cam.lockOnSideSmooth) * sideT;
				}

				// L2ã‚’æŠ¼ã—ãŸç¬é–“ã€Follow<->LookAtã‚’ãƒˆã‚°ãƒ«åˆ‡ã‚Šæ›¿ãˆ
				if (input->lockOnTrigger.pressed)
				{
					if (cam.mode == Mode::Follow)
					{
						// Playerã«ä¸€ç•ªè¿‘ã„Enemyã‚’é¸ã¶
						auto enemies = FindAllByCategory(registry, EntityCategory::Enemy);

						entt::entity best = entt::null;
						float bestDistSq = FLT_MAX;

						for (auto e : enemies)
						{

							if (!registry.valid(e)) continue;
							auto* enemyTransform = registry.try_get<TransformComponent>(e);
							if (!enemyTransform) continue;

							float dx = enemyTransform->position.x - followTransform->position.x;
							float dy = enemyTransform->position.y - followTransform->position.y;
							float dz = enemyTransform->position.z - followTransform->position.z;
							float distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz;
							if (distSq < 0.0001f) continue;

							if (distSq < bestDistSq)
							{
								bestDistSq = distSq;
								best = e;
							}
						}

						if (best != entt::null)
						{
							cam.lookAtTarget = best;
							cam.mode = Mode::LookAt;
						}
					}
					else if (cam.mode == Mode::LookAt)
					{
						// LockOnã‹ã‚‰Followã«æˆ»ã™éš›ã«é€†ç®—ã—ã¦åŒæœŸã•ã›ã‚‹
						float dx = cam.base.currentEye.x - followTransform->position.x;
						float dy = cam.base.currentEye.y - followTransform->position.y - cam.followCameraHeight;
						float dz = cam.base.currentEye.z - followTransform->position.z;
						float horizDist = sqrtf(dx * dx + dz * dz);

						if (horizDist > 0.0001f)
						{
							cam.yaw = atan2f(-dx, -dz);
							cam.pitch = std::clamp(atan2f(dy, horizDist), -0.8f, 1.0f);
						}

						cam.mode = Mode::Follow;
					}
				}
			}

			// LookAtä¸­ã«ã€æ³¨è¦–å¯¾è±¡ãŒç ´æ£„ã•ã‚Œã¦ã„ãŸã‚‰ã€Followã¸è‡ªå‹•çš„ã«æˆ»ã™
			if (cam.mode == Mode::LookAt && !registry.valid(cam.lookAtTarget))
			{
				cam.mode = Mode::Follow;
			}


			// æ³¨è¦–ç‚¹ã‚’ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©ã®ãƒ¢ãƒ¼ãƒ‰ã«å¿œã˜ã¦å¤‰æ›´
			switch (cam.mode)
			{
			case Mode::Follow: // è¿½å¾“å¯¾è±¡è‡ªèº«ã‚’è¦‹ã‚‹
				UpdateFollowEye(cam, *followTransform, elapsedTime);
				UpdateFollowTarget(cam, *followTransform, elapsedTime);
				break;
			case Mode::LookAt: // ãƒ­ãƒƒã‚¯ã‚ªãƒ³å…ˆã‚’è¦‹ã‚‹
				UpdateLockOnEye(cam, registry, elapsedTime);
				UpdateLookAtTarget(cam, registry, elapsedTime);
				break;
			}
		});
}

// Camera ã‚¯ãƒ©ã‚¹ã«çµæœã‚’åæ˜ 
void GameCameraSystem::ApplyToCamera(entt::registry& registry, Camera& camera)
{
	auto view = registry.view<GameCameraComponent>();
	for (auto entity : view)
	{
		auto& cam = view.get<GameCameraComponent>(entity);

		// followTarget ãŒæœªè¨­å®šãªã‚‰ã‚¹ã‚­ãƒƒãƒ—
		if (cam.followTarget == entt::null) continue;

		// eye ã¨ target ãŒåŒã˜ä½ç½®ãªã‚‰ã‚¹ã‚­ãƒƒãƒ—
		if (cam.base.currentEye.x == cam.base.currentTarget.x &&
			cam.base.currentEye.y == cam.base.currentTarget.y &&
			cam.base.currentEye.z == cam.base.currentTarget.z) continue;

		// Camera ã‚¯ãƒ©ã‚¹ã« eye ã¨ target ã‚’åæ˜ 
		camera.SetLookAt(
			cam.base.currentEye,
			cam.base.currentTarget,
			{ 0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f }
		);
		break;
	}
}

// ãƒ‡ãƒãƒƒã‚°æç”»
void GameCameraSystem::DrawDebug(
	entt::registry& registry,
	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc, 
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& view,
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& projection, 
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& lightDirection)
{
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4 eyeColor        = { 0.2f, 0.5f, 1.0f, 1.0f }; // eye         ï¼šé’
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4 targetColor     = { 1.0f, 0.6f, 0.1f, 1.0f }; // target      ï¼šã‚ªãƒ¬ãƒ³ã‚¸
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4 centerColor     = { 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f }; // ä¸­å¿ƒ O      ï¼šç™½
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4 ringColor       = { 0.2f, 0.5f, 1.0f, 0.6f }; // å††å‘¨        ï¼šè–„ã„é’
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4 targetRingColor = { 0.6f, 0.3f, 1.0f, 0.6f }; // targetã®å††å‘¨ï¼šè–„ã„ç´«

	auto camView = registry.view<GameCameraComponent>();
	for (auto entity : camView)
	{
		auto& cam = camView.get<GameCameraComponent>(entity);
		if (cam.mode != Mode::LookAt) continue;

		// ç¾åœ¨ã®eye/target
		shapeRenderer.DrawSphere(cam.base.currentEye, 0.15f, eyeColor);
		shapeRenderer.DrawSphere(cam.base.currentTarget, 0.15f, targetColor);

		if (registry.valid(cam.followTarget) && registry.valid(cam.lookAtTarget)) 
		{
			DirectX::XMFLOAT3 playerPos = registry.get<TransformComponent>(cam.followTarget).position;
			DirectX::XMFLOAT3 enemyPos = registry.get<TransformComponent>(cam.lookAtTarget).position;

			// Enemyã‹ã‚‰Playerã®æ–¹å‘ãƒ™ã‚¯ãƒˆãƒ«
			float backX = playerPos.x - enemyPos.x;
			float backZ = playerPos.z - enemyPos.z;
			float len = sqrtf(backX * backX + backZ * backZ);

			if (len <= 0.0001f) continue;


			backX /= len;
			backZ /= len;

			// å††ã®ä¸­å¿ƒ O
			float centerDistByRatio = len * cam.lockOnCenterRatio;
			float centerDist = (std::min)(centerDistByRatio, cam.lockOnFarCenterMaxDistance);

			DirectX::XMFLOAT3 center =
			{
				playerPos.x - backX * centerDist,
				playerPos.y,
				playerPos.z - backZ * centerDist
			};

			shapeRenderer.DrawSphere(center, 0.15f, centerColor);

			// è¿‘è·é›¢/é è·é›¢ã§ã€eyeã®åŠå¾„ãƒ»é«˜ã•ãƒ»targetã®é«˜ã•ã‚’åˆ‡ã‚Šæ›¿ãˆã‚‹
			float eyeRadius, eyeHeight, targetYUp;
			if (cam.lockOnRange == LockOnRangeState::Near)
			{
				eyeRadius = cam.lockOnNearCameraDistance;
				eyeHeight = cam.lockOnNearCameraHeight;
				targetYUp = cam.lockOnNearCameraTargetYUp;
			}
			else
			{
				eyeRadius = cam.lockOnFarCameraDistance;
				eyeHeight = cam.lockOnFarCameraHeight;
				targetYUp = cam.lockOnFarCameraTargetYUp;

			}

			// ãƒ‡ãƒ•ã‚©ãƒ«ãƒˆè§’åº¦ã®ã‚ªãƒ•ã‚»ãƒƒãƒˆ
			float nearT = 1.0f - std::clamp(len / cam.lockOnRangeSwitchDistance, 0.0f, 1.0f);
			float angleOffset = DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(cam.lockOnNearAngleOffsetMax) * nearT * cam.lockOnSideSmooth;

			// eyeã®å††å‘¨ã‚’ã€ç‚¹ã®é›†ã¾ã‚Šã§æã
			const int segments = 32;
			for (int i = 0; i < segments; ++i)
			{
				float t = (float)i / (float)segments * DirectX::XM_2PI + angleOffset;
				DirectX::XMFLOAT3 p =
				{
					center.x + sinf(t) * eyeRadius,
					center.y + eyeHeight,
					center.z + cosf(t) * eyeRadius
				};
				shapeRenderer.DrawSphere(p, 0.05f, ringColor);
			}

			// targetã®å††å‘¨ã‚’ã€ç‚¹ã®é›†ã¾ã‚Šã§æã
			const float targetRadius = (std::min)(len * 0.25f, cam.lockOnTargetCircleRadiusMax);
			for (int i = 0; i < segments; ++i)
			{
				float t = (float)i / (float)segments * DirectX::XM_2PI + angleOffset;
				DirectX::XMFLOAT3 p =
				{
					center.x + sinf(t) * targetRadius,
					center.y + targetYUp,
					center.z + cosf(t) * targetRadius
				};
				shapeRenderer.DrawSphere(p, 0.05f, targetRingColor);
			}
		}
	}

	shapeRenderer.Render(dc, view, projection, lightDirection);
}

// Followã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©ã®æ›´æ–°
void GameCameraSystem::UpdateFollowTarget(GameCameraComponent& cam, const TransformComponent& followTransform, float elapsedTime)
{
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 targetPos = followTransform.position;

	// targetã®è£œé–“é€Ÿåº¦ã‚’æ­£è¦åŒ–
	float t = std::clamp(cam.followCameraTargetSpeedRatio / 100.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);

	// X,Zã¯lerpã§è£œé–“ã€Yã¯followCameraTargetYUpã‚’åŠ ç®—ã—ã¦lerpã§è£œé–“
	cam.base.currentTarget.x += (targetPos.x - cam.base.currentTarget.x) * t;
	cam.base.currentTarget.y += ((targetPos.y + cam.followCameraTargetYUp) - cam.base.currentTarget.y) * t;
	cam.base.currentTarget.z += (targetPos.z - cam.base.currentTarget.z) * t;
}
// Followç”¨ã®currentEyeè¨ˆç®—
void GameCameraSystem::UpdateFollowEye(GameCameraComponent& cam, const TransformComponent& followTransform, float elapsedTime)
{
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 basePos = followTransform.position;

	// yaw, pitchã‹ã‚‰ç†æƒ³ã®eyeä½ç½®ã‚’è¨ˆç®—
	float sinYaw = sinf(cam.yaw);
	float cosYaw = cosf(cam.yaw);
	float cosPitch = cosf(cam.pitch);
	float sinPitch = sinf(cam.pitch);

	// FollowTargetã‹ã‚‰Distanceã ã‘é›¢ã‚ŒãŸçƒé¢ä¸Šã®ç‚¹ã‚’æ±‚ã‚ã‚‹
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 idealEye =
	{
		basePos.x - sinYaw * cosPitch * cam.followCameraDistance,
		basePos.y + sinPitch * cam.followCameraDistance + cam.followCameraHeight,
		basePos.z - cosYaw * cosPitch * cam.followCameraDistance
	};

	// eyeã®è£œé–“é€Ÿåº¦ã‚’æ­£è¦åŒ–
	float t = std::clamp(cam.followCameraEyeSpeedRatio / 100.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);

	// X,Zã¯lerpã§è£œé–“
	cam.base.currentEye.x += (idealEye.x - cam.base.currentEye.x) * t;
	cam.base.currentEye.z += (idealEye.z - cam.base.currentEye.z) * t;

	// æ¸›è¡°ã°ã­
	auto springAxis = [&](float current, float ideal, float& velocity) -> float
		{
			// åŠ é€Ÿåº¦ = ãƒãƒã®åŠ›ï¼ˆç›®æ¨™ã¨ã®å·®ï¼‰ - ãƒ€ãƒ³ãƒ‘ãƒ¼ã®åŠ›ï¼ˆé€Ÿåº¦ã«æ¯”ä¾‹ã—ã¦æ¸›è¡°ï¼‰
			float accel = cam.eyeSpringStiffness * (ideal - current) - cam.eyeSpringDamping * velocity;
			velocity += accel * elapsedTime;
			return current + velocity * elapsedTime;
		};

	// Yã¯æ¸›è¡°ã°ã­ã§è£œé–“
	cam.base.currentEye.y = springAxis(cam.base.currentEye.y, idealEye.y, cam.eyeVelocity.y);
}

// LookAtã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©ã®æ›´æ–°
void GameCameraSystem::UpdateLookAtTarget(GameCameraComponent& cam, entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime)
{
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 playerPos = registry.get<TransformComponent>(cam.followTarget).position;
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 enemyPos = registry.get<TransformComponent>(cam.lookAtTarget).position;

	// Enemyã‹ã‚‰Playeræ–¹å‘ã®ãƒ™ã‚¯ãƒˆãƒ«ç®—å‡º
	float backX = playerPos.x - enemyPos.x;
	float backZ = playerPos.z - enemyPos.z;
	float len = sqrtf(backX * backX + backZ * backZ);

	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 idealTarget = cam.base.currentTarget;

	if (len > 0.0001f)
	{
		backX /= len;
		backZ /= len;

		// å††ã®ä¸­å¿ƒ O
		float centerDistByRatio = len * cam.lockOnCenterRatio;
		float centerDist = (std::min)(centerDistByRatio, cam.lockOnFarCenterMaxDistance);

		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 center =
		{
			playerPos.x - backX * centerDist,
			playerPos.y,
			playerPos.z - backZ * centerDist
		};

		// targetã®å††ã®åŠå¾„
		float targetRadius = (std::min)(len * 0.25f, cam.lockOnTargetCircleRadiusMax);

		// targetã®é«˜ã•(è¿‘è·é›¢/é è·é›¢ã§åˆ‡ã‚Šæ›¿ãˆ)
		float targetYUp = (cam.lockOnRange == LockOnRangeState::Near)
			? cam.lockOnNearCameraTargetYUp
			: cam.lockOnFarCameraTargetYUp;

		// è·é›¢ã«å¿œã˜ã¦ãªã‚ã‚‰ã‹ã«å¤‰åŒ–
		float nearT = 1.0f - std::clamp(len / cam.lockOnRangeSwitchDistance, 0.0f, 1.0f);
		float angleOffset = DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(cam.lockOnNearAngleOffsetMax) * nearT * cam.lockOnSideSmooth;

		// Cameraã¨åŒã˜è§’åº¦ã§å›è»¢ã•ã›ãŸæ–¹å‘ã‚’æ±‚ã‚ã€åå¯¾æ–¹å‘ã«é…ç½®ã™ã‚‹
		float angle = cam.lockOnOrbitAngle + angleOffset;
		float cosA = cosf(angle);
		float sinA = sinf(angle);
		float rotatedX = backX * cosA - backZ * sinA;
		float rotatedZ = backX * sinA + backZ * cosA;

		// orbitPitchã‚‚ã€eyeã¨ã¯ä¸Šä¸‹é€†æ–¹å‘ã«ã™ã‚‹ã“ã¨ã§ã€ç‚¹å¯¾ç§°ã‚’ä¿ã¤
		float cosP = cosf(cam.lockOnOrbitPitch);
		float sinP = sinf(cam.lockOnOrbitPitch);

		idealTarget =
		{
			center.x - rotatedX * targetRadius * cosP,
			center.y + targetYUp - targetRadius * sinP,
			center.z - rotatedZ * targetRadius * cosP
		};
	}

	// targetã®è£œé–“é€Ÿåº¦ã‚’æ­£è¦åŒ–
	float t = std::clamp(cam.lockOnCameraTargetSpeedRatio / 100.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);

	// X,Zã¯lerpã§è£œé–“
	cam.base.currentTarget.x += (idealTarget.x - cam.base.currentTarget.x) * t;
	cam.base.currentTarget.z += (idealTarget.z - cam.base.currentTarget.z) * t;

	// æ¸›è¡°ã°ã­
	auto springAxis = [&](float current, float ideal, float& velocity) -> float
		{
			float accel = cam.eyeSpringStiffness * (ideal - current) - cam.eyeSpringDamping * velocity;
			velocity += accel * elapsedTime;
			return current + velocity * elapsedTime;
		};

	// Yã¯æ¸›è¡°ã°ã­ã§è£œé–“
	cam.base.currentTarget.y = springAxis(cam.base.currentTarget.y, idealTarget.y, cam.targetVelocity.y);
}
// LookAtç”¨ã®currentEyeè¨ˆç®—
void GameCameraSystem::UpdateLockOnEye(GameCameraComponent& cam, entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime)
{
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 playerPos = registry.get<TransformComponent>(cam.followTarget).position;
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 enemyPos = registry.get<TransformComponent>(cam.lookAtTarget).position;

	// Enemyã‹ã‚‰Playeræ–¹å‘ã®ãƒ™ã‚¯ãƒˆãƒ«ç®—å‡º
	float backX = playerPos.x - enemyPos.x;
	float backZ = playerPos.z - enemyPos.z;
	float len = sqrtf(backX * backX + backZ * backZ);

	// Enemyã¨PlayerãŒåŒã˜ä½ç½®ã®å ´åˆã¯ç¾åœ¨ã®ä½ç½®ã‚’ç¶­æŒã™ã‚‹
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 idealEye = cam.base.currentEye;
	if (len > 0.0001f)
	{
		// æ­£è¦åŒ–
		backX /= len;
		backZ /= len;
	
		// è·é›¢ã«å¿œã˜ã¦ è¿‘è·é›¢ / é è·é›¢ ã‚’åˆ‡ã‚Šæ›¿ãˆã‚‹
		cam.lockOnRange = (len >= cam.lockOnRangeSwitchDistance)
			? LockOnRangeState::Far
			: LockOnRangeState::Near;

		// è¿‘è·é›¢ / é è·é›¢ ã§ã€åŠå¾„ãƒ»é«˜ã•ã¨ã€ãƒ‡ãƒ•ã‚©ãƒ«ãƒˆè§’åº¦ã®ã‚ªãƒ•ã‚»ãƒƒãƒˆã‚’åˆ‡ã‚Šæ›¿ãˆã‚‹
		float eyeRadius, eyeHeight;
		if (cam.lockOnRange == LockOnRangeState::Near)
		{
			eyeRadius = cam.lockOnNearCameraDistance;
			eyeHeight = cam.lockOnNearCameraHeight;
		}
		else
		{
			eyeRadius = cam.lockOnFarCameraDistance;
			eyeHeight = cam.lockOnFarCameraHeight;
		}

		// ãƒ‡ãƒ•ã‚©ãƒ«ãƒˆè§’åº¦ã®offsetEnemyã«è¿‘ã¥ãç¨‹æ»‘ã‚‰ã‹ã«è§’åº¦ã‚’å¢—ã‚„ã™
		float nearT = 1.0f - std::clamp(len / cam.lockOnRangeSwitchDistance, 0.0f, 1.0f);
		float angleOffset = DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(cam.lockOnNearAngleOffsetMax) * nearT * cam.lockOnSideSmooth;

		// å††ã®ä¸­å¿ƒOã‚’æ±‚ã‚ã‚‹
		float centerDistByRatio = len * cam.lockOnCenterRatio;
		float centerDist = (std::min)(centerDistByRatio, cam.lockOnFarCenterMaxDistance);

		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 center =
		{
			playerPos.x - backX * centerDist,
			playerPos.y,
			playerPos.z - backZ * centerDist
		};

		// backãƒ™ã‚¯ãƒˆãƒ«ï¼ˆEnemyã®åå¯¾æ–¹å‘ï¼è§’åº¦0ã®åŸºæº–ï¼‰ã‚’ã€(orbitAngle + angleOffset)ã¶ã‚“å›è»¢ã•ã›ã‚‹
		float angle = cam.lockOnOrbitAngle + angleOffset;
		float cosA = cosf(angle);
		float sinA = sinf(angle);
		float rotatedX = backX * cosA - backZ * sinA;
		float rotatedZ = backX * sinA + backZ * cosA;

		// orbitPitchã§ã€æ°´å¹³æ–¹å‘ã®åºƒãŒã‚Š(cosP)ã¨ã€ä¸Šä¸‹æ–¹å‘ã®é«˜ã•(sinP)ã«åˆ†é…ã™ã‚‹
		float cosP = cosf(cam.lockOnOrbitPitch);
		float sinP = sinf(cam.lockOnOrbitPitch);

		idealEye =
		{
			center.x + rotatedX * eyeRadius * cosP,
			center.y + eyeHeight + eyeRadius * sinP,
			center.z + rotatedZ * eyeRadius * cosP
		};
		
	}

	// eyeã®è£œé–“é€Ÿåº¦ã‚’æ­£è¦åŒ–
	float t = std::clamp(cam.lockOnCameraEyeSpeedRatio / 100.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);

	// X,Zã¯lerpã§è£œé–“
	cam.base.currentEye.x += (idealEye.x - cam.base.currentEye.x) * t;
	cam.base.currentEye.z += (idealEye.z - cam.base.currentEye.z) * t;

	// æ¸›è¡°ã°ã­
	auto springAxis = [&](float current, float ideal, float& velocity) -> float
		{
			float accel = cam.eyeSpringStiffness * (ideal - current) - cam.eyeSpringDamping * velocity;
			velocity += accel * elapsedTime;
			return current + velocity * elapsedTime;
		};

	// Yã¯æ¸›è¡°ã°ã­ã§è£œé–“
	cam.base.currentEye.y = springAxis(cam.base.currentEye.y, idealEye.y, cam.eyeVelocity.y);
}
 
 
===== GameInputSystem.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\GameInputSystem.cpp 
ï»¿#include "GameInputSystem.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

// æ›´æ–°
void GameInputSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, const ControllerSettings& settings)
{
    // ControllerComponent ã‚’æ¢ã™ï¼ˆãªã‘ã‚Œã°ä½•ã‚‚ã—ãªã„ï¼‰
    ControllerComponent* ctrl = nullptr;
    auto ctrlView = registry.view<ControllerComponent>();
    if (!ctrlView.empty())
    {
        ctrl = &ctrlView.get<ControllerComponent>(*ctrlView.begin());
    }

    // GameInputComponent ã‚’æŒã¤ Entity ã‚’æ›´æ–°
    auto inputView = registry.view<GameInputComponent>();
    inputView.each([&](GameInputComponent& input)
        {
            // ãƒªã‚»ãƒƒãƒˆ
            input.moveStick.x = 0.0f;
            input.moveStick.y = 0.0f;
            input.moveStick.hasInput = false;

            input.cameraStick.x = 0.0f;
            input.cameraStick.y = 0.0f;
            input.cameraStick.hasInput = false;

            input.dodgeTrigger.value = 0.0f;

            input.attack = false;

            // ControllerComponent ãŒãªã‘ã‚Œã°ã‚¹ã‚­ãƒƒãƒ—
            if (!ctrl || !ctrl->connected)
            {
                // æ¥ç¶šãŒåˆ‡ã‚ŒãŸå ´åˆã‚‚ã€ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³ã¯æŠ¼ã•ã‚Œã¦ã„ãªã„çŠ¶æ…‹ã«ã™ã‚‹
                UpdateButtonState(input.dodgeTrigger, false);
                UpdateButtonState(input.lockOnTrigger, false);
                UpdateButtonState(input.jump, false);
                return;
            }

            // ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯ï¼ˆãƒ‡ãƒƒãƒ‰ã‚¾ãƒ¼ãƒ³é©ç”¨ï¼‰
            float lx = ApplyStickDeadzone(ctrl->leftStickX, settings.leftStickDeadzone);
            float ly = ApplyStickDeadzone(ctrl->leftStickY, settings.leftStickDeadzone);
            float rx = ApplyStickDeadzone(ctrl->rightStickX, settings.rightStickDeadzone);
            float ry = ApplyStickDeadzone(ctrl->rightStickY, settings.rightStickDeadzone);

            // ç§»å‹•ï¼ˆå·¦ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯ï¼‰
            input.moveStick.x = std::clamp(lx, -1.0f, 1.0f);
            input.moveStick.y = std::clamp(ly, -1.0f, 1.0f);
            input.moveStick.hasInput = (input.moveStick.x != 0.0f || input.moveStick.y != 0.0f);

            // ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©ï¼ˆå³ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯ï¼‰
            input.cameraStick.x = rx;
            input.cameraStick.y = -ry;
            input.cameraStick.hasInput = (input.cameraStick.x != 0.0f || input.cameraStick.y != 0.0f);

            // ãƒˆãƒªã‚¬ãƒ¼ï¼ˆãƒ‡ãƒƒãƒ‰ã‚¾ãƒ¼ãƒ³é©ç”¨å¾Œã®å€¤ã§åˆ¤å®šï¼‰
            float lt = ApplyTriggerDeadzone(ctrl->leftTrigger, settings.leftTriggerDeadzone);
            float rt = ApplyTriggerDeadzone(ctrl->rightTrigger, settings.rightTriggerDeadzone);
            (void)lt;

            input.dodgeTrigger.value = rt;

            // R2ã®æŠ¼ä¸‹åˆ¤å®š(ãƒ’ã‚¹ãƒ†ãƒªã‚·ã‚¹è¾¼ã¿)
            bool r2Held = ctrl->IsPressed(ControllerButtons::R2);
            UpdateButtonState(input.dodgeTrigger, r2Held);

			// L2ã®æŠ¼ä¸‹åˆ¤å®š(ãƒ’ã‚¹ãƒ†ãƒªã‚·ã‚¹è¾¼ã¿)
            bool l2Held = ctrl->IsPressed(ControllerButtons::L2);
            UpdateButtonState(input.lockOnTrigger, l2Held);

            // ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³åˆ¤å®š
            bool crossHeld = ctrl->IsPressed(ControllerButtons::Cross);
            UpdateButtonState(input.jump, crossHeld);

            input.attack = ctrl->IsPressed(ControllerButtons::Square);
        });
}

// ãƒ‡ãƒƒãƒ‰ã‚¾ãƒ¼ãƒ³é©ç”¨
float GameInputSystem::ApplyStickDeadzone(float value, float deadzone)
{
    if (fabsf(value) < deadzone) return 0.0f;
    float sign = value > 0.0f ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
    return sign * (fabsf(value) - deadzone) / (1.0f - deadzone);
}

// ãƒ‡ãƒƒãƒ‰ã‚¾ãƒ¼ãƒ³é©ç”¨
float GameInputSystem::ApplyTriggerDeadzone(float value, float deadzone)
{
    if (value < deadzone) return 0.0f;
    return (value - deadzone) / (1.0f - deadzone);
}

// ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³çŠ¶æ…‹ã®æ›´æ–°
void GameInputSystem::UpdateButtonState(TriggerState& state, bool isHeldNow)
{
    state.pressed = isHeldNow && !state.wasHeld;
    state.released = !isHeldNow && state.wasHeld;
    state.held = isHeldNow;
    state.wasHeld = isHeldNow;
}
void GameInputSystem::UpdateButtonState(ButtonState& state, bool isHeldNow)
{
    state.pressed = isHeldNow && !state.wasHeld;
    state.released = !isHeldNow && state.wasHeld;
    state.held = isHeldNow;
    state.wasHeld = isHeldNow;
}
 
 
===== PlayerDodgeSystem.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerDodgeSystem.cpp 
#include "PlayerDodgeSystem.h"
#include <cmath>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerIntentComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerDodgeComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Systems/PlayerMovementUtils.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StateMachineComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Systems/StateMachineSystem.h"

namespace
{
	// ‘Ì‚ÌŒü‚«‚©‚çŒ©‚½Šp“x·‚©‚çA4•ûŒü‚Ì‚Ç‚ê‚©‚ğŒˆ‚ß‚é
	PlayerDodgeDirection ClassifyDirection(float diff)
	{
		const float quarter = DirectX::XM_PIDIV4; // 45“x

		if (diff > -quarter && diff <= quarter)         return PlayerDodgeDirection::Front;
		if (diff > quarter && diff <= quarter * 3.0f)   return PlayerDodgeDirection::Right;
		if (diff > -quarter * 3.0f && diff <= -quarter) return PlayerDodgeDirection::Left;
		return PlayerDodgeDirection::Back;
	}
}

PlayerDodgeSystem::PlayerDodgeSystem()
{
	StateMachineSystem::RequireState<PlayerDodgeComponent>("Dodge", "PlayerDodgeSystem");
}

void PlayerDodgeSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerIntentComponent, PlayerDodgeComponent, StateMachineComponent, PlayerLocomotionComponent, TransformComponent>();
	
	view.each([&](PlayerIntentComponent& intent, PlayerDodgeComponent& dodge, const StateMachineComponent& stateMachine,
		PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, const TransformComponent& transform)
		{

			if (!stateMachine.IsIn("Dodge")) return;

			// “ü‚Á‚½uŠÔ ‰ñ”ğ•ûŒü‚ğŒˆ‚ß‚é
			if (stateMachine.enteredThisFrame)
			{
				// ƒXƒeƒBƒbƒN“ü—Í‚ª‚ ‚ê‚Î‚»‚Ì•ûŒüA–³‚¯‚ê‚Î‘Ì‚ÌŒã‚ë
				float dodgeYaw = intent.wantsMove
					? PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(intent.moveDir.x, intent.moveDir.z)
					: transform.rotation.y + DirectX::XM_PI;
				dodge.dodgeDir = PlayerMovementUtils::YawToForward(dodgeYaw);

				// ƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚Ì‘I‘ğ—pF4•ûŒü‚Í“ü‚Á‚½uŠÔ‚¾‚¯Œˆ‚ßA‰ñ”ğ’†‚Í•Ï‚¦‚È‚¢
				dodge.direction = ClassifyDirection(PlayerMovementUtils::AngleDiff(transform.rotation.y, dodgeYaw));
				dodge.isFront = (dodge.direction == PlayerDodgeDirection::Front);
				dodge.isBack = (dodge.direction == PlayerDodgeDirection::Back);
				dodge.isLeft = (dodge.direction == PlayerDodgeDirection::Left);
				dodge.isRight = (dodge.direction == PlayerDodgeDirection::Right);

				dodge.elapsed = 0.0f;
				dodge.finished = false;

				// “ü‚Á‚½ƒXƒe[ƒg‚ª“ü—Í‚ğÁ”ï‚·‚é
				intent.dodge.Consume();
			}
			// ‰ñ”ğ’† ƒXƒeƒBƒbƒN“ü—Í‚ª‚ ‚ê‚ÎA‰ñ”ğ•ûŒü‚ğ“ü—Í‚Ì‚Ù‚¤‚Ö‹È‚°‚é
			else if (intent.wantsMove)
			{
				float dodgeYaw = PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(dodge.dodgeDir.x, dodge.dodgeDir.z);
				float inputYaw = PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(intent.moveDir.x, intent.moveDir.z);
				dodgeYaw = PlayerMovementUtils::RotateTowards(dodgeYaw, inputYaw, dodge.turnSpeed, elapsedTime);
				dodge.dodgeDir = PlayerMovementUtils::YawToForward(dodgeYaw);
			}

			// –ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ ŠÔ‚ği‚ßA‘Ì‚©‚çŒ©‚½‰ñ”ğ•ûŒü‚ğ‹‚ß‚é
			dodge.elapsed += elapsedTime;

			float localDiff = PlayerMovementUtils::AngleDiff(
				transform.rotation.y,
				PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(dodge.dodgeDir.x, dodge.dodgeDir.z));
			dodge.localX = sinf(localDiff);
			dodge.localZ = cosf(localDiff);

			// I‚í‚è‚Ì”»’èFÅ’áŠÔ‚ğ‰ß‚¬‚ÄR2‚ğ—£‚µ‚½A‚Ü‚½‚ÍÅ‘åŠÔ‚É’B‚µ‚½
			if (!dodge.finished)
			{
				bool released = !intent.dodge.held && dodge.elapsed >= dodge.minTime;
				bool timeUp = dodge.elapsed >= dodge.maxTime;
				dodge.finished = released || timeUp;
			}

			// –ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€FLocomotion ‚Ö‚Ìw¦
			LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;
			cmd.mode = LocomotionMode::Directional;
			cmd.moveDir = dodge.dodgeDir;
			cmd.targetSpeed = dodge.dodgeSpeed;
			cmd.accelHalfLife = dodge.accelHalfLife;

			// ‘–‚èo‚·‚ÍA”²‚¯‚é‘O‚ÉŒü‚«‚ğ‰ñ”ğ•ûŒü‚Ö‡‚í‚¹‚Ä‚¨‚­
			cmd.faceMoveDir = dodge.finished && intent.wantsMove;
		});
}

	StateMachineSystem::RequireState<PlayerIdleComponent>("Idle", "PlayerIdleSystem");
}

void PlayerIdleSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)elapsedTime;
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerIdleComponent, StateMachineComponent, PlayerLocomotionComponent>();
	view.each([&](const PlayerIdleComponent& idle, const StateMachineComponent& stateMachine, PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco)
		{
			if (!stateMachine.IsIn("Idle")) return;

			// ‚»‚Ìê‚Å~‚Ü‚é(Œü‚«‚Í•Ï‚¦‚È‚¢)
			LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;
			cmd.mode = LocomotionMode::Ground;
			cmd.moveDir = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
			cmd.targetSpeed = 0.0f;
			cmd.decelHalfLife = idle.stopHalfLife;
			cmd.turnSpeed = 0.0f;
		});
} 
 
===== PlayerIntentSystem.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerIntentSystem.cpp 
#include "PlayerIntentSystem.h"
#include <algorithm>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/GameInputComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerIntentComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Systems/PlayerMovementUtils.h"

namespace
{
	// ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“1‚Â‚Ô‚ñ‚ÌˆÓ}‚ğXV‚·‚é
	void UpdateAction(ActionIntent& action, bool pressed, bool held, float bufferTime, float elapsedTime)
	{
		// ‰Ÿ‚µ‘±‚¯‚Ä‚¢‚éŠÔ
		action.held = held;
		action.heldTime = held ? action.heldTime + elapsedTime : 0.0f;

		// æs“ü—ÍF‰Ÿ‚³‚ê‚½uŠÔ‚Éó‚¯•t‚¯Aó•tŠÔ‚ğ‰ß‚¬‚½‚çæ‚èÁ‚·
		if (pressed)
		{
			action.buffered = true;
			action.bufferTimer = 0.0f;
		}
		else if (action.buffered)
		{
			action.bufferTimer += elapsedTime;
			if (action.bufferTimer > bufferTime)
			{
				action.buffered = false;
			}
		}
	}
}

void PlayerIntentSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	// ƒJƒƒ‰‚ª‚È‚¢ê‡‚Íˆ—‚µ‚È‚¢
	if (!context.camera)return;

	// ƒJƒƒ‰‚Ì‘O•ûŒü‚Æ‰E•ûŒü‚ğæ“¾
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 front = context.camera->GetFront();
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 right = context.camera->GetRight();

	auto view = registry.view<GameInputComponent, PlayerIntentComponent>();
	view.each([&](const GameInputComponent& input, PlayerIntentComponent& intent)
		{
			// ¶‚Ì“ü—Í‚ğAƒJƒƒ‰Šî€‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒh•ûŒü‚É•ÏŠ·
			auto dir = PlayerMovementUtils::StickToWorldVelocity(input.moveStick.x, input.moveStick.y, front, right, 1.0f);
			intent.hasRawInput = (dir.inputLength > 0.0f);

			// “®‚«‚½‚¢‚©(—P—\•t‚«)
			if (intent.hasRawInput)
			{
				// “ü—Í‚ ‚èF•ûŒü‚Æ“|‚µ‹ï‡‚ğXV‚µ‚ÄA—P—\‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg
				intent.moveDir = dir.velocity; // ’·‚³1
				intent.moveAmount = (std::min)(dir.inputLength, 1.0f);
				intent.graceTimer = 0.0f;
				intent.wantsMove = true;
			}
			else
			{
				// “ü—Í‚È‚µF—P—\‚ÌŠÔ‚ÍAÅŒã‚Ì•ûŒü‚Æ“|‚µ‹ï‡‚ğ•Û‚Â
				intent.graceTimer += elapsedTime;
				if (intent.graceTimer >= intent.moveGraceTime)
				{
					intent.wantsMove = false;
					intent.moveAmount = 0.0f;
				}
			}

			// ‘–‚è‚½‚¢‚©(ƒqƒXƒeƒŠƒVƒX)
			if (!intent.wantsMove)
			{
				intent.wantsRun = false;
			}
			else if (intent.moveAmount >= intent.runEnterThreshold)
			{
				intent.wantsRun = true;
			}
			else if (intent.moveAmount < intent.runExitThreshold)
			{
				intent.wantsRun = false;
			}

			// ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ÌˆÓ}
			UpdateAction(intent.jump,  input.jump.pressed,         input.jump.held,         intent.jumpBufferTime,  elapsedTime);
			UpdateAction(intent.dodge, input.dodgeTrigger.pressed, input.dodgeTrigger.held, intent.dodgeBufferTime, elapsedTime);
		});
} 
 
===== PlayerJumpSystem.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerJumpSystem.cpp 
#include "PlayerJumpSystem.h"
#include <algorithm>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerIntentComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerJumpComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StateMachineComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Systems/StateMachineSystem.h"

PlayerJumpSystem::PlayerJumpSystem()
{
	StateMachineSystem::RequireState<PlayerJumpComponent>("Jump", "PlayerJumpSystem");
}

void PlayerJumpSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)elapsedTime;
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerIntentComponent, PlayerJumpComponent, StateMachineComponent, PlayerLocomotionComponent, MovementComponent>();
	view.each([&](PlayerIntentComponent& intent, PlayerJumpComponent& jump, const StateMachineComponent& stateMachine,
		PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, MovementComponent& movement)
		{
			if (!stateMachine.IsIn("Jump")) return;

			// “ü‚Á‚½uŠÔFƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv“ü—Í‚ª‚ ‚èA‚Ü‚¾’ˆ‚É•‚‚¢‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¯‚ê‚Î’µ‚ÔB‚»‚¤‚Å‚È‚¯‚ê‚Î—‚¿‚é‚¾‚¯
			if (stateMachine.enteredThisFrame)
			{
				jump.fromGround = intent.jump.buffered && !loco.isAirborne;
				jump.takeoffSpeed = loco.horizontalSpeed;

				if (jump.fromGround)
				{
					// 1‰ñŒÀ‚è‚Ì‰‘¬‚ÍAƒXƒe[ƒg‚ª’¼Ú‘‚¢‚Ä‚æ‚¢(ˆÈ~‚Íd—Í‚É”C‚¹‚é)
					movement.velocity.y = jump.jumpSpeed;
					intent.jump.Consume();
				}
			}

			// –ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€F‹ó’†‚Ìw¦
			LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;
			cmd.mode = LocomotionMode::Air;
			cmd.moveDir = intent.wantsMove ? intent.moveDir : DirectX::XMFLOAT3{ 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
			cmd.targetSpeed = (std::max)(jump.takeoffSpeed, jump.airSpeed); // “¥‚İØ‚è‚Ì¨‚¢‚ğ‹ó’†‚Å¸‚í‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É
			cmd.accelHalfLife = jump.airControlHalfLife;
			cmd.turnSpeed = jump.airTurnSpeed;
		});
} 
 
===== PlayerLocomotionSystem.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerLocomotionSystem.cpp 
#include "PlayerLocomotionSystem.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
#include <DirectXMath.h>

#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Systems/PlayerMovementUtils.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"

namespace
{
	// w”“I‚ÈŒ¸Š‚Í0‚É“Í‚©‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅA‚±‚ê‚æ‚è’x‚­‚È‚Á‚½‚ç0‚É‚·‚é
	constexpr float kStopSpeed = 0.05f;

	float PlanarLength(float x, float z) { return sqrtf(x * x + z * z); }

	bool HasDirection(const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& dir) { return PlanarLength(dir.x, dir.z) > 0.0001f; }

	// w¦‚³‚ê‚½•ûŒü‚Ö‘Ì‚ğ‰ñ‚µA‰ñ‚Á‚½Œã‚ÌŒü‚«‚Ì‚¸‚ê(rad)‚ğ•Ô‚·
	float TurnTowards(const LocomotionCommand& cmd, TransformComponent& transform, float elapsedTime)
	{
		if (!HasDirection(cmd.moveDir)) return 0.0f;

		float targetYaw = PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(cmd.moveDir.x, cmd.moveDir.z);
		transform.rotation.y = PlayerMovementUtils::RotateTowards(transform.rotation.y, targetYaw, cmd.turnSpeed, elapsedTime);
		return PlayerMovementUtils::AngleDiff(transform.rotation.y, targetYaw);
	}

	// Ground ‚É“ü‚éFÀÛ‚Ì‘¬“x‚ğu‘Ì‚ÌŒü‚«‚É‰ˆ‚Á‚½‘¬‚³v‚Æu‚¿‰z‚µ‚Ì‘¬“xv‚É•ª‚¯‚Äæ‚è‚Ş
	void AbsorbIntoGround(PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, const MovementComponent& movement, const TransformComponent& transform)
	{
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 forward = PlayerMovementUtils::YawToForward(transform.rotation.y);

		// ‘Ì‚ÌŒü‚«‚É‰ˆ‚Á‚½¬•ª(Œã‚ëŒü‚«‚Í0‚Æ‚·‚é)
		float along = movement.velocity.x * forward.x + movement.velocity.z * forward.z;
		loco.currentSpeed = (std::max)(0.0f, along);

		// ‚»‚êˆÈŠO(‰¡EŒã‚ëŒü‚«)‚ÍA‚¿‰z‚µ‚Ì‘¬“x‚Æ‚µ‚Äc‚·
		loco.carryVelocity.x = movement.velocity.x - forward.x * loco.currentSpeed;
		loco.carryVelocity.z = movement.velocity.z - forward.z * loco.currentSpeed;
	}

	// ’nãF‘Ì‚ÌŒü‚«‚É‰ˆ‚Á‚Äi‚Ş
	void DriveGround(PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, const LocomotionCommand& cmd,
		MovementComponent& movement, TransformComponent& transform, float elapsedTime)
	{
		// Œü‚«
		float facingError = TurnTowards(cmd, transform, elapsedTime);

		// ©•ª‚Å‘–‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‘¬‚³
		float alignment = (std::max)(0.0f, cosf(facingError));
		float desiredSpeed = cmd.targetSpeed * alignment;

		float halfLife = (desiredSpeed > loco.currentSpeed) ? cmd.accelHalfLife : cmd.decelHalfLife;
		loco.currentSpeed = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(loco.currentSpeed, desiredSpeed, halfLife, elapsedTime);
		if (desiredSpeed <= 0.0f && loco.currentSpeed < kStopSpeed)
		{
			loco.currentSpeed = 0.0f;
		}

		// ‚¿‰z‚µ‚Ì‘¬“x
		loco.carryVelocity.x = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(loco.carryVelocity.x, 0.0f, cmd.carryHalfLife, elapsedTime);
		loco.carryVelocity.z = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(loco.carryVelocity.z, 0.0f, cmd.carryHalfLife, elapsedTime);
		if (PlanarLength(loco.carryVelocity.x, loco.carryVelocity.z) < kStopSpeed)
		{
			loco.carryVelocity = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
		}

		// ‘¬“x = ‘Ì‚ÌŒü‚« ~ ‘¬‚³ { ‚¿‰z‚µ
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 forward = PlayerMovementUtils::YawToForward(transform.rotation.y);
		movement.velocity.x = forward.x * loco.currentSpeed + loco.carryVelocity.x;
		movement.velocity.z = forward.z * loco.currentSpeed + loco.carryVelocity.z;

		// Šm”F—p
		loco.desiredSpeed = desiredSpeed;
		loco.facingErrorDeg = DirectX::XMConvertToDegrees(facingError);
	}

	// ‹ó’†F‘¬“x‚ÌƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ğ•Û‚Â
	void DriveAir(PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, const LocomotionCommand& cmd,
		MovementComponent& movement, TransformComponent& transform, float elapsedTime)
	{
		// Œü‚«
		float facingError = TurnTowards(cmd, transform, elapsedTime);

		// ‘¬“xF“ü—Í‚ª–³‚¯‚ê‚Î¨‚¢‚ğ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü•Û‚Â
		if (HasDirection(cmd.moveDir))
		{
			float targetX = cmd.moveDir.x * cmd.targetSpeed;
			float targetZ = cmd.moveDir.z * cmd.targetSpeed;
			movement.velocity.x = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(movement.velocity.x, targetX, cmd.accelHalfLife, elapsedTime);
			movement.velocity.z = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(movement.velocity.z, targetZ, cmd.accelHalfLife, elapsedTime);
		}

		// ’…’n‚µ‚½Œã‚Å‚àQÆ‚Å‚«‚é‚æ‚¤‚É
		loco.landingHorizontalSpeed = PlanarLength(movement.velocity.x, movement.velocity.z);
		loco.landingVerticalSpeed = movement.velocity.y;

		// Šm”F—p
		loco.currentSpeed = loco.landingHorizontalSpeed;
		loco.desiredSpeed = cmd.targetSpeed;
		loco.facingErrorDeg = DirectX::XMConvertToDegrees(facingError);
	}

	// Œü‚«‚Æ‚Í•Ê‚Ì•ûŒü‚Éi‚ŞF‘Ì‚ÌŒü‚«‚Í•Ï‚¦‚¸A‘¬“x‚¾‚¯‚ğw¦‚³‚ê‚½•ûŒü‚ÖŠñ‚¹‚é
	void DriveDirectional(PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, const LocomotionCommand& cmd,
		MovementComponent& movement, float elapsedTime)
	{
		// ‘¬“xFw¦‚³‚ê‚½•ûŒü ~ ‘¬‚³ ‚Öƒ_ƒ“ƒp[‚Å‹ß‚Ã‚¯‚é
		float targetX = cmd.moveDir.x * cmd.targetSpeed;
		float targetZ = cmd.moveDir.z * cmd.targetSpeed;
		movement.velocity.x = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(movement.velocity.x, targetX, cmd.accelHalfLife, elapsedTime);
		movement.velocity.z = PlayerMovementUtils::Damper(movement.velocity.z, targetZ, cmd.accelHalfLife, elapsedTime);

		// Šm”F—p
		loco.currentSpeed = PlanarLength(movement.velocity.x, movement.velocity.z);
		loco.desiredSpeed = cmd.targetSpeed;
		loco.facingErrorDeg = 0.0f;
	}
}

void PlayerLocomotionSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime)
{
	auto view = registry.view<PlayerLocomotionComponent, MovementComponent, TransformComponent>();
	view.each([&](PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco, MovementComponent& movement, TransformComponent& transform)
		{
			const LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;

			// ‘Ì‚Ìó‘ÔFÚ’n‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢ŠÔ
			loco.airTime = movement.isGrounded ? 0.0f : loco.airTime + elapsedTime;
			loco.isAirborne = (loco.airTime >= loco.fallGraceTime);

			// w¦‚ª‚ ‚ê‚ÎAŒü‚«‚ği‚Ş•ûŒü‚Öˆê‹C‚É‡‚í‚¹‚é
			if (cmd.faceMoveDir && HasDirection(cmd.moveDir))
			{
				transform.rotation.y = PlayerMovementUtils::CalcFacingYaw(cmd.moveDir.x, cmd.moveDir.z);
			}

			// ‘¼‚Ìƒ‚[ƒh‚©‚ç Ground ‚ÉØ‚è‘Ö‚í‚Á‚½‚¾‚¯AÀÛ‚Ì‘¬“x‚ğæ‚è‚Ş
			if (cmd.mode == LocomotionMode::Ground && loco.lastMode != LocomotionMode::Ground) 
			{
				AbsorbIntoGround(loco, movement, transform);
			}

			switch (cmd.mode)
			{
			case LocomotionMode::Ground:
				DriveGround(loco, cmd, movement, transform, elapsedTime);
				break;

			case LocomotionMode::Air:
				DriveAir(loco, cmd, movement, transform, elapsedTime);
				break;

			case LocomotionMode::Directional:
				DriveDirectional(loco, cmd, movement, elapsedTime);
				break;

			case LocomotionMode::External:
				// velocity.xz ‚ÍŠO•”‚ª‘‚­
				loco.desiredSpeed = 0.0f;
				loco.facingErrorDeg = 0.0f;
				break;
			}

			// ‘Ì‚Ìó‘ÔF¡‚Ì¨‚¢
			loco.horizontalSpeed = PlanarLength(movement.velocity.x, movement.velocity.z);
			loco.verticalSpeed = movement.velocity.y;

			// w¦‚Í1ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ŒÀ‚è
			loco.lastMode = cmd.mode;
			loco.command = LocomotionCommand{};
		});
} 
 
===== PlayerMovementUtils.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Game\ECS\Systems\PlayerMovementUtils.cpp 
#include "PlayerMovementUtils.h"
#include <cmath>

namespace PlayerMovementUtils
{
	WorldVelocityResult StickToWorldVelocity(
		float inputX, float inputZ,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraFront,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraRight,
		float maxSpeed)
	{
		WorldVelocityResult result;

		float frontLen = sqrtf(cameraFront.x * cameraFront.x + cameraFront.z * cameraFront.z);
		float rightLen = sqrtf(cameraRight.x * cameraRight.x + cameraRight.z * cameraRight.z);

		float frontX = (frontLen > 0.0f) ? cameraFront.x / frontLen : 0.0f;
		float frontZ = (frontLen > 0.0f) ? cameraFront.z / frontLen : 0.0f;
		float rightX = (rightLen > 0.0f) ? cameraRight.x / rightLen : 0.0f;
		float rightZ = (rightLen > 0.0f) ? cameraRight.z / rightLen : 0.0f;

		float dirX = frontX * inputZ + rightX * inputX;
		float dirZ = frontZ * inputZ + rightZ * inputX;
		float len = sqrtf(dirX * dirX + dirZ * dirZ);

		result.inputLength = len;
		
		if (len > 0.0f)
		{
			result.velocity.x = (dirX / len) * maxSpeed;
			result.velocity.z = (dirZ / len) * maxSpeed;
		}

		return result;
	}

	WorldVelocityResult ResolvePlanarVelocity(
		float inputX, float inputZ,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraFront,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& cameraRight,
		float targetSpeed,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& currentVelocity,
		float elapsedTime,
		float acceleration)
	{
		// –Ú•W(•ûŒü‚Í“ü—Í•ûŒüA‘å‚«‚³‚Í targetSpeed)
		WorldVelocityResult result = StickToWorldVelocity(inputX, inputZ, cameraFront, cameraRight, targetSpeed);

		// •‰‚È‚ç]—ˆ‚Ç‚¨‚èA‘¦À‚É–Ú•W‘¬“x
		if (acceleration < 0.0f) return result;
		if (targetSpeed <= 0.0f) return result;

		// Œ»İ‚Ì…•½•ûŒü‚Ì‘¬‚³
		float currentSpeed = sqrtf(currentVelocity.x * currentVelocity.x + currentVelocity.z * currentVelocity.z);

		// ‘¬‚³‚¾‚¯‚ğA1ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ ‚½‚èÅ‘å acceleration * elapsedTime ‚¸‚Â–Ú•W‚Ö‹ß‚Ã‚¯‚é
		float maxStep = acceleration * elapsedTime;
		float diff = targetSpeed - currentSpeed;
		float newSpeed = (fabsf(diff) <= maxStep)
			? targetSpeed
			: currentSpeed + (diff > 0.0f ? maxStep : -maxStep);

		// •ûŒü‚Í“ü—Í•ûŒü‚Ì‚Ü‚ÜA‘å‚«‚³‚¾‚¯·‚µ‘Ö‚¦‚é
		float scale = newSpeed / targetSpeed;
		result.velocity.x *= scale;
		result.velocity.z *= scale;

		return result;
	}

	float CalcFacingYaw(float worldDirX, float worldDirZ)
	{
		return atan2f(-worldDirX, -worldDirZ);
	}
	float RotateTowards(float currentYaw, float targetYaw, float turnSpeed, float elapsedTime)
	{
		float diff = targetYaw - currentYaw;
		while (diff > DirectX::XM_PI)  diff -= DirectX::XM_2PI;
		while (diff < -DirectX::XM_PI) diff += DirectX::XM_2PI;

		float maxStep = turnSpeed * elapsedTime;
		if (diff > maxStep)       diff = maxStep;
		else if (diff < -maxStep) diff = -maxStep;

		float result = currentYaw + diff;

		while (result > DirectX::XM_PI)  result -= DirectX::XM_2PI;
		while (result < -DirectX::XM_PI) result += DirectX::XM_2PI;

		return result;
	}

	float AngleDiff(float fromYaw, float toYaw)
	{
		float diff = toYaw - fromYaw;
		while (diff > DirectX::XM_PI)  diff -= DirectX::XM_2PI;
		while (diff < -DirectX::XM_PI) diff += DirectX::XM_2PI;
		return diff;
	}

	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 YawToForward(float yaw)
	{
		// CalcFacingYaw ‚ª atan2(-x, -z) ‚È‚Ì‚ÅA‚»‚Ì‹t
		return { -sinf(yaw), 0.0f, -cosf(yaw) };
	}

	float MoveTowards(float current, float target, float maxStep)
	{
		float diff = target - current;
		if (fabsf(diff) <= maxStep) return target;
		return current + (diff > 0.0f ? maxStep : -maxStep);
	}

	float Damper(float current, float target, float halfLife, float elapsedTime)
	{
		if (halfLife <= 0.0f) return target;
		// Œo‰ßŠÔ‚ª halfLife 1‚Â•ª‚È‚çA·‚Í 2^-1 = ”¼•ª‚É‚È‚é
		return target + (current - target) * exp2f(-elapsedTime / halfLife);
	}
} 
 
===== PlayerMoveSystem.cpp =====

#include "PlayerMoveSystem.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerIntentComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerMoveComponent.h"
#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StateMachineComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Systems/StateMachineSystem.h"

PlayerMoveSystem::PlayerMoveSystem()
{
	StateMachineSystem::RequireState<PlayerMoveComponent>("Move", "PlayerMoveSystem");
}

void PlayerMoveSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)elapsedTime;
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerIntentComponent, PlayerMoveComponent, StateMachineComponent, PlayerLocomotionComponent>();
	view.each([&](const PlayerIntentComponent& intent, const PlayerMoveComponent& move,
		const StateMachineComponent& stateMachine, PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco)
		{
			if (!stateMachine.IsIn("Move")) return;

			// ˆÓ}‚ğALocomotion ‚Ö‚Ìw¦‚É•Ï‚¦‚é
			LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;
			cmd.mode = LocomotionMode::Ground;
			cmd.moveDir = intent.moveDir;
			cmd.targetSpeed = intent.wantsRun ? move.runSpeed : move.walkSpeed;
			cmd.accelHalfLife = move.accelHalfLife;
			cmd.decelHalfLife = move.decelHalfLife;
			cmd.turnSpeed = move.turnSpeed;
		});
}

void PlayerSprintSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	(void)elapsedTime;
	(void)context;

	auto view = registry.view<PlayerIntentComponent, PlayerSprintComponent, StateMachineComponent, PlayerLocomotionComponent>();
	view.each([&](const PlayerIntentComponent& intent, const PlayerSprintComponent& sprint,
		const StateMachineComponent& stateMachine, PlayerLocomotionComponent& loco)
		{
			if (!stateMachine.IsIn("Sprint")) return;

			// ˆÓ}‚ğALocomotion ‚Ö‚Ìw¦‚É•Ï‚¦‚é
			LocomotionCommand& cmd = loco.command;
			cmd.mode = LocomotionMode::Ground;
			cmd.moveDir = intent.moveDir;
			cmd.targetSpeed = sprint.sprintSpeed;
			cmd.accelHalfLife = sprint.accelHalfLife;
			cmd.decelHalfLife = sprint.decelHalfLife;
			cmd.turnSpeed = sprint.turnSpeed;
		});
}

ï»¿#include "SceneGame.h"
#include <imgui.h>

void SceneGame::Initialize(const SceneContext& context)
{
	// å‰›ä½“ã‚³ãƒ³ãƒãƒ¼ãƒãƒ³ãƒˆã®ç ´æ£„æ™‚ã«ã€PhysXã®å®Ÿä½“ã‚’è‡ªå‹•ã§ç ´æ£„äºˆç´„ã™ã‚‹ã‚ˆã†ã«ã™ã‚‹
	PhysicsSystem::ConnectDestroyListeners(registry);
	// ç‰©ç†ã‚·ãƒ¼ãƒ³åˆæœŸåŒ–
	InitializePhysicsScene(*context.physX);

	// æç”»ã‚·ã‚¹ãƒ†ãƒ åˆæœŸåŒ–
	renderSystem.Initialize(context.device);

	// SkyBoxåˆæœŸåŒ–
	skyBox.Initialize(
		context.device,
		//"Data/SkyBox/BlueSky"
		"Data/SkyBox/DynamicSky"
		//"Data/SkyBox/GoldenGateHills"
	);
	// IBLãƒ†ã‚¯ã‚¹ãƒãƒ£ã‚»ãƒƒãƒˆ
	renderSystem.SetIBLTextures(
		skyBox.GetDiffuseIem(),
		skyBox.GetSRV(),
		skyBox.GetLutGgx()
	);

	lightManager.Initialize(context.device);
	lightManager.SetDirectionalLight(
		{ 0.5f, -1.0f, 0.5f },
		{ 1.0f,  1.0f, 1.0f },
		1.0f
	);
	physicsDebugRenderer.Initialize(context.device);


	const int lightCount = 20;
	const float radius = 5.0f;
	const float height = 1.0f;

	for (int i = 0; i < lightCount; i++)
	{
		float angle = (2.0f * 3.14159265f / lightCount) * i;
		float x = radius * cosf(angle);
		float z = radius * sinf(angle);

		float r = (sinf(angle) + 1.0f) * 0.5f;
		float g = (sinf(angle + 2.0f) + 1.0f) * 0.5f;
		float b = (sinf(angle + 4.0f) + 1.0f) * 0.5f;

		lightManager.AddPointLight(
			{ x, height, z }, // ä½ç½®
			{ r, g, b },      // è‰²
			5.0f,             // ç¯„å›²
			3.0f              // å¼·åº¦
		);
	}

	gameCameraSystem.Initialize(context.device);

#if WaveDemo && !defined(_DEBUG)
	// ãƒ‡ãƒ¢åˆæœŸåŒ–
	waveDemo.Initialize(context.device, registry, 500, 1.0f);
#endif
}

	// ç‰©ç†ã‚·ãƒ¼ãƒ³çµ‚äº†å‡¦ç†
	FinalizePhysicsScene();
}

void SceneGame::Update(float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	ZoneScoped;

	// F2ã§ç‰©ç†ãƒ‡ãƒãƒƒã‚°æç”»ã®è¡¨ç¤ºåˆ‡ã‚Šæ›¿ãˆ
	static bool prevF2 = false;
	bool currF2 = (GetAsyncKeyState(VK_F2) & 0x8000) != 0;
	if (currF2 && !prevF2)
	{
		DrawDebug = !DrawDebug;
	}
	prevF2 = currF2;

	// ãƒ‡ãƒ¢æ›´æ–°
	waveDemo.Update(elapsedTime);

	// PhysXæ›´æ–°
	{
		ZoneScopedN("UpdatePhysX");
		UpdatePhysicsScene(elapsedTime);
	}

	UpdateGameplay(elapsedTime, context);
	UpdateAnimation(elapsedTime, context);
	UpdateResources(elapsedTime, context);
	UpdateCamera(elapsedTime, context);
}

void SceneGame::Render(const SceneContext& context)
{
	ZoneScoped;

	{
		ZoneScopedN("CbScene Update");

		// CbSceneæ›´æ–°
		CbScene cbSceneData{};
		cbSceneData.view = context.view;
		cbSceneData.projection = context.projection;

		// ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©ä½ç½®(æç”»ã«ä½¿ã†ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©ã‹ã‚‰å–ã‚‹)
		if (context.renderCamera)
		{
			const auto& eye = context.renderCamera->GetEye();
			cbSceneData.cameraPosition = { eye.x, eye.y, eye.z, 0.0f };
		}
		lightManager.Bind(context.deviceContext);

		context.deviceContext->UpdateSubresource(
			renderSystem.GetCbScene(), 0, nullptr, &cbSceneData, 0, 0
		);

		// é ‚ç‚¹ã‚·ã‚§ãƒ¼ãƒ€ãƒ¼ã«è¨­å®š
		context.deviceContext->VSSetConstantBuffers(
			0, 1, renderSystem.GetCbSceneAddress()
		);

		// ãƒ”ã‚¯ã‚»ãƒ«ã‚·ã‚§ãƒ¼ãƒ€ãƒ¼ã«ã‚‚è¨­å®š
		context.deviceContext->PSSetConstantBuffers(
			0, 1, renderSystem.GetCbSceneAddress()
		);
	}

	// ãƒ•ãƒ©ã‚¹ã‚¿ãƒ è¨ˆç®—
	renderSystem.SetFrustum(context.view, context.projection);


	// ã‚¹ã‚«ã‚¤ãƒœãƒƒã‚¯ã‚¹
	{
		ZoneScopedN("SkyBox::Render");
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 eye = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
		if (context.renderCamera)
		{
			const auto& e = context.renderCamera->GetEye();
			eye = { e.x, e.y, e.z };
		}
		skyBox.Render(context.deviceContext, context.view, context.projection, eye);
	}

	// æç”»
	{
		ZoneScopedN("RenderSystem::Render");
		renderSystem.Render(
			registry,
			context.deviceContext,
			context.renderTargetView,
			context.depthStencilView,
			context.device,
			context.screenWidth,
			context.screenHeight
		);
	}

#if WaveDemo && !defined(_DEBUG)
	// ãƒ‡ãƒ¢æç”»
	{
		ZoneScopedN("WaveDemo Loop+Render");

		const auto& lightDir4 = lightManager.GetDirectionalLight().direction;
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 lightDir3 = { lightDir4.x, lightDir4.y, lightDir4.z };

		waveDemo.Render(
			registry,
			context.deviceContext,
			context.view,
			context.projection,
			lightDir3
		);
	}
#endif

	if (DrawDebug)
	{
		physicsDebugRenderer.Render(
			context.deviceContext,
			GetPhysicsScene(),
			GetControllerManager(),
			context.view,
			context.projection,
			lightManager.GetDirectionalLight().direction);

		DirectX::XMFLOAT4 lightDir4 = lightManager.GetDirectionalLight().direction;
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 lightDir3 = { lightDir4.x, lightDir4.y, lightDir4.z };
		gameCameraSystem.DrawDebug(
			registry,
			context.deviceContext,
			context.view,
			context.projection,
			lightDir3);
	}
}

// ã‚¢ãƒ‹ãƒ¡ãƒ¼ã‚·ãƒ§ãƒ³ç³»
void SceneGame::UpdateAnimation(float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{

#pragma once

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include "entt.hpp"

// Player ‚ÌˆÚ“®‚Ì—lq
class MovementMonitorWindow
{
public:
	// ’l‚ğ1‚Â‹L˜^‚·‚é
	void Record(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime);

	// ‹L˜^‚ğƒOƒ‰ƒt‚É•`‚­
	void Draw();

	// •\¦‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é‚©
	bool isOpen = false;

private:
	// 1‰ñ•ª‚Ì‹L˜^
	struct Sample
	{
		float time = 0.0f;            // ‹L˜^‚ğn‚ß‚Ä‚©‚ç‚ÌŠÔ
		float horizontalSpeed = 0.0f; // …•½‚Ì‘¬‚³
		float desiredSpeed = 0.0f;    // –Ú•W‚Ì‘¬‚³
		float verticalSpeed = 0.0f;   // c‚Ì‘¬‚³
		int   stateIndex = 0;         // ƒXƒe[ƒg‚Ì”Ô†
	};

	// ƒOƒ‰ƒt‚É•`‚­1–{‚Ìü
	struct Series
	{
		float Sample::* value = nullptr; // ‚Ç‚Ì’l‚ğ•`‚­‚©
		unsigned int    color = 0;
		float           thickness = 1.0f;
	};

	// ƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ğ1‚Â•`‚­
	void DrawGraph(float height, float minValue, float maxValue, float gridStep, const Series* series, int seriesCount);

	// ƒXƒe[ƒg–¼‚ğ”Ô†‚É•Ï‚¦‚é
	int FindOrAddState(const std::string& name);

	std::deque<Sample>       samples;    // ŒÃ‚¢‡
	std::vector<std::string> stateNames; // ƒXƒe[ƒg–¼

	float totalTime = 0.0f;   // ‹L˜^‚ğn‚ß‚Ä‚©‚ç‚ÌŠÔ
	bool  paused = false;     // ˆê’â~’†‚Í‹L˜^‚µ‚È‚¢

	static constexpr float kViewSeconds = 3.0f;  // •\¦‚·‚éŠÔ‚Ì•
	static constexpr float kKeepSeconds = 10.0f; // ‹L˜^‚Æ‚µ‚Äc‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­’·‚³
};

void GameInspectors::Register() 
{
	// ƒQ[ƒ€ƒJƒƒ‰
	REGISTER_INSPECTOR(GameCameraComponent,
		[](auto& reg, auto e)
		{
			auto* _c = reg.try_get<GameCameraComponent>(e);
			if (!_c) return;

			int mode = (int)_c->mode;

				_c->mode = (Mode)mode;

			// FollowTarget‚Í©“®‚Å’T‚·(Playerƒ^ƒO)

===== MovementMonitorWindow.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\GameTool\MovementMonitorWindow.cpp 
#include "MovementMonitorWindow.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

#include "Engine/ECS/EntityFinder.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/StateMachineComponent.h"

#include "Game/ECS/Components/PlayerLocomotionComponent.h"

namespace
{
	constexpr ImU32 kBackColor =       IM_COL32(25, 25, 30, 255);
	constexpr ImU32 kGridColor =       IM_COL32(60, 60, 70, 255);
	constexpr ImU32 kZeroColor =       IM_COL32(130, 130, 140, 255);
	constexpr ImU32 kLabelColor =      IM_COL32(150, 150, 160, 255);
	constexpr ImU32 kHorizontalColor = IM_COL32(66, 150, 250, 255);  // …•½‚Ì‘¬‚³(Â)
	constexpr ImU32 kDesiredColor =    IM_COL32(250, 200, 60, 220);  // –Ú•W‚Ì‘¬‚³(‰©)
	constexpr ImU32 kVerticalColor =   IM_COL32(120, 220, 140, 255); // c‚Ì‘¬‚³(—Î)

	constexpr float kBandHeight = 16.0f; // ƒXƒe[ƒg‚Ì‘Ñ‚Ì‚‚³

	// ƒXƒe[ƒg‚ÌF(‰‚ß‚Äo‚Ä‚«‚½‡‚Ég‚¤)
	constexpr ImU32 kStateColors[] =
	{
		IM_COL32(90, 110, 140, 255),
		IM_COL32(70, 140, 100, 255),
		IM_COL32(160, 110, 60, 255),
		IM_COL32(130, 80, 150, 255),
		IM_COL32(150, 70, 80, 255),
		IM_COL32(120, 120, 60, 255),
	};
	constexpr int kStateColorCount = sizeof(kStateColors) / sizeof(kStateColors[0]);
}

// ’l‚ğ1‚Â‹L˜^‚·‚é
void MovementMonitorWindow::Record(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime)
{
	if (paused) return;

	// ƒJƒeƒSƒŠ‚ª Player ‚ÌƒGƒ“ƒeƒBƒeƒB‚ğ‹L˜^‚·‚é
	entt::entity player = FindFirstByCategory(registry, EntityCategory::Player);
	if (player == entt::null) return;

	const auto* movement = registry.try_get<MovementComponent>(player);
	if (!movement) return;
	const auto* stateMachine = registry.try_get<StateMachineComponent>(player);
	const auto* locomotion = registry.try_get<PlayerLocomotionComponent>(player);

	totalTime += elapsedTime;

	Sample s;
	s.time = totalTime;
	s.horizontalSpeed = sqrtf(movement->velocity.x * movement->velocity.x + movement->velocity.z * movement->velocity.z);
	s.desiredSpeed = locomotion ? locomotion->desiredSpeed : 0.0f;
	s.verticalSpeed = movement->velocity.y;
	s.stateIndex = FindOrAddState(stateMachine ? stateMachine->currentStateName : "(no state)");
	samples.push_back(s);

	// ŒÃ‚¢‹L˜^‚ğÌ‚Ä‚é
	while (!samples.empty() && samples.front().time < totalTime - kKeepSeconds)
	{
		samples.pop_front();
	}
}

// ‹L˜^‚ğƒOƒ‰ƒt‚É•`‚­
void MovementMonitorWindow::Draw()
{
	if (!isOpen) return;

	if (!ImGui::Begin("Movement Monitor", &isOpen))
	{
		ImGui::End();
		return;
	}

	ImGui::Checkbox("Stop", &paused);

	if (samples.size() < 2)
	{
		ImGui::TextDisabled("No data");
		ImGui::End();
		return;
	}

	// •\¦”ÍˆÍ‚ÌÅ‘å’l‚É‡‚í‚¹‚ÄAc‚Ì–Ú·‚è‚ğŒˆ‚ß‚é
	const float startTime = samples.back().time - kViewSeconds;
	float maxHorizontal = 6.0f;
	float maxVertical = 10.0f;
	for (const Sample& s : samples)
	{
		if (s.time < startTime) continue;
		maxHorizontal = (std::max)(maxHorizontal, (std::max)(s.horizontalSpeed, s.desiredSpeed));
		maxVertical = (std::max)(maxVertical, fabsf(s.verticalSpeed));
	}

	ImGui::TextUnformatted("Horizontal (m/s)");
	const Series horizontal[] =
	{
		{ &Sample::desiredSpeed,    kDesiredColor,    1.5f },
		{ &Sample::horizontalSpeed, kHorizontalColor, 2.0f },
	};
	DrawGraph(180.0f, 0.0f, maxHorizontal * 1.1f, 1.0f, horizontal, 2);

	ImGui::TextUnformatted("Vertical (m/s)");
	const Series vertical[] =
	{
		{ &Sample::verticalSpeed, kVerticalColor, 2.0f },
	};
	DrawGraph(140.0f, -maxVertical * 1.1f, maxVertical * 1.1f, 5.0f, vertical, 1);

	ImGui::End();
}

// ƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ğ1‚Â•`‚­
void MovementMonitorWindow::DrawGraph(float height, float minValue, float maxValue, float gridStep, const Series* series, int seriesCount)
{
	ImDrawList* dl = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();

	const ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
	const float width = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;
	const float plotHeight = height - kBandHeight;

	const ImVec2 plotMin = pos;
	const ImVec2 plotMax = ImVec2(pos.x + width, pos.y + plotHeight);
	const ImVec2 bandMax = ImVec2(pos.x + width, pos.y + height);

	// ŠÔ ¨ XÀ•WA’l ¨ YÀ•W
	const float endTime = samples.back().time;
	const float startTime = endTime - kViewSeconds;
	auto ToX = [&](float t) { return plotMin.x + (t - startTime) / kViewSeconds * width; };
	auto ToY = [&](float v)
		{
			float rate = std::clamp((v - minValue) / (maxValue - minValue), 0.0f, 1.0f);
			return plotMax.y - rate * plotHeight;
		};

	dl->PushClipRect(plotMin, bandMax, true);

	// ”wŒi
	dl->AddRectFilled(plotMin, plotMax, kBackColor);

	// ‰¡‚Ì–Ú·‚è
	for (float v = ceilf(minValue / gridStep) * gridStep; v <= maxValue; v += gridStep)
	{
		float y = ToY(v);
		bool isZero = fabsf(v) < 0.001f;
		dl->AddLine(ImVec2(plotMin.x, y), ImVec2(plotMax.x, y), isZero ? kZeroColor : kGridColor);

		char label[16];
		snprintf(label, sizeof(label), "%.0f", v);
		dl->AddText(ImVec2(plotMin.x + 2.0f, y - 14.0f), kLabelColor, label);
	}

	// c‚Ì–Ú·‚è
	for (float t = ceilf(startTime); t <= endTime; t += 1.0f)
	{
		float x = ToX(t);
		dl->AddLine(ImVec2(x, plotMin.y), ImVec2(x, plotMax.y), kGridColor);
	}

	// ’l‚Ìü
	for (int k = 0; k < seriesCount; ++k)
	{
		const Series& s = series[k];
		for (size_t i = 1; i < samples.size(); ++i)
		{
			const Sample& a = samples[i - 1];
			const Sample& b = samples[i];
			if (b.time < startTime) continue;

			dl->AddLine(ImVec2(ToX(a.time), ToY(a.*(s.value))), ImVec2(ToX(b.time), ToY(b.*(s.value))), s.color, s.thickness);
		}
	}

	// ƒXƒe[ƒg‚Ì‘Ñ
	size_t runStart = 0;
	for (size_t i = 1; i <= samples.size(); ++i)
	{
		bool runEnds = (i == samples.size()) || (samples[i].stateIndex != samples[runStart].stateIndex);
		if (!runEnds) continue;

		const Sample& first = samples[runStart];
		const Sample& end = samples[i - 1];
		float x0 = ToX(first.time);
		float x1 = ToX(end.time);

		if (end.time >= startTime)
		{
			ImU32 color = kStateColors[first.stateIndex % kStateColorCount];
			dl->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x0, plotMax.y), ImVec2(x1, bandMax.y), color);

			// ‹æŠÔ‚Éû‚Ü‚é‚¾‚¯AƒXƒe[ƒg–¼‚ğ‘‚­
			const char* name = stateNames[first.stateIndex].c_str();
			float textWidth = ImGui::CalcTextSize(name).x;
			float textX = (std::max)(x0, plotMin.x) + 3.0f;
			if (textX + textWidth < x1)
			{
				dl->AddText(ImVec2(textX, plotMax.y + 1.0f), IM_COL32_WHITE, name);
			}
		}

		runStart = i;
	}

	dl->PopClipRect();

	// •`‚¢‚½•ª‚ÌêŠ‚ğŠm•Û‚·‚é
	ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(width, height));
}

// ƒXƒe[ƒg–¼‚ğ”Ô†‚É•Ï‚¦‚é
int MovementMonitorWindow::FindOrAddState(const std::string& name)
{
	for (int i = 0; i < (int)stateNames.size(); ++i)
	{
		if (stateNames[i] == name) return i;
	}
	stateNames.push_back(name);
	return (int)stateNames.size() - 1;
} 
 
===== Launcher.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Launcher\Launcher.cpp 
#include <windows.h>
#include <commctrl.h>
#include <string>

// ƒ^ƒXƒNƒ_ƒCƒAƒƒO(ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“•t‚«‚Ì‘I‘ğ‰æ–Ê)‚ğg‚¤‚½‚ß‚Ìİ’è
#pragma comment(lib, "comctl32.lib")
#pragma comment(linker, "\"/manifestdependency:type='win32' name='Microsoft.Windows.Common-Controls' version='6.0.0.0' processorArchitecture='*' publicKeyToken='6595b64144ccf1df' language='*'\"")

namespace
{
	// ‘I‘ğ‰æ–Ê‚Ìƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ÌID
	enum
	{
		kButtonEditor = 100,
		kButtonGame = 101,
	};

	// ‹N“®‚·‚éƒAƒvƒŠ‚ğ‘I‘ğ‰æ–Ê‚Å‘I‚Ô
	int ShowSelectDialog()
	{
		TASKDIALOG_BUTTON buttons[] =
		{
			{ kButtonEditor, L"Editor\nƒGƒfƒBƒ^•t‚«‚Å‹N“®‚·‚é" },
			{ kButtonGame,   L"Game\nƒQ[ƒ€’P‘Ì‚Å‹N“®‚·‚é" },
		};

		TASKDIALOGCONFIG config = {};
		config.cbSize = sizeof(config);
		config.dwFlags = TDF_USE_COMMAND_LINKS | TDF_ALLOW_DIALOG_CANCELLATION;
		config.pszWindowTitle = L"Launcher";
		config.pszMainInstruction = L"‹N“®‚·‚éƒAƒvƒŠ‚ğ‘I‚ñ‚Å‚­‚¾‚³‚¢";
		config.pButtons = buttons;
		config.cButtons = ARRAYSIZE(buttons);
		config.nDefaultButton = kButtonEditor;

		int selected = 0;
		if (FAILED(TaskDialogIndirect(&config, &selected, nullptr, nullptr))) return 0;

		return (selected == kButtonEditor || selected == kButtonGame) ? selected : 0;
	}

	// ƒ‰ƒ“ƒ`ƒƒ[‚Æ“¯‚¶ƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚É‚ ‚éƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ÌƒpƒX‚ğì‚é
	std::wstring GetSiblingPath(const wchar_t* fileName)
	{
		wchar_t path[MAX_PATH] = {};
		GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, path, MAX_PATH);

		std::wstring dir(path);
		dir = dir.substr(0, dir.find_last_of(L"\\/") + 1);
		return dir + fileName;
	}

	// •ÊƒvƒƒZƒX‚Æ‚µ‚Ä‹N“®‚·‚é
	bool Launch(const std::wstring& exePath)
	{
		STARTUPINFOW si = {};
		si.cb = sizeof(si);
		PROCESS_INFORMATION pi = {};

		std::wstring commandLine = L"\"" + exePath + L"\"";
		if (!CreateProcessW(exePath.c_str(), commandLine.data(),
			nullptr, nullptr, FALSE, 0, nullptr, nullptr, &si, &pi))
		{
			return false;
		}

		// ‹N“®‚µ‚½ƒvƒƒZƒX‚ÌI—¹‚Í‘Ò‚½‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅAƒnƒ“ƒhƒ‹‚Í‚·‚®•Â‚¶‚é
		CloseHandle(pi.hThread);
		CloseHandle(pi.hProcess);
		return true;
	}
}

int WINAPI wWinMain(_In_ HINSTANCE, _In_opt_ HINSTANCE, _In_ LPWSTR, _In_ int)
{
	int selected = ShowSelectDialog();
	if (selected == 0) return 0;

	const wchar_t* exeName = (selected == kButtonEditor) ? L"EditorApp.exe" : L"GameApp.exe";
	std::wstring exePath = GetSiblingPath(exeName);

	if (!Launch(exePath))
	{
		std::wstring message = L"‹N“®‚Å‚«‚Ü‚¹‚ñ‚Å‚µ‚½:\n" + exePath;
		MessageBoxW(nullptr, message.c_str(), L"3dgp", MB_OK | MB_ICONERROR);
		return 1;
	}
	return 0;
} 
 
===== AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.h 
#pragma once
#include <vector>
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/ControllerComponent.h"

class AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow
{
public:
    void Draw(ControllerComponent& ctrl);

private:
    // ƒ][ƒ“‚Ìó‘Ô
    struct ZoneState
    {
        bool  exists = false;
        float start = 0.0f;
        float end = 1.0f;
        std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint> localPoints;
        int   selectedIndex = -1;
    };

    struct RangeDragState
    {
        enum class Mode { None, Left, Right, Move };
        Mode mode = Mode::None;
        float startMouseX = 0.0f;
        float startZoneStart = 0.0f;
        float startZoneEnd = 0.0f;
    };

    void DrawTriggerSection(const char* label, AdaptiveTrigger::TriggerEffect& effect, ZoneState& zone, RangeDragState& rangeDrag, float currentValue);
    void DrawRangeBar(ZoneState& zone, float width, RangeDragState& rangeDrag, float currentValue);
    void DrawGraph(std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint>& points, int& selectedIndex, int& otherSelectedIndex, float localCurrentValue, bool showIndicator);
    void DrawSelectedPointPanel(std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint>& points, int& selectedIndex);

    ZoneState leftZone;
    ZoneState rightZone;
    RangeDragState leftRangeDrag;
    RangeDragState rightRangeDrag;
}; 
 
===== AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.cpp 
#include "AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

namespace
{
    constexpr ImU32 AccentColor = IM_COL32(66, 150, 250, 255);   // ImGui‚ç‚µ‚¢Â
    constexpr ImU32 IndicatorColor = IM_COL32(255, 70, 70, 255); // Œ»İ’n‚ÌÔü
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::Draw(ControllerComponent& ctrl)
{
    if (!ImGui::Begin("Adaptive Trigger Tool"))
    {
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }

    if (ImGui::BeginTable("##layout", 2, ImGuiTableFlags_None))
    {
        ImGui::TableSetupColumn("main", ImGuiTableColumnFlags_WidthStretch);
        ImGui::TableSetupColumn("panel", ImGuiTableColumnFlags_WidthFixed, 160.0f);
        ImGui::TableNextRow();

        ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(0);
        DrawTriggerSection("L2", ctrl.leftTriggerEffect, leftZone, leftRangeDrag, ctrl.leftTrigger);
        ImGui::Spacing();
        DrawTriggerSection("R2", ctrl.rightTriggerEffect, rightZone, rightRangeDrag, ctrl.rightTrigger);

        ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(1);
        if (leftZone.selectedIndex >= 0)
        {
            DrawSelectedPointPanel(leftZone.localPoints, leftZone.selectedIndex);
        }
        else if (rightZone.selectedIndex >= 0)
        {
            DrawSelectedPointPanel(rightZone.localPoints, rightZone.selectedIndex);
        }
        else
        {
            ImGui::TextDisabled(u8"ƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ğ‘I‘ğ‚µ‚Ä‚­‚¾‚³‚¢");
        }

        ImGui::EndTable();
    }

    ImGui::End();
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::DrawTriggerSection(const char* label, AdaptiveTrigger::TriggerEffect& effect, ZoneState& zone, RangeDragState& rangeDrag, float currentValue)
{
    ImGui::PushID(label);

    ImGui::Text(label);

    if (!zone.exists)
    {
        ImGui::SameLine(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x - 80.0f + ImGui::GetCursorPosX());
        if (ImGui::Button(u8"+ ƒ][ƒ“’Ç‰Á"))
        {
            zone.exists = true;
            zone.start = 0.0f;
            zone.end = 1.0f;
            zone.localPoints = { {0.0f, 0.5f}, {1.0f, 0.5f} };
        }
    }
    else
    {
        ImGui::SameLine(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x - 60.0f + ImGui::GetCursorPosX());
        if (ImGui::Button(u8"íœ"))
        {
            zone.exists = false;
            zone.selectedIndex = -1;
        }
    }

    float width = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;

    DrawRangeBar(zone, width, rangeDrag, currentValue);

    ImGui::Spacing();

    if (zone.exists)
    {
        bool withinZone = (currentValue >= zone.start && currentValue <= zone.end && zone.end > zone.start);
        float localCurrent = withinZone ? (currentValue - zone.start) / (zone.end - zone.start) : 0.0f;

        int dummyOther = -1;
        DrawGraph(zone.localPoints, zone.selectedIndex, dummyOther, localCurrent, withinZone);
    }

    auto& points = effect.forcePoints;
    points.clear();
    if (zone.exists)
    {
        for (const auto& lp : zone.localPoints)
        {
            float absPos = zone.start + lp.position * (zone.end - zone.start);
            points.push_back({ absPos, lp.value });
        }
    }

    ImGui::PopID();
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::DrawRangeBar(ZoneState& zone, float width, RangeDragState& rangeDrag, float currentValue)
{
    ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
    const float height = 24.0f;
    ImDrawList* drawList = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();

    drawList->AddRectFilled(pos, ImVec2(pos.x + width, pos.y + height), IM_COL32(60, 60, 60, 255), 4.0f);

    ImGui::InvisibleButton("##rangeBar", ImVec2(width, height));
    bool barClicked = ImGui::IsItemClicked();
    bool barActive = ImGui::IsItemActive();
    ImVec2 mousePos = ImGui::GetMousePos();

    if (zone.exists)
    {
        float x1 = pos.x + zone.start * width;
        float x2 = pos.x + zone.end * width;

        drawList->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x1, pos.y), ImVec2(x2, pos.y + height), AccentColor);

        const float handleHalf = 3.0f;
        drawList->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x1 - handleHalf, pos.y - 3.0f), ImVec2(x1 + handleHalf, pos.y + height + 3.0f), IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255));
        drawList->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x2 - handleHalf, pos.y - 3.0f), ImVec2(x2 + handleHalf, pos.y + height + 3.0f), IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255));

        const float pickRange = 8.0f;

        if (barClicked)
        {
            if (std::abs(mousePos.x - x1) <= pickRange)
            {
                rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::Left;
            }
            else if (std::abs(mousePos.x - x2) <= pickRange)
            {
                rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::Right;
            }
            else if (mousePos.x >= x1 && mousePos.x <= x2)
            {
                rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::Move;
                rangeDrag.startMouseX = mousePos.x;
                rangeDrag.startZoneStart = zone.start;
                rangeDrag.startZoneEnd = zone.end;
            }
            else
            {
                rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::None;
            }
        }

        if (barActive)
        {
            float mouseNormX = std::clamp((mousePos.x - pos.x) / width, 0.0f, 1.0f);

            if (rangeDrag.mode == RangeDragState::Mode::Left)
            {
                zone.start = std::clamp(mouseNormX, 0.0f, zone.end - 0.01f);
            }
            else if (rangeDrag.mode == RangeDragState::Mode::Right)
            {
                zone.end = std::clamp(mouseNormX, zone.start + 0.01f, 1.0f);
            }
            else if (rangeDrag.mode == RangeDragState::Mode::Move)
            {
                float rawDelta = (mousePos.x - rangeDrag.startMouseX) / width;
                float zoneWidth = rangeDrag.startZoneEnd - rangeDrag.startZoneStart;
                float clampedDelta = std::clamp(rawDelta, -rangeDrag.startZoneStart, 1.0f - rangeDrag.startZoneEnd);
                zone.start = rangeDrag.startZoneStart + clampedDelta;
                zone.end = rangeDrag.startZoneStart + clampedDelta + zoneWidth;
            }
        }

        if (!ImGui::IsMouseDown(ImGuiMouseButton_Left))
        {
            rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::None;
        }
    }

    // Œ»İ‚ÌƒgƒŠƒK[‰Ÿ‚µ‚İˆÊ’uiÔüj
    float curX = pos.x + std::clamp(currentValue, 0.0f, 1.0f) * width;
    drawList->AddLine(ImVec2(curX, pos.y - 4.0f), ImVec2(curX, pos.y + height + 4.0f), IndicatorColor, 2.0f);

    ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(width, height));

    ImGui::TextDisabled("0");
    ImGui::SameLine(width - 24.0f);
    ImGui::TextDisabled("255");
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::DrawGraph(std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint>& points, int& selectedIndex, int& otherSelectedIndex, float localCurrentValue, bool showIndicator)
{
    const float margin = 8.0f;

    ImVec2 graphSize(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x, 150.0f);
    ImVec2 graphPos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();

    ImGui::InvisibleButton("##graphBg", graphSize);
    bool bgClicked = ImGui::IsItemClicked();
    bool bgActive = ImGui::IsItemActive();
    ImVec2 mousePos = ImGui::GetMousePos();

    ImDrawList* drawList = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
    drawList->AddRect(graphPos, ImVec2(graphPos.x + graphSize.x, graphPos.y + graphSize.y), IM_COL32(150, 150, 150, 255));

    float innerX = graphPos.x + margin;
    float innerWidth = graphSize.x - margin * 2.0f;
    float innerY = graphPos.y + margin;
    float innerHeight = graphSize.y - margin * 2.0f;

    auto ToScreen = [&](const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& p) -> ImVec2
        {
            return ImVec2(innerX + p.position * innerWidth, innerY + (1.0f - p.value) * innerHeight);
        };
    auto FromScreen = [&](ImVec2 screen) -> AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint
        {
            AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint p;
            p.position = std::clamp((screen.x - innerX) / innerWidth, 0.0f, 1.0f);
            p.value = std::clamp(1.0f - (screen.y - innerY) / innerHeight, 0.0f, 1.0f);
            return p;
        };

    for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < points.size(); ++i)
    {
        drawList->AddLine(ToScreen(points[i]), ToScreen(points[i + 1]), AccentColor, 2.0f);
    }

    for (size_t i = 0; i < points.size(); ++i)
    {
        ImVec2 p = ToScreen(points[i]);
        bool isSelected = (static_cast<int>(i) == selectedIndex);
        float radius = isSelected ? 7.0f : 5.0f;
        drawList->AddCircleFilled(p, radius, AccentColor);
        if (isSelected)
        {
            drawList->AddCircle(p, radius + 2.0f, IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255), 0, 2.0f);
        }
    }

    // Œ»İ’n‚ÌÔüiƒ][ƒ““à‚É‚¢‚é‚¾‚¯•\¦j
    if (showIndicator)
    {
        float curX = innerX + std::clamp(localCurrentValue, 0.0f, 1.0f) * innerWidth;
        drawList->AddLine(ImVec2(curX, graphPos.y), ImVec2(curX, graphPos.y + graphSize.y), IndicatorColor, 2.0f);
    }

    const float pickRadius = 8.0f;
    const int lastIndex = static_cast<int>(points.size()) - 1;

    if (bgClicked)
    {
        int picked = -1;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < points.size(); ++i)
        {
            ImVec2 p = ToScreen(points[i]);
            float dx = mousePos.x - p.x;
            float dy = mousePos.y - p.y;
            if ((dx * dx + dy * dy) <= (pickRadius * pickRadius))
            {
                picked = static_cast<int>(i);
                break;
            }
        }

        if (picked == -1)
        {
            AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint newPoint = FromScreen(mousePos);

            // ––”ö‚Ì‹«ŠE“_(100%)‚Ìè‘O‚É‘}“ü‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çA’†ŠÔ•”•ª‚¾‚¯•À‚Ñ‘Ö‚¦‚é
            points.insert(points.end() - 1, newPoint);
            if (points.size() > 2)
            {
                std::sort(points.begin() + 1, points.end() - 1,
                    [](const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& a, const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& b)
                    {
                        return a.position < b.position;
                    });
            }

            // ‘}“ü‚µ‚½ƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ÌV‚µ‚¢index‚ğ’T‚·
            for (size_t i = 0; i < points.size(); ++i)
            {
                if (points[i].position == newPoint.position && points[i].value == newPoint.value)
                {
                    picked = static_cast<int>(i);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        selectedIndex = picked;
        otherSelectedIndex = -1;
    }

    if (bgActive && selectedIndex >= 0 && selectedIndex < static_cast<int>(points.size()))
    {
        // æ“ªE––”öiƒ][ƒ“‚Ì‹«ŠEj‚ÍXŒÅ’èAY‚¾‚¯“®‚©‚·
        if (selectedIndex == 0)
        {
            points[selectedIndex].value = FromScreen(mousePos).value;
            points[selectedIndex].position = 0.0f;
        }
        else if (selectedIndex == lastIndex)
        {
            points[selectedIndex].value = FromScreen(mousePos).value;
            points[selectedIndex].position = 1.0f;
        }
        else
        {
            points[selectedIndex] = FromScreen(mousePos);
        }
    }

    if (!ImGui::IsMouseDown(ImGuiMouseButton_Left) &&
        selectedIndex > 0 && selectedIndex < lastIndex) // ‹«ŠE“_‚Í•À‚Ñ‘Ö‚¦•s—v
    {
        AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint selected = points[selectedIndex];
        std::sort(points.begin() + 1, points.end() - 1,
            [](const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& a, const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& b)
            {
                return a.position < b.position;
            });
        for (size_t i = 1; i + 1 < points.size(); ++i)
        {
            if (points[i].position == selected.position && points[i].value == selected.value)
            {
                selectedIndex = static_cast<int>(i);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::DrawSelectedPointPanel(std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint>& points, int& selectedIndex)
{
    if (selectedIndex < 0 || selectedIndex >= static_cast<int>(points.size()))
    {
        ImGui::TextDisabled(u8"ƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ğ‘I‘ğ‚µ‚Ä‚­‚¾‚³‚¢");
        return;
    }

    const int lastIndex = static_cast<int>(points.size()) - 1;
    const bool isBoundary = (selectedIndex == 0 || selectedIndex == lastIndex);

    ImGui::PushID(selectedIndex);
    ImGui::TextDisabled(u8"‘I‘ğ’†‚Ìƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg");
    ImGui::Spacing();

    AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& p = points[selectedIndex];

    ImGui::Text(u8"X (ƒ][ƒ““àˆÊ’u)");
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1);
    if (isBoundary)
    {
        ImGui::BeginDisabled();
        float fixedX = p.position;
        ImGui::DragFloat("##posX", &fixedX, 0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f, "%.3f");
        ImGui::EndDisabled();
    }
    else
    {
        ImGui::DragFloat("##posX", &p.position, 0.005f, 0.0f, 1.0f, "%.3f");
    }

    ImGui::Spacing();

    ImGui::Text(u8"Y (‹­‚³)");
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1);
    ImGui::DragFloat("##posY", &p.value, 0.005f, 0.0f, 1.0f, "%.3f");

    ImGui::Spacing();

    if (isBoundary)
    {
        ImGui::BeginDisabled();
        ImGui::Button(u8"íœi‹«ŠE‚Ì‚½‚ß•s‰Âj", ImVec2(-1, 0));
        ImGui::EndDisabled();
    }
    else if (ImGui::Button(u8"íœ", ImVec2(-1, 0)))
    {
        points.erase(points.begin() + selectedIndex);
        selectedIndex = -1;
        ImGui::PopID();
        return;
    }

    ImGui::PopID();
}

===== EditorContext.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Editor\EditorContext.h 
#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include "entt.hpp"

// ƒJƒƒ‰ƒ‚[ƒh
enum class CameraMode
{
    Debug,  // ƒfƒoƒbƒOƒJƒƒ‰iƒ}ƒEƒXEƒL[ƒ{[ƒhj
    Game,   // ƒQ[ƒ€ƒJƒƒ‰  iƒRƒ“ƒgƒ[ƒ‰[j
};

// StudioƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚ğŠJ‚­ƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg
struct StudioOpenRequest
{
    bool        pending = false;
    std::string assetPath;
    std::string graphPath;
};

// ƒV[ƒ“‚Ì•Û‘¶E“Ç‚İ‚İ‚ÌƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg
enum class SceneAction
{
    None,
    Save,   // ¡‚ÌƒV[ƒ“‚Éã‘‚«
    SaveAs, // –¼‘O‚ğ•t‚¯‚Ä•Û‘¶
    Open,   // “Ç‚İ‚İ
};

struct EditorContext
{
    entt::entity selectedEntity = entt::null;
    std::unordered_map<entt::entity, std::string> entityNames;

    // ƒJƒƒ‰ƒ‚[ƒh
    CameraMode cameraMode = CameraMode::Debug;
    // ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ğ‰Ÿ‚µ‚½‚©
    bool rightClickPressed = false;  

    // StudioƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEŠJ‚­ƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg
    StudioOpenRequest studioRequest;

    std::string GetName(entt::entity e) const
    {
        auto it = entityNames.find(e);
        if (it != entityNames.end()) return it->second;
        return "Entity " + std::to_string((uint32_t)e);
    }

    void SetName(entt::entity e, const std::string& name)
    {
        entityNames[e] = name;
    }

    void Select(entt::entity e) { selectedEntity = e; }
    void Deselect() { selectedEntity = entt::null; }

    bool IsSelected(entt::entity e) const
    {
        return selectedEntity == e;
    }

    // ƒƒjƒ…[‚ª—§‚Ä‚éƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg
    SceneAction sceneRequest = SceneAction::None;
    // ¡ŠJ‚¢‚Ä‚¢‚éƒV[ƒ“‚Ìƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹
    std::string currentScenePath;
};

===== ImGuiManager.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Editor\ImGuiManager.h 
#pragma once
#include <windows.h>
#include <d3d11.h>

#include <imgui.h>
#include <imgui_internal.h>
#include <imgui_impl_dx11.h>
#include <imgui_impl_win32.h>

extern LRESULT ImGui_ImplWin32_WndProcHandler(HWND hWnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wParam, LPARAM lParam);
extern ImWchar glyphRangesJapanese[];


class ImGuiManager
{
public:
	ImGuiManager() = default;
	~ImGuiManager();

	ImGuiManager(const ImGuiManager&) = delete;				   // ƒRƒs[ƒRƒ“ƒXƒgƒ‰ƒNƒ^‹Ö~

	ImGuiManager& operator=(ImGuiManager&&) noexcept = delete; // ƒ€[ƒu‘ã“ü‹Ö~

    // ‰Šú‰»

    // ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ŠJn
