===== ComponentInspectorRegistry.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Editor\ComponentInspectorRegistry.cpp 
#include "ComponentInspectorRegistry.h"

std::unordered_map<std::string, std::function<void(entt::registry&, entt::entity)>> ComponentInspectorRegistry::registry;

void ComponentInspectorRegistry::Register(const std::string& name, std::function<void(entt::registry&, entt::entity)> drawImGui)
{
	registry[name] = std::move(drawImGui);
}

const std::function<void(entt::registry&, entt::entity)>* ComponentInspectorRegistry::Get(const std::string& name)
{
	auto it = registry.find(name);
	if (it == registry.end()) return nullptr;
	return &it->second;
} 
 
===== ComponentRegistryInitializer.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Editor\ComponentRegistryInitializer.cpp 
#include "ComponentRegistryInitializer.h"
#include "Tool/Editor/ComponentInspectorRegistry.h"

#include <imgui.h>
#include <Windows.h>
#include <commdlg.h>
#include <filesystem>
#include <algorithm>

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/ControllerComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/AnimatorComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/SkinnedMeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/FreeCameraComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/PhysicsComponents.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/IKComponent.h"

#include "MetaRegistry.h"

#define DRAW_COMPONENT(Type, Label, body)                                   \
    [](auto& reg, auto e)                                                   \
    {                                                                       \
        auto* _c = reg.try_get<Type>(e);                                    \
        if (!_c) return;                                                    \
        if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader(Label, ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) \
        { body }                                                            \
    }

void ComponentRegistryInitializer::Initialize()
{
	// ƒAƒjƒ[ƒ^[
	REGISTER_INSPECTOR(AnimatorComponent,
		[](auto& reg, auto e)
		{
			auto* anim = reg.try_get<AnimatorComponent>(e);
			auto* skinned = reg.try_get<SkinnedMeshComponent>(e);
			if (!anim) return;

			if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Animator", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) return;

			if (!skinned || !skinned->model)
			{
				ImGui::TextColored({ 1, 0.5f, 0, 1 }, "No SkinnedMesh found.");
				return;
			}
			const auto& animations = skinned->model->GetAnimations();
			if (animations.empty())
			{
				ImGui::TextColored({ 1, 0.5f, 0, 1 }, "No animations found.");
				return;
			}

			if (ImGui::BeginCombo("Animation", anim->currentAnimation.c_str()))
			{
				for (const auto& animation : animations)
				{
					bool selected = (anim->currentAnimation == animation.name);
					if (ImGui::Selectable(animation.name.c_str(), selected))
					{
						anim->currentAnimation = animation.name;
						anim->animationTime = 0.0f;
						anim->isPlaying = true;
						anim->isEnd = false;
					}
					if (selected) ImGui::SetItemDefaultFocus();
				}
				ImGui::EndCombo();
			}

			if (anim->isPlaying)
			{
				if (ImGui::Button("Stop")) anim->isPlaying = false;
			}
			else
			{
				if (ImGui::Button("Play")) { anim->isPlaying = true; anim->isEnd = false; }
			}
			MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::resolve<AnimatorComponent>(), entt::forward_as_meta(*anim));
		});

	// ƒtƒŠ[ƒJƒƒ‰
	REGISTER_INSPECTOR(FreeCameraComponent,
		DRAW_COMPONENT(FreeCameraComponent, "FreeCamera",
			{
				ImGui::DragFloat("Camera Speed", &_c->cameraSpeed, 0.1f, 0.1f, 100.0f);
				ImGui::DragFloat("Yaw",          &_c->yaw,         0.01f);
				ImGui::DragFloat("Pitch",        &_c->pitch,       0.01f, -1.5f, 1.5f);
				ImGui::Separator();
				ImGui::DragFloat3("Eye",    &_c->base.currentEye.x,    0.1f);
				ImGui::DragFloat3("Target", &_c->base.currentTarget.x, 0.1f);
			}));

	// ƒRƒ“ƒgƒ[ƒ‰[
	REGISTER_INSPECTOR(ControllerComponent,
		DRAW_COMPONENT(ControllerComponent, "Controller",
			{
				if (_c->connected)
				{
					const char* typeName = _c->type == ControllerType::PS5 ? "PS5 DualSense" : "Xbox";
					ImGui::TextColored({ 0.3f, 1.0f, 0.3f, 1.0f }, u8"[%s] Ú‘±’†", typeName);
				}
				else
				{
					ImGui::TextColored({ 0.6f, 0.6f, 0.6f, 1.0f }, u8"–¢Ú‘±");
				}
			}));

	// •¨—(Às‚Ì“o˜^‚ÍEngineComponents‚Ås‚¤B‚±‚±‚Í•`‰æ‚Ì‚İ)
	REGISTER_INSPECTOR(PhysicsBodyTypeComponent,
		[](auto& reg, auto e)
		{
			auto* body = reg.try_get<PhysicsBodyTypeComponent>(e);
			if (!body) return;
			if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Physics", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) return;

			// type‚ğ‘‚«Š·‚¦‚é‚¾‚¯B„‘Ì‚Ì¶¬E”jŠü‚ÍAPhysicsSystem‚ª’Ç]‚µ‚Äs‚¤
			ImGui::PushID("fields");
			MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::resolve<PhysicsBodyTypeComponent>(), entt::forward_as_meta(*body));
			ImGui::PopID();

			switch (body->type)
			{
			case PhysicsBodyType::Static:
			{
				auto* staticBody = reg.try_get<StaticBodyComponent>(e);
				if (staticBody)
				{
					ImGui::Text(staticBody->actor ? u8"ó‘Ô: ¶¬Ï‚İ" : u8"ó‘Ô: –¢¶¬");
				}
				break;
			}
			case PhysicsBodyType::Dynamic:
				ImGui::TextColored({ 1, 0.5f, 0, 1 }, u8"Dynamic‚Í–¢À‘•‚Å‚·");
				break;
			case PhysicsBodyType::Character:
			{
				auto* ctrl = reg.try_get<CharacterControllerComponent>(e);
				if (ctrl)
				{
					bool created = (ctrl->controller != nullptr);
					ImGui::Text(created ? u8"ó‘Ô: ¶¬Ï‚İ" : u8"ó‘Ô: –¢¶¬");
				}

				// ˆÚ“®‚Ìó‘ÔiCharacter‚Æˆê‚É•t—^‚³‚ê‚éMovementComponentj
				auto* movement = reg.try_get<MovementComponent>(e);
				if (movement && ImGui::TreeNode("Movement"))
				{
					MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::resolve<MovementComponent>(), entt::forward_as_meta(*movement));
					ImGui::TreePop();
				}
				break;
			}

			default:
				break;
			}
		});

	// IK
	REGISTER_INSPECTOR(IKComponent,
		[](auto& reg, auto e)
		{
			auto* ik = reg.try_get<IKComponent>(e);
			auto* skinned = reg.try_get<SkinnedMeshComponent>(e);
			if (!ik) return;
			if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("IK", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) return;
			if (!skinned || !skinned->model)
			{
				ImGui::TextColored({ 1, 0.5f, 0, 1 }, "No SkinnedMesh found.");
				return;
			}
			ImGui::Checkbox("Enabled", &ik->enabled);
			int removeIndex = -1; // ‚±‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Åíœ‚µ‚½‚¢foot‚ÌƒCƒ“ƒfƒbƒNƒX(-1‚È‚çíœ‚È‚µ)
			for (size_t i = 0; i < ik->feet.size(); ++i)
			{
				ImGui::PushID((int)i);
				std::string label = "Foot " + std::to_string(i);
				if (ImGui::TreeNode(label.c_str()))
				{
					FootIKSettings& foot = ik->feet[i];
					auto& nodes = skinned->model->GetNodes();
					auto drawBoneCombo = [&](const char* comboLabel, int& boneIndex)
						{
							const char* previewName = (boneIndex >= 0 && boneIndex < (int)nodes.size())
								? nodes[boneIndex].name.c_str()
								: "(Invalid Index)";

							if (ImGui::BeginCombo(comboLabel, previewName))
							{
								for (int n = 0; n < (int)nodes.size(); ++n)
								{
									bool isSelected = (boneIndex == n);
									if (ImGui::Selectable(nodes[n].name.c_str(), isSelected))
									{
										boneIndex = n;
									}
									if (isSelected)
									{
										ImGui::SetItemDefaultFocus();
									}
								}
								ImGui::EndCombo();
							}
						};
					drawBoneCombo("Root Bone", foot.rootBoneIndex);
					drawBoneCombo("Mid Bone", foot.midBoneIndex);
					drawBoneCombo("End Bone", foot.endBoneIndex);
					ImGui::DragFloat("Ray Start Offset", &foot.rayStartOffset, 0.01f);
					ImGui::DragFloat("Ray Max Distance", &foot.rayMaxDistance, 0.01f);
					ImGui::DragFloat("Ankle Height", &foot.ankleHeight, 0.01f);
					ImGui::Checkbox("Pole Vector Forward (Z-)", &foot.poleVectorForward);
					ImGui::DragFloat3("Target Pos", &foot.targetPos.x, 0.01f);
					if (ImGui::Button("Reset Target Pos"))
					{
						foot.hasInitializedTarget = false;
					}
					if (ImGui::Button("Remove This Foot"))
					{
						removeIndex = (int)i;
					}
					ImGui::TreePop();
				}
				ImGui::PopID();
			}
			if (removeIndex >= 0)
			{
				ik->feet.erase(ik->feet.begin() + removeIndex);
			}
			if (ImGui::Button("Add Foot"))
			{
				ik->feet.push_back(FootIKSettings{});
			}
		});
} 
 
===== DebugCameraSystem.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Editor\DebugCameraSystem.cpp 
#include "DebugCameraSystem.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

void DebugCameraSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry, float elapsedTime, bool viewportHovered)
{
    auto view = registry.view<FreeCameraComponent>();
    view.each([&](auto entity, FreeCameraComponent& free)
        {
            UpdateFreeCamera(free, elapsedTime, viewportHovered);
        });
}

void DebugCameraSystem::ApplyToCamera(entt::registry& registry, Camera& camera)
{
    auto view = registry.view<FreeCameraComponent>();
    for (auto entity : view)
    {
        auto& free = view.get<FreeCameraComponent>(entity);
        camera.SetLookAt(
            free.base.currentEye,
            free.base.currentTarget,
            { 0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f }
        );
        // Å‰‚Ì1‚Â‚¾‚¯”½‰f
        break;
    }
}

void DebugCameraSystem::UpdateFreeCamera(FreeCameraComponent& free, float elapsedTime, bool viewportHovered)
{
    // Gameƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒgã‚Éƒ}ƒEƒX‚ª–³‚¢‚Íˆ—‚µ‚È‚¢
    if (!viewportHovered) return;

    // ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN’†‚¾‚¯‘€ì‚ğó‚¯•t‚¯‚é
    if (!(GetAsyncKeyState(VK_RBUTTON) & 0x8000))
    {
        free.firstMouse = true;
        return;
    }

    // ƒzƒC[ƒ‹‚Å‘¬“x•ÏX
    float wheel = ImGui::GetIO().MouseWheel;
    if (wheel != 0.0f)
    {
        free.cameraSpeed += wheel * 2.0f;
        free.cameraSpeed = std::clamp(free.cameraSpeed, 0.1f, 100.0f);
    }

    float move = free.cameraSpeed * elapsedTime;

    // currentEye / currentTarget ‚©‚ç‘OŒã¶‰E‚ğŒvZ
    DirectX::XMVECTOR eyeV = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&free.base.currentEye);
    DirectX::XMVECTOR targetV = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&free.base.currentTarget);
    DirectX::XMVECTOR frontV = DirectX::XMVector3Normalize(
        DirectX::XMVectorSubtract(targetV, eyeV));
    DirectX::XMVECTOR upV = DirectX::XMVectorSet(0, 1, 0, 0);
    DirectX::XMVECTOR rightV = DirectX::XMVector3Normalize(
        DirectX::XMVector3Cross(frontV, upV));

    DirectX::XMFLOAT3 front, right;
    DirectX::XMStoreFloat3(&front, frontV);
    DirectX::XMStoreFloat3(&right, rightV);

    DirectX::XMFLOAT3 eye = free.base.currentEye;
    DirectX::XMFLOAT3 target = free.base.currentTarget;

    // WASD ‘OŒã¶‰EˆÚ“®
    if (GetAsyncKeyState('W') & 0x8000)
    {
        eye.x += front.x * move; eye.y += front.y * move; eye.z += front.z * move;
        target.x += front.x * move; target.y += front.y * move; target.z += front.z * move;
    }
    if (GetAsyncKeyState('S') & 0x8000)
    {
        eye.x -= front.x * move; eye.y -= front.y * move; eye.z -= front.z * move;
        target.x -= front.x * move; target.y -= front.y * move; target.z -= front.z * move;
    }
    if (GetAsyncKeyState('D') & 0x8000)
    {
        eye.x -= right.x * move; eye.y -= right.y * move; eye.z -= right.z * move;
        target.x -= right.x * move; target.y -= right.y * move; target.z -= right.z * move;
    }
    if (GetAsyncKeyState('A') & 0x8000)
    {
        eye.x += right.x * move; eye.y += right.y * move; eye.z += right.z * move;
        target.x += right.x * move; target.y += right.y * move; target.z += right.z * move;
    }

    // QE ã‰ºˆÚ“®
    if (GetAsyncKeyState('E') & 0x8000) { eye.y += move; target.y += move; }
    if (GetAsyncKeyState('Q') & 0x8000) { eye.y -= move; target.y -= move; }

    // ƒ}ƒEƒX‚Å‹“_‰ñ“]
    POINT mousePos;
    GetCursorPos(&mousePos);

    if (free.firstMouse)
    {
        free.lastMousePos = mousePos;
        free.firstMouse = false;

        // Œ»İ‚ÌŒü‚«‚©‚ç yaw / pitch ‚ğ•œŒ³
        DirectX::XMFLOAT3 dir =
        {
            target.x - eye.x,
            target.y - eye.y,
            target.z - eye.z
        };
        float len = sqrtf(dir.x * dir.x + dir.y * dir.y + dir.z * dir.z);
        if (len > 0.0f)
        {
            dir.x /= len; dir.y /= len; dir.z /= len;
        }
        free.pitch = asinf(dir.y);
        free.yaw = atan2f(dir.x, dir.z);
    }

    float dx = static_cast<float>(mousePos.x - free.lastMousePos.x) * 0.005f;
    float dy = static_cast<float>(mousePos.y - free.lastMousePos.y) * 0.005f;

    free.yaw += dx;
    free.pitch -= dy;
    free.pitch = std::clamp(free.pitch,
        -DirectX::XM_PIDIV2 + 0.1f,
        DirectX::XM_PIDIV2 - 0.1f);

    free.lastMousePos = mousePos;

    // ‘O•ûŒüƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚©‚ç target ‚ğXV
    DirectX::XMFLOAT3 direction;
    direction.x = cosf(free.pitch) * sinf(free.yaw);
    direction.y = sinf(free.pitch);
    direction.z = cosf(free.pitch) * cosf(free.yaw);

    target.x = eye.x + direction.x;
    target.y = eye.y + direction.y;
    target.z = eye.z + direction.z;

    free.base.currentEye = eye;
    free.base.currentTarget = target;
} 
 
===== FileDialog.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Editor\FileDialog.cpp 
#include "FileDialog.h"
#include <commdlg.h>
#include <filesystem>

#pragma comment(lib, "comdlg32.lib")

namespace
{
	bool EndsWith(const std::string& text, const std::string& suffix)
	{
		return text.size() >= suffix.size()
			&& text.compare(text.size() - suffix.size(), suffix.size(), suffix) == 0;
	}

	// Šg’£q‚ğu.scene.jsonv‚É‘µ‚¦‚é
	std::string EnsureSceneExtension(std::string path)
	{
		const std::string sceneExtension = ".scene.json";
		if (EndsWith(path, sceneExtension)) return path;
		if (EndsWith(path, ".json")) path.erase(path.size() - 5);
		return path + sceneExtension;
	}
}

namespace FileDialog
{
	bool SaveScene(HWND owner, std::string& path)
	{
		// •Û‘¶æ‚Ì‰ŠúƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_i–³‚¯‚ê‚Îì‚éjBƒ_ƒCƒAƒƒO‚É‚Íâ‘ÎƒpƒX‚Å“n‚·
		std::error_code ec;
		std::filesystem::create_directories("Data/Scene", ec);
		std::string initialDir = std::filesystem::absolute("Data/Scene").string();

		char fileName[MAX_PATH] = {};
		strncpy_s(fileName, path.c_str(), _TRUNCATE);

		OPENFILENAMEA ofn = {};
		ofn.lStructSize = sizeof(ofn);
		ofn.hwndOwner = owner;
		ofn.lpstrFilter = "Scene (*.scene.json)\0*.scene.json\0";
		ofn.lpstrFile = fileName;
		ofn.nMaxFile = MAX_PATH;
		ofn.lpstrInitialDir = initialDir.c_str();
		ofn.lpstrTitle = "Save Scene";
		// OFN_NOCHANGEDIRFƒ_ƒCƒAƒƒO‚ªAƒJƒŒƒ“ƒgƒfƒBƒŒƒNƒgƒŠ‚ğ•Ï‚¦‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚éi‘Š‘ÎƒpƒX‚ğg‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚½‚ßj
		ofn.Flags = OFN_NOCHANGEDIR | OFN_PATHMUSTEXIST;

		if (!GetSaveFileNameA(&ofn)) return false;

		std::string selected = EnsureSceneExtension(fileName);

		// Šg’£q‚ğ•â‚Á‚½Œã‚Ìƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ªŠù‚É‚ ‚ê‚ÎAã‘‚«‚ÌŠm”F‚ğo‚·
		// iƒ_ƒCƒAƒƒO•W€‚ÌŠm”F‚ÍA•â‚¤‘O‚Ì–¼‘O‚Å”»’è‚³‚ê‚é‚½‚ßAg‚í‚È‚¢j
		if (std::filesystem::exists(selected))
		{
			std::string message = "'" + std::filesystem::path(selected).filename().string()
				+ "' already exists.\nDo you want to replace it?";
			if (MessageBoxA(owner, message.c_str(), "Save Scene", MB_YESNO | MB_ICONWARNING) != IDYES)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		path = selected;
		return true;
	}
	bool OpenScene(HWND owner, std::string& path)
	{
		std::error_code ec;
		std::filesystem::create_directories("Data/Scene", ec);
		std::string initialDir = std::filesystem::absolute("Data/Scene").string();

		char fileName[MAX_PATH] = {};

		OPENFILENAMEA ofn = {};
		ofn.lStructSize = sizeof(ofn);
		ofn.hwndOwner = owner;
		ofn.lpstrFilter = "Scene (*.scene.json)\0*.scene.json\0";
		ofn.lpstrFile = fileName;
		ofn.nMaxFile = MAX_PATH;
		ofn.lpstrInitialDir = initialDir.c_str();
		ofn.lpstrTitle = "Open Scene";
		ofn.Flags = OFN_NOCHANGEDIR | OFN_PATHMUSTEXIST | OFN_FILEMUSTEXIST;

		if (!GetOpenFileNameA(&ofn)) return false;

		path = fileName;
		return true;
	}
} 
 
===== HierarchyWindow.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Editor\HierarchyWindow.cpp 
#include "HierarchyWindow.h"
#include "imgui_internal.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MaterialComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/CategoryComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/PrefabInstanceComponent.h"

#include "Engine/Resource/EntitySerializer.h"

#include "tracy/Tracy.hpp"

void HierarchyWindow::Draw(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
    ZoneScoped;

    ImGui::Begin("Hierarchy");

    // ‹ó‚Ì Entity ‚ğ’Ç‰Á‚·‚éƒ{ƒ^ƒ“
    if (ImGui::Button("+ Add Entity"))
    {
        entt::entity e = registry.create();

        registry.emplace<ComponentCategory>(e);
        registry.emplace<TransformComponent>(e);

        // –¼‘O‚ğ©“®İ’è
        context.SetName(e, "Entity");

        // ì¬‚µ‚½ Entity ‚ğ‘I‘ğó‘Ô‚É
        context.Select(e);
    }
    // ‘I‘ğ’†‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚¾‚¯ Delete ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ğ—×‚É•\¦
    if (context.selectedEntity != entt::null)
    {
        ImGui::SameLine();
        if (ImGui::Button("Delete"))
        {
            registry.destroy(context.selectedEntity);
            context.Deselect();
        }
    }

    ImGui::Separator();
    // ƒZƒpƒŒ[ƒ^‚Ì‰º‚ÌÀ•W‚ğ‹L˜^
    ImVec2 dropAreaMin = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();

    // ‘S Entity ‚ğ—ñ‹“(‰æ–Ê‚É‰f‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é”ÍˆÍ‚¾‚¯ˆ—‚·‚é)
    auto view = registry.view<entt::entity>();

    // entt::view ‚Íƒ‰ƒ“ƒ_ƒ€ƒAƒNƒZƒX‚Å‚«‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅAˆê’U”z—ñ‚ÉW‚ß‚é
    // (‚±‚ê©‘Ì‚ÍŒy‚¢ˆ—:ƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒ^‘Š“–‚Ìentity’l‚ğƒRƒs[‚·‚é‚¾‚¯)
    std::vector<entt::entity> entities(view.begin(), view.end());

    ImGuiListClipper clipper;
    clipper.Begin(static_cast<int>(entities.size()));
    while (clipper.Step())
    {
        for (int i = clipper.DisplayStart; i < clipper.DisplayEnd; i++)
        {
            entt::entity e = entities[i];

            std::string name = context.GetName(e);

            // ƒvƒŒƒnƒu‚ÌƒCƒ“ƒXƒ^ƒ“ƒX‚ÍA–¼‘O‚ÌF‚ğ•Ï‚¦‚ÄŒ©•ª‚¯‚ç‚ê‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
            bool isPrefabInstance = registry.any_of<PrefabInstanceComponent>(e);
            if (isPrefabInstance)
            {
                ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_Text, ImVec4(0.4f, 0.7f, 1.0f, 1.0f)); // ”–‚¢Â
            }

            // ‘I‘ğó‘Ô‚ğƒnƒCƒ‰ƒCƒg
            bool isSelected = context.IsSelected(e);
            if (ImGui::Selectable(
                (name + "##" + std::to_string((uint32_t)e)).c_str(),
                isSelected))
            {
                context.Select(e);
            }

            if (isPrefabInstance)
            {
                ImGui::PopStyleColor();
            }

            // Entity ‚ğƒhƒ‰ƒbƒO‚Å‚«‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
            if (ImGui::BeginDragDropSource(ImGuiDragDropFlags_SourceAllowNullID))
            {
                // Entity ID ‚ğƒyƒCƒ[ƒh‚Æ‚µ‚Ä“n‚·
                ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
                    "ENTITY",
                    &e,
                    sizeof(entt::entity)
                );
                // ƒhƒ‰ƒbƒO’†‚ÌƒvƒŒƒrƒ…[
                ImGui::Text(name.c_str());
                ImGui::EndDragDropSource();
            }
        }
    }

    // ƒZƒpƒŒ[ƒ^ˆÈ‰ºƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE––”ö‚Ü‚Å‚ğƒhƒƒbƒvƒ^[ƒQƒbƒg‚É
    ImVec2 dropAreaMax = ImGui::GetWindowPos();
    dropAreaMax.x += ImGui::GetWindowSize().x;
    dropAreaMax.y += ImGui::GetWindowSize().y;

    ImRect dropRect(dropAreaMin, dropAreaMax);

	// ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚Ìƒhƒƒbƒvó‚¯æ‚è
    if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTargetCustom(dropRect, ImGui::GetCurrentWindow()->ID))
    {
        if (const ImGuiPayload* payload =
            ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("MODEL_PATH"))
        {                
            // ƒpƒX‚ğó‚¯æ‚é
            std::string modelPath(static_cast<const char*>(payload->Data));

            // Entity ¶¬
            entt::entity e = registry.create();

            registry.emplace<ComponentCategory>(e);

            auto& t = registry.emplace<TransformComponent>(e);
            t.position = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
            t.scale = { 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f };

            registry.emplace<MeshComponent>(e, modelPath);
            registry.emplace<MaterialComponent>(e);

            // ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹–¼‚©‚ç Entity –¼‚ğİ’è
            std::filesystem::path p(modelPath);
            context.SetName(e, p.stem().string());
            context.Select(e);
        }

        // ƒvƒŒƒnƒu‚Ìƒhƒƒbƒvó‚¯æ‚è
        if (const ImGuiPayload* payload =
            ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("PREFAB_PATH"))
        {
            // ƒvƒŒƒnƒu‚ÌƒpƒX‚ğó‚¯æ‚é
            std::string prefabPath(static_cast<const char*>(payload->Data));

            // ƒvƒŒƒnƒu‚©‚çEntity‚ğ¶¬‚·‚éi“Ç‚İ‚İ‚É¸”s‚µ‚½ê‡‚ÍA‰½‚à’u‚©‚ê‚È‚¢j
            entt::entity e = EntitySerializer::InstantiatePrefab(registry, prefabPath);
            if (e != entt::null)
            {
                // ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹–¼i.prefab.json‚ğœ‚¢‚½•”•ªj‚©‚ç Entity –¼‚ğİ’è
                std::filesystem::path p(prefabPath);
                std::string stem = p.stem().string(); // "xxx.prefab"
                std::filesystem::path stemPath(stem);
                context.SetName(e, stemPath.stem().string()); // "xxx"

                context.Select(e);
            }
        }

        ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
    }
    ImGui::End();
}

#include "ImGuiManager.h"

ImGuiManager::~ImGuiManager()
{
	// I—¹‰»
	Shutdown();
}

// ‰Šú‰»
void ImGuiManager::Initialize(HWND hWnd, ID3D11Device* device, ID3D11DeviceContext* dc)
{
	IMGUI_CHECKVERSION();
	ImGui::CreateContext();

	ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();

	// ƒhƒbƒLƒ“ƒO—LŒø‰»
	io.ConfigFlags |= ImGuiConfigFlags_DockingEnable;
	//ƒ}ƒ‹ƒ`ƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒg—LŒø‰»
	io.ConfigFlags |= ImGuiConfigFlags_ViewportsEnable;

	// “ú–{ŒêƒtƒHƒ“ƒgİ’è
	io.Fonts->AddFontFromFileTTF(
		"SystemData/Fonts/NotoSansJP-Regular.ttf",
		16.0f, nullptr, io.Fonts->GetGlyphRangesJapanese());

	ImGui_ImplWin32_Init(hWnd);
	ImGui_ImplDX11_Init(device, dc);

	ImGui::StyleColorsClassic(); // ƒNƒ‰ƒVƒbƒN
}

// ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ŠJn
void ImGuiManager::BeginFrame()
{
	ImGui_ImplDX11_NewFrame();
	ImGui_ImplWin32_NewFrame();
	ImGui::NewFrame();

	// ƒhƒbƒLƒ“ƒOƒXƒy[ƒXi‰æ–Ê‘S‘Ìj
	ImGuiWindowFlags dockFlags =
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoDocking |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoTitleBar |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoCollapse |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoResize |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoMove |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoBringToFrontOnFocus |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoNavFocus |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoBackground;

	ImGuiViewport* viewport = ImGui::GetMainViewport();
	ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(viewport->Pos);
	ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(viewport->Size);
	ImGui::SetNextWindowViewport(viewport->ID);

	ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_WindowRounding, 0.0f);
	ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_WindowBorderSize, 0.0f);
	ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_WindowPadding, ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f));

	ImGui::Begin("DockSpace", nullptr, dockFlags);
	ImGui::PopStyleVar(3);

	// ƒhƒbƒLƒ“ƒOƒXƒy[ƒXID¶¬
	ImGuiID dockSpaceId = ImGui::GetID("MainDockSpace");
	ImGui::DockSpace(dockSpaceId, ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f), ImGuiDockNodeFlags_PassthruCentralNode);

	ImGui::End();
}

// ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€I—¹/•`‰æ
void ImGuiManager::EndFrame()
{
	ImGui::Render();
	ImGui_ImplDX11_RenderDrawData(ImGui::GetDrawData());

	// ƒ}ƒ‹ƒ`ƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒgXV
	ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
	if (io.ConfigFlags & ImGuiConfigFlags_ViewportsEnable)
	{
		ImGui::UpdatePlatformWindows();
		ImGui::RenderPlatformWindowsDefault();
	}
}

// I—¹‰»
void ImGuiManager::Shutdown()
{
	ImGui_ImplDX11_Shutdown();
	ImGui_ImplWin32_Shutdown();
	ImGui::DestroyContext();
}

// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEƒƒbƒZ[ƒWˆ—
bool ImGuiManager::HandleMessage(HWND hWnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wparam, LPARAM lparam)
{
	return ImGui_ImplWin32_WndProcHandler(hWnd, msg, wparam, lparam);
}
 
 
===== InspectorWindow.cpp =====

#include "InspectorWindow.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include "imgui_internal.h"
#include "Engine/Resource/ComponentRegistry.h"
#include "Tool/Editor/ComponentInspectorRegistry.h"
#include "Tool/Editor/MetaRegistry.h"
#include "tracy/Tracy.hpp"

// •`‰æ
void InspectorWindow::Draw(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
    ZoneScoped;

    ImGui::Begin("Inspector");

    // ‰½‚à‘I‘ğ‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¯‚ê‚Î‰½‚à•\¦‚µ‚È‚¢
    if (context.selectedEntity == entt::null)
    {
        ImGui::Text("No entity selected.");
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }

    // –¼‘O•ÒW
    DrawNameField(registry, context);

    ImGui::Separator();

    // ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒgˆê——
    DrawComponents(registry, context);

    ImGui::Separator();

    // ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg’Ç‰Áƒ{ƒ^ƒ“
    DrawAddComponent(registry, context);

    ImGui::End();
}

// –¼‘O•ÒWUI
void InspectorWindow::DrawNameField(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
    entt::entity e = context.selectedEntity;
    std::string name = context.GetName(e);

    char buf[256] = {};
    strncpy_s(buf, name.c_str(), sizeof(buf) - 1);

    if (ImGui::InputText("Name", buf, sizeof(buf)))
    {
        context.SetName(e, buf);
    }
}

// ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒgˆê——UI
void InspectorWindow::DrawComponents(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
    entt::entity e = context.selectedEntity;

    for (const auto& name : ComponentRegistry::GetTypeNames())
    {
        const ComponentInfo* info = ComponentRegistry::Get(name);
        if (!info) continue;
        // ‚±‚ÌEntity‚ªƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ğ‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚©
        if (!info->has(registry, e)) continue;

        ImGui::PushID(name.c_str());

        // íœƒ{ƒ^ƒ“
        if (ImGui::Button("x"))
        {
            info->remove(registry, e);
            ImGui::PopID();
            // íœŒã‚ÍƒCƒeƒŒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ğ’†’f
            break;
        }
        ImGui::SameLine();


        // ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ÌImGui‚ğ•`‰æ
        const auto* drawImGui = ComponentInspectorRegistry::Get(name);
        if (drawImGui)
        {
            // “Æ©‚ÌUI‚ª“o˜^‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚Î‚»‚ê‚ğg‚¤
            (*drawImGui)(registry, e);
        }
        else if (info->getMeta)
        {
            entt::meta_any firstInstance = info->getMeta(registry, e);
            if (firstInstance)
            {
                entt::meta_type metaType = firstInstance.type();
                TypeInfo* typeInfo = metaType.custom();
                std::string label = typeInfo ? typeInfo->category : name;

                if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader(label.c_str(), ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))
                {
                    // •Ê‚ÌID‹óŠÔ‚ÅˆÍ‚Ş
                    ImGui::PushID("fields");
                    MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(metaType, info->getMeta(registry, e));
                    ImGui::PopID();
                }
            }
        }

        ImGui::PopID();
    }
}

// ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg’Ç‰ÁUI
void InspectorWindow::DrawAddComponent(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
    entt::entity e = context.selectedEntity;

    if (ImGui::Button("+ Add Component"))
    {
        ImGui::OpenPopup("AddComponentPopup");
    }

    if (ImGui::BeginPopup("AddComponentPopup"))
    {
        ImGui::Text("Select Component");
        ImGui::Separator();

        for (const auto& name : ComponentRegistry::GetTypeNames())
        {
            const ComponentInfo* info = ComponentRegistry::Get(name);
            if (!info) continue;

            // ‚·‚Å‚É‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚éƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ÍƒOƒŒ[ƒAƒEƒg
            if (info->has(registry, e))
            {
                ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", info->displayName.c_str());
            }
            else
            {
                if (ImGui::Selectable(info->displayName.c_str()))
                {
                    info->add(registry, e);
                    ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
                }
            }
        }
        ImGui::EndPopup();
    }
}
 
 
===== LogWindow.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Editor\LogWindow.cpp 
#include "LogWindow.h"

void LogWindow::Draw()
{
	ImGui::Begin("Log");

	// ƒƒO‚ªXV‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚½‚çA•\¦—p‚ÌÊ‚µ‚ğæ‚è’¼‚·
	uint64_t version = LogBuffer::Version();
	if (version != cachedVersion)
	{
		cache = LogBuffer::Snapshot();
		cachedVersion = version;
	}

	filter.Draw("Filter", -50.0f);
	ImGui::Separator();

	// ƒtƒBƒ‹ƒ^[‚É‡‚¤ƒƒO‚Ì”Ô†‚¾‚¯‚ğW‚ß‚é
	std::vector<int> visible;
	visible.reserve(cache.size());
	for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(cache.size()); ++i)
	{
		if (filter.PassFilter(cache[i].message.c_str()))
		{
			visible.push_back(i);
		}
	}

	ImGui::BeginChild("LogScroll", ImVec2(0, 0), false, ImGuiWindowFlags_HorizontalScrollbar);

	// ‰æ–Ê‚ÉŒ©‚¦‚Ä‚¢‚és‚¾‚¯‚ğ•`‰æ‚·‚é
	ImGuiListClipper clipper;
	clipper.Begin(static_cast<int>(visible.size()));
	while (clipper.Step())
	{
		for (int row = clipper.DisplayStart; row < clipper.DisplayEnd; ++row)
		{
			const auto& entry = cache[visible[row]];
			ImGui::Text("[%s] %s", entry.time.c_str(), entry.message.c_str());
		}
	}
	clipper.End();

	// ˆê”Ô‰º‚Ü‚ÅŒ©‚Ä‚¢‚é‚ÍAV‚µ‚¢ƒƒO‚É’Ç]‚·‚é
	if (ImGui::GetScrollY() >= ImGui::GetScrollMaxY())
	{
		ImGui::SetScrollHereY(1.0f);
	}

	ImGui::EndChild();
	ImGui::End();
} 
 
===== MetaRegistry.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Editor\MetaRegistry.cpp 
#include "MetaRegistry.h"
#include <DirectXMath.h>
#include <filesystem>
#include <imgui.h>
#include <vector>
#include <utility>

using namespace entt::literals;

// “o˜^Ï‚İƒƒ^î•ñ‚©‚çInspectorUI‚ğ©“®•`‰æ
void MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::meta_type metaType, entt::meta_any instance)
{
	if (!metaType) return;

	for (auto&& [id, metaData] : metaType.data())
	{
		ImGui::PushID(static_cast<int>(id));

		FieldInfo* fieldInfo = metaData.custom();
		std::string label = fieldInfo ? fieldInfo->label : std::string(metaData.name());

		entt::meta_any value = metaData.get(instance);

		if (float* f = value.try_cast<float>())
		{
			if (fieldInfo && HasFlag(fieldInfo->flags, FieldFlags::VisibleOnly)) 
			{
				ImGui::Text("%s: %.2f", label.c_str(), *f);
			}
			else
			{
				float minV = fieldInfo ? fieldInfo->minValue : 0.0f;
				float maxV = fieldInfo ? fieldInfo->maxValue : 0.0f;
				float speed = fieldInfo ? fieldInfo->dragSpeed : 0.1f;

				if (ImGui::DragFloat(label.c_str(), f, speed, minV, maxV))
				{
					metaData.set(instance, *f);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (DirectX::XMFLOAT3* v = value.try_cast<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>())
		{
			float speed = fieldInfo ? fieldInfo->dragSpeed : 0.1f;

			if (ImGui::DragFloat3(label.c_str(), &v->x, speed))
			{
				metaData.set(instance, *v);
			}
		}
		else if (bool* b = value.try_cast<bool>())
		{
			if (fieldInfo && HasFlag(fieldInfo->flags, FieldFlags::VisibleOnly)) 
			{
				ImGui::Text("%s: %s", label.c_str(), *b ? "true" : "false");
			}
			else
			{
				if (ImGui::Checkbox(label.c_str(), b))
				{
					metaData.set(instance, *b);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (std::string* s = value.try_cast<std::string>())
		{
			if (fieldInfo && !fieldInfo->dragDropPayload.empty())
			{
				std::string displayName = s->empty()
					? "None"
					: std::filesystem::path(*s).stem().string();

				ImGui::InputText(label.c_str(), displayName.data(),
					displayName.size() + 1, ImGuiInputTextFlags_ReadOnly);

				if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget())
				{
					if (const ImGuiPayload* payload = ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload(fieldInfo->dragDropPayload.c_str()))
					{
						std::string newPath(static_cast<const char*>(payload->Data));
						metaData.set(instance, newPath);
					}
					ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
				}
			}
			else if (fieldInfo && HasFlag(fieldInfo->flags, FieldFlags::VisibleOnly))
			{
				ImGui::Text("%s: %s", label.c_str(), s->c_str());
			}
			else
			{
				char buf[256];
				strncpy_s(buf, s->c_str(), sizeof(buf) - 1);
				if (ImGui::InputText(label.c_str(), buf, sizeof(buf)))
				{
					metaData.set(instance, std::string(buf));
				}
			}
		}
		else if (value.type().is_enum())
		{
			entt::meta_type enumType = value.type();

			// enum‚ÌŠe’l(REFLECT_ENUM_VALUE‚Å“o˜^‚µ‚½’è”)‚ğAƒ‰ƒxƒ‹•t‚«‚Å—ñ‹“‚·‚é
			std::vector<std::pair<std::string, entt::meta_any>> options;
			std::string currentLabel = "(Unknown)";

			for (auto&& [enumId, enumData] : enumType.data())
			{
				EnumValueInfo* enumInfo = enumData.custom();
				std::string optLabel = enumInfo ? enumInfo->label : std::string(enumData.name());

				entt::meta_any optValue = enumData.get({});
				options.push_back({ optLabel, optValue });

				if (optValue == value)
				{
					currentLabel = optLabel;
				}
			}

			if (ImGui::BeginCombo(label.c_str(), currentLabel.c_str()))
			{
				for (auto& [optLabel, optValue] : options)
				{
					bool selected = (optLabel == currentLabel);
					if (ImGui::Selectable(optLabel.c_str(), selected))
					{
						metaData.set(instance, optValue);
					}
					if (selected) ImGui::SetItemDefaultFocus();
				}
				ImGui::EndCombo();
			}
		}
		else if (value.type().data().begin() != value.type().data().end())
		{
			// REFLECT‚³‚ê‚½\‘¢‘ÌF’†g‚ğ“¯‚¶ŠÖ”‚Å•`‰æ‚·‚é
			if (ImGui::TreeNode(label.c_str()))
			{
				DrawMetaFields(value.type(), value.as_ref());
				ImGui::TreePop();

				// value‚ÍƒRƒs[‚È‚Ì‚ÅA•ÒWŒ‹‰Ê‚ğŒ³‚ÌƒCƒ“ƒXƒ^ƒ“ƒX‚Ö‘‚«–ß‚·
				metaData.set(instance, value);
			}
		}
		else
		{
			ImGui::TextDisabled("%s: (unsupported type)", label.c_str());
		}

		ImGui::PopID();
	}
} 
 
===== PrefabWindow.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Editor\PrefabWindow.cpp 
#include "PrefabWindow.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <fstream>
#include "entt.hpp"
#include "Engine/Resource/EntitySerializer.h"

// ‰Šú‰»
void PrefabWindow::Initialize(const std::string& root)
{
	rootPath = root;
	itemState.selectedPath = rootPath;

	if (!std::filesystem::exists(rootPath))
	{
		std::filesystem::create_directories(rootPath);
	}
}

// •`‰æ
void PrefabWindow::Draw(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
	RefreshFolderTreeIfNeeded();
	RefreshFileGridIfNeeded();

	ImGui::Begin("Prefab");

	if (ImGui::BeginTable("PrefabLayout", 2,
		ImGuiTableFlags_Resizable |
		ImGuiTableFlags_BordersInnerV))
	{
		ImGui::TableSetupColumn("Folders", ImGuiTableColumnFlags_WidthFixed, 200.0f);
		ImGui::TableSetupColumn("Files", ImGuiTableColumnFlags_WidthStretch);

		ImGui::TableNextRow();

		ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(0);
		ImGui::BeginChild("PrefabFolderTree", ImVec2(0, 0), false);
		DrawFolderTree(folderTreeCache, registry, context);
		ImGui::EndChild();

		ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(1);
		ImGui::BeginChild("PrefabFileGrid", ImVec2(0, 0), false);
		DrawFileGrid(registry, context);
		ImGui::EndChild();

		ImGui::EndTable();
	}

	DrawDeleteConfirmPopup();

	ImGui::End();
}

// d•¡‚µ‚È‚¢–¼‘O‚Åì¬
std::string PrefabWindow::GenerateUniqueName(const std::filesystem::path& parentDir, const std::string& baseName)
{
	std::filesystem::path candidate = parentDir / baseName;
	if (!std::filesystem::exists(candidate))
	{
		return baseName;
	}

	std::filesystem::path basePath(baseName);
	std::string stem = basePath.stem().string();
	std::string ext = basePath.extension().string();

	int suffix = 2;
	while (true)
	{
		std::string name = stem + " (" + std::to_string(suffix) + ")" + ext;
		if (!std::filesystem::exists(parentDir / name))
		{
			return name;
		}
		++suffix;
	}
}


// ¶FƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_ƒcƒŠ[
void PrefabWindow::DrawFolderTree(FolderNode& node, entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
	const std::filesystem::path& path = node.path;
	bool isRenamingThis = (itemState.mode == PrefabItemMode::Renaming && itemState.targetPath == path);

	bool hasSubFolder = !node.children.empty();

	// ƒŠƒl[ƒ€’†‚ÍƒcƒŠ[s‚Ì‘ã‚í‚è‚É“ü—Í—“‚ğ•\¦‚·‚é
	if (isRenamingThis)
	{
		ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(160.0f);
		ImGui::SetKeyboardFocusHere();

		bool confirmedByEnter = ImGui::InputText("##rename", itemState.editBuffer, sizeof(itemState.editBuffer), ImGuiInputTextFlags_EnterReturnsTrue);
		bool confirmedByBlur = ImGui::IsItemDeactivated() && !confirmedByEnter;

		if (confirmedByEnter || confirmedByBlur)
		{
			CommitRename();
		}
		// ƒŠƒl[ƒ€’†‚ÍƒTƒuƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ÌÄ‹A•`‰æ‚ğs‚í‚È‚¢
		return;
	}

	ImGuiTreeNodeFlags flags = ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_SpanAvailWidth;

	if (itemState.selectedPath == path)
	{
		flags |= ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_Selected;
	}

	if (!hasSubFolder)
	{
		flags |= ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_Leaf;
		flags |= ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_NoTreePushOnOpen;
	}

	bool open = ImGui::TreeNodeEx(node.name.c_str(), flags);

	if (ImGui::IsItemClicked())
	{
		itemState.selectedPath = path;
	}

	// Entity‚Ìƒhƒƒbƒv‚ğó‚¯æ‚èA‚±‚ÌƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ÉƒvƒŒƒnƒu‚Æ‚µ‚Ä•Û‘¶‚·‚é
	if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget())
	{
		if (const ImGuiPayload* payload = ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("ENTITY"))
		{
			entt::entity droppedEntity = *(const entt::entity*)payload->Data;
			SaveEntityAsPrefab(registry, context, droppedEntity, path);
		}
		ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
	}

	// ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ÅƒRƒ“ƒeƒLƒXƒgƒƒjƒ…[
	if (ImGui::BeginPopupContextItem(path.string().c_str()))
	{
		if (ImGui::BeginMenu(u8"V‹Kì¬"))
		{
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"ƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_"))
			{
				std::string newName = GenerateUniqueName(path, "New Folder");
				std::filesystem::create_directory(path / newName);
			}

			ImGui::EndMenu();
		}

		ImGui::Separator();
		if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"–¼‘O‚Ì•ÏX"))
		{
			BeginRename(path);
		}

		// ƒ‹[ƒgƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚Ííœ‚µ‚È‚¢
		if (path != rootPath)
		{
			ImGui::Separator();
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"íœ"))
			{
				itemState.mode = PrefabItemMode::ConfirmingDelete;
				itemState.targetPath = path;
			}
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}

	if (open && hasSubFolder)
	{
		for (auto& child : node.children)
		{
			DrawFolderTree(child, registry, context);
		}
		ImGui::TreePop();
	}
}
// ‰EFƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹ˆê——
void PrefabWindow::DrawFileGrid(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
	if (itemState.selectedPath.empty()) return;

	const float iconSize = 64.0f;
	const float padding = 16.0f;
	const float cellSize = iconSize + padding;

	float windowWidth = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;
	int columns = (std::max)(1, (int)(windowWidth / cellSize));

	//ImGui::InvisibleButton("##fileGridDropArea", ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail());
	//if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget())
	//{
	//	if (const ImGuiPayload* payload = ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("ENTITY"))
	//	{
	//		entt::entity droppedEntity = *(const entt::entity*)payload->Data;
	//		SaveEntityAsPrefab(registry, context, droppedEntity, itemState.selectedPath);
	//	}
	//	ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
	//}
	//ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImGui::GetItemRectMin());

	int index = 0;

	for (auto& file : fileGridCache)
	{
		const std::string& filename = file.filename;
		std::string prefabPath = file.path.string();
		std::replace(prefabPath.begin(), prefabPath.end(), '\\', '/');

		if (index % columns != 0)
		{
			ImGui::SameLine();
		}

		ImGui::BeginGroup();

		ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();

		// ƒvƒŒƒnƒu—p‚ÌƒAƒCƒRƒ“i’PF‚ÌlŠp‚Å‰¼•\¦j
		ImGui::GetWindowDrawList()->AddRectFilled(
			pos,
			ImVec2(pos.x + iconSize, pos.y + iconSize),
			IM_COL32(90, 170, 90, 255),
			8.0f
		);
		ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize));

		ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(pos);
		ImGui::PushID(index);
		ImGui::InvisibleButton("##icon", ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize));

		// Šù‘¶‚ÌƒvƒŒƒnƒu‚ÉAEntity‚ªƒhƒƒbƒv‚³‚ê‚½‚çA‚»‚Ìƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ğã‘‚«•Û‘¶‚·‚é
		if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget())
		{
			if (const ImGuiPayload* payload = ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("ENTITY"))
			{
				entt::entity droppedEntity = *(const entt::entity*)payload->Data;
				OverwritePrefab(registry, context, droppedEntity, file.path);
			}
			ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
		}

		// ƒhƒ‰ƒbƒOŠJniHierarchy‚Öƒhƒƒbƒv‚µ‚Ä”z’u‚·‚é‚½‚ßj
		if (ImGui::BeginDragDropSource(ImGuiDragDropFlags_SourceAllowNullID))
		{
			ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
				"PREFAB_PATH",
				prefabPath.c_str(),
				prefabPath.size() + 1
			);
			ImGui::Text(filename.c_str());
			ImGui::EndDragDropSource();
		}

		// ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Åu–¼‘O‚Ì•ÏXvuíœv
		if (ImGui::BeginPopupContextItem("##fileContext"))
		{
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"–¼‘O‚Ì•ÏX"))
			{
				BeginRename(file.path);
			}
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"íœ"))
			{
				itemState.mode = PrefabItemMode::ConfirmingDelete;
				itemState.targetPath = file.path;
			}
			ImGui::EndPopup();
		}

		ImGui::PopID();

		bool isRenamingThis = (itemState.mode == PrefabItemMode::Renaming && itemState.targetPath == file.path);
		if (isRenamingThis)
		{
			ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(iconSize + padding);
			ImGui::SetKeyboardFocusHere();

			bool confirmedByEnter = ImGui::InputText("##rename", itemState.editBuffer, sizeof(itemState.editBuffer), ImGuiInputTextFlags_EnterReturnsTrue);
			bool confirmedByBlur = ImGui::IsItemDeactivated() && !confirmedByEnter;

			// Šg’£q‚Íu.prefabv‚Ì•”•ª‚¾‚¯ŒÅ’è•\¦‚·‚éi.json‚Ì•”•ª‚Í“à•”À‘•‚Æ‚µ‚Ä‰B‚·j
			ImGui::TextDisabled(".prefab");

			if (confirmedByEnter || confirmedByBlur)
			{
				CommitRename();
			}
		}
		else
		{
			std::string displayName = filename;
			if (displayName.size() > 10)
				displayName = displayName.substr(0, 9) + "...";
			ImGui::TextUnformatted(displayName.c_str());
		}

		ImGui::EndGroup();
		index++;
	}

	// ‹ó”’•”•ª‚Ì‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ÅAV‹KƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_ì¬
	if (ImGui::BeginPopupContextWindow("PrefabFileGridContext", ImGuiPopupFlags_MouseButtonRight | ImGuiPopupFlags_NoOpenOverExistingPopup))
	{
		if (ImGui::BeginMenu(u8"V‹Kì¬"))
		{
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"ƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_"))
			{
				std::string newName = GenerateUniqueName(itemState.selectedPath, "New Folder");
				std::filesystem::create_directory(itemState.selectedPath / newName);
			}
			ImGui::EndMenu();
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}
}

// Entity‚ğAw’èƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ÉƒvƒŒƒnƒu‚Æ‚µ‚Ä•Û‘¶‚·‚é
void PrefabWindow::SaveEntityAsPrefab(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context, entt::entity e, const std::filesystem::path& folder)
{
	if (!registry.valid(e)) return;

	std::string name = context.GetName(e);
	std::string fileName = GenerateUniqueName(folder, name + ".prefab.json");

	nlohmann::json data = EntitySerializer::SerializeEntity(registry, e);

	std::ofstream ofs(folder / fileName);
	if (ofs)
	{
		ofs << data.dump(4);
	}
}
// Entity‚ğAŠù‘¶‚ÌƒvƒŒƒnƒuƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚Éã‘‚«•Û‘¶‚·‚é
void PrefabWindow::OverwritePrefab(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context, entt::entity e, const std::filesystem::path& filePath)
{
	if (!registry.valid(e)) return;

	nlohmann::json data = EntitySerializer::SerializeEntity(registry, e);

	std::ofstream ofs(filePath);
	if (ofs)
	{
		ofs << data.dump(4);
	}
}

// ŠÔŠu‚ªŒo‰ß‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚ÎAƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_ƒcƒŠ[‚ÌƒLƒƒƒbƒVƒ…‚ğì‚è’¼‚·
void PrefabWindow::RefreshFolderTreeIfNeeded()
{
	auto now = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
	if (now - lastFolderScanTime < folderScanInterval) return;

	folderTreeCache = BuildFolderNode(rootPath);
	lastFolderScanTime = now;
}
PrefabWindow::FolderNode PrefabWindow::BuildFolderNode(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	FolderNode node;
	node.path = path;
	node.name = path.filename().string();
	if (node.name.empty()) node.name = path.string();

	for (auto& entry : std::filesystem::directory_iterator(path))
	{
		if (entry.is_directory())
		{
			node.children.push_back(BuildFolderNode(entry.path()));
		}
	}
	return node;
}

// ‘I‘ğƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚½^ŠÔŠu‚ªŒo‰ß‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚ÎAƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹ƒOƒŠƒbƒh‚ÌƒLƒƒƒbƒVƒ…‚ğì‚è’¼‚·
void PrefabWindow::RefreshFileGridIfNeeded()
{
	bool folderChanged = (itemState.selectedPath != fileGridCachedPath);
	auto now = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
	bool timeElapsed = (now - lastFileGridScanTime >= std::chrono::seconds(2));

	if (!folderChanged && !timeElapsed) return;

	fileGridCache = BuildFileGrid(itemState.selectedPath);
	fileGridCachedPath = itemState.selectedPath;
	lastFileGridScanTime = now;
}
std::vector<PrefabWindow::FileEntry> PrefabWindow::BuildFileGrid(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	std::vector<FileEntry> result;

	for (auto& entry : std::filesystem::directory_iterator(path))
	{
		if (entry.is_directory()) continue;
		if (!IsPrefabFile(entry.path())) continue;

		FileEntry file;
		file.path = entry.path();
		file.filename = entry.path().filename().string();
		result.push_back(file);
	}
	return result;
}

// ƒvƒŒƒnƒuƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚Ì”»’è
bool PrefabWindow::IsPrefabFile(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	if (path.extension() != ".json") return false;

	std::string stem = path.stem().string();
	std::filesystem::path stemPath(stem);
	return stemPath.extension() == ".prefab";
}

// íœŠm”Fƒ|ƒbƒvƒAƒbƒv
void PrefabWindow::DrawDeleteConfirmPopup()
{
	if (itemState.mode != PrefabItemMode::ConfirmingDelete) return;

	ImGui::OpenPopup(u8"íœ‚ÌŠm”F");

	bool open = true;
	if (ImGui::BeginPopupModal(u8"íœ‚ÌŠm”F", &open, ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize))
	{
		std::string name = itemState.targetPath.filename().string();
		ImGui::Text(u8"u%sv‚ğíœ‚µ‚Ü‚·‚©H", name.c_str());
		ImGui::Spacing();

		if (ImGui::Button(u8"íœ", ImVec2(120, 0)))
		{
			std::error_code ec;
			std::filesystem::remove_all(itemState.targetPath, ec);

			if (itemState.selectedPath == itemState.targetPath)
			{
				itemState.selectedPath = rootPath;
			}

			itemState.mode = PrefabItemMode::None;
			itemState.targetPath.clear();
			ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
		}

		ImGui::SameLine();

		if (ImGui::Button(u8"ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹", ImVec2(120, 0)))
		{
			itemState.mode = PrefabItemMode::None;
			itemState.targetPath.clear();
			ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
		}

		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}

	if (!open && itemState.mode == PrefabItemMode::ConfirmingDelete)
	{
		itemState.mode = PrefabItemMode::None;
		itemState.targetPath.clear();
	}
}

// ƒŠƒl[ƒ€ŠJn
void PrefabWindow::BeginRename(const std::filesystem::path& target)
{
	itemState.mode = PrefabItemMode::Renaming;
	itemState.targetPath = target;

	// ƒvƒŒƒnƒuƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹(.prefab.json)‚ÍA2’iŠK‚ÌŠg’£q‚ğ—¼•ûæ‚èœ‚­BƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚Í‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü
	std::string currentStem;
	if (IsPrefabFile(target))
	{
		currentStem = std::filesystem::path(target.stem()).stem().string(); // "xxx.prefab" -> "xxx"
	}
	else
	{
		currentStem = target.stem().string();
	}

	strncpy_s(itemState.editBuffer, currentStem.c_str(), sizeof(itemState.editBuffer) - 1);
}
// ƒŠƒl[ƒ€Šm’è
void PrefabWindow::CommitRename()
{
	std::string newStem = itemState.editBuffer;

	// ƒvƒŒƒnƒuƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ÍAí‚Éu.prefab.jsonv‚ğ•t‚¯’¼‚·BƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚Í‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü(Šg’£q‚È‚µ)
	std::string newName = IsPrefabFile(itemState.targetPath)
		? newStem + ".prefab.json" : newStem;

	if (!newStem.empty() && newName != itemState.targetPath.filename().string())
	{
		std::filesystem::path newPath = itemState.targetPath.parent_path() / newName;

		std::error_code ec;
		std::filesystem::rename(itemState.targetPath, newPath, ec);

		if (!ec)
		{
			if (itemState.selectedPath == itemState.targetPath)
			{
				itemState.selectedPath = newPath;
			}
		}
	}

	itemState.mode = PrefabItemMode::None;
	itemState.targetPath.clear();
}

void ProjectWindow::Initialize(const std::string& rootPath)
{
	this->rootPath = rootPath;
	// ‰Šú‘I‘ğƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚Íƒ‹[ƒg
	itemState.selectedPath = rootPath;
}

// ƒAƒCƒRƒ““Ç‚İ‚İ
void ProjectWindow::LoadIcons(ID3D11Device* device)
{
	// Data/Sprite/System/folder.png ‚ğ“Ç‚İ‚Ş

		device,

void ProjectWindow::Draw(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context, bool asDrawer)
{
	ZoneScoped;

	// 2•bŒo‰ß‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚ÎƒfƒBƒXƒN‚ğŒ©‚ÄƒLƒƒƒbƒVƒ…‚ğì‚è’¼‚·
	RefreshFolderTreeIfNeeded();
	RefreshFileGridIfNeeded();

	ImGui::Begin("Project");

	if (ImGui::BeginTable("ProjectLayout", 2,
		ImGuiTableFlags_Resizable |
		ImGuiTableFlags_BordersInnerV))
	{
		// ¶‘¤‚Ì‰Šú•‚ğİ’è
		ImGui::TableSetupColumn("Folders", ImGuiTableColumnFlags_WidthFixed, 200.0f);
		ImGui::TableSetupColumn("Files", ImGuiTableColumnFlags_WidthStretch);

		ImGui::TableNextRow();

		// ¶‘¤FƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_ƒcƒŠ[
		ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(0);
		ImGui::BeginChild("FolderTree", ImVec2(0, 0), false);
		DrawFolderTree(folderTreeCache);
		ImGui::EndChild();

		// ‰E‘¤Fƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹ƒOƒŠƒbƒh
		ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(1);
		ImGui::BeginChild("FileGrid", ImVec2(0, 0), false);
		DrawFileGrid(registry, context);
		ImGui::EndChild();

		ImGui::EndTable();
	}

	DrawDeleteConfirmPopup();

	ImGui::End();
}

// d•¡‚µ‚È‚¢–¼‘O‚Åì¬
std::string ProjectWindow::GenerateUniqueName(const std::filesystem::path& parentDir, const std::string& baseName) 
{
	std::filesystem::path candidate = parentDir / baseName;
	if (!std::filesystem::exists(candidate))
	{
		return baseName;
	}

	// Šg’£q‚Ìè‘O‚É˜A”Ô‚ğ‹²‚Ş
	std::filesystem::path basePath(baseName);
	std::string stem = basePath.stem().string();
	std::string ext = basePath.extension().string();

	int suffix = 2;
	while (true)
	{
		std::string name = stem + " (" + std::to_string(suffix) + ")" + ext;
		if (!std::filesystem::exists(parentDir / name))
		{
			return name;
		}
		++suffix;
	}
}

// ƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_ƒcƒŠ[
void ProjectWindow::DrawFolderTree(FolderNode& node)
{
	const std::filesystem::path& path = node.path;
	bool isRenamingThis = (itemState.mode == ProjectItemMode::Renaming && itemState.targetPath == path);

	// ƒLƒƒƒbƒVƒ…‚ğŠm”F‚µ‚ÄƒTƒuƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ğŠm”F‚·‚é
	bool hasSubFolder = !node.children.empty();

	// ƒŠƒl[ƒ€’†‚ÍƒcƒŠ[s‚Ì‘ã‚í‚è‚É“ü—Í—“‚ğ•\¦‚·‚é
	if (isRenamingThis)
	{
		ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(160.0f);
		ImGui::SetKeyboardFocusHere();

		bool confirmedByEnter = ImGui::InputText("##rename", itemState.editBuffer, sizeof(itemState.editBuffer), ImGuiInputTextFlags_EnterReturnsTrue);
		bool confirmedByBlur = ImGui::IsItemDeactivated() && !confirmedByEnter;

		if (confirmedByEnter || confirmedByBlur)
		{
			CommitRename();
		}
		// ƒŠƒl[ƒ€’†‚ÍƒTƒuƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ÌÄ‹A•`‰æ‚ğs‚í‚È‚¢
		return;
	}

	ImGuiTreeNodeFlags flags = ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_SpanAvailWidth;

	// ‘I‘ğ’†ƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ğƒnƒCƒ‰ƒCƒg
	if (itemState.selectedPath == path)
	{
		flags |= ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_Selected;
	}

	// Å‰º‘w‚Ìê‡‚Í—tƒm[ƒh‚Æ‚µ‚Ä•\¦
	if (!hasSubFolder)
	{
		flags |= ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_Leaf;
		flags |= ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_NoTreePushOnOpen;
	}

	bool open = ImGui::TreeNodeEx(node.name.c_str(), flags);

	// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Å‘I‘ğ
	if (ImGui::IsItemClicked())
	{
		itemState.selectedPath = path;
	}

	// ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ÅƒRƒ“ƒeƒLƒXƒgƒƒjƒ…[i‚±‚ÌƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚Ì’†‚Éì‚éj
	if (ImGui::BeginPopupContextItem(path.string().c_str()))
	{
		if (ImGui::BeginMenu(u8"V‹Kì¬"))
		{
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"ƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_"))
			{
				std::string newName = GenerateUniqueName(path, "New Folder");
				std::filesystem::create_directory(path / newName);
			}

			ImGui::EndMenu();
		}

		ImGui::Separator();
		if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"–¼‘O‚Ì•ÏX"))
		{
			BeginRename(path);
		}

		// ƒ‹[ƒgƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚Ííœ‚³‚¹‚È‚¢
		if (path != rootPath)
		{
			ImGui::Separator();
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"íœ"))
			{
				itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::ConfirmingDelete;
				itemState.targetPath = path;
			}
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}

	// Å‰º‘w‚ÍTreePop‚ª•s—v
	if (open && hasSubFolder)
	{
		for (auto& child : node.children)
		{
			DrawFolderTree(child);
		}
		ImGui::TreePop();
	}

}
// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹ˆê——
void ProjectWindow::DrawFileGrid(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
	if (itemState.selectedPath.empty()) return;

	// ƒAƒCƒRƒ“ƒTƒCƒY‚Æ—]”’
	const float iconSize = 64.0f;
	const float padding = 16.0f;
	const float cellSize = iconSize + padding;

	// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE•‚©‚ç—ñ”‚ğŒvZ
	float windowWidth = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;
	int   columns = (std::max)(1, (int)(windowWidth / cellSize));

	int index = 0;

	for (auto& file : fileGridCache)
	{
		bool isModel = file.isModel;
		bool isAnimGraph = file.isStateGraph;
		bool isSequencer = file.isSequencer;
		bool isAnimSpeed = file.isAnimSpeed;

		const std::string& filename = file.filename;
		std::string modelPath = file.path.string();
		std::replace(modelPath.begin(), modelPath.end(), '\\', '/');

		// —ñ‚ÌÜ‚è•Ô‚µ
		if (index % columns != 0)
		{
			ImGui::SameLine();
		}

		// ƒOƒ‹[ƒv‰»‚µ‚ÄƒAƒCƒRƒ“{–¼‘O‚ğ‚Ü‚Æ‚ß‚é
		ImGui::BeginGroup();

		ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();

		// ƒAƒCƒRƒ“•\¦iƒ‚ƒfƒ‹—pƒAƒCƒRƒ“‚ª–³‚¢/ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹ˆÈŠO‚ÍF•t‚«‹éŒ`‚Å‘ã—pj
		if (isModel && modelIconSRV)
		{
			ImGui::Image(
				(ImTextureID)modelIconSRV.Get(),
				ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize)
			);
		}
		else
		{
			ImU32 color = isAnimGraph ? IM_COL32(200, 130, 60, 255)
				: isSequencer ? IM_COL32(120, 200, 130, 255)
				: isAnimSpeed ? IM_COL32(200, 90, 160, 255)
				: IM_COL32(80, 120, 200, 255);
			ImGui::GetWindowDrawList()->AddRectFilled(
				pos,
				ImVec2(pos.x + iconSize, pos.y + iconSize),
				color,
				8.0f
			);
			ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize));
		}

		// ‰æ‘œ‚Ìã‚É“§–¾ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ğd‚Ë‚Äƒhƒ‰ƒbƒO‚ğæ‚é
		ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(pos);
		ImGui::PushID(index);
		ImGui::InvisibleButton("##icon", ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize));

		// ƒ_ƒuƒ‹ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ÅStudio‚ğŠJ‚­ƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒgiƒ‚ƒfƒ‹^ƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ƒOƒ‰ƒtj
		if (ImGui::IsItemHovered() && ImGui::IsMouseDoubleClicked(0))
		{
			if (isModel)
			{
				context.studioRequest.pending = true;
				context.studioRequest.assetPath = modelPath;
				context.studioRequest.graphPath = "";
			}
		}

		// ƒhƒ‰ƒbƒOŠJniƒ‚ƒfƒ‹^ƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ƒOƒ‰ƒt/ƒV[ƒPƒ“ƒT[/ƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‘¬“xj
		if ((isModel || isAnimGraph || isSequencer || isAnimSpeed) && ImGui::BeginDragDropSource(ImGuiDragDropFlags_SourceAllowNullID))
		{
			if (isModel)
			{
				ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
					"MODEL_PATH",
					modelPath.c_str(),
					modelPath.size() + 1
				);
			}
			else if (isAnimGraph)
			{
				ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
					"STATEGRAPH_PATH",
					modelPath.c_str(),
					modelPath.size() + 1
				);
			}
			else if (isSequencer)
			{
				ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
					"SEQUENCER_PATH",
					modelPath.c_str(),
					modelPath.size() + 1
				);
			}
			else // isAnimSpeed
			{
				ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
					"ANIMSPEED_PATH",
					modelPath.c_str(),
					modelPath.size() + 1
				);
			}

			if (isModel && modelIconSRV)
			{
				ImGui::Image(
					(ImTextureID)modelIconSRV.Get(),
					ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize)
				);
			}
			ImGui::Text(filename.c_str());
			ImGui::EndDragDropSource();
		}

		// ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Åu–¼‘O‚Ì•ÏXvuíœv
		if (ImGui::BeginPopupContextItem("##fileContext"))
		{
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"–¼‘O‚Ì•ÏX"))
			{
				BeginRename(file.path);
			}
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"íœ"))
			{
				itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::ConfirmingDelete;
				itemState.targetPath = file.path;
			}
			ImGui::EndPopup();
		}

		ImGui::PopID();

		// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹–¼•\¦ or ƒŠƒl[ƒ€’†‚ÍƒCƒ“ƒ‰ƒCƒ““ü—Í—“
		bool isRenamingThis = (itemState.mode == ProjectItemMode::Renaming && itemState.targetPath == file.path);
		if (isRenamingThis)
		{
			std::string extension = file.path.extension().string();

			// “ü—Í—“‚ÍƒAƒCƒRƒ“•‚¢‚Á‚Ï‚¢‚Ég‚¤iŠg’£q‚ÍŸ‚Ìs‚É•\¦‚·‚é‚½‚ß•‚ğ•ª‚¯‚é•K—v‚ª–³‚¢j
			ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(iconSize + padding);
			ImGui::SetKeyboardFocusHere();

			bool confirmedByEnter = ImGui::InputText("##rename", itemState.editBuffer, sizeof(itemState.editBuffer), ImGuiInputTextFlags_EnterReturnsTrue);
			bool confirmedByBlur = ImGui::IsItemDeactivated() && !confirmedByEnter;

			// Šg’£q‚Í“ü—Í—“‚Ì‰º‚Ìs‚ÉŒÅ’è•\¦i•ÒW•s‰Âj
			if (!extension.empty())
			{
				ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", extension.c_str());
			}

			if (confirmedByEnter || confirmedByBlur)
			{
				CommitRename();
			}
		}
		else
		{
			std::string displayName = filename;
			if (displayName.size() > 10)
				displayName = displayName.substr(0, 9) + "...";
			ImGui::TextUnformatted(displayName.c_str());
		}

		ImGui::EndGroup();
		index++;
	}

	// ‹ó”’•”•ª‚Ì‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ÅV‹Kì¬ƒƒjƒ…[
	if (ImGui::BeginPopupContextWindow("FileGridContext", ImGuiPopupFlags_MouseButtonRight | ImGuiPopupFlags_NoOpenOverExistingPopup))
	{
		if (ImGui::BeginMenu(u8"V‹Kì¬"))
		{
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"ƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_"))
			{
				std::string newName = GenerateUniqueName(itemState.selectedPath, "New Folder");
				std::filesystem::create_directory(itemState.selectedPath / newName);
			}
			ImGui::EndMenu();
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}
}

// ˆê’èŠÔŠu‚ªŒo‰ß‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚ÎAƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_ƒcƒŠ[‚ÌƒLƒƒƒbƒVƒ…‚ğì‚è’¼‚·(2•b)
void ProjectWindow::RefreshFolderTreeIfNeeded()
{
	auto now = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
	if (now - lastFolderScanTime < folderScanInterval) return;

	folderTreeCache = BuildFolderNode(rootPath);
	lastFolderScanTime = now;
}
// ƒfƒBƒXƒN‚ğÀÛ‚É‘–¸‚µ‚ÄAw’èƒpƒXˆÈ‰º‚ÌFolderNode‚ğ\’z‚·‚éiÄ‹Aj
ProjectWindow::FolderNode ProjectWindow::BuildFolderNode(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	FolderNode node;
	node.path = path;
	node.name = path.filename().string();

	if (!std::filesystem::is_directory(path)) return node;

	for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::directory_iterator(path))
	{
		if (entry.is_directory())
		{
			node.children.push_back(BuildFolderNode(entry.path()));
		}
	}

	return node;
}

// ‘I‘ğƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚½^ˆê’èŠÔŠu‚ªŒo‰ß‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚ÎAƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹ƒOƒŠƒbƒh‚ÌƒLƒƒƒbƒVƒ…‚ğì‚è’¼‚·
void ProjectWindow::RefreshFileGridIfNeeded()
{
	// ‘I‘ğƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ª‘O‰ñ‚Æˆá‚¤ê‡‚ÍA‘Ò‚½‚¸‚É‘¦À‚Éì‚è’¼‚·
	bool folderChanged = (itemState.selectedPath != fileGridCachedPath);

	auto now = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
	bool intervalElapsed = (now - lastFileGridScanTime >= folderScanInterval);

	if (!folderChanged && !intervalElapsed) return;

	if (itemState.selectedPath.empty() || !std::filesystem::exists(itemState.selectedPath))
	{
		fileGridCache.clear();
	}
	else
	{
		fileGridCache = BuildFileGrid(itemState.selectedPath);
	}

	fileGridCachedPath = itemState.selectedPath;
	lastFileGridScanTime = now;
}
// ƒfƒBƒXƒN‚ğÀÛ‚É‘–¸‚µ‚ÄAw’èƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_’¼‰º‚ÌFileEntryˆê——‚ğ\’z‚·‚é
std::vector<ProjectWindow::FileEntry> ProjectWindow::BuildFileGrid(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	std::vector<FileEntry> result;

	for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::directory_iterator(path))
	{
		if (!entry.is_regular_file()) continue;

		bool isModel = IsModelFile(entry.path());
		bool isAnimGraph = IsStateGraphFile(entry.path());
		bool isSequencer = IsSequencerFile(entry.path());
		bool isAnimSpeed = IsAnimSpeedFile(entry.path());
		if (!isModel && !isAnimGraph && !isSequencer && !isAnimSpeed) continue;

		FileEntry file;
		file.path = entry.path();
		file.filename = entry.path().stem().string();
		file.isModel = isModel;
		file.isStateGraph = isAnimGraph;
		file.isSequencer = isSequencer;
		file.isAnimSpeed = isAnimSpeed;

		result.push_back(std::move(file));
	}

	return result;
}

// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ªƒ‚ƒfƒ‹ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚©”»’è
bool ProjectWindow::IsModelFile(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	std::string ext = path.extension().string();

	// ¬•¶š‚É•ÏŠ·‚µ‚Ä”äŠr
	std::transform(ext.begin(), ext.end(), ext.begin(), ::tolower);

	return ext == ".gltf" || ext == ".glb";
}
// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ªƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ƒOƒ‰ƒtƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚©”»’è
bool ProjectWindow::IsStateGraphFile(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	std::string ext = path.extension().string();
	std::transform(ext.begin(), ext.end(), ext.begin(), ::tolower);

	return ext == ".stategraph";
}
// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ªƒV[ƒPƒ“ƒT[ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚©”»’è
bool ProjectWindow::IsSequencerFile(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	std::string ext = path.extension().string();
	std::transform(ext.begin(), ext.end(), ext.begin(), ::tolower);

	return ext == ".sequencer";
}

// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ªƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‘¬“xƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚©”»’è
bool ProjectWindow::IsAnimSpeedFile(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	std::string ext = path.extension().string();
	std::transform(ext.begin(), ext.end(), ext.begin(), ::tolower);

	return ext == ".animspeed";
}


// íœŠm”Fƒ|ƒbƒv
void ProjectWindow::DrawDeleteConfirmPopup()
{
	if (itemState.mode != ProjectItemMode::ConfirmingDelete) return;

	ImGui::OpenPopup(u8"íœ‚ÌŠm”F");

	bool open = true;
	if (ImGui::BeginPopupModal(u8"íœ‚ÌŠm”F", &open, ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize))
	{
		std::string name = itemState.targetPath.filename().string();
		ImGui::Text(u8"u%sv‚ğíœ‚µ‚Ü‚·‚©H", name.c_str());
		ImGui::Spacing();

		if (ImGui::Button(u8"íœ", ImVec2(120, 0)))
		{
			std::error_code ec;
			std::filesystem::remove_all(itemState.targetPath, ec);

			if (itemState.selectedPath == itemState.targetPath)
			{
				itemState.selectedPath = rootPath;
			}

			itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::None;
			itemState.targetPath.clear();
			ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
		}

		ImGui::SameLine();

		if (ImGui::Button(u8"ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹", ImVec2(120, 0)))
		{
			itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::None;
			itemState.targetPath.clear();
			ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
		}

		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}

	// ~ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚Å•Â‚¶‚ç‚ê‚½ê‡‚àó‘Ô‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg
	if (!open && itemState.mode == ProjectItemMode::ConfirmingDelete)
	{
		itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::None;
		itemState.targetPath.clear();
	}
}

// ƒŠƒl[ƒ€ŠJn
void ProjectWindow::BeginRename(const std::filesystem::path& target)
{
	itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::Renaming;
	itemState.targetPath = target;

	std::string currentStem = target.stem().string();
	strncpy_s(itemState.editBuffer, currentStem.c_str(), sizeof(itemState.editBuffer) - 1);
}
// ƒŠƒl[ƒ€Šm’è
void ProjectWindow::CommitRename()
{
	std::string newStem = itemState.editBuffer;
	std::string extension = itemState.targetPath.extension().string(); // Šg’£q‚ÍŒÅ’èi•ÏX•s‰Âj
	std::string newName = newStem + extension;

	// ‹ó—“‚â•ÏX‚È‚µ‚Ìê‡‚Í‰½‚à‚¹‚¸I—¹
	if (!newStem.empty() && newName != itemState.targetPath.filename().string())
	{
		std::filesystem::path newPath = itemState.targetPath.parent_path() / newName;

		std::error_code ec;
		std::filesystem::rename(itemState.targetPath, newPath, ec);

		if (!ec)
		{
			// ‘I‘ğ’†ƒpƒX‚ªƒŠƒl[ƒ€‘ÎÛ‚¾‚Á‚½ê‡AV‚µ‚¢ƒpƒX‚É’Ç]‚³‚¹‚é
			if (itemState.selectedPath == itemState.targetPath)
			{
				itemState.selectedPath = newPath;
			}
		}
	}

	itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::None;
	itemState.targetPath.clear();
}

// ImGui•`‰æ
void ViewportWindow::Draw(GameView& gameView, const std::function<void()>& drawMenus)
{
    ImGui::Begin("Game", nullptr, ImGuiWindowFlags_MenuBar | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoBringToFrontOnFocus);

    // ƒƒjƒ…[ƒo[
    if (ImGui::BeginMenuBar())
    {
        if (drawMenus)
        {
            drawMenus();
        }

        ImGui::EndMenuBar();
    }

    // Gameê—p‚ÌDockSpace
    ImGuiID dockSpaceId = ImGui::GetID("GameDockSpace");
    ImGui::DockSpace(dockSpaceId, ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f), ImGuiDockNodeFlags_None);

    // ‰‰ñƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚¾‚¯ƒfƒtƒHƒ‹ƒgƒŒƒCƒAƒEƒg‚ğ‘g‚Ş
    if (firstDockSetup)
    {
        firstDockSetup = false;

        ImGui::DockBuilderRemoveNode(dockSpaceId);
        ImGui::DockBuilderAddNode(dockSpaceId, ImGuiDockNodeFlags_None);
        ImGui::DockBuilderSetNodeSize(dockSpaceId, ImVec2(1280, 720));

        ImGuiID dockMain = dockSpaceId;
        ImGuiID dockBottom = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
            dockMain, ImGuiDir_Down, dockRatioBottom, nullptr, &dockMain);
        ImGuiID dockLeft = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
            dockMain, ImGuiDir_Left, dockRatioLeft, nullptr, &dockMain);
        ImGuiID dockRight = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
            dockMain, ImGuiDir_Right, dockRatioRight, nullptr, &dockMain);
        ImGuiID dockBottomRight = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
            dockBottom, ImGuiDir_Right, dockRatioPrefab, nullptr, &dockBottom);
        ImGuiID dockLog = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
            dockBottomRight, ImGuiDir_Right, dockRatioLog, nullptr, &dockBottomRight);

        ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow("Viewport", dockMain);
        ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow("Hierarchy", dockLeft);
        ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow("Inspector", dockRight);
        ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow("Project", dockBottom);
        ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow("Prefab", dockBottomRight);
        ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow("Log", dockLog);
        ImGui::DockBuilderFinish(dockSpaceId);
    }


    // Viewportƒpƒlƒ‹
    ImGui::Begin("Viewport");

    ImVec2 region = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail();
    if (region.x > 1 && region.y > 1)
    {
        // “¯‚¶Viewport‚É•`‚­‚Ì‚ÅA—¼•û‚ÌƒJƒƒ‰‚É“¯‚¶c‰¡”ä‚ğ“–‚Ä‚é
        float aspect = region.x / region.y;
        gameView.SetAspect(aspect);
        debugCamera.SetPerspectiveFov(
            DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(debugCamera.GetViewingAngle()),
            aspect,
            0.1f,
            1000.0f
        );
    }

    ImGui::Image(reinterpret_cast<ImTextureID>(gameView.GetSRV()), region);

    // ƒJƒƒ‰‘€ì‚ÌŠJn”»’è‚ÍViewport’P‘Ì‚Ìƒzƒo[‚¾‚¯‚ğŒ©‚é
    viewportHovered = ImGui::IsWindowHovered();

    ImGui::End(); // Viewport

    ImGui::End(); // Game
}

===== NodeEditorCanvas.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\NodeEditor\NodeEditorCanvas.cpp 
#include "NodeEditorCanvas.h"

NodeEditorCanvas::~NodeEditorCanvas()
{
    if (editorContext)
    {
        ed::DestroyEditor(editorContext);
        editorContext = nullptr;
    }
}

void NodeEditorCanvas::Initialize()
{
    ed::Config config;
    config.SettingsFile = nullptr;
    editorContext = ed::CreateEditor(&config);
}

void NodeEditorCanvas::Begin(const char* canvasId)
{
    ed::SetCurrentEditor(editorContext);
    ed::Begin(canvasId);
}

void NodeEditorCanvas::End()
{
    ed::End();
    ed::SetCurrentEditor(nullptr);
}

	static void ReadPosition(int nodeId, float& x, float& y);


	// --- ƒsƒ“‚ÌID --- //

	// ƒm[ƒh‚Ì’†‚Å‚Ìƒsƒ“‚ÌˆÊ’u
	enum PinSlot : uint64_t
	{
		kPinOut = 1, // o—Í
		kPinInA = 2, // “ü—ÍA
		kPinInB = 3, // “ü—ÍB
	};
	// ƒm[ƒh‚ÌID‚Æd‚È‚ç‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É•t‚¯‚éˆó
	static constexpr uint64_t kPinIdTag = 1ull << 40;

	// ƒm[ƒh‚ÌID‚ÆˆÊ’u -> ƒsƒ“‚ÌID
	static ed::PinId ToPinId(int nodeId, PinSlot slot);
	// ƒsƒ“‚ÌID -> ƒm[ƒh‚ÌID‚ÆˆÊ’u
	static bool FromPinId(ed::PinId pinId, int& outNodeId, PinSlot& outSlot);


	// --- ƒf[ƒ^ --- //

	// ‚±‚ÌƒNƒ‰ƒXê—p‚Ìƒm[ƒhƒGƒfƒBƒ^‚ÌƒRƒ“ƒeƒLƒXƒg
	NodeEditorCanvas canvas;
	// •ÒW’†‚ÌğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt
	ConditionGraph* graph = nullptr;


	// --- ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒX‚Ìó‘Ô --- //

	// ƒf[ƒ^‚ÌˆÊ’u‚ğƒm[ƒh‚É”½‰f‚·‚é‚©
	bool needsApplyPositions = true;
	// Ÿ‚ÉƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒX‚ğ•`‚­‚Æ‚«‚ÉAƒm[ƒhƒGƒfƒBƒ^‘¤‚Ì‘I‘ğ‚ğÁ‚·‚©
	bool clearSelectionRequested = false;

	// ”wŒi‚Ì‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ÌˆÊ’u
	ImVec2 pendingCreatePos{};

	// ‘I‘ğ’†‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚ÆƒŠƒ“ƒN
	std::vector<int> selectedNodeIds;
	int selectedLinkId = -1;

#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "StudioConditionTarget.h"
#include "Engine/Data/StateMachineData.h"

// Ê‚·Œ³‚ÌŒó•â1Œ
struct ConditionCopyItem
{
	ConditionTarget target; // ‚Ç‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚©
	std::string group;      // Œ©o‚µ(q‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚ÍState–¼Ae‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚Í‹ó)
	std::string label;      // •\¦–¼
};

// İ’è‚ğÊ‚·ˆË—Š
struct ConditionCopyRequest
{
	ConditionTarget source; // Ê‚·Œ³
	ConditionTarget target; // Ê‚·æ

	bool IsValid() const { return source.IsValid() && target.IsValid(); }
};

// “¯‚¶ŠK‘w‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚©‚çAİ’è‚ğÊ‚·‚½‚ß‚ÌƒŠƒXƒg
namespace ConditionCopyList
{
	// ƒhƒ‰ƒbƒO‚Å‰^‚Ô•¨‚Ìí—Ş
	inline constexpr const char* kPayloadType = "CONDITION_LINK";

	// e‚ÌŠK‘w‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğW‚ß‚é
	std::vector<ConditionCopyItem> CollectParent(const StateMachineData& data, const ConditionTarget& self);
	// q‚ÌŠK‘w‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğW‚ß‚é
	std::vector<ConditionCopyItem> CollectChild(const StateMachineData& data, const ConditionTarget& self);

	// ƒŠƒXƒg‚ğ•`‚­
	void Draw(const std::vector<ConditionCopyItem>& items);

	// ó‚¯Œû‚ğ•`‚­
	ConditionTarget DrawDropTarget(const ConditionTarget& self);
} 
 
===== StudioConditionTarget.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Studio\StudioConditionTarget.h 
#pragma once

// ğŒƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚Å•ÒW‚·‚éğŒ‚ª‚Ç‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚©‚ç—ˆ‚½‚à‚Ì‚©
struct ConditionTarget
{
	enum class Kind
	{
		None,      // ‰½‚à•ÒW‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢
		StateLink, // e‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN(ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[ ¨ State E State ¨ State)
		EntryLink, // q‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN(ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[ ¨ ƒNƒŠƒbƒv)
		ClipLink,  // q‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN(ƒNƒŠƒbƒv @¨ ƒNƒŠƒbƒv)
	};

	Kind kind = Kind::None;
	int stateId = -1; // q‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ì‚Æ‚«F‚Ç‚ÌState‚Ìq‚©
	int linkId = -1;

	bool IsValid() const { return kind != Kind::None; }
	bool operator==(const ConditionTarget& o) const { return kind == o.kind && stateId == o.stateId && linkId == o.linkId; }
	bool operator!=(const ConditionTarget& o) const { return !(*this == o); }
};
