// ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒX‚ğ•`‚­
void StudioStateChildCanvas::Draw(StateMachineData& data, const std::vector<std::string>& animations)
{
	// •`‰æ’†‚¾‚¯Aó‚¯æ‚Á‚½ƒf[ƒ^‚ğw‚·
	this->data = &data;
	state = data.FindState(stateId);
	if (!state) return;

	CollectEntries();

	ImGui::Columns(2, "StateChildColumns", true);
	if (!columnWidthInitialized)
	{
		ImGui::SetColumnWidth(0, ImGui::GetWindowWidth() * 0.7f);
		columnWidthInitialized = true;
	}

	// ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒXì¬
	canvas.Begin("StateChildCanvas");

	// íœ‚â“Ç‚İ‚İ‚ÌŒã‚Íƒm[ƒhƒGƒfƒBƒ^‘¤‚Éc‚Á‚½‘I‘ğ‚ğÁ‚·
	if (clearSelectionRequested)
	{
		ed::ClearSelection();
		clearSelectionRequested = false;
	}

	// ƒm[ƒh‚Ì‹éŒ`‚Í–ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ì‚è’¼‚·
	nodeRects.clear();

	// ƒm[ƒh•`‰æ
	DrawEntryNodes();
	DrawClipNodes();

	// ˆÊ’u‚Ì”½‰f‚ÍAŠJ‚¢‚½’¼Œã‚Ì1ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚¾‚¯
	needsApplyPositions = false;

	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN•`‰æ
	DrawLinks();
	DrawPendingLink();

	// ƒƒjƒ…[•`‰æ
	DrawBackgroundMenu();
	DrawNodeMenu();

	// “ü—ÍXV
	UpdateLinkCreation();
	UpdateSelection();

	// ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒXI—¹
	canvas.End();

	ImGui::NextColumn();

	// ‰EFƒCƒ“ƒXƒyƒNƒ^[
	DrawInspector(animations);

	// ‘I‘ğ‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é•¨‚Ìíœ(•¶š‚ğ“ü—Í’†‚Íœ‚­)
	if (ImGui::IsWindowFocused(ImGuiFocusedFlags_RootAndChildWindows) &&
		!ImGui::GetIO().WantTextInput &&
		ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Delete))
	{
		DeleteSelection(data);
	}

	ImGui::Columns(1);
}

// ‘I‘ğ‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é•¨‚ğíœ
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DeleteSelection(StateMachineData& data)
{
	// ‘I‘ğ’†‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğíœ
	if (selectedLinkId != -1)
	{
		const auto& entryLinks = state->child.entryLinks;
		bool isEntryLink = std::any_of(entryLinks.begin(), entryLinks.end(),
			[this](const EntryLink& l) { return l.id == selectedLinkId; });

		if (isEntryLink) data.RemoveEntryLink(stateId, selectedLinkId);
		else             data.RemoveClipLink(stateId, selectedLinkId);

		selectedLinkId = -1;
		return;
	}

	// ‘I‘ğ’†‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ğíœ (Entry‚Ííœ•s‰Â)
	for (int key : selectedNodeKeys)
	{
		if (!IsEntry(key))
		{
			data.RemoveClip(stateId, key);
		}
	}
	selectedNodeKeys.clear();
	clearSelectionRequested = true;
}

// “ü‚Á‚Ä‚­‚éƒŠƒ“ƒN‚©‚çƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚Ìˆê——‚ğì‚é
void StudioStateChildCanvas::CollectEntries()
{
	entries.clear();

	// e‚ÌEntry‚©‚ç“ü‚Á‚Ä‚­‚éƒŠƒ“ƒN
	for (const auto& t : data->entry.transitions)
	{
		if (t.toStateId == stateId) entries.push_back({ t.id, StateMachineData::kEntryId });
	}

	// ŠeState‚©‚ç“ü‚Á‚Ä‚­‚éƒŠƒ“ƒN
	for (const auto& s : data->states)
	{
		for (const auto& t : s.transitions)
		{
			if (t.toStateId == stateId) entries.push_back({ t.id, s.id });
		}
	}
}

// ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[ƒm[ƒh•`‰æ
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawEntryNodes()
{
	for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(entries.size()); ++i)
	{
		const EntryInfo& e = entries[i];
		ed::NodeId nodeId(static_cast<uint64_t>(e.transitionId));

		// ˆÊ’u‚ª‚Ü‚¾–³‚¯‚ê‚ÎA¶‘¤‚Éc‚É•À‚×‚Ä’u‚­
		auto& posMap = state->child.entryEditorPos;
		if (posMap.find(e.transitionId) == posMap.end())
		{
			posMap[e.transitionId] = { 50.0f, 100.0f + 80.0f * i };
			ed::SetNodePosition(nodeId, ImVec2(posMap[e.transitionId].x, posMap[e.transitionId].y));
		}
		else if (needsApplyPositions)
		{
			ed::SetNodePosition(nodeId, ImVec2(posMap[e.transitionId].x, posMap[e.transitionId].y));
		}

		// ‚Ç‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚É‚à‚Â‚È‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚ÍA˜g‚ğÔ‚­‚·‚é
		bool connected = std::any_of(state->child.entryLinks.begin(), state->child.entryLinks.end(),
			[&e](const EntryLink& l) { return l.entryTransitionId == e.transitionId; });

		// ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚Í—Î‚Å•`‚­
		ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBg, ImColor(46, 125, 50, 255));
		ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBorder, connected ? ImColor(27, 94, 32, 255) : ImColor(230, 90, 90, 255));
		ed::BeginNode(nodeId);
		ImGui::Text("From: %s", GetSourceName(e.fromKey).c_str());
		ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(80.0f, 0.0f)); // ƒm[ƒh‚ÌÅ¬‚Ì•
		ed::EndNode();
		ed::PopStyleColor(2);

		// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÌŒvZ‚Ég‚¤‹éŒ`‚ğŠo‚¦‚Ä‚¨‚­
		nodeRects[e.transitionId] = { ImGui::GetItemRectMin(), ImGui::GetItemRectMax() };

		// ƒm[ƒh‚ğ“®‚©‚µ‚½‚çAƒf[ƒ^‚ÌˆÊ’u‚É‘‚«–ß‚·
		ImVec2 pos = ed::GetNodePosition(nodeId);
		posMap[e.transitionId] = { pos.x, pos.y };
	}
}

// ƒNƒŠƒbƒvƒm[ƒh•`‰æ
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawClipNodes()
{
	for (auto& c : state->child.clips)
	{
		ed::NodeId nodeId(static_cast<uint64_t>(c.id));

		// “Ç‚İ‚İ’¼Œã‚Íƒf[ƒ^‚ÌˆÊ’u‚Éƒm[ƒh‚ğ”½‰f
		if (needsApplyPositions)
		{
			ed::SetNodePosition(nodeId, ImVec2(c.editorPos.x, c.editorPos.y));
		}

		ed::BeginNode(nodeId);
		ImGui::Text("%s", c.name.c_str());
		// Ä¶‚·‚éƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“(–¢İ’è‚È‚çŠDF‚Å•\¦)
		ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", c.clipName.empty() ? "(no clip)" : c.clipName.c_str());
		ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(80.0f, 0.0f)); // ƒm[ƒh‚ÌÅ¬‚Ì•
		ed::EndNode();

		// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÌŒvZ‚Ég‚¤‹éŒ`‚ğ‹L˜^
		nodeRects[c.id] = { ImGui::GetItemRectMin(), ImGui::GetItemRectMax() };

		// ƒm[ƒh‚ğ“®‚©‚µ‚½‚çAƒf[ƒ^‚ÌˆÊ’u‚É‘‚«–ß‚·
		ImVec2 pos = ed::GetNodePosition(nodeId);
		c.editorPos = { pos.x, pos.y };
	}
}

// ƒŠƒ“ƒN•`‰æ
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawLinks()
{
	ImDrawList* drawList = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();

	// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN”»’è—p‚Ìü‚Í–ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ì‚è’¼‚·
	linkSegments.clear();

	// 2ŒÂ‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚ÌŠÔ‚É•¡”ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ª‚ ‚é‚Æ‚«‰½–{–Ú‚©‹L˜^‚·‚é
	std::unordered_map<int64_t, int> laneCounts;

	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN1–{•ª‚ğ•`‚­
	auto drawOne = [&](int linkId, int fromKey, int toKey, bool hasReverse, bool always, const ConditionGraph& condition)
		{
			// ‘JˆÚŒ³‚Æ‘JˆÚæ‚Ì‘g‚İ‡‚í‚¹‚ğ1‚Â‚Ì’l‚É‚Ü‚Æ‚ß‚Ä‰½–{–Ú‚©‚ğ”‚¦‚é
			int64_t pairKey = (static_cast<int64_t>(fromKey) << 32) | static_cast<uint32_t>(toKey);
			int lane = laneCounts[pairKey]++;

			ImVec2 start, end;
			if (!CalcLinkPoints(fromKey, toKey, hasReverse, lane, start, end)) return;

			bool selected = (linkId == selectedLinkId);
			bool invalid = (!always && condition.IsEmpty());

			// ³í(”’F) ğŒ‚È‚µ(ÔF) ‘I‘ğ’†(‰©F)
			ImU32 color = IM_COL32(210, 210, 210, 255);
			if (invalid)  color = IM_COL32(230, 90, 90, 255);
			if (selected) color = IM_COL32(255, 200, 80, 255);

			StateGraphDraw::DrawArrow(drawList, start, end, color, selected ? 3.0f : 2.0f);

			// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN”»’è‚Ì‚½‚ß‚É•`‚¢‚½ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğ‹L˜^
			linkSegments.push_back({ linkId, start, end });
		};

	// Entry‚©‚ço‚éƒŠƒ“ƒN
	for (const auto& l : state->child.entryLinks)
	{
		drawOne(l.id, l.entryTransitionId, l.toClipId, false, l.always, l.condition);
	}

	// ŠeState‚©‚ço‚éƒŠƒ“ƒN
	for (const auto& l : state->child.clipLinks)
	{
		drawOne(l.id, l.fromClipId, l.toClipId, HasClipLink(l.toClipId, l.fromClipId), l.always, l.condition);
	}
}

// ì¬’†‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN•`‰æ
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawPendingLink()
{
	if (pendingFromKey == -1) return;

	auto it = nodeRects.find(pendingFromKey);
	if (it == nodeRects.end()) return;

	// ‘JˆÚŒ³‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚Ì‰‚©‚çAƒ}ƒEƒX‚ÌˆÊ’u‚Ü‚Åü‚ğˆø‚­
	ImVec2 mouse = ImGui::GetMousePos();
	ImVec2 start = StateGraphDraw::GetEdgePoint(it->second.first, it->second.second, mouse);
	StateGraphDraw::DrawArrow(ImGui::GetWindowDrawList(), start, mouse, IM_COL32(255, 200, 80, 255), 2.0f);
}

// ”wŒi‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Ìƒƒjƒ…[
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawBackgroundMenu()
{
	ed::Suspend();

	// ƒ|ƒbƒv‚ğŠJ‚­ƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg
	if (ed::ShowBackgroundContextMenu())
	{
		pendingCreatePos = ImGui::GetMousePos(); // ƒ}ƒEƒXˆÊ’u•Û‘¶
		ImGui::OpenPopup("StateChildBackgroundMenu");
	}

	// ƒ|ƒbƒv“à—e
	if (ImGui::BeginPopup("StateChildBackgroundMenu"))
	{
		// ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚µ‚½ˆÊ’u‚ÉƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ğì‚é
		if (ImGui::MenuItem("Clip"))
		{
			ImVec2 canvasPos = ed::ScreenToCanvas(pendingCreatePos);
			int newId = data->AddClip(stateId, "New Clip", { canvasPos.x, canvasPos.y });
			if (newId != -1)
			{
				ed::SetNodePosition(ed::NodeId(static_cast<uint64_t>(newId)), canvasPos);
			}
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}

	ed::Resume();
}

// ƒm[ƒh‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Ìƒƒjƒ…[
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawNodeMenu()
{
	ed::Suspend();

	ed::NodeId nodeId;
	if (ed::ShowNodeContextMenu(&nodeId))
	{
		// ƒƒjƒ…[‚ğ•Â‚¶‚é‚Ü‚Å‚Ç‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚Ìƒƒjƒ…[‚©‚ğ‹L˜^
		contextNodeKey = static_cast<int>(nodeId.Get());

		// ƒ|ƒbƒv‚ğŠJ‚­ƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg
		ImGui::OpenPopup("StateChildNodeMenu");
	}

	if (ImGui::BeginPopup("StateChildNodeMenu"))
	{
		// ƒŠƒ“ƒNì¬ŠJn
		if (ImGui::MenuItem("Link"))
		{
			pendingFromKey = contextNodeKey;
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}

	ed::Resume();
}

// ƒŠƒ“ƒNì¬’†‚Ì“ü—Í
void StudioStateChildCanvas::UpdateLinkCreation()
{
	if (pendingFromKey == -1) return;

	// Esc‚Åæ‚èÁ‚µ
	if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Escape))
	{
		pendingFromKey = -1;
		return;
	}

	// 
	if (!ImGui::IsMouseClicked(ImGuiMouseButton_Left)) return;

	// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚µ‚½ˆÊ’u‚Éƒm[ƒh‚ª‚ ‚ê‚Î‚»‚±‚ğs‚«æ‚É‚·‚é
	ed::NodeId hovered = ed::GetHoveredNode();
	if (hovered)
	{
		int toKey = static_cast<int>(hovered.Get());

		// Entry‚Ö‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÆA©•ª©g‚Ö‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Íì‚ç‚È‚¢
		if (!IsEntry(toKey) && toKey != pendingFromKey)
		{
			int newId = IsEntry(pendingFromKey)
				? data->AddEntryLink(stateId, pendingFromKey, toKey)  // ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[ ‚©‚ç ƒNƒŠƒbƒv
				: data->AddClipLink(stateId, pendingFromKey, toKey);  // ƒNƒŠƒbƒv ‚©‚ç ƒNƒŠƒbƒv

			if (newId != -1)
			{
				// ì‚Á‚½ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğ‘I‘ğó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
				selectedLinkId = newId;
				selectedNodeKeys.clear();
				ed::ClearSelection();
			}
		}
	}

	// ƒm[ƒhˆÈŠO‚ğƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚µ‚½ê‡‚àAì¬‚ÍI‚í‚é
	pendingFromKey = -1;
}

// ‘I‘ğ‚ÌXV
void StudioStateChildCanvas::UpdateSelection()
{
	// ƒm[ƒh‚Ì‘I‘ğ
	int count = ed::GetSelectedObjectCount();
	std::vector<ed::NodeId> nodeIds(count > 0 ? count : 1);
	int nodeCount = (count > 0) ? ed::GetSelectedNodes(nodeIds.data(), count) : 0;

	std::vector<int> keys;
	for (int i = 0; i < nodeCount; ++i)
	{
		int key = static_cast<int>(nodeIds[i].Get());
		// íœ‚µ‚½’¼Œã‚È‚Ç‚ÅAƒf[ƒ^‚É–³‚¢ƒm[ƒh‚ªc‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚Ä‚à‘I‘ğ‚Æ‚µ‚Äˆµ‚í‚È‚¢
		if (IsExistingNode(key)) keys.push_back(key);
	}

	if (!keys.empty())
	{
		selectedNodeKeys = std::move(keys);
		selectedLinkId = -1;
	}
	else if (selectedLinkId == -1)
	{
		// ƒm[ƒh‚àƒŠƒ“ƒN‚à‘I‚Î‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢
		selectedNodeKeys.clear();
	}

	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Ì”»’è
	if (pendingFromKey != -1) return;
	if (!ed::IsBackgroundClicked()) return;

	// ƒm[ƒhƒGƒfƒBƒ^‚Ì’†‚Å‚ÍAƒ}ƒEƒX‚ÌˆÊ’u‚ÍƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒX‚ÌÀ•W‚Å•Ô‚é(ü‚ÌÀ•W‚Æ“¯‚¶)
	ImVec2 mouse = ImGui::GetMousePos();

	// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚µ‚½ˆÊ’u‚©‚çˆê”Ô‹ß‚¢ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğ’T‚·(‰“‚·‚¬‚éê‡‚Í‘I‚Î‚È‚¢)
	const float hitDistance = 6.0f;
	int hitId = -1;
	float best = hitDistance;
	for (const auto& seg : linkSegments)
	{
		float d = StateGraphDraw::DistanceToSegment(mouse, seg.start, seg.end);
		if (d < best)
		{
			best = d;
			hitId = seg.linkId;
		}
	}

	// ü‚Ì‚È‚¢‚Æ‚±‚ë‚ğƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚µ‚½‚ç‘I‘ğ‰ğœ
	selectedLinkId = hitId;
	if (hitId != -1)
	{
		// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğ‘I‚ñ‚¾‚çAƒm[ƒh‚Ì‘I‘ğ‚ÍŠO‚·
		selectedNodeKeys.clear();
		ed::ClearSelection();
	}
}

// ƒf[ƒ^‚É‘¶İ‚·‚éƒm[ƒh‚©”»’è
bool StudioStateChildCanvas::IsExistingNode(int key) const
{
	return IsEntry(key) || state->child.FindClip(key) != nullptr;
}
// ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚©”»’è
bool StudioStateChildCanvas::IsEntry(int key) const
{
	return std::any_of(entries.begin(), entries.end(),
		[key](const EntryInfo& e) { return e.transitionId == key; });
}

// ƒCƒ“ƒXƒyƒNƒ^[•`‰æ
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawInspector(const std::vector<std::string>& animations)
{
	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‘I‘ğ’†
	if (selectedLinkId != -1)
	{
		DrawLinkInspector(selectedLinkId);
		return;
	}

	// ƒm[ƒh•¡”‘I‘ğ
	if (selectedNodeKeys.size() > 1)
	{
		DrawMultiInspector();
		return;
	}

	// ƒm[ƒh1‚Â‘I‘ğ
	if (selectedNodeKeys.size() == 1)
	{
		int key = selectedNodeKeys[0];
		if (IsEntry(key))
		{
			DrawEntryInspector(key);
			return;
		}
		for (auto& c : state->child.clips)
		{
			if (c.id == key)
			{
				DrawClipInspector(c, animations);
				return;
			}
		}
	}

	// –¢‘I‘ğ
	ImGui::TextDisabled("Nothing selected.");
}

// ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚Ìİ’è
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawEntryInspector(int transitionId)
{
	ImGui::Text("Entry");
	ImGui::Separator();

	// ‚Ç‚±‚©‚ç“ü‚Á‚Ä‚­‚é‚©
	int fromKey = -1;
	for (const auto& e : entries)
	{
		if (e.transitionId == transitionId) fromKey = e.fromKey;
	}
	ImGui::Text("From: %s", GetSourceName(fromKey).c_str());

	// ƒuƒŒƒ“ƒh•\¦
	if (const StateTransition* t = std::as_const(*data).FindTransition(transitionId))
	{
		if (t->blend.enabled) ImGui::TextDisabled("Blend: %.2f s (parent link)", t->blend.duration);
		else                  ImGui::TextDisabled("Blend: Off (parent link)");
	}
	ImGui::Separator();

	// o‚Ä‚¢‚­ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìˆê——
	DrawOutLinkList(transitionId, true);
}

// ƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚Ìİ’è
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawClipInspector(ClipNode& clip, const std::vector<std::string>& animations)
{
	ImGui::Text("Clip");
	ImGui::Separator();

	// ‘I‚ÔƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚½‚çA“ü—Í—“‚ğ¡‚Ì–¼‘O‚Åì‚è’¼‚·
	if (nameEditingClipId != clip.id)
	{
		nameEditingClipId = clip.id;
		strncpy_s(nameBuffer, clip.name.c_str(), sizeof(nameBuffer) - 1);
	}

	// –¼‘O—“
	if (ImGui::InputText("Name", nameBuffer, sizeof(nameBuffer), ImGuiInputTextFlags_EnterReturnsTrue))
	{
		if (nameBuffer[0] != '\0') clip.name = nameBuffer;
	}
	// “ü—Í—“‚©‚ç—£‚ê‚½‚çAŠm’è‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢•¶š‚Í¡‚Ì–¼‘O‚É–ß‚·
	if (ImGui::IsItemDeactivated() && clip.name != nameBuffer)
	{
		strncpy_s(nameBuffer, clip.name.c_str(), sizeof(nameBuffer) - 1);
	}
	ImGui::Separator();

	// Ä¶‚·‚éƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“
	DrawAnimationList(clip, animations);
	ImGui::Separator();

	// Ä¶‚Ìİ’è
	ImGui::Checkbox("Loop", &clip.loop);
	ImGui::Checkbox("Root Motion", &clip.rootMotion);
	ImGui::Separator();

	// o‚Ä‚¢‚­ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìˆê——
	DrawOutLinkList(clip.id, false);

	// íœ
	if (StateGraphDraw::DeleteButton("Delete Clip"))
	{
		DeleteSelection(*data);
	}
}

// •¡”‘I‘ğ
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawMultiInspector()
{
	ImGui::Text("%d nodes selected", static_cast<int>(selectedNodeKeys.size()));
	ImGui::Separator();

	// ‚Ü‚Æ‚ß‚Äíœ
	if (StateGraphDraw::DeleteButton("Delete Clips"))
	{
		DeleteSelection(*data);
	}
}

// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìİ’è
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawLinkInspector(int linkId)
{
	EntryLink* entryLink = FindEntryLink(linkId);
	ClipLink* clipLink = FindClipLink(linkId);
	if (!entryLink && !clipLink)
	{
		ImGui::TextDisabled("Nothing selected.");
		return;
	}

	ImGui::Text("Link");
	ImGui::Separator();

	// Œ³ ¨ æ ‚ÆA“¯‚¶‘JˆÚŒ³‚Ì’†‚Å‰½”Ô–Ú‚©
	int index = 0;
	int count = 0;
	if (entryLink)
	{
		ImGui::Text("Entry(%s)  ->  %s",
			GetSourceName([&] { for (const auto& e : entries) if (e.transitionId == entryLink->entryTransitionId) return e.fromKey; return -1; }()).c_str(),
			GetClipName(entryLink->toClipId).c_str());

		for (const auto& l : state->child.entryLinks)
		{
			if (l.entryTransitionId != entryLink->entryTransitionId) continue;
			if (l.id == linkId) index = count;
			++count;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		ImGui::Text("%s  ->  %s", GetClipName(clipLink->fromClipId).c_str(), GetClipName(clipLink->toClipId).c_str());

		for (const auto& l : state->child.clipLinks)
		{
			if (l.fromClipId != clipLink->fromClipId) continue;
			if (l.id == linkId) index = count;
			++count;
		}
	}

	// —Dæ“x
	ImGui::Text("Priority: %d / %d", index + 1, count);
	ImGui::Spacing();

	// í‚É¬—§
	bool& always = entryLink ? entryLink->always : clipLink->always;
	ImGui::Checkbox("Always", &always);

	// ƒuƒŒƒ“ƒh
	if (clipLink)
	{
		ImGui::Checkbox("Blend", &clipLink->blend.enabled);
		ImGui::BeginDisabled(!clipLink->blend.enabled);
		ImGui::DragFloat("Blend Duration", &clipLink->blend.duration, 0.01f, 0.0f, 5.0f, "%.2f s");
		ImGui::EndDisabled();
	}
	ImGui::Spacing();

	// ğŒ‚Ìó‘Ô
	const ConditionGraph& condition = entryLink ? entryLink->condition : clipLink->condition;
	if (always)
	{
		ImGui::TextDisabled("Condition: Always");
	}
	else if (condition.IsEmpty())
	{
		ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(1.0f, 0.4f, 0.4f, 1.0f), "Condition: Empty");
	}
	else
	{
		ImGui::Text("Condition: Set");
	}

	// ğŒ‚Ì•ÒW‚Ö
	if (ImGui::Button("Edit Condition", ImVec2(-1.0f, 0.0f)))
	{
		ConditionTarget::Kind kind = entryLink ? ConditionTarget::Kind::EntryLink : ConditionTarget::Kind::ClipLink;
		editConditionRequest = { kind, stateId, linkId };
	}

	// íœ
	if (StateGraphDraw::DeleteButton("Delete Link"))
	{
		DeleteSelection(*data);
	}

	// “¯‚¶ŠK‘w‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚©‚çAİ’è‚ğÊ‚·‚½‚ß‚ÌƒŠƒXƒg
	ConditionTarget::Kind kind = entryLink ? ConditionTarget::Kind::EntryLink : ConditionTarget::Kind::ClipLink;
	ConditionTarget self{ kind, stateId, linkId };
	ImGui::Spacing();
	ImGui::Separator();
	ImGui::Text("Copy From");

	// ó‚¯Œû‚É—‚Æ‚³‚ê‚½‚çAÊ‚·ˆË—Š‚ğo‚·
	ConditionTarget source = ConditionCopyList::DrawDropTarget(self);
	if (source.IsValid()) copyRequest = { source, self };

	// Ê‚·Œ³‚ÌƒŠƒXƒg
	ConditionCopyList::Draw(ConditionCopyList::CollectChild(*data, self));
}

// ƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ÌƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ˆê——
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawAnimationList(ClipNode& clip, const std::vector<std::string>& animations)
{
	ImGui::Text("Animation");

	// ˆê——‚ğ•\¦‚·‚éƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚½‚çA¡‚ÌƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ÌˆÊ’u‚ÖƒXƒNƒ[ƒ‹‚·‚é
	if (animationListClipId != clip.id)
	{
		animationListClipId = clip.id;
		scrollToCurrent = true;
	}

	// İ’è‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚é–¼‘O‚ªAƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚ÌƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚É–³‚¢
	bool missing = !clip.clipName.empty() &&
		std::find(animations.begin(), animations.end(), clip.clipName) == animations.end();
	if (missing)
	{
		ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(1.0f, 0.4f, 0.4f, 1.0f), "%s (missing)", clip.clipName.c_str());
	}

	// i‚è‚İ‚Ì•¶š
	std::string filter = animationFilter;
	std::transform(filter.begin(), filter.end(), filter.begin(),
		[](unsigned char ch) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(ch)); });

	// ˆê——•\¦
	ImGui::BeginChild("##AnimationList", ImVec2(0.0f, 200.0f), true);
	for (const auto& name : animations)
	{
		if (!filter.empty())
		{
			std::string lower = name;
			std::transform(lower.begin(), lower.end(), lower.begin(),
				[](unsigned char ch) { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(ch)); });
			if (lower.find(filter) == std::string::npos) continue;
		}

		bool current = (name == clip.clipName);

		// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ÅŠm’è
		if (ImGui::Selectable(name.c_str(), current))
		{
			clip.clipName = name;
		}

		// ¡‚ÌƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ªŒ©‚¦‚éˆÊ’u‚Ü‚ÅƒXƒNƒ[ƒ‹‚·‚é
		if (current && scrollToCurrent)
		{
			ImGui::SetScrollHereY(0.5f);
		}
	}
	ImGui::EndChild();
	scrollToCurrent = false;

	// ŒŸõ—““ü—Í
	ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1.0f);
	if (ImGui::InputTextWithHint("##AnimationFilter", "Filter...", animationFilter, sizeof(animationFilter)))
	{
		// i‚è‚İ‚ğ•Ï‚¦‚½‚çA¡‚ÌƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ªc‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚Î‚»‚ÌˆÊ’u‚Ö
		scrollToCurrent = true;
	}

}

// o‚Ä‚¢‚­ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìˆê——
void StudioStateChildCanvas::DrawOutLinkList(int fromKey, bool fromEntry)
{
	ImGui::Text("Links (priority order)");

	// “ü‚ê‘Ö‚¦‚Í•`‰æ‚ªI‚í‚Á‚Ä‚©‚ç
	int moveId = -1;
	int moveOffset = 0;
	int number = 0;

	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN1–{•ª
	auto drawRow = [&](int linkId, int toClipId, bool always, const ConditionGraph& condition)
		{
			ImGui::PushID(linkId);

			// ã‰º‚Å—Dæ“x‚ğ“ü‚ê‘Ö‚¦‚é
			if (ImGui::ArrowButton("##Up", ImGuiDir_Up)) { moveId = linkId; moveOffset = -1; }
			ImGui::SameLine();
			if (ImGui::ArrowButton("##Down", ImGuiDir_Down)) { moveId = linkId; moveOffset = +1; }
			ImGui::SameLine();

			// ğŒ‚Ìİ’è˜R‚ê‚ÍÔ‚Å•\¦
			bool invalid = (!always && condition.IsEmpty());
			if (invalid) ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_Text, ImVec4(1.0f, 0.4f, 0.4f, 1.0f));

			// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Å‚»‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğ‘I‚Ô
			std::string label = std::to_string(++number) + ". -> " + GetClipName(toClipId);
			if (ImGui::Selectable(label.c_str(), linkId == selectedLinkId))
			{
				selectedLinkId = linkId;
				selectedNodeKeys.clear();
				clearSelectionRequested = true; // ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒX‘¤‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚Ì‘I‘ğ‚àŠO‚·
			}

			if (invalid) ImGui::PopStyleColor();
			ImGui::PopID();
		};

	// ‘JˆÚŒ³‚É‡‚¤ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğA”z—ñ‚Ì•À‚Ñ‡‚É•`‚­
	if (fromEntry)
	{
		for (const auto& l : state->child.entryLinks)
		{
			if (l.entryTransitionId == fromKey) drawRow(l.id, l.toClipId, l.always, l.condition);
		}
	}
	else
	{
		for (const auto& l : state->child.clipLinks)
		{
			if (l.fromClipId == fromKey) drawRow(l.id, l.toClipId, l.always, l.condition);
		}
	}

	if (number == 0)
	{
		ImGui::TextDisabled("No links.");
	}

	// •`‰æ‚ªI‚í‚Á‚Ä‚©‚ç“ü‚ê‘Ö‚¦‚é
	if (moveId != -1)
	{
		if (fromEntry) data->MoveEntryLink(stateId, moveId, moveOffset);
		else           data->MoveClipLink(stateId, moveId, moveOffset);
	}
}

// ID‚©‚çƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğ’T‚·
EntryLink* StudioStateChildCanvas::FindEntryLink(int linkId)
{
	for (auto& l : state->child.entryLinks)
	{
		if (l.id == linkId) return &l;
	}
	return nullptr;
}
ClipLink* StudioStateChildCanvas::FindClipLink(int linkId)
{
	for (auto& l : state->child.clipLinks)
	{
		if (l.id == linkId) return &l;
	}
	return nullptr;
}

// ‘JˆÚŒ³‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚ÌƒL[‚©‚çA•\¦—p‚Ì–¼‘O‚ğì‚é
std::string StudioStateChildCanvas::GetSourceName(int fromKey) const
{
	if (fromKey == StateMachineData::kEntryId) return "Entry";
	if (const StateNode* s = std::as_const(*data).FindState(fromKey)) return s->name;
	return "(missing)";
}

// ƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ÌID‚©‚çA•\¦—p‚Ì–¼‘O‚ğì‚é
std::string StudioStateChildCanvas::GetClipName(int clipId) const
{
	if (const ClipNode* c = state->child.FindClip(clipId)) return c->name;
	return "(missing)";
}

// fromClipId‚©‚çtoClipId‚Ö‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ª‚ ‚é‚©
bool StudioStateChildCanvas::HasClipLink(int fromClipId, int toClipId) const
{
	return std::any_of(state->child.clipLinks.begin(), state->child.clipLinks.end(),
		[=](const ClipLink& l) { return l.fromClipId == fromClipId && l.toClipId == toClipId; });
}

// ü‚Ìn“_‚ÆI“_‚ğ‹‚ß‚é
bool StudioStateChildCanvas::CalcLinkPoints(int fromKey, int toKey, bool hasReverse, int lane, ImVec2& outStart, ImVec2& outEnd) const
{
	auto fromIt = nodeRects.find(fromKey);
	auto toIt = nodeRects.find(toKey);
	if (fromIt == nodeRects.end() || toIt == nodeRects.end()) return false;

	StateGraphDraw::CalcLinkPoints(
		fromIt->second.first, fromIt->second.second, fromKey,
		toIt->second.first, toIt->second.second, toKey,
		hasReverse, lane,
		outStart, outEnd);
	return true;
}

#include "StudioStateGraph.h"
#include <fstream>
#include <filesystem>

namespace
{
	// Ÿ‚Éì‚éƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚Ì‚¿å‚ğAStudio‚Ìƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒg‚É‚·‚é
	void SetNextWindowOwner(ImGuiID ownerViewportId)
	{
		if (ownerViewportId == 0) return;

		ImGuiWindowClass windowClass;
		windowClass.ParentViewportId = ownerViewportId;
		ImGui::SetNextWindowClass(&windowClass);
	}

	// ReturnˆÈŠO‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚ª‚ ‚é‚©(Ê‚·‚ÆÁ‚¦‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤•¨‚ª‚ ‚é‚©)
	bool HasNodesBesidesReturn(const ConditionGraph& condition)
	{
		return !condition.variableNodes.empty()
			|| !condition.getterNodes.empty()
			|| !condition.operatorNodes.empty()
			|| !condition.logicNodes.empty()
			|| !condition.notNodes.empty();
	}
}

void StudioStateGraph::Initialize()
{
	// eŠK‘wƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒX‰Šú‰»
	parentCanvas.Initialize();
	// ğŒ‚ÌƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒX‰Šú‰»
	conditionCanvas.Initialize();
}

void StudioStateGraph::DrawGUI(entt::registry& registry, StudioContext& context)
{
	// q‚ÌƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚É“n‚·ƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚Ìˆê——
	RefreshAnimationNames(registry);

	// •R‚Ã‚¢‚½ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚½‚ç“Ç‚İ‚Ş
	if (!loaded && !context.attachedGraphPath.empty())
	{
		Load(context);
		loaded = true;
	}

	std::string panelName = "StateGraph##" + std::to_string(windowId);
	bool open = ImGui::Begin(panelName.c_str());

	// StaticModel‚Í’e‚­
	if (open && !HasAnimatedModel(registry))
	{
		ImGui::Text("No skinned model.");
	}
	else if (open)
	{
		DrawToolbar(context);

		ImGui::Columns(2, "StateGraphColumns", true);
		if (!columnWidthInitialized)
		{
			ImGui::SetColumnWidth(0, ImGui::GetWindowWidth() * 0.75f);
			columnWidthInitialized = true;
		}

		// ¶FƒOƒ‰ƒt
		parentCanvas.Draw(data);
		DrawDropTarget(context);

		ImGui::NextColumn();

		// ‰EFƒCƒ“ƒXƒyƒNƒ^[
		parentCanvas.DrawInspector(data);

		// ‘I‘ğ‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é•¨‚Ìíœ(•¶š‚ğ“ü—Í’†‚ÍADeleteƒL[‚ğ•¶š‚Ìíœ‚Ég‚¤‚Ì‚Åœ‚­)
		if (ImGui::IsWindowFocused(ImGuiFocusedFlags_RootAndChildWindows) &&
			!ImGui::GetIO().WantTextInput &&
			ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Delete))
		{
			parentCanvas.DeleteSelection(data);
		}

		ImGui::Columns(1);

		// eŠK‘w‚©‚çqŠK‘w‚ÌƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒX‚ğŠJ‚­ˆË—Š‚ª‚ ‚ê‚ÎŠJ‚­
		int openRequest = parentCanvas.ConsumeOpenChildRequest();
		if (openRequest != -1) OpenChildWindow(openRequest);

		// e‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÌğŒ‚ğ•ÒW‚·‚éˆË—Š‚ª‚ ‚ê‚ÎŠJ‚­
		ConditionTarget conditionRequest = parentCanvas.ConsumeEditConditionRequest();
		if (conditionRequest.IsValid()) OpenConditionWindow(conditionRequest);

		// e‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìİ’è‚ğÊ‚·ˆË—Š
		ConditionCopyRequest copyRequest = parentCanvas.ConsumeCopyRequest();
		if (copyRequest.IsValid()) RequestCopy(copyRequest);
	}

	ImGui::End();

	// qŠK‘wƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE
	DrawChildWindows(context.studioViewportId);
	// ğŒ‚ÌƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE
	DrawConditionWindow(context.studioViewportId);

	// ã‘‚«‚ÌŠm”F
	DrawCopyConfirmPopup();
}

// ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚½‚Æ‚«‚ÉŒÄ‚Ô
void StudioStateGraph::OnModelChanged(StudioContext& context)
{
	data.Clear();

	// ‘I‘ğ‚È‚Ç‚ğ–ß‚·
	parentCanvas.Reset();
	childCanvases.clear();
	CloseConditionWindow();
	pendingCopy = {};
	openCopyConfirm = false;

	context.attachedGraphPath.clear();
	loaded = true;     // ‹ó‚ÌƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ğA“Ç‚İ‚İÏ‚İ‚Æ‚µ‚Äˆµ‚¤
	hasErrors = false; // ì‚èn‚ß‚È‚Ì‚ÅAŒŸØ‚Í•Û‘¶‚És‚¤
	message.clear();
}

// ƒc[ƒ‹ƒo[
void StudioStateGraph::DrawToolbar(StudioContext& context)
{
	if (ImGui::Button("Save"))
	{
		Save(context);
	}
	ImGui::SameLine();
	if (!context.attachedGraphPath.empty())
	{
		ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", context.attachedGraphPath.c_str());
	}
	else
	{
		ImGui::TextDisabled("Not saved yet.");
	}

	// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚Ì“Ç‚İ‘‚«‚Ì¸”s‚È‚Ç
	if (!message.empty())
	{
		ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(1.0f, 0.4f, 0.4f, 1.0f), "%s", message.c_str());
	}
	// ŒŸØƒGƒ‰[
	if (hasErrors)
	{
		ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(1.0f, 0.8f, 0.3f, 1.0f), "Validation errors. See Log.");
	}

	ImGui::Separator();
}

// .stategraph‚Ìƒhƒƒbƒvó‚¯Œû
void StudioStateGraph::DrawDropTarget(StudioContext& context)
{
	if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget())
	{
		if (const ImGuiPayload* payload = ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("STATEGRAPH_PATH"))
		{
			std::string path(static_cast<const char*>(payload->Data));
			TryOpen(path, context);
		}
		ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
	}
}

// ƒhƒƒbƒv‚³‚ê‚½ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ªŠJ‚¯‚é‚©Šm”F
void StudioStateGraph::TryOpen(const std::string& path, StudioContext& context)
{
	std::ifstream ifs(path);
	if (!ifs.is_open())
	{
		message = "Failed to open file.";
		return;
	}

	std::string sourceModelPath;
	try
	{
		nlohmann::json j;
		ifs >> j;
		sourceModelPath = j.value("sourceModelPath", "");
	}
	catch (const std::exception&)
	{
		message = "Invalid json.";
		return;
	}

	// •Ê‚Ìƒ‚ƒfƒ‹—p‚ÌƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ÍŠJ‚©‚È‚¢
	if (!sourceModelPath.empty() && sourceModelPath != context.currentModelPath)
	{
		message = "This graph belongs to a different model.";
		return;
	}

	message.clear();
	context.attachedGraphPath = path;
	loaded = false; // Ÿ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Å“Ç‚İ‚Ş
}

// •Û‘¶
void StudioStateGraph::Save(StudioContext& context)
{
	// ‚Ü‚¾•Û‘¶æ‚ª–³‚¯‚ê‚Î ©“®‚ÅŒˆ‚ß‚é
	if (context.attachedGraphPath.empty())
	{
		context.attachedGraphPath = BuildNewSavePath(context);
		loaded = true; // ¡‚Ìƒf[ƒ^‚ª‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü•Û‘¶æ‚Ì“à—e‚É‚È‚é‚Ì‚ÅA“Ç‚İ’¼‚³‚È‚¢
	}

	// ì‚è‚©‚¯‚Å‚à•Û‘¶‚Í‚Å‚«‚éAƒGƒ‰[‚Ì—L–³‚Íƒc[ƒ‹ƒo[‚É•\¦‚·‚é
	hasErrors = !data.Validate(context.attachedGraphPath);

	nlohmann::ordered_json j = data.ToJson();
	j["sourceModelPath"] = context.currentModelPath;

	std::ofstream ofs(context.attachedGraphPath);
	if (!ofs.is_open())
	{
		message = "Failed to save file.";
		return;
	}
	ofs << j.dump(4);
	message.clear();
}
// “Ç‚İ‚İ
void StudioStateGraph::Load(StudioContext& context)
{
	std::ifstream ifs(context.attachedGraphPath);
	if (!ifs.is_open())
	{
		message = "Failed to open file.";
		return;
	}

	try
	{
		nlohmann::json j;
		ifs >> j;

		// ŒŸØ‚ÅƒGƒ‰[‚ª‚ ‚Á‚Ä‚àA’¼‚·‚½‚ß‚É“Ç‚İ‚ñ‚¾ó‘Ô‚ÅŠJ‚­
		hasErrors = !data.FromJson(j, context.attachedGraphPath);

		// ‘I‘ğ‚È‚Ç‚ğ–ß‚·
		parentCanvas.Reset();
		childCanvases.clear();
		CloseConditionWindow();
		pendingCopy = {};
		openCopyConfirm = false;

		message.clear();
	}
	catch (const std::exception&)
	{
		message = "Invalid json.";
	}
}

// •Û‘¶æ‚ÌƒpƒX‚ğŒˆ‚ß‚é
std::string StudioStateGraph::BuildNewSavePath(const StudioContext& context) const
{
	std::filesystem::path saveDir = "Data/Json/StateGraph";
	std::filesystem::create_directories(saveDir);

	std::string modelName = std::filesystem::path(context.currentModelPath).stem().string();
	if (modelName.empty()) modelName = "NewStateGraph";

	std::string uniqueName = ProjectWindow::GenerateUniqueName(saveDir, modelName + ".stategraph");
	std::string result = (saveDir / uniqueName).string();
	std::replace(result.begin(), result.end(), '\\', '/');
	return result;
}

// ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚éƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚Ì–¼‘O‚Ìˆê——‚ğì‚è’¼‚·
void StudioStateGraph::RefreshAnimationNames(entt::registry& registry)
{
	animationNames.clear();

	auto view = registry.view<SkinnedMeshComponent>();
	if (view.begin() == view.end()) return;

	auto& skinned = view.get<SkinnedMeshComponent>(*view.begin());
	if (!skinned.model) return;

	for (const auto& anim : skinned.model->GetAnimations())
	{
		animationNames.push_back(anim.name);
	}
}

// ƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ğÄ¶‚Å‚«‚éƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚©
bool StudioStateGraph::HasAnimatedModel(entt::registry& registry)
{
	auto view = registry.view<SkinnedMeshComponent, AnimatorComponent>();
	return view.begin() != view.end();
}

// q‚ÌƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚ğŠJ‚­
void StudioStateGraph::OpenChildWindow(int stateId)
{
	// ‚·‚Å‚ÉŠJ‚¢‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚ÎAV‚µ‚­ŠJ‚©‚¸‚Éè‘O‚Éo‚·
	for (const auto& child : childCanvases)
	{
		if (child->GetStateId() == stateId)
		{
			focusChildStateId = stateId;
			return;
		}
	}

	auto child = std::make_unique<StudioStateChildCanvas>(stateId);
	child->Initialize();
	childCanvases.push_back(std::move(child));
	focusChildStateId = stateId;
}

// ŠJ‚¢‚Ä‚¢‚éq‚ÌƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚ğ•`‚­
void StudioStateGraph::DrawChildWindows(ImGuiID ownerViewportId)
{
	for (auto it = childCanvases.begin(); it != childCanvases.end(); )
	{
		StudioStateChildCanvas& child = **it;

		// State‚ªÁ‚¦‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚Î•Â‚¶‚é
		const StateNode* state = std::as_const(data).FindState(child.GetStateId());
		if (!state)
		{
			it = childCanvases.erase(it);
			continue;
		}

		// •\¦‚ÍState–¼AID‚ÍState‚ÌID‚Åì‚é
		std::string title = "Child: " + state->name +
			"###StateChild_" + std::to_string(windowId) + "_" + std::to_string(state->id);

		// ƒ^ƒCƒgƒ‹ƒo[‚ğState‚ÌF‚É‚·‚é
		const float* c = state->editorColor;
		ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_TitleBg, ImVec4(c[0] * 0.7f, c[1] * 0.7f, c[2] * 0.7f, 1.0f));
		ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_TitleBgActive, ImVec4(c[0], c[1], c[2], 1.0f));

		// ŠJ‚¢‚½’¼Œã‚¾‚¯‘å‚«‚³‚ğŒˆ‚ß‚é
		ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(ImVec2(700.0f, 450.0f), ImGuiCond_FirstUseEver);
		if (focusChildStateId == state->id)
		{
			ImGui::SetNextWindowFocus();
			focusChildStateId = -1;
		}

		// ƒAƒvƒŠ‚ÌŠO‚Éo‚µ‚½‚Æ‚«AStudio‚æ‚èè‘O‚Éo‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
		SetNextWindowOwner(ownerViewportId);

		bool open = true;
		bool visible = ImGui::Begin(title.c_str(), &open);
		ImGui::PopStyleColor(2);

		if (visible)
		{
			child.Draw(data, animationNames);
		}
		ImGui::End();

		// q‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÌğŒ‚ğ•ÒW‚·‚éˆË—Š‚ª‚ ‚ê‚ÎŠJ‚­
		ConditionTarget conditionRequest = child.ConsumeEditConditionRequest();
		if (conditionRequest.IsValid()) OpenConditionWindow(conditionRequest);

		// q‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìİ’è‚ğÊ‚·ˆË—Š
		ConditionCopyRequest copyRequest = child.ConsumeCopyRequest();
		if (copyRequest.IsValid()) RequestCopy(copyRequest);

		// ~‚Å•Â‚¶‚ç‚ê‚½‚çAˆê——‚©‚çŠO‚·
		if (!open) it = childCanvases.erase(it);
		else ++it;
	}
}

// ğŒ‚ÌƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚ğŠJ‚­
void StudioStateGraph::OpenConditionWindow(const ConditionTarget& target)
{
	// •Ê‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÉØ‚è‘Ö‚¦‚é‚Æ‚«‚ÍAÀ‘Ì‚ª“¯‚¶êŠ‚Å‚à•K‚¸‰æ–Ê‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg‚·‚é
	if (target != conditionTarget) conditionPointer = nullptr;

	conditionTarget = target;
	focusConditionWindow = true;
}

// ğŒ‚ÌƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚ğ•`‚­
void StudioStateGraph::DrawConditionWindow(ImGuiID ownerViewportId)
{
	if (!conditionTarget.IsValid()) return;

	// •ÒW‚·‚éğŒ‚ÌÀ‘Ì‚ğA–ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€’T‚µ’¼‚·
	std::string description;
	ImVec4 color;
	ConditionGraph* condition = ResolveCondition(conditionTarget, description, color);

	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ªÁ‚¦‚Ä‚¢‚½‚ç•Â‚¶‚é
	if (!condition)
	{
		CloseConditionWindow();
		return;
	}

	// À‘Ì‚ÌêŠ‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚½‚çğŒ‚Ì‰æ–Ê‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg‚·‚é
	if (condition != conditionPointer)
	{
		conditionCanvas.SetTarget(condition);
		conditionPointer = condition;
	}

	std::string title = "Condition: " + description + "###StateCondition_" + std::to_string(windowId);

	// ƒ^ƒCƒgƒ‹ƒo[‚ğAƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ì‚¿å‚ÌState‚ÌF‚É‚·‚é
	ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_TitleBg, ImVec4(color.x * 0.7f, color.y * 0.7f, color.z * 0.7f, 1.0f));
	ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_TitleBgActive, color);

	// ŠJ‚¢‚½’¼Œã‚¾‚¯‘å‚«‚³‚ğŒˆ‚ß‚é
	ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(ImVec2(800.0f, 500.0f), ImGuiCond_FirstUseEver);
	if (focusConditionWindow)
	{
		ImGui::SetNextWindowFocus();
		focusConditionWindow = false;
	}

	// ƒAƒvƒŠ‚ÌŠO‚Éo‚µ‚½‚Æ‚«AStudio‚æ‚èè‘O‚Éo‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
	SetNextWindowOwner(ownerViewportId);

	bool open = true;
	bool visible = ImGui::Begin(title.c_str(), &open);
	ImGui::PopStyleColor(2);

	if (visible)
	{
		// ¶FğŒ‚ÌƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒXA‰EFğŒ‚ÌƒCƒ“ƒXƒyƒNƒ^[
		ImGui::Columns(2, "StateConditionColumns", true);
		if (!conditionColumnInitialized)
		{
			ImGui::SetColumnWidth(0, ImGui::GetWindowWidth() * 0.7f);
			conditionColumnInitialized = true;
		}

		conditionCanvas.Draw();
		ImGui::NextColumn();
		conditionCanvas.DrawInspector();

		ImGui::Columns(1);
	}
	ImGui::End();

	// ~‚Å•Â‚¶‚ç‚ê‚½
	if (!open) CloseConditionWindow();
}

// ğŒ‚ÌƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚ğ•Â‚¶‚é
void StudioStateGraph::CloseConditionWindow()
{
	conditionTarget = {};
	conditionPointer = nullptr;
	conditionCanvas.SetTarget(nullptr);
}

// •ÒW‚·‚éğŒ‚ÌÀ‘Ì‚ğ’T‚·
ConditionGraph* StudioStateGraph::ResolveCondition(const ConditionTarget& target, std::string& outTitle, ImVec4& outColor)
{
	switch (target.kind)
	{
	case ConditionTarget::Kind::StateLink:
	{
		// e‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒNF‘JˆÚŒ³ ¨ ‘JˆÚæ
		StateTransition* t = data.FindTransition(target.linkId);
		if (!t) return nullptr;

		std::string fromName;
		if (!FindTransitionSource(t->id, fromName, outColor)) return nullptr;

		const StateNode* to = std::as_const(data).FindState(t->toStateId);
		outTitle = fromName + " -> " + (to ? to->name : "(missing)");
		return &t->condition;
	}

	case ConditionTarget::Kind::EntryLink:
	case ConditionTarget::Kind::ClipLink:
	{
		// q‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒNFState–¼ / Œ³ ¨ æ
		StateNode* state = data.FindState(target.stateId);
		if (!state) return nullptr;

		const float* c = state->editorColor;
		outColor = ImVec4(c[0], c[1], c[2], 1.0f);

		auto clipName = [state](int clipId) -> std::string
			{
				const ClipNode* clip = state->child.FindClip(clipId);
				return clip ? clip->name : "(missing)";
			};

		if (target.kind == ConditionTarget::Kind::EntryLink)
		{
			for (auto& l : state->child.entryLinks)
			{
				if (l.id != target.linkId) continue;

				std::string fromName;
				ImVec4 unused;
				FindTransitionSource(l.entryTransitionId, fromName, unused);
				outTitle = state->name + " / Entry(" + fromName + ") -> " + clipName(l.toClipId);
				return &l.condition;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			for (auto& l : state->child.clipLinks)
			{
				if (l.id != target.linkId) continue;

				outTitle = state->name + " / " + clipName(l.fromClipId) + " -> " + clipName(l.toClipId);
				return &l.condition;
			}
		}
		return nullptr;
	}

	default:
		return nullptr;
	}
}

// e‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÌID‚©‚çA‘JˆÚŒ³‚Ì–¼‘O‚ÆF‚ğ’T‚·
bool StudioStateGraph::FindTransitionSource(int transitionId, std::string& outName, ImVec4& outColor) const
{
	// Entry‚©‚ço‚éƒŠƒ“ƒN
	for (const auto& t : data.entry.transitions)
	{
		if (t.id != transitionId) continue;
		outName = "Entry";
		outColor = ImVec4(46.0f / 255.0f, 125.0f / 255.0f, 50.0f / 255.0f, 1.0f);
		return true;
	}

	// ŠeState‚©‚ço‚éƒŠƒ“ƒN
	for (const auto& s : data.states)
	{
		for (const auto& t : s.transitions)
		{
			if (t.id != transitionId) continue;
			outName = s.name;
			outColor = ImVec4(s.editorColor[0], s.editorColor[1], s.editorColor[2], 1.0f);
			return true;
		}
	}
	return false;
}

// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìİ’è‚ÌêŠ‚ğ’T‚·
bool StudioStateGraph::ResolveLinkSettings(const ConditionTarget& target, LinkSettingsRef& out)
{
	out = {};

	switch (target.kind)
	{
	case ConditionTarget::Kind::StateLink:
	{
		// e‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒNFğŒAalwaysAƒuƒŒƒ“ƒh
		StateTransition* t = data.FindTransition(target.linkId);
		if (!t) return false;
		out = { &t->condition, &t->always, &t->blend };
		return true;
	}

	case ConditionTarget::Kind::EntryLink:
	{
		// q‚ÌƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒNFğŒAalways(ƒuƒŒƒ“ƒh‚Íe‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ª‚Â‚Ì‚Å–³‚¢)
		StateNode* state = data.FindState(target.stateId);
		if (!state) return false;
		for (auto& l : state->child.entryLinks)
		{
			if (l.id != target.linkId) continue;
			out = { &l.condition, &l.always, nullptr };
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	case ConditionTarget::Kind::ClipLink:
	{
		// q‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒNFğŒAalwaysAƒuƒŒƒ“ƒh
		StateNode* state = data.FindState(target.stateId);
		if (!state) return false;
		for (auto& l : state->child.clipLinks)
		{
			if (l.id != target.linkId) continue;
			out = { &l.condition, &l.always, &l.blend };
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	default:
		return false;
	}
}

// Ê‚·ˆË—Š‚ğó‚¯æ‚é
void StudioStateGraph::RequestCopy(const ConditionCopyRequest& request)
{
	LinkSettingsRef dst;
	if (!ResolveLinkSettings(request.target, dst)) return;

	// æ‚ÉReturnˆÈŠO‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚ª‚ ‚ê‚ÎAŠm”F‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çÊ‚·
	if (HasNodesBesidesReturn(*dst.condition))
	{
		pendingCopy = request;
		openCopyConfirm = true;
		return;
	}

	CopyLinkSettings(request);
}

// Ê‚·
void StudioStateGraph::CopyLinkSettings(const ConditionCopyRequest& request)
{
	if (request.source == request.target) return;

	// Šm”F‚ÌŠÔ‚ÉƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ªÁ‚¦‚Ä‚¢‚éê‡‚à‚ ‚é‚Ì‚ÅA‚±‚±‚Å’T‚µ’¼‚·
	LinkSettingsRef src, dst;
	if (!ResolveLinkSettings(request.source, src)) return;
	if (!ResolveLinkSettings(request.target, dst)) return;

	// ğŒ‚Æalways‚ğÊ‚·
	*dst.condition = *src.condition;
	*dst.always = *src.always;

	// ƒuƒŒƒ“ƒh‚ÍA—¼•û‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Æ‚«‚¾‚¯Ê‚·
	if (src.blend && dst.blend) *dst.blend = *src.blend;

	// Ê‚µ‚½æ‚ğğŒƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚ÅŠJ‚¢‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚ÎA‰æ–Ê‚ğ•t‚¯’¼‚·
	if (conditionTarget == request.target) conditionPointer = nullptr;
}

// ã‘‚«‚ÌŠm”F‚Ìƒ|ƒbƒvƒAƒbƒv
void StudioStateGraph::DrawCopyConfirmPopup()
{
	// Studio‚ÌƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚²‚Æ‚É–¼‘O‚ğ•ª‚¯‚é
	std::string popupName = "Copy Link Settings##" + std::to_string(windowId);

	// ˆË—Š‚ª—ˆ‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚ÎŠJ‚­
	if (openCopyConfirm)
	{
		ImGui::OpenPopup(popupName.c_str());
		openCopyConfirm = false;
	}

	// ‰æ–Ê‚Ì’†‰›‚Éo‚·
	ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImGui::GetMainViewport()->GetCenter(), ImGuiCond_Appearing, ImVec2(0.5f, 0.5f));
	if (!ImGui::BeginPopupModal(popupName.c_str(), nullptr, ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize)) return;

	// Œ³‚Ææ‚Ì–¼‘O
	std::string fromTitle, toTitle;
	ImVec4 unused;
	bool exists = ResolveCondition(pendingCopy.source, fromTitle, unused) != nullptr
		&& ResolveCondition(pendingCopy.target, toTitle, unused) != nullptr;
	if (!exists)
	{
		pendingCopy = {};
		ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
		ImGui::EndPopup();
		return;
	}

	ImGui::Text("The condition of this link will be replaced:");
	ImGui::Text("    %s", toTitle.c_str());
	ImGui::Text("Copy from:");
	ImGui::Text("    %s", fromTitle.c_str());
	ImGui::Spacing();

	if (ImGui::Button("Replace", ImVec2(120.0f, 0.0f)))
	{
		CopyLinkSettings(pendingCopy);
		pendingCopy = {};
		ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
	}
	ImGui::SameLine();
	if (ImGui::Button("Cancel", ImVec2(120.0f, 0.0f)))
	{
		pendingCopy = {};
		ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
	}

	ImGui::EndPopup();
} 
 
===== StudioStateGraphDraw.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Studio\StudioStateGraphDraw.cpp 
#include "StudioStateGraphDraw.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <cfloat>

namespace StateGraphDraw
{
	// ‹éŒ`‚Ì’†S‚©‚çtarget‚ÖL‚Î‚µ‚½ü‚ªA‹éŒ`‚Ì‰‚ÆŒğ‚í‚é“_
	ImVec2 GetEdgePoint(const ImVec2& rectMin, const ImVec2& rectMax, const ImVec2& target)
	{
		ImVec2 center((rectMin.x + rectMax.x) * 0.5f, (rectMin.y + rectMax.y) * 0.5f);
		float halfW = (rectMax.x - rectMin.x) * 0.5f;
		float halfH = (rectMax.y - rectMin.y) * 0.5f;

		ImVec2 dir(target.x - center.x, target.y - center.y);
		if (dir.x == 0.0f && dir.y == 0.0f) return center;

		// ‰¡‚Ì•Ó‚Æc‚Ì•Ó‚Ì‚¤‚¿Aæ‚É‚Ô‚Â‚©‚é‚Ù‚¤‚Ì”{—¦‚ğg‚¤
		float scaleX = (dir.x != 0.0f) ? halfW / fabsf(dir.x) : FLT_MAX;
		float scaleY = (dir.y != 0.0f) ? halfH / fabsf(dir.y) : FLT_MAX;
		float scale = (scaleX < scaleY) ? scaleX : scaleY;
		return ImVec2(center.x + dir.x * scale, center.y + dir.y * scale);
	}

	// 2‚Â‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚ğŒ‹‚ÔƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìn“_‚ÆI“_‚ğ‹‚ß‚é
	void CalcLinkPoints(
		const ImVec2& fromMin, const ImVec2& fromMax, int fromKey,
		const ImVec2& toMin, const ImVec2& toMax, int toKey,
		bool hasReverse, int lane,
		ImVec2& outStart, ImVec2& outEnd)
	{
		ImVec2 fromCenter((fromMin.x + fromMax.x) * 0.5f, (fromMin.y + fromMax.y) * 0.5f);
		ImVec2 toCenter((toMin.x + toMax.x) * 0.5f, (toMin.y + toMax.y) * 0.5f);

		// ƒm[ƒh‚Ì’†S“¯m‚ğŒ‹‚Ôü‚ªA‚»‚ê‚¼‚ê‚Ì‰‚ÆŒğ‚í‚é“_‚ğAn“_‚ÆI“_‚É‚·‚é
		outStart = GetEdgePoint(fromMin, fromMax, toCenter);
		outEnd = GetEdgePoint(toMin, toMax, fromCenter);

		float offset = (hasReverse ? 8.0f : 0.0f) + lane * 10.0f;
		if (offset <= 0.0f) return;

		// ‰•œ‚Ìü‚ª”½‘Î‘¤‚É‚¸‚ê‚é‚æ‚¤‚ÉAƒL[‚Ì¬‚³‚¢‚Ù‚¤‚©‚çŒ©‚½Œü‚«‚ğŠî€‚É‚·‚é
		bool fromIsSmaller = fromKey < toKey;
		ImVec2 baseFrom = fromIsSmaller ? fromCenter : toCenter;
		ImVec2 baseTo = fromIsSmaller ? toCenter : fromCenter;
		ImVec2 dir(baseTo.x - baseFrom.x, baseTo.y - baseFrom.y);
		float len = sqrtf(dir.x * dir.x + dir.y * dir.y);
		if (len < 0.0001f) return;

		// Šî€‚ÌŒü‚«‚É‚’¼‚È•ûŒü‚Ö‚¸‚ç‚·(‹tŒü‚«‚Ìü‚Í”½‘Î‘¤‚Ö)
		float sign = (hasReverse && !fromIsSmaller) ? -1.0f : 1.0f;
		ImVec2 perp(-dir.y / len * offset * sign, dir.x / len * offset * sign);
		outStart = ImVec2(outStart.x + perp.x, outStart.y + perp.y);
		outEnd = ImVec2(outEnd.x + perp.x, outEnd.y + perp.y);
	}

	// –îˆó•`‰æ
	void DrawArrow(ImDrawList* drawList, const ImVec2& start, const ImVec2& end, ImU32 color, float thickness)
	{
		ImVec2 dir(end.x - start.x, end.y - start.y);
		float len = sqrtf(dir.x * dir.x + dir.y * dir.y);
		if (len < 0.0001f) return;
		dir.x /= len;
		dir.y /= len;

		const float arrowLength = 14.0f;
		const float arrowWidth = 11.0f;

		// ü‚Í–îˆó‚ÌªŒ³‚Ü‚Å
		ImVec2 base(end.x - dir.x * arrowLength, end.y - dir.y * arrowLength);
		drawList->AddLine(start, base, color, thickness);

		// –îˆó‚ÌOŠpŒ`
		ImVec2 perp(-dir.y, dir.x);
		ImVec2 left(base.x + perp.x * arrowWidth * 0.5f, base.y + perp.y * arrowWidth * 0.5f);
		ImVec2 right(base.x - perp.x * arrowWidth * 0.5f, base.y - perp.y * arrowWidth * 0.5f);
		drawList->AddTriangleFilled(end, left, right, color);
	}

	// “_‚Æü•ª‚Ì‹——£
	float DistanceToSegment(const ImVec2& p, const ImVec2& a, const ImVec2& b)
	{
		ImVec2 ab(b.x - a.x, b.y - a.y);
		ImVec2 ap(p.x - a.x, p.y - a.y);
		float lenSq = ab.x * ab.x + ab.y * ab.y;

		// “_p‚ğü•ªab‚É“Š‰e‚µ‚½ˆÊ’u(0‚ªaA1‚ªb)Bü•ª‚ÌŠO‚Éo‚½‚ç’[‚ÉŠñ‚¹‚é
		float t = (lenSq > 0.0f) ? (ap.x * ab.x + ap.y * ab.y) / lenSq : 0.0f;
		t = (t < 0.0f) ? 0.0f : (t > 1.0f ? 1.0f : t);

		// ü•ªã‚Åˆê”Ô‹ß‚¢“_‚Æ‚Ì‹——£
		ImVec2 closest(a.x + ab.x * t, a.y + ab.y * t);
		float dx = p.x - closest.x;
		float dy = p.y - closest.y;
		return sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
	}

	// íœƒ{ƒ^ƒ“
	bool DeleteButton(const char* label)
	{
		ImGui::Spacing();
		ImGui::Spacing();

		ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_Button, ImVec4(0.7f, 0.15f, 0.15f, 1.0f));
		ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_ButtonHovered, ImVec4(0.85f, 0.2f, 0.2f, 1.0f));
		ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_ButtonActive, ImVec4(0.6f, 0.1f, 0.1f, 1.0f));
		bool pressed = ImGui::Button(label, ImVec2(-1.0f, 0.0f));
		ImGui::PopStyleColor(3);

		return pressed;
	}
}

#include "StudioStateParentCanvas.h"
#include <imgui.h>

void StudioStateParentCanvas::Initialize()
{
	// ƒm[ƒhƒGƒfƒBƒ^‚ÌƒRƒ“ƒeƒLƒXƒg‚ğì¬
	canvas.Initialize();
}

// ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒX‚ğ•`‚­
void StudioStateParentCanvas::Draw(StateMachineData& graph)
{
	data = &graph;

	// ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒXì¬
	canvas.Begin("StateParentCanvas");

	// íœ‚â“Ç‚İ‚İ‚ÌŒã‚Íƒm[ƒhƒGƒfƒBƒ^‘¤‚Éc‚Á‚½‘I‘ğ‚ğÁ‚·
	if (clearSelectionRequested)
	{
		ed::ClearSelection();
		clearSelectionRequested = false;
	}

	// ƒm[ƒh‚Ì‹éŒ`‚Í–ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ì‚è’¼‚·
	nodeRects.clear();

	// ƒm[ƒh•`‰æ
	DrawEntryNode();
	DrawStateNodes();

	// ˆÊ’u‚Ì”½‰f‚ÍA“Ç‚İ‚İ’¼Œã‚Ì1ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚¾‚¯
	needsApplyPositions = false; 

	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN•`‰æ
	DrawLinks();
	DrawPendingLink();

	// ƒƒjƒ…[•`‰æ
	DrawBackgroundMenu();
	DrawNodeMenu();

	// “ü—ÍXV
	UpdateLinkCreation();
	UpdateSelection();

	// ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒXI—¹
	canvas.End();
}

// ‘I‘ğ‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é•¨‚ğíœ
void StudioStateParentCanvas::DeleteSelection(StateMachineData& graph)
{
	// ‘I‘ğ’†‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Æ‘JˆÚæ‚ÌƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚ğíœ
	if (selectedLinkId != -1)
	{
		graph.RemoveTransition(selectedLinkId);
		selectedLinkId = -1;
		return;
	}

	// ‘I‘ğ’†‚ÌƒXƒe[ƒgíœ (Entry‚Ííœ•s‰Â)
	for (int key : selectedNodeKeys)
	{
		if (key != StateMachineData::kEntryId)
		{
			graph.RemoveState(key);
		}
	}
	selectedNodeKeys.clear();
	clearSelectionRequested = true;
}

// “Ç‚İ‚İEV‹Kì¬‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚ÉA‘I‘ğ‚È‚Ç‚ğ–ß‚·
void StudioStateParentCanvas::Reset()
{
	needsApplyPositions = true;
	selectedNodeKeys.clear();
	selectedLinkId = -1;
	pendingFromKey = -1;
	contextNodeKey = -1;
	clearSelectionRequested = true;
}

// ƒCƒ“ƒXƒyƒNƒ^[•`‰æ
void StudioStateParentCanvas::DrawInspector(StateMachineData& data)
{
	// •`‰æ’†‚¾‚¯Aó‚¯æ‚Á‚½ƒf[ƒ^‚ğw‚·
	this->data = &data;

	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‘I‘ğ
	if (selectedLinkId != -1)
	{
		DrawLinkInspector(selectedLinkId);
		return;
	}

	// ƒm[ƒh•¡”‘I‘ğ
	if (selectedNodeKeys.size() > 1)
	{
		DrawMultiStateInspector();
		return;
	}

	// ƒm[ƒh1‚Â‘I‘ğ
	if (selectedNodeKeys.size() == 1)
	{
		int key = selectedNodeKeys[0];
		if (key == StateMachineData::kEntryId)
		{
			DrawEntryInspector();
			return;
		}
		if (StateNode* state = data.FindState(key))
		{
			DrawStateInspector(*state);
			return;
		}
	}

	// –¢‘I‘ğ
	ImGui::TextDisabled("Nothing selected.");
}

// ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[ƒm[ƒh•`‰æ
void StudioStateParentCanvas::DrawEntryNode()
{
	ed::NodeId nodeId = ToNodeId(StateMachineData::kEntryId);
	
	// “Ç‚İ‚İ’¼Œã‚Íƒf[ƒ^‚ÌˆÊ’u‚Éƒm[ƒh‚ğ”½‰f
	if (needsApplyPositions)
	{
		ed::SetNodePosition(nodeId, ImVec2(data->entry.editorPos.x, data->entry.editorPos.y));
	}

	// Entry‚Í—Î‚Å•`‰æ
	ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBg, ImColor(46, 125, 50, 255));
	ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBorder, ImColor(27, 94, 32, 255));
	ed::BeginNode(nodeId);
	ImGui::Text("Entry");
	ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(80.0f, 0.0f));
	ed::EndNode();
	ed::PopStyleColor(2);

	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÌŒvZ‚Ég‚¤‹éŒ`‚ğ‹L˜^
	nodeRects[StateMachineData::kEntryId] = { ImGui::GetItemRectMin(), ImGui::GetItemRectMax() };

	// ƒm[ƒh‚ğ“®‚©‚µ‚½‚çAƒf[ƒ^‚ÌˆÊ’u‚É‘‚«–ß‚·
	ImVec2 pos = ed::GetNodePosition(nodeId);
	data->entry.editorPos = { pos.x, pos.y };
}

// ƒXƒe[ƒg•`‰æ
void StudioStateParentCanvas::DrawStateNodes()
{
	for (auto& s : data->states)
	{
		ed::NodeId nodeId = ToNodeId(s.id);

		// “Ç‚İ‚İ’¼Œã‚Íƒf[ƒ^‚ÌˆÊ’u‚Éƒm[ƒh‚ğ”½‰f
		if (needsApplyPositions)
		{
			ed::SetNodePosition(nodeId, ImVec2(s.editorPos.x, s.editorPos.y));
		}

		// State–ˆ‚ÌF‚ğ‘‚­
		const float* c = s.editorColor;
		ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBg, ImColor(c[0], c[1], c[2], 1.0f));
		ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBorder, ImColor(c[0] * 0.6f, c[1] * 0.6f, c[2] * 0.6f, 1.0f));
		ed::BeginNode(nodeId);
		ImGui::Text("%s", s.name.c_str());
		ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(80.0f, 0.0f));
		ed::EndNode();
		ed::PopStyleColor(2);

		// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÌŒvZ‚Ég‚¤‹éŒ`‚ğ‹L˜^
		nodeRects[s.id] = { ImGui::GetItemRectMin(), ImGui::GetItemRectMax() };

		// ƒm[ƒh‚ğ“®‚©‚µ‚½‚çAƒf[ƒ^‚ÌˆÊ’u‚É‘‚«–ß‚·
		ImVec2 pos = ed::GetNodePosition(nodeId);
		s.editorPos = { pos.x, pos.y };
	}
}

// ƒŠƒ“ƒN•`‰æ
void StudioStateParentCanvas::DrawLinks()
{
	ImDrawList* drawList = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();

	// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN”»’è—p‚Ìü‚Í–ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ì‚è’¼‚·
	linkSegments.clear();

	// 2ŒÂ‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚ÌŠÔ‚É•¡”ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ª‚ ‚é‚Æ‚«‰½–{–Ú‚©‹L˜^‚·‚é
	std::unordered_map<int64_t, int> laneCounts;

	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN1–{•ª‚ğ•`‰æ
	auto drawOne = [&](int fromKey, const StateTransition& t)
		{
			// ‘JˆÚŒ³‚Æ‘JˆÚæ‚Ì‘g‚İ‡‚í‚¹‚ğ1‚Â‚Ì’l‚É‚Ü‚Æ‚ß‚Ä‰½–{–Ú‚©‚ğ”‚¦‚é
			int64_t pairKey = (static_cast<int64_t>(fromKey) << 32) | static_cast<uint32_t>(t.toStateId);
			int lane = laneCounts[pairKey]++;

			ImVec2 start, end;
			if (!CalcLinkPoints(fromKey, t.toStateId, lane, start, end)) return;

			bool selected = (t.id == selectedLinkId);
			bool invalid = (!t.always && t.condition.IsEmpty());

			// ³í(”’F) ğŒ‚È‚µ(ÔF) ‘I‘ğ’†(‰©F)
			ImU32 color = IM_COL32(210, 210, 210, 255);
			if (invalid)  color = IM_COL32(230, 90, 90, 255);   // ğŒ‚Ìİ’è˜R‚ê
			if (selected) color = IM_COL32(255, 200, 80, 255);  // ‘I‘ğ’†

			StateGraphDraw::DrawArrow(drawList, start, end, color, selected ? 3.0f : 2.0f);

			// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN”»’è‚Ì‚½‚ß‚É•`‚¢‚½ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğ‹L˜^
			linkSegments.push_back({ t.id, start, end });
		};

	// Entry‚©‚ço‚éƒŠƒ“ƒN
	for (const auto& t : data->entry.transitions) drawOne(StateMachineData::kEntryId, t);
	
	// ŠeState‚©‚ço‚éƒŠƒ“ƒN
	for (const auto& s : data->states)
	{
		for (const auto& t : s.transitions) drawOne(s.id, t);
	}
}

// ì¬’†‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN•`‰æ
void StudioStateParentCanvas::DrawPendingLink()
{
	if (pendingFromKey == -1) return;

	auto it = nodeRects.find(pendingFromKey);
	if (it == nodeRects.end()) return;

	// ‘JˆÚŒ³‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚Ì‰‚©‚çAƒ}ƒEƒX‚ÌˆÊ’u‚Ü‚Åü‚ğˆø‚­
	ImVec2 mouse = ImGui::GetMousePos();
	ImVec2 start = StateGraphDraw::GetEdgePoint(it->second.first, it->second.second, mouse);
	StateGraphDraw::DrawArrow(ImGui::GetWindowDrawList(), start, mouse, IM_COL32(255, 200, 80, 255), 2.0f);
}

// ”wŒi‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Ìƒƒjƒ…[
void StudioStateParentCanvas::DrawBackgroundMenu()
{
	ed::Suspend();

	// ƒ|ƒbƒv‚ğŠJ‚­ƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg
	if (ed::ShowBackgroundContextMenu())
	{
		pendingCreatePos = ImGui::GetMousePos(); // ƒ}ƒEƒXˆÊ’u•Û‘¶
		ImGui::OpenPopup("StateParentBackgroundMenu");
	}

	// ƒ|ƒbƒv“à—e
	if (ImGui::BeginPopup("StateParentBackgroundMenu"))
	{
		// ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚µ‚½ˆÊ’u‚ÉState‚ğì‚é
		if (ImGui::MenuItem("Create State"))
		{
			ImVec2 canvasPos = ed::ScreenToCanvas(pendingCreatePos);
			int newId = data->AddState("New State", { canvasPos.x, canvasPos.y });
			ed::SetNodePosition(ToNodeId(newId), canvasPos);
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}

	ed::Resume();
}

// ƒm[ƒh‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Ìƒƒjƒ…[
void StudioStateParentCanvas::DrawNodeMenu()
{
	ed::Suspend();

	ed::NodeId nodeId;
	if (ed::ShowNodeContextMenu(&nodeId))
	{
		// ƒƒjƒ…[‚ğ•Â‚¶‚é‚Ü‚Å‚Ç‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚Ìƒƒjƒ…[‚©‚ğ‹L˜^
		contextNodeKey = ToNodeKey(nodeId);

		// ƒ|ƒbƒv‚ğŠJ‚­ƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg
		ImGui::OpenPopup("StateParentNodeMenu");
	}

	// ƒ|ƒbƒv“à—e
	if (ImGui::BeginPopup("StateParentNodeMenu"))
	{
		// ƒŠƒ“ƒNì¬ŠJn
		if (ImGui::MenuItem("Make Link"))
		{
			pendingFromKey = contextNodeKey;
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}

	ed::Resume();
}

// ƒŠƒ“ƒNì¬’†‚Ì“ü—Í
void StudioStateParentCanvas::UpdateLinkCreation()
{
	if (pendingFromKey == -1) return;

	// Esc‚Åæ‚èÁ‚µ
	if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Escape))
	{
		pendingFromKey = -1;
		return;
	}

	// 
	if (!ImGui::IsMouseClicked(ImGuiMouseButton_Left)) return;

	// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚µ‚½ˆÊ’u‚Éƒm[ƒh‚ª‚ ‚ê‚Î‚»‚±‚ğs‚«æ‚É‚·‚é
	ed::NodeId hovered = ed::GetHoveredNode();
	if (hovered)
	{
		int toKey = ToNodeKey(hovered);

		// Entry‚Ö‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÆA©•ª©g‚Ö‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Íì‚ç‚È‚¢
		if (toKey != StateMachineData::kEntryId && toKey != pendingFromKey)
		{
			int newId = data->AddTransition(pendingFromKey, toKey);
			if (newId != -1)
			{
				// ì‚Á‚½ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğ‘I‘ğó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
				selectedLinkId = newId;
				selectedNodeKeys.clear();
				ed::ClearSelection();
			}
		}
	}

	// ƒm[ƒhˆÈŠO‚ğƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚µ‚½ê‡‚àAì¬‚ÍI‚í‚é
	pendingFromKey = -1;
}

// ‘I‘ğ‚ÌXV
void StudioStateParentCanvas::UpdateSelection()
{
	// State‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚ğƒ_ƒuƒ‹ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚µ‚½‚çAq‚ÌƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚ğŠJ‚­
	if (ed::NodeId doubleClicked = ed::GetDoubleClickedNode())
	{
		int key = ToNodeKey(doubleClicked);
		if (key != StateMachineData::kEntryId) openChildRequest = key;
	}

	// ƒm[ƒh‚Ì‘I‘ğ
	int count = ed::GetSelectedObjectCount();
	std::vector<ed::NodeId> nodeIds(count > 0 ? count : 1);
	int nodeCount = (count > 0) ? ed::GetSelectedNodes(nodeIds.data(), count) : 0;

	std::vector<int> keys;
	for (int i = 0; i < nodeCount; ++i)
	{
		int key = ToNodeKey(nodeIds[i]);
		// íœ‚µ‚½’¼Œã‚È‚Ç‚ÅAƒf[ƒ^‚É–³‚¢ƒm[ƒh‚ªc‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚Ä‚à‘I‘ğ‚Æ‚µ‚Äˆµ‚í‚È‚¢
		if (IsExistingNode(key)) keys.push_back(key);
	}

	if (!keys.empty())
	{
		selectedNodeKeys = std::move(keys);
		selectedLinkId = -1;
	}
	else if (selectedLinkId == -1)
	{
		// ƒm[ƒh‚àƒŠƒ“ƒN‚à‘I‚Î‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢
		selectedNodeKeys.clear();
	}

	// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Ì”»’è
	if (pendingFromKey != -1) return;
	if (!ed::IsBackgroundClicked()) return;

	// ƒm[ƒhƒGƒfƒBƒ^‚Ì’†‚Å‚ÍAƒ}ƒEƒX‚ÌˆÊ’u‚ÍƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒX‚ÌÀ•W‚Å•Ô‚é(ü‚ÌÀ•W‚Æ“¯‚¶)
	ImVec2 mouse = ImGui::GetMousePos();
	const float hitDistance = 6.0f;

	// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚µ‚½ˆÊ’u‚©‚çˆê”Ô‹ß‚¢ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğ’T‚·(‰“‚·‚¬‚éê‡‚Í‘I‚Î‚È‚¢)
	int hitId = -1;
	float best = hitDistance;
	for (const auto& seg : linkSegments)
	{
		float d = StateGraphDraw::DistanceToSegment(mouse, seg.start, seg.end);
		if (d < best)
		{
			best = d;
			hitId = seg.linkId;
		}
	}

	// ü‚Ì‚È‚¢‚Æ‚±‚ë‚ğƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚µ‚½‚ç‘I‘ğ‰ğœ
	selectedLinkId = hitId;
	if (hitId != -1)
	{
		// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğ‘I‚ñ‚¾‚çAƒm[ƒh‚Ì‘I‘ğ‚ÍŠO‚·
		selectedNodeKeys.clear();
		ed::ClearSelection();
	}
}

// ƒf[ƒ^‚É‘¶İ‚·‚éƒm[ƒh‚©
bool StudioStateParentCanvas::IsExistingNode(int key) const
{
	if (key == StateMachineData::kEntryId) return true;
	return std::as_const(*data).FindState(key) != nullptr;
}

// ƒXƒe[ƒg‚Ìî•ñ
void StudioStateParentCanvas::DrawStateInspector(StateNode& state)
{
	ImGui::Text("State");


	// ‘I‚ÔState‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚½‚çA“ü—Í—“‚ğ¡‚Ì–¼‘O‚Åì‚è’¼‚·
	if (nameEditingStateId != state.id)
	{
		nameEditingStateId = state.id;
		strncpy_s(nameBuffer, state.name.c_str(), sizeof(nameBuffer) - 1);
		nameError.clear();
	}

	// –¼‘O
	if (ImGui::InputText("Name", nameBuffer, sizeof(nameBuffer), ImGuiInputTextFlags_EnterReturnsTrue))
	{
		if (data->RenameState(state.id, nameBuffer))
		{
			nameError.clear();
		}
		else
		{
			nameError = (nameBuffer[0] == '\0') ? "Name is empty." : "Name already used.";
		}
	}

	// “ü—Í—“‚©‚ç—£‚ê‚½‚çAŠm’è‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢•¶š‚Í¡‚Ì–¼‘O‚É–ß‚·
	if (ImGui::IsItemDeactivated() && state.name != nameBuffer)
	{
		strncpy_s(nameBuffer, state.name.c_str(), sizeof(nameBuffer) - 1);
	}
	if (!nameError.empty())
	{
		ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(1.0f, 0.4f, 0.4f, 1.0f), "%s", nameError.c_str());
	}

	// F
	ImGui::ColorEdit3("Color", state.editorColor);
	ImGui::Spacing();

	// o‚Ä‚¢‚­ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìˆê——
	DrawLinkList(state.id, state.transitions);
	ImGui::Spacing();

	// qƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ÖˆÚ“®
	if (ImGui::Button("Open Child Graph", ImVec2(-1.0f, 0.0f)))
	{
		openChildRequest = state.id;
	}

	// íœ
	if (StateGraphDraw::DeleteButton("Delete State"))
	{
		DeleteSelection(*data);
	}
}

// ƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ[‚Ìİ’è

{
	ImGui::Text("Entry");
	ImGui::Separator();

	// o‚Ä‚¢‚­ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìˆê——
	DrawLinkList(StateMachineData::kEntryId, data->entry.transitions);
}

// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìİ’è
void StudioStateParentCanvas::DrawLinkInspector(int linkId)
{
	StateTransition* link = data->FindTransition(linkId);
	int fromKey = -1, index = -1, count = 0;
	if (!link || !FindLinkOwner(linkId, fromKey, index, count))
	{
		ImGui::TextDisabled("Nothing selected.");
		return;
	}

	ImGui::Text("Link");


	// ‘JˆÚŒ³ ¨ ‘JˆÚæ
	ImGui::Text("%s  ->  %s", GetNodeName(fromKey).c_str(), GetNodeName(link->toStateId).c_str());

	// —Dæ“x
	ImGui::Text("Priority: %d / %d", index + 1, count);	ImGui::Spacing();

	// í‚É¬—§
	ImGui::Checkbox("Always", &link->always);

	// ƒuƒŒƒ“ƒh
	ImGui::Checkbox("Blend", &link->blend.enabled);
	ImGui::BeginDisabled(!link->blend.enabled);
	ImGui::DragFloat("Blend Duration", &link->blend.duration, 0.01f, 0.0f, 5.0f, "%.2f s");
	ImGui::EndDisabled();
	ImGui::Spacing();

	// ğŒ‚Ìó‘Ô
	if (link->always)
	{
		ImGui::TextDisabled("Condition: Always");
	}
	else if (link->condition.IsEmpty())
	{
		ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(1.0f, 0.4f, 0.4f, 1.0f), "Condition: Empty");
	}
	else
	{
		ImGui::Text("Condition: Set");
	}

	// ğŒ‚ÌƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚Å•ÒW‚·‚é
	if (ImGui::Button("Edit Condition", ImVec2(-1.0f, 0.0f)))
	{
		editConditionRequest = { ConditionTarget::Kind::StateLink, -1, linkId };
	}

	// íœ
	if (StateGraphDraw::DeleteButton("Delete Link"))
	{
		DeleteSelection(*data);
	}

	// “¯‚¶ŠK‘w‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚©‚çAİ’è‚ğÊ‚·‚½‚ß‚ÌƒŠƒXƒg
	ConditionTarget self{ ConditionTarget::Kind::StateLink, -1, linkId };
	ImGui::Spacing();
	ImGui::Separator();
	ImGui::Text("Copy From");

	// ó‚¯Œû‚É—‚Æ‚³‚ê‚½‚çAÊ‚·ˆË—Š‚ğo‚·
	ConditionTarget source = ConditionCopyList::DrawDropTarget(self);
	if (source.IsValid()) copyRequest = { source, self };

	// Ê‚·Œ³‚ÌƒŠƒXƒg
	ConditionCopyList::Draw(ConditionCopyList::CollectParent(*data, self));
}

// o‚Ä‚¢‚­ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìˆê——
void StudioStateParentCanvas::DrawLinkList(int fromKey, std::vector<StateTransition>& links)
{
	ImGui::Text("Links (priority order)");

	if (links.empty())
	{
		ImGui::TextDisabled("No links.");
		return;
	}

	// “ü‚ê‘Ö‚¦‚Í•`‰æ‚ªI‚í‚Á‚Ä‚©‚ç
	int moveId = -1;
	int moveOffset = 0;

	for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(links.size()); ++i)
	{
		const StateTransition& t = links[i];
		ImGui::PushID(t.id);

		// ã‰º‚Å—Dæ“x‚ğ“ü‚ê‘Ö‚¦‚é
		if (ImGui::ArrowButton("##Up", ImGuiDir_Up)) { moveId = t.id; moveOffset = -1; }
		ImGui::SameLine();
		if (ImGui::ArrowButton("##Down", ImGuiDir_Down)) { moveId = t.id; moveOffset = +1; }
		ImGui::SameLine();

		// ğŒ‚Ìİ’è˜R‚ê‚ÍÔ‚Å•\¦
		bool invalid = (!t.always && t.condition.IsEmpty());
		if (invalid) ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_Text, ImVec4(1.0f, 0.4f, 0.4f, 1.0f));

		// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Å‚»‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğ‘I‚Ô
		std::string label = std::to_string(i + 1) + ". -> " + GetNodeName(t.toStateId);
		if (ImGui::Selectable(label.c_str(), t.id == selectedLinkId))
		{
			selectedLinkId = t.id;
			selectedNodeKeys.clear();
			clearSelectionRequested = true; // ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒoƒX‘¤‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚Ì‘I‘ğ‚àŠO‚·
		}

		if (invalid) ImGui::PopStyleColor();
		ImGui::PopID();
	}

	if (moveId != -1)
	{
		data->MoveTransition(moveId, moveOffset);
	}
	(void)fromKey;
}

// ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ì‘JˆÚŒ³‚ÆA‘JˆÚŒ³‚Ì’†‚Å‚Ì‰½”Ô–Ú‚©‚ğ’T‚·
bool StudioStateParentCanvas::FindLinkOwner(int linkId, int& outFromKey, int& outIndex, int& outCount) const
{
	auto search = [&](int fromKey, const std::vector<StateTransition>& list)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(list.size()); ++i)
			{
				if (list[i].id == linkId)
				{
					outFromKey = fromKey;
					outIndex = i;
					outCount = static_cast<int>(list.size());
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		};

	if (search(StateMachineData::kEntryId, data->entry.transitions)) return true;
	for (const auto& s : data->states)
	{
		if (search(s.id, s.transitions)) return true;
	}
	return false;
}

// ƒm[ƒh‚ÌƒL[‚©‚çA•\¦—p‚Ì–¼‘O‚ğì‚é
std::string StudioStateParentCanvas::GetNodeName(int key) const
{
	if (key == StateMachineData::kEntryId) return "Entry";
	if (const StateNode* s = std::as_const(*data).FindState(key)) return s->name;
	return "(missing)";
}

// fromKey‚©‚çtoStateId‚Ö‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ª‚ ‚é‚©
bool StudioStateParentCanvas::HasLink(int fromKey, int toStateId) const
{
	// ‘JˆÚŒ³‚ÌƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Ìˆê——
	const std::vector<StateTransition>* list = nullptr;
	if (fromKey == StateMachineData::kEntryId)
	{
		list = &data->entry.transitions;
	}
	else if (const StateNode* s = std::as_const(*data).FindState(fromKey))
	{
		list = &s->transitions;
	}
	if (!list) return false;

	for (const auto& t : *list)
	{
		if (t.toStateId == toStateId) return true;
	}
	return false;
}

// ü‚Ìn“_‚ÆI“_‚ğ‹‚ß‚é
bool StudioStateParentCanvas::CalcLinkPoints(int fromKey, int toStateId, int lane, ImVec2& outStart, ImVec2& outEnd) const
{
	auto fromIt = nodeRects.find(fromKey);
	auto toIt = nodeRects.find(toStateId);
	if (fromIt == nodeRects.end() || toIt == nodeRects.end()) return false;

	StateGraphDraw::CalcLinkPoints(
		fromIt->second.first, fromIt->second.second, fromKey,
		toIt->second.first, toIt->second.second, toStateId,
		HasLink(toStateId, fromKey), lane,
		outStart, outEnd);
	return true;
}

// ƒm[ƒh‚ÌƒL[ ¨ ƒm[ƒhƒGƒfƒBƒ^‚ÌID
ed::NodeId StudioStateParentCanvas::ToNodeId(int key)
{
	if (key == StateMachineData::kEntryId) return ed::NodeId(static_cast<uint64_t>(kEntryNodeId));
	return ed::NodeId(static_cast<uint64_t>(key));
}

// ƒm[ƒhƒGƒfƒBƒ^‚ÌID ¨ ƒm[ƒh‚ÌƒL[
int StudioStateParentCanvas::ToNodeKey(ed::NodeId nodeId)
{
	if (nodeId.Get() == static_cast<uint64_t>(kEntryNodeId)) return StateMachineData::kEntryId;
	return static_cast<int>(nodeId.Get());
} 
 
===== StudioTimeline.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Studio\StudioTimeline.cpp 
ï»¿#include "StudioTimeline.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <string>

void StudioTimeline::DrawGUI(entt::registry& registry, StudioContext& context)
{
    if (!context.showTimeline) return;

    std::string panelName = "Timeline##" + std::to_string(windowId);
    ImGui::Begin(panelName.c_str());

    auto view = registry.view<SkinnedMeshComponent, AnimatorComponent>();

    if (view.begin() == view.end())
    {
        ImGui::Text("No skinned model.");
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }

    auto entity = *view.begin();
    auto& skinned = view.get<SkinnedMeshComponent>(entity);
    auto& animator = view.get<AnimatorComponent>(entity);

    if (!skinned.model)
    {
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }

    // ã‚¢ãƒ‹ãƒ¡ãƒ¼ã‚·ãƒ§ãƒ³é•·ã‚’å–å¾—
    float animLength = 0.0f;
    const auto& animations = skinned.model->GetAnimations();
    for (const auto& anim : animations)
    {
        if (anim.name == animator.currentAnimation)
        {
            animLength = anim.secondsLength;
            break;
        }
    }

    // å…ˆé ­ã«æˆ»ã‚‹
    if (ImGui::Button("<<"))
    {
        animator.animationTime = 0.0f;
        animator.isPlaying = false;
        animator.isEnd = false;
    }
    ImGui::SameLine();

    // å†ç”Ÿ/åœæ­¢
    if (animator.isPlaying)
    {
        if (ImGui::Button(u8"â– "))
        {
            animator.isPlaying = false;
        }
    }
    else
    {
        if (ImGui::Button(u8"â–¶"))
        {
            if (animator.animationTime >= animLength)
                animator.animationTime = 0.0f;
            animator.isPlaying = true;
            animator.isEnd = false;
        }
    }
    ImGui::SameLine();

    // ãƒ«ãƒ¼ãƒ—
    ImGui::Checkbox("Loop", &animator.loop);
    ImGui::SameLine();

    // ç¾åœ¨ã®ã‚¢ãƒ‹ãƒ¡ãƒ¼ã‚·ãƒ§ãƒ³åï¼ˆç·‘è‰²ï¼‰
    ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f),
        animator.currentAnimation.c_str());

    // æ™‚åˆ»è¡¨ç¤º
    ImGui::Text("%.2f / %.2f s", animator.animationTime, animLength);
    ImGui::SameLine();

    // å†ç”Ÿæ™‚åˆ»ã‚¹ãƒ©ã‚¤ãƒ€ãƒ¼ï¼ˆå…¨å¹…ï¼‰
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1);
    if (ImGui::SliderFloat("##animTime",
        &animator.animationTime, 0.0f, animLength))
    {
        // ã‚¹ã‚¯ãƒ©ãƒ–ä¸­ã¯åœæ­¢ã—ã¦ãƒãƒ¼ã‚ºã‚’ç¢ºèª
        animator.isPlaying = false;
        animator.isEnd = false;
        context.requestPoseUpdate = true;
    }

    ImGui::End();
}
 
 
===== StudioWindow.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Code\Tool\Studio\StudioWindow.cpp 
ï»¿#include "StudioWindow.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <imgui_internal.h>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

namespace
{
	// æœ€å¤§åŒ–ã—ãŸã¨ãã®åºƒã’ã‚‹å…ˆã‚’æ±‚ã‚ã‚‹
	void GetMaximizeArea(ImVec2 windowPos, ImVec2 windowSize, ImVec2& outPos, ImVec2& outSize)
	{
		// ã‚¢ãƒ—ãƒªã®ä¸­ã«ã„ã‚‹
		ImGuiViewport* mainViewport = ImGui::GetMainViewport();
		if (ImGui::GetWindowViewport() == mainViewport)
		{
			outPos = mainViewport->WorkPos;
			outSize = mainViewport->WorkSize;
			return;
		}

		// ã‚¢ãƒ—ãƒªã®å¤–ï¼šã‚¦ã‚£ãƒ³ãƒ‰ã‚¦ã®ä¸­å¿ƒãŒã‚ã‚‹ãƒ¢ãƒ‹ã‚¿ãƒ¼ã‚’æ¢ã™
		ImVec2 center(windowPos.x + windowSize.x * 0.5f, windowPos.y + windowSize.y * 0.5f);
		const ImGuiPlatformIO& platformIO = ImGui::GetPlatformIO();
		for (const ImGuiPlatformMonitor& monitor : platformIO.Monitors)
		{
			bool inside =
				center.x >= monitor.MainPos.x && center.x < monitor.MainPos.x + monitor.MainSize.x &&
				center.y >= monitor.MainPos.y && center.y < monitor.MainPos.y + monitor.MainSize.y;
			if (inside)
			{
				outPos = monitor.WorkPos;
				outSize = monitor.WorkSize;
				return;
			}
		}

		// è¦‹ã¤ã‹ã‚‰ãªã‘ã‚Œã°æœ€åˆã®ãƒ¢ãƒ‹ã‚¿ãƒ¼
		if (!platformIO.Monitors.empty())
		{
			outPos = platformIO.Monitors[0].WorkPos;
			outSize = platformIO.Monitors[0].WorkSize;
			return;
		}

		// ãƒ¢ãƒ‹ã‚¿ãƒ¼ã®æƒ…å ±ãŒç„¡ã„å ´åˆã¯ã€ã‚¢ãƒ—ãƒªã®ã‚¦ã‚£ãƒ³ãƒ‰ã‚¦
		outPos = mainViewport->WorkPos;
		outSize = mainViewport->WorkSize;
	}

	// 2ã¤ã®å€¤ãŒã»ã¼åŒã˜ã‹
	bool NearlyEqual(ImVec2 a, ImVec2 b)
	{
		return std::fabs(a.x - b.x) < 1.0f && std::fabs(a.y - b.y) < 1.0f;
	}
}

void StudioWindow::Initialize(ID3D11Device* device, StudioSharedContext* shared, UINT width, UINT height)
{
	renderWidth = width;
	renderHeight = height;
	context.device = device;
	this->shared = shared;

	renderSystem.Initialize(device);
	lightManager.Initialize(device);

	// IBLãƒ†ã‚¯ã‚¹ãƒãƒ£ã‚»ãƒƒãƒˆ
	renderSystem.SetIBLTextures(
		shared->skyBox.GetDiffuseIem(),
		shared->skyBox.GetSRV(),
		shared->skyBox.GetLutGgx()
	);

	stadoiModelViewer.Initialize(device, &shared->resourceManager);
	stateGraph.Initialize();

	ResizeRenderTarget();
}

// æ›´æ–°
void StudioWindow::Update(float elapsedTime)
{
	// ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©æ›´æ–°
	camera.Update(viewportHovered, elapsedTime);

	// ãƒ¢ãƒ‡ãƒ«ãƒ“ãƒ¥ãƒ¯ãƒ¼æ›´æ–°
	stadoiModelViewer.Update(elapsedTime, context);

	// ã‚¢ãƒ‹ãƒ¡ãƒ¼ã‚·ãƒ§ãƒ³é€Ÿåº¦æ›´æ–°
	animationSpeedSystem.Update(studioRegistry);

	if (context.requestPoseUpdate)
	{
		// ã‚¹ã‚¯ãƒ©ãƒ–æ“ä½œæ™‚ã¯æ™‚é–“ã‚’é€²ã‚ãšãƒãƒ¼ã‚ºã ã‘æ›´æ–°
		animationSystem.EvaluatePose(studioRegistry);
		context.requestPoseUpdate = false;
	}
	else
	{
		animationSystem.Update(studioRegistry, elapsedTime);
	}

	// ã‚¢ã‚¿ãƒƒãƒãƒ¡ãƒ³ãƒˆæ›´æ–°
	for (auto& attachment : attachments)
	{
		attachment->Update(elapsedTime, context);
	}
}

// æç”»
void StudioWindow::Render()
{
	// ãƒ›ãƒãƒ¼ä¸­ã¯æ¯ãƒ•ãƒ¬ãƒ¼ãƒ ã€é›¢ã‚ŒãŸã‚‰1/10
	if (!isHovered)
	{
		renderSkipCount++;
		if (renderSkipCount < 10) return;
		renderSkipCount = 0;
	}
	else
	{
		renderSkipCount = 0;
	}

	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc = context.deviceContext;
	if (!dc) return;

	// æç”»å…ˆã‚’è‡ªå‰RTVã«åˆ‡ã‚Šæ›¿ãˆ
	Begin();

	// CbSceneæ›´æ–°
	CbScene cbSceneData{};
	cbSceneData.view = camera.GetView();
	cbSceneData.projection = camera.GetProjection();
	const auto& eye = camera.GetEye();
	cbSceneData.cameraPosition = { eye.x, eye.y, eye.z, 0.0f };

	dc->UpdateSubresource(renderSystem.GetCbScene(), 0, nullptr, &cbSceneData, 0, 0);
	dc->VSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, renderSystem.GetCbSceneAddress());
	dc->PSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, renderSystem.GetCbSceneAddress());

	// ãƒ©ã‚¤ãƒˆã‚’GPUã«è»¢é€
	lightManager.Bind(dc);

	// ãƒ•ãƒ©ã‚¹ã‚¿ãƒ æ›´æ–°
	renderSystem.SetFrustum(camera.GetView(), camera.GetProjection());

	// ã‚¹ã‚«ã‚¤ãƒœãƒƒã‚¯ã‚¹æç”»
	shared->skyBox.Render(
		dc,
		camera.GetView(),
		camera.GetProjection(),
		camera.GetEye()
	);

	// ãƒ¢ãƒ‡ãƒ«ãŒã‚ã‚‹æ™‚ã ã‘RenderSystemã‚’èµ°ã‚‰ã›ã‚‹
	if (!studioRegistry.storage<TransformComponent>().empty())
	{
		// æç”»
		renderSystem.Render(
			studioRegistry,
			dc,
			rtv.Get(),
			dsv.Get(),
			context.device,
			renderWidth,
			renderHeight
		);
	}

	// ã‚¢ã‚¿ãƒƒãƒãƒ¡ãƒ³ãƒˆã®3Dæç”»
	for (auto& attachment : attachments)
	{
		attachment->OnRender(
			context.deviceContext,
			camera.GetView(),
			camera.GetProjection(),
			studioRegistry,
			context
		);
	}

	// æç”»å…ˆã‚’ãƒ¡ã‚¤ãƒ³ã«æˆ»ã™
	End();
}

// ImGuiæç”»
void StudioWindow::DrawWindow(const char* windowName)
{
	// æœ€å¤§åŒ–ãƒ»å…ƒã«æˆ»ã™ã®äºˆç´„ãŒã‚ã‚Œã°ã€Beginã®å‰ã«ä½ç½®ã¨å¤§ãã•ã‚’ä¼ãˆã‚‹
	if (applyWindowRect)
	{
		ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(applyPos, ImGuiCond_Always);
		ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(applySize, ImGuiCond_Always);
		applyWindowRect = false;
	}


	bool open = true;
	ImGui::Begin(windowName, &open, ImGuiWindowFlags_MenuBar);
	if (!open) closeRequested = true;

	// StudioãŒã„ã‚‹ãƒ“ãƒ¥ãƒ¼ãƒãƒ¼ãƒˆ
	context.studioViewportId = ImGui::GetWindowViewport()->ID;

	// æœ€å¤§åŒ–ä¸­ã«ã€ãƒ‰ãƒ©ãƒƒã‚°ã§å‹•ã‹ã—ãŸãƒ»å¤§ãã•ã‚’å¤‰ãˆãŸãƒ»ãƒ‰ãƒƒã‚­ãƒ³ã‚°ã—ãŸã‚‰ã€æœ€å¤§åŒ–ã‚’å¤–ã™
	if (maximized)
	{
		bool changed = !NearlyEqual(ImGui::GetWindowPos(), applyPos) || !NearlyEqual(ImGui::GetWindowSize(), applySize);
		if (changed || ImGui::IsWindowDocked()) maximized = false;
	}

	// å³ç«¯ï¼šæœ€å¤§åŒ–ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³
	DrawMaximizeButton();

	// Studioã‚¦ã‚£ãƒ³ãƒ‰ã‚¦å…¨ä½“ã§ãƒ›ãƒãƒ¼åˆ¤å®š
	isHovered = ImGui::IsWindowHovered(ImGuiHoveredFlags_ChildWindows | ImGuiHoveredFlags_DockHierarchy);

	// ãƒ¡ãƒ‹ãƒ¥ãƒ¼ãƒãƒ¼
	if (ImGui::BeginMenuBar())
	{
		if (ImGui::BeginMenu("Window"))
		{
			ImGui::MenuItem("Animator", nullptr, &context.showAnimator);
			ImGui::MenuItem("Timeline", nullptr, &context.showTimeline);
			ImGui::MenuItem("Sequencer", nullptr, &context.showSequencer);
			ImGui::MenuItem("State Graph", nullptr, &context.showStateGraph);
			ImGui::MenuItem("AnimationSpeed", nullptr, &context.showAnimationSpeed);

			ImGui::EndMenu();
		}
		ImGui::EndMenuBar();
	}

	// ã“ã®Studioå°‚ç”¨ã®DockSpace
	std::string dockSpaceName = "StudioDockSpace##" + std::to_string(windowId);
	ImGuiID dockSpaceId = ImGui::GetID(dockSpaceName.c_str());
	ImGui::DockSpace(dockSpaceId, ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f), ImGuiDockNodeFlags_None);

	// åˆå›ãƒ•ãƒ¬ãƒ¼ãƒ ã ã‘ãƒ‡ãƒ•ã‚©ãƒ«ãƒˆãƒ¬ã‚¤ã‚¢ã‚¦ãƒˆã‚’çµ„ã‚€
	if (firstDockSetup)
	{
		firstDockSetup = false;

		ImGui::DockBuilderRemoveNode(dockSpaceId);
		ImGui::DockBuilderAddNode(dockSpaceId, ImGuiDockNodeFlags_None);
		ImGui::DockBuilderSetNodeSize(dockSpaceId, ImVec2(1280, 720));

		ImGuiID dockMain = dockSpaceId;
		ImGuiID dockLeft = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
			dockMain, ImGuiDir_Left, 0.2f, nullptr, &dockMain);
		ImGuiID dockRight = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
			dockMain, ImGuiDir_Right, 0.7f, nullptr, &dockMain);
		ImGuiID dockBottom = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
			dockMain, ImGuiDir_Down, 0.2f, nullptr, &dockMain);
		ImGuiID dockSequencer = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
			dockBottom, ImGuiDir_Down, 0.5f, nullptr, &dockBottom);

		std::string viewportName = "Viewport##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string animatorName = "Animator##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string timelineName = "Timeline##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string sequencerName = "Sequencer##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string stateGraphName = "StateGraph##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string animationSpeedName = "Animation Speed##" + std::to_string(windowId);

		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(viewportName.c_str(), dockMain);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(animatorName.c_str(), dockLeft);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(timelineName.c_str(), dockBottom);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(sequencerName.c_str(), dockSequencer);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(stateGraphName.c_str(), dockRight);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(animationSpeedName.c_str(), dockSequencer);

		ImGui::DockBuilderFinish(dockSpaceId);
	}

	// Viewportãƒ‘ãƒãƒ«
	std::string viewportName = "Viewport##" + std::to_string(windowId);
	ImGui::Begin(viewportName.c_str());

	// ç”»è³ªç¢ºèª
	// ImGui::Text("Render Resolution: %ux%u", renderWidth, renderHeight);

	ImVec2 region = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail();
	if (region.x > 1 && region.y > 1)
	{
		camera.UpdateAspect(region.x, region.y);
	}

	ImGui::Image(reinterpret_cast<ImTextureID>(srv.Get()), region);

	// ãƒ¢ãƒ‡ãƒ«ã®ãƒ‰ãƒ­ãƒƒãƒ—ã‚’å—ã‘å–ã‚‹
	if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget())
	{
		if (const ImGuiPayload* payload = ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("MODEL_PATH"))
		{
			std::string droppedModelPath(static_cast<const char*>(payload->Data));
			LoadAsset(droppedModelPath);
		}
		ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
	}

	// ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©æ“ä½œã®é–‹å§‹åˆ¤å®šã¯Viewportå˜ä½“ã®ãƒ›ãƒãƒ¼ã ã‘ã‚’è¦‹ã‚‹
	viewportHovered = ImGui::IsWindowHovered();

	ImGui::End(); // Viewport

	// Animatorãƒ‘ãƒãƒ«
	if (context.showAnimator)
	{
		studioAnimator.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	// Timelineãƒ‘ãƒãƒ«
	if (context.showTimeline)
	{
		studioTimeline.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	// Sequencerãƒ‘ãƒãƒ«
	if (context.showSequencer)
	{
		studioSequencer.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	// State Graphãƒ‘ãƒãƒ«
	if (context.showStateGraph)
	{
		stateGraph.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	// AnimationSpeedãƒ‘ãƒãƒ«
	if (context.showAnimationSpeed)
	{
		studioAnimationSpeed.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	stadoiModelViewer.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);

	for (auto& attachment : attachments)
		attachment->DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);

	ImGui::End(); // Studio
}

// ã‚¢ã‚¿ãƒƒãƒãƒ¡ãƒ³ãƒˆè¿½åŠ 
void StudioWindow::AddAttachment(std::unique_ptr<IStudioAttachment> attachment)
{
	attachments.push_back(std::move(attachment));
}

// ã‚¢ã‚»ãƒƒãƒˆèª­ã¿è¾¼ã¿
void StudioWindow::LoadAsset(const std::string& assetPath)
{
	stadoiModelViewer.LoadModel(studioRegistry, assetPath);
	context.currentModelPath = assetPath;

	// ãƒ¢ãƒ‡ãƒ«ãŒå¤‰ã‚ã£ãŸã“ã¨ã‚’ä¼ãˆã‚‹
	stateGraph.OnModelChanged(context);
}

void StudioWindow::Begin()
{
	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc = context.deviceContext;
	if (!dc) return;

	// ç¾åœ¨ã®ãƒ“ãƒ¥ãƒ¼ãƒãƒ¼ãƒˆã‚’ä¿å­˜
	UINT numVP = 1;
	dc->RSGetViewports(&numVP, &savedViewport);

	// è‡ªå‰ã®ãƒ“ãƒ¥ãƒ¼ãƒãƒ¼ãƒˆã‚’ã‚»ãƒƒãƒˆ
	D3D11_VIEWPORT vp{};
	vp.Width = static_cast<float>(renderWidth);
	vp.Height = static_cast<float>(renderHeight);
	vp.MinDepth = 0.0f;
	vp.MaxDepth = 1.0f;
	dc->RSSetViewports(1, &vp);

	// æç”»å…ˆã‚’è‡ªå‰RTVã«åˆ‡ã‚Šæ›¿ãˆ
	dc->OMSetRenderTargets(1, rtv.GetAddressOf(), dsv.Get());

	// ã‚¯ãƒªã‚¢
	float clear[4] = { 0.15f, 0.15f, 0.15f, 1.0f };
	dc->ClearRenderTargetView(rtv.Get(), clear);
	dc->ClearDepthStencilView(dsv.Get(), D3D11_CLEAR_DEPTH | D3D11_CLEAR_STENCIL, 1.0f, 0);
}
void StudioWindow::End()
{
	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc = context.deviceContext;
	if (!dc) return;

	// ãƒ“ãƒ¥ãƒ¼ãƒãƒ¼ãƒˆã‚’å…ƒã«æˆ»ã™
	dc->RSSetViewports(1, &savedViewport);

	// æç”»å…ˆã‚’ãƒ¡ã‚¤ãƒ³ã«æˆ»ã™
	ID3D11RenderTargetView* nullRTV = nullptr;
	dc->OMSetRenderTargets(1, &nullRTV, nullptr);
}
void StudioWindow::ResizeRenderTarget()
{
	ID3D11Device* device = context.device;
	if (!device) return;
	if (renderWidth == 0 || renderHeight == 0) return;

	// å¤ã„ãƒªã‚½ãƒ¼ã‚¹ã‚’è§£æ”¾
	srv.Reset();
	rtv.Reset();
	renderTexture.Reset();
	dsv.Reset();
	depthTexture.Reset();

	HRESULT hr;

	// ã‚«ãƒ©ãƒ¼ãƒ†ã‚¯ã‚¹ãƒãƒ£å†ç”Ÿæˆ
	{
		D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC desc{};
		desc.Width = renderWidth;
		desc.Height = renderHeight;
		desc.MipLevels = 1;
		desc.ArraySize = 1;
		desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT;
		desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
		desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
		desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_RENDER_TARGET | D3D11_BIND_SHADER_RESOURCE;

		hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&desc, nullptr, renderTexture.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
		hr = device->CreateRenderTargetView(renderTexture.Get(), nullptr, rtv.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
		hr = device->CreateShaderResourceView(renderTexture.Get(), nullptr, srv.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
	}

	// æ·±åº¦ãƒ†ã‚¯ã‚¹ãƒãƒ£å†ç”Ÿæˆ
	{
		D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC desc{};
		desc.Width = renderWidth;
		desc.Height = renderHeight;
		desc.MipLevels = 1;
		desc.ArraySize = 1;
		desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_D24_UNORM_S8_UINT;
		desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
		desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
		desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_DEPTH_STENCIL;

		hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&desc, nullptr, depthTexture.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
		hr = device->CreateDepthStencilView(depthTexture.Get(), nullptr, dsv.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
	}
}

// æœ€å¤§åŒ–ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³
void StudioWindow::DrawMaximizeButton()
{
	ImGuiWindow* window = ImGui::GetCurrentWindow();

	// ãƒ‰ãƒƒã‚­ãƒ³ã‚°ä¸­ã¯ã‚¿ã‚¤ãƒˆãƒ«ãƒãƒ¼ãŒã‚¿ãƒ–ã«ãªã‚‹ã®ã§å‡ºã•ãªã„
	if (window->DockIsActive || (window->Flags & ImGuiWindowFlags_NoTitleBar)) return;

	ImGuiStyle& style = ImGui::GetStyle();
	ImRect titleBar = window->TitleBarRect();

	// Ã—ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³ã¨åŒã˜å¤§ãã•ã§ã€Ã—ã®å·¦ã«ä¸¦ã¹ã‚‹(ImGuiãŒÃ—ã‚’ç½®ãä½ç½®ã®è¨ˆç®—ã«åˆã‚ã›ã‚‹)
	float buttonSize = ImGui::GetFontSize();
	float closeX = titleBar.Max.x - window->WindowBorderSize - style.FramePadding.x - buttonSize;
	ImVec2 pos(closeX - style.ItemInnerSpacing.x - buttonSize, titleBar.Min.y + style.FramePadding.y);
	ImRect bb(pos, ImVec2(pos.x + buttonSize, pos.y + buttonSize));

	// ã‚¿ã‚¤ãƒˆãƒ«ãƒãƒ¼ã¯ä¸­èº«ã®æç”»ç¯„å›²ã®å¤–ãªã®ã§ã€ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³ã‚’ä½œã‚‹é–“ã ã‘ç¯„å›²ã‚’åºƒã’ã‚‹
	ImGui::PushClipRect(titleBar.Min, titleBar.Max, false);

	// ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³ã®åˆ¤å®š(Ã—ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³ã¨åŒã˜ä½œã‚Šæ–¹)
	ImGuiID id = window->GetID("#MAXIMIZE");
	ImGui::ItemAdd(bb, id);
	bool hovered = false;
	bool held = false;
	bool pressed = ImGui::ButtonBehavior(bb, id, &hovered, &held);

	// ãƒã‚¦ã‚¹ãŒä¹—ã£ãŸã‚‰ã€Ã—ãƒœã‚¿ãƒ³ã¨åŒã˜ãä¸¸ã„èƒŒæ™¯ã‚’å‡ºã™
	ImDrawList* drawList = window->DrawList;
	ImVec2 center = bb.GetCenter();
	if (hovered)
	{
		ImU32 bg = ImGui::GetColorU32(held ? ImGuiCol_ButtonActive : ImGuiCol_ButtonHovered);
		drawList->AddCircleFilled(center, ImMax(2.0f, buttonSize * 0.5f + 1.0f), bg);
	}

	// å°ï¼šé€šå¸¸ã¯å››è§’1ã¤(æœ€å¤§åŒ–)ã€æœ€å¤§åŒ–ä¸­ã¯å››è§’2ã¤ã‚’é‡ã­ã‚‹(å…ƒã«æˆ»ã™)
	ImU32 color = ImGui::GetColorU32(ImGuiCol_Text);
	float half = buttonSize * 0.25f;
	if (!maximized)
	{
		drawList->AddRect(ImVec2(center.x - half, center.y - half), ImVec2(center.x + half, center.y + half), color);
	}
	else
	{
		float offset = half * 0.4f;
		drawList->AddRect(ImVec2(center.x - half + offset, center.y - half - offset), ImVec2(center.x + half + offset, center.y + half - offset), color);
		drawList->AddRect(ImVec2(center.x - half - offset, center.y - half + offset), ImVec2(center.x + half - offset, center.y + half + offset), color);
	}

	ImGui::PopClipRect();

	// èª¬æ˜
	if (hovered)
	{
		ImGui::SetTooltip("%s", maximized ? "Restore" : "Maximize");
	}

	if (!pressed) return;

	if (!maximized)
	{
		// ä»Šã®ä½ç½®ã¨å¤§ãã•ã‚’è¦šãˆã¦ã€åºƒã’ã‚‹å…ˆã‚’äºˆç´„ã™ã‚‹
		restorePos = ImGui::GetWindowPos();
		restoreSize = ImGui::GetWindowSize();
		GetMaximizeArea(restorePos, restoreSize, applyPos, applySize);
		maximized = true;
	}
	else
	{
		// è¦šãˆã¦ãŠã„ãŸä½ç½®ã¨å¤§ãã•ã«æˆ»ã™
		applyPos = restorePos;
		applySize = restoreSize;
		maximized = false;
	}

	// ä½ç½®ã¨å¤§ãã•ã¯ã€æ¬¡ã®ãƒ•ãƒ¬ãƒ¼ãƒ ã®Beginã®å‰ã«åæ˜ ã™ã‚‹
	applyWindowRect = true;
}
